




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

saṃyuttanikāyau


mahāvaggau


1. maggasaṃyuttaṃ

1. avij‍jāvaggau

1. avij‍jāsuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘avij‍jā, bhikkhavai, pubbaṅgamā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā, anvadaiva anudaiva (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ| avij‍jāgatassa, bhikkhavai, aviddasunau micchādiṭṭhi pahauti; micchādiṭṭhissa micchāsaṅkappau pahauti; micchāsaṅkappassa micchāvācā pahauti; micchāvācassa micchākammantau pahauti; micchākammantassa micchāājīvau pahauti; micchāājīvassa micchāvāyāmau pahauti; micchāvāyāmassa micchāsati pahauti; micchāsatissa micchāsamādhi pahauti|

‘‘vij‍jā  ca khau, bhikkhavai, pubbaṅgamā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā, anvadaiva hirauttappaṃ| vij‍jāgatassa, bhikkhavai, viddasunau sammādiṭṭhi  pahauti; sammādiṭṭhissa sammāsaṅkappau pahauti; sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācā pahauti; sammāvācassa sammākammantau pahauti; sammākammantassa sammāājīvau pahauti; sammāājīvassa sammāvāyāmau pahauti; sammāvāyāmassa sammāsati pahauti; sammāsatissa sammāsamādhi pahautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. upaḍḍhasuttaṃ

2. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sakyaisu viharati nagarakaṃ nāma nāgarakaṃ nāma (sī॰), sak‍karaṃ nāma (syā॰ ka॰) sakyānaṃ nigamau| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘upaḍḍhamidaṃ, bhantai, brahmacariyaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā kalyāṇasahāyatā kalyāṇasampavaṅkatā’’ti|

‘‘mā haivaṃ, ānanda, mā haivaṃ, ānanda! sakalamaividaṃ, ānanda, brahmacariyaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā kalyāṇasahāyatā kalyāṇasampavaṅkatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idhānanda, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ; sammāsaṅkappaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ  …pai॰… sammāvācaṃ bhāvaiti  …pai॰… sammākammantaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāājīvaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāvāyāmaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsatiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti|

‘‘tadamināpaitaṃ, ānanda, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sakalamaividaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā kalyāṇasahāyatā kalyāṇasampavaṅkatā| mamañhi, ānanda, kalyāṇamittaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuc‍canti; jarādhammā sattā jarāya parimuc‍canti; maraṇadhammā sattā maraṇaina parimuc‍canti; saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā sattā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍canti| iminā khau aitaṃ, ānanda, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sakalamaividaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā kalyāṇasahāyatā kalyāṇasampavaṅkatā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sāriputtasuttaṃ

3. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sakalamidaṃ, bhantai, brahmacariyaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā kalyāṇasahāyatā kalyāṇasampavaṅkatā’’ti|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, sāriputta! sakalamidaṃ, sāriputta, brahmacariyaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā kalyāṇasahāyatā kalyāṇasampavaṅkatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ  bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, sāriputta, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti?

‘‘idha, sāriputta, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ  vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, sāriputta, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti|

‘‘tadamināpaitaṃ, sāriputta, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sakalamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā kalyāṇasahāyatā kalyāṇasampavaṅkatā| mamañhi, sāriputta, kalyāṇamittaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā  parimuc‍canti; jarādhammā sattā jarāya parimuc‍canti; maraṇadhammā sattā maraṇaina parimuc‍canti; saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā sattā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsaihi parimuc‍canti| iminā khau aitaṃ, sāriputta, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ  yathā sakalamidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā kalyāṇasahāyatā kalyāṇasampavaṅkatā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. jāṇussauṇibrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

4. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau jāṇussauṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ sabbasaitaina vaḷavābhirathaina vaḷabhīrathaina (sī॰) sāvatthiyā niyyāyantaṃ| saitā sudaṃ assā yuttā haunti saitālaṅkārā, saitau rathau, saitaparivārau, saitā rasmiyau, saitā pataudalaṭṭhi, saitaṃ chattaṃ, saitaṃ uṇhīsaṃ, saitāni vatthāni, saitā upāhanā, saitāya sudaṃ vālabījaniyā bījīyati| tamainaṃ janau disvā aivamāha – ‘‘brahmaṃ vata, bhau, yānaṃ! brahmayānarūpaṃ vata, bhau’’ti!!

atha khau āyasmā ānandau sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| addasaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, jāṇussauṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ sabbasaitaina vaḷavābhirathaina sāvatthiyā niyyāyantaṃ| saitā sudaṃ assā yuttā haunti saitālaṅkārā, saitau rathau, saitaparivārau, saitā rasmiyau, saitā pataudalaṭṭhi, saitaṃ chattaṃ, saitaṃ uṇhīsaṃ, saitāni vatthāni, saitā  upāhanā, saitāya sudaṃ vālabījaniyā bījīyati| tamainaṃ janau disvā aivamāha – ‘brahmaṃ vata, bhau, yānaṃ! brahmayānarūpaṃ vata, bhau’ti!! sak‍kā nu khau, bhantai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai brahmayānaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti?

‘‘sak‍kā, ānandā’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘imassaiva khau aitaṃ, ānanda, ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa adhivacanaṃ – ‘brahmayānaṃ’ itipi, ‘dhammayānaṃ’ itipi, ‘anuttarau saṅgāmavijayau’ itipī’’ti|

‘‘sammādiṭṭhi, ānanda, bhāvitā bahulīkatā rāgavinayapariyausānā hauti, dausavinayapariyausānā hauti, mauhavinayapariyausānā hauti| sammāsaṅkappau, ānanda, bhāvitau bahulīkatau rāgavinayapariyausānau hauti, dausavinayapariyausānau hauti, mauhavinayapariyausānau hauti| sammāvācā, ānanda, bhāvitā bahulīkatā rāgavinayapariyausānā hauti, dausa…pai॰… mauhavinayapariyausānā hauti| sammākammantau, ānanda, bhāvitau bahulīkatau rāgavinayapariyausānau hauti, dausa… mauhavinayapariyausānau hauti| sammāājīvau, ānanda, bhāvitau bahulīkatau rāgavinayapariyausānau hauti, dausa… mauhavinayapariyausānau hauti| sammāvāyāmau, ānanda, bhāvitau bahulīkatau rāgavinayapariyausānau  hauti, dausa… mauhavinayapariyausānau hauti| sammāsati, ānanda, bhāvitā bahulīkatā rāgavinayapariyausānā hauti, dausa… mauhavinayapariyausānā hauti| sammāsamādhi, ānanda, bhāvitau bahulīkatau rāgavinayapariyausānau  hauti, dausa… mauhavinayapariyausānau hauti|

‘‘iminā khau aitaṃ, ānanda, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā imassaivaitaṃ ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa adhivacanaṃ – ‘brahmayānaṃ’ itipi, ‘dhammayānaṃ’ itipi, ‘anuttarau saṅgāmavijayau’ itipī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘yassa saddhā ca pañ‍ñā ca, dhammā yuttā sadā dhuraṃ|

hirī īsā manau yauttaṃ, sati ārakkhasārathi||

‘‘rathau sīlaparikkhārau, jhānakkhau cak‍kavīriyau|

upaikkhā dhurasamādhi, anicchā parivāraṇaṃ||

‘‘abyāpādau avihiṃsā, vivaikau yassa āvudhaṃ|

titikkhā cammasan‍nāhau vammasan‍nāhau (sī॰), yaugakkhaimāya vattati||

‘‘aitadattani  sambhūtaṃ, brahmayānaṃ anuttaraṃ|

niyyanti dhīrā laukamhā, añ‍ñadatthu jayaṃ jaya’’nti|| catutthaṃ|

5. kimatthiyasuttaṃ

5. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘idha nau, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā amhai aivaṃ pucchanti – ‘kimatthiyaṃ, āvusau, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā mayaṃ, bhantai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ  paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākarauma – ‘dukkhassa khau, āvusau, pariñ‍ñatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti| kac‍ci mayaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ puṭṭhā aivaṃ byākaramānā vuttavādinau caiva bhagavatau hauma, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhāma, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ  byākarauma, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’’ti?

‘‘taggha tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ puṭṭhā aivaṃ byākaramānā vuttavādinau caiva mai hautha, na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhatha, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākarautha, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati| dukkhassa hi pariñ‍ñatthaṃ mayi brahmacariyaṃ vussati| sacai vau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘atthi panāvusau, maggau, atthi paṭipadā aitassa dukkhassa  pariñ‍ñāyā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau, atthi paṭipadā aitassa dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāyā’’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, maggau, katamā paṭipadā aitassa dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāyāti? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau, ayaṃ paṭipadā aitassa dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāyāti| aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyāthā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamaañ‍ñatarabhikkhusuttaṃ

6. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘brahmacariyaṃ, brahmacariya’nti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, bhantai, brahmacariyaṃ, katamaṃ brahmacariyapariyausāna’’nti?

‘‘ayamaiva khau, bhikkhu, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau brahmacariyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| yau khau, bhikkhu, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ brahmacariyapariyausāna’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyaañ‍ñatarabhikkhusuttaṃ

7. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘rāgavinayau dausavinayau mauhavinayau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kissa nu khau aitaṃ, bhantai, adhivacanaṃ – ‘rāgavinayau dausavinayau mauhavinayau’’’ti? ‘‘nibbānadhātuyā khau aitaṃ, bhikkhu, adhivacanaṃ – ‘rāgavinayau dausavinayau mauhavinayau’ti| āsavānaṃ khayau taina vuc‍catī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘amataṃ, amata’nti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, bhantai, amataṃ, katamau amatagāmimaggau’’ti? ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhu, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati amataṃ| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau amatagāmimaggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. vibhaṅgasuttaṃ

8. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘ariyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ daisaissāmi vibhajissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhai  ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai  ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammādiṭṭhi|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau? yau khau, bhikkhavai, naikkhammasaṅkappau, abyāpādasaṅkappau, avihiṃsāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāsaṅkappau|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sammāvācā? yā khau, bhikkhavai, musāvādā vairamaṇī, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāvācā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammākammantau? yā khau, bhikkhavai, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, abrahmacariyā vairamaṇī – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammākammantau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammāājīvau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau micchāājīvaṃ pahāya sammāājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāājīvau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammāvāyāmau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati – ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāvāyāmau|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sammāsati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; cittai cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau  satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati  ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāsati|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, sammāsamādhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāsamādhī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sūkasuttaṃ

9. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sālisūkaṃ vā yavasūkaṃ vā micchāpaṇihitaṃ hatthaina vā pādaina vā ak‍kantaṃ hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā bhindissati bhaicchati (ka॰), lauhitaṃ vā uppādaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? micchāpaṇihitattā, bhikkhavai, sūkassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, sau vata bhikkhu micchāpaṇihitāya diṭṭhiyā micchāpaṇihitāya maggabhāvanāya avij‍jaṃ bhindissati, vij‍jaṃ uppādaissati, nibbānaṃ sacchikarissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? micchāpaṇihitattā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiyā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sālisūkaṃ vā yavasūkaṃ vā sammāpaṇihitaṃ hatthaina vā pādaina vā ak‍kantaṃ hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā bhindissati, lauhitaṃ vā uppādaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? sammāpaṇihitattā, bhikkhavai, sūkassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, sau vata bhikkhu sammāpaṇihitāya diṭṭhiyā sammāpaṇihitāya maggabhāvanāya avij‍jaṃ  bhindissati, vij‍jaṃ uppādaissati, nibbānaṃ sacchikarissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? sammāpaṇihitattā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiyā|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammāpaṇihitāya diṭṭhiyā sammāpaṇihitāya maggabhāvanāya avij‍jaṃ bhindati, vij‍jaṃ uppādaiti, nibbānaṃ sacchikarautīti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ  vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ  bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammāpaṇihitāya diṭṭhiyā sammāpaṇihitāya maggabhāvanāya avij‍jaṃ bhindati, vij‍jaṃ uppādaiti, nibbānaṃ sacchikarautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. nandiyasuttaṃ

10. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau nandiyau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau nandiyau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, bhau gautama, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā nibbānaṅgamā haunti nibbānaparāyanā nibbānapariyausānā’’ti?

‘‘aṭṭhimai khau, nandiya, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā nibbānaṅgamā haunti nibbānaparāyanā nibbānapariyausānā| katamai aṭṭha? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| imai khau, nandiya, aṭṭha dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā nibbānaṅgamā haunti nibbānaparāyanā nibbānapariyausānā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai nandiyau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama  …pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| dasamaṃ|

avij‍jāvaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

avij‍jañ‍ca upaḍḍhañ‍ca, sāriputtau ca brāhmaṇau|

kimatthiyau ca dvai bhikkhū, vibhaṅgau sūkanandiyāti||

2. vihāravaggau

1. paṭhamavihārasuttaṃ

11. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aḍḍhamāsaṃ paṭisal‍liyituṃ| namhi kainaci upasaṅkamitabbau, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakainā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti  khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissutvā nāssudha kauci bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamati, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina|

atha khau bhagavā tassa aḍḍhamāsassa ac‍cayaina paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yaina svāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, vihāraina paṭhamābhisambuddhau viharāmi, tassa padaisaina vihāsiṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāmi – ‘micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ…pai॰…  micchāsamādhipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; sammāsamādhipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; chandapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; vitak‍kapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; sañ‍ñāpac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; chandau ca avūpasantau hauti, vitak‍kau ca avūpasantau hauti, sañ‍ñā ca avūpasantā hauti, tappac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; chandau  ca vūpasantau hauti, vitak‍kau ca vūpasantau hauti, sañ‍ñā ca vūpasantā hauti, tappac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; appattassa pattiyā atthi āyāmaṃ vāyāmaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), tasmimpi ṭhānai anuppattai tappac‍cayāpi vaidayita’’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyavihārasuttaṃ

12. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhikkhavai, taimāsaṃ paṭisal‍liyituṃ| namhi kainaci upasaṅkamitabbau, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakainā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissutvā nāssudha kauci bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamati, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina|

atha khau bhagavā tassa taimāsassa ac‍cayaina paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yaina svāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, vihāraina paṭhamābhisambuddhau viharāmi, tassa padaisaina vihāsiṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāmi – ‘micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; micchādiṭṭhivūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; sammādiṭṭhivūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ…pai॰…  micchāsamādhipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; micchāsamādhivūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ, sammāsamādhipac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; sammāsamādhivūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; chandapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; chandavūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; vitak‍kapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; vitak‍kavūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; sañ‍ñāpac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; sañ‍ñāvūpasamapac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; chandau ca avūpasantau hauti, vitak‍kau ca avūpasantau hauti, sañ‍ñā ca avūpasantā hauti, tappac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; chandau  ca vūpasantau hauti, vitak‍kau ca vūpasantau hauti, sañ‍ñā ca vūpasantā hauti, tappac‍cayāpi vaidayitaṃ; appattassa pattiyā atthi āyāmaṃ vāyāmaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), tasmimpi ṭhānai anuppattai tappac‍cayāpi vaidayita’’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saikkhasuttaṃ

13. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘‘saikkhau, saikkhau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, saikkhau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, saikkhāya sammādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti…pai॰… saikkhaina sammāsamādhinā saman‍nāgatau hauti| aittāvatā khau, bhikkhu, saikkhau hautī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamauppādasuttaṃ

14. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra tathāgatassa pātubhāvā arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| katamai aṭṭha? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra tathāgatassa pātubhāvā arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyauppādasuttaṃ

15. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai aṭṭha? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamaparisuddhasuttaṃ

16. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā parisuddhā pariyaudātā anaṅgaṇā vigatūpak‍kilaisā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra tathāgatassa pātubhāvā arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| katamai aṭṭha? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha dhammā parisuddhā pariyaudātā  anaṅgaṇā vigatūpak‍kilaisā  anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra tathāgatassa pātubhāvā arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyaparisuddhasuttaṃ

17. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā parisuddhā pariyaudātā anaṅgaṇā vigatūpak‍kilaisā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai aṭṭha? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha dhammā parisuddhā pariyaudātā anaṅgaṇā vigatūpak‍kilaisā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭhamakuk‍kuṭārāmasuttaṃ

18. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca ānandau āyasmā ca bhaddau pāṭaliputtai viharanti kuk‍kuṭārāmai| atha khau āyasmā bhaddau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ  kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā bhaddau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘abrahmacariyaṃ, abrahmacariya’nti, āvusau ānanda, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, āvusau, abrahmacariya’’nti? ‘‘sādhu  sādhu, āvusau bhadda! bhaddakau khau tai, āvusau bhadda, ummaṅgau, bhaddakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ, kalyāṇī paripucchā| aivañhi tvaṃ, āvusau bhadda, pucchasi – ‘abrahmacariyaṃ, abrahmacariyanti, āvusau ānanda, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, āvusau, abrahmacariya’’’nti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, aṭṭhaṅgikau micchāmaggau abrahmacariyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāsamādhī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyakuk‍kuṭārāmasuttaṃ

19. pāṭaliputtanidānaṃ| ‘‘‘brahmacariyaṃ, brahmacariya’nti, āvusau ānanda, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, āvusau, brahmacariyaṃ, katamaṃ brahmacariyapariyausāna’’nti? ‘‘sādhu sādhu, āvusau bhadda! bhaddakau khau tai, āvusau bhadda, ummaṅgau, bhaddakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ, kalyāṇī paripucchā| aivañhi tvaṃ, āvusau bhadda, pucchasi – ‘brahmacariyaṃ, brahmacariyanti, āvusau ānanda, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, āvusau, brahmacariyaṃ, katamaṃ brahmacariyapariyausāna’’’nti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau brahmacariyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| yau khau, āvusau, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ brahmacariyapariyausāna’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. tatiyakuk‍kuṭārāmasuttaṃ

20. pāṭaliputtanidānaṃ| ‘‘‘brahmacariyaṃ, brahmacariya’nti, āvusau ānanda, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, āvusau, brahmacariyaṃ, katamau brahmacārī, katamaṃ brahmacariyapariyausāna’’nti? ‘‘sādhu  sādhu, āvusau bhadda! bhaddakau khau tai, āvusau bhadda, ummaṅgau, bhaddakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ, kalyāṇī paripucchā| aivañhi tvaṃ, āvusau bhadda, pucchasi – ‘brahmacariyaṃ, brahmacariyanti, āvusau  ānanda, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, āvusau, brahmacariyaṃ, katamau brahmacārī, katamaṃ brahmacariyapariyausāna’’’nti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau  maggau brahmacariyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| yau khau, āvusau, iminā ariyaina aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina saman‍nāgatau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati brahmacārī| yau khau, āvusau, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – idaṃ brahmacariyapariyausāna’’nti| dasamaṃ|

tīṇi suttantāni aikanidānāni|vihāravaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dvai vihārā ca saikkhau ca, uppādā aparai duvai|

parisuddhaina dvai vuttā, kuk‍kuṭārāmaina tayauti||

3. micchattavaggau

1. micchattasuttaṃ

21. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘micchattañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, sammattañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, micchattaṃ? saiyyathidaṃ – micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, micchattaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sammattaṃ? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammatta’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. akusaladhammasuttaṃ

22. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘akusalai ca khau, bhikkhavai, dhammai daisaissāmi, kusalai ca dhammai| taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, akusalā dhammā? saiyyathidaṃ – micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāsamādhi| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, akusalā dhammā| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, kusalā dhammā? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, kusalā dhammā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamapaṭipadāsuttaṃ

23. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘micchāpaṭipadañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, sammāpaṭipadañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipadā? saiyyathidaṃ – micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipadā| katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, sammāpaṭipadā? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāpaṭipadā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyapaṭipadāsuttaṃ

24. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘gihinau vāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, pabbajitassa vā micchāpaṭipadaṃ na vaṇṇaimi| gihi vā, bhikkhavai, pabbajitau vā micchāpaṭipan‍nau micchāpaṭipattādhikaraṇahaitu  nārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ’’|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipadā? saiyyathidaṃ – micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipadā| gihinau vāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, pabbajitassa vā micchāpaṭipadaṃ na vaṇṇaimi| gihi vā, bhikkhavai, pabbajitau vā micchāpaṭipan‍nau micchāpaṭipattādhikaraṇahaitu nārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘gihinau vāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, pabbajitassa vā sammāpaṭipadaṃ vaṇṇaimi| gihi vā, bhikkhavai, pabbajitau vā sammāpaṭipan‍nau sammāpaṭipattādhikaraṇahaitu ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusalaṃ| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sammāpaṭipadā? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāpaṭipadā| gihinau vāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, pabbajitassa vā sammāpaṭipadaṃ vaṇṇaimi| gihi vā, bhikkhavai, pabbajitau vā sammāpaṭipan‍nau sammāpaṭipattādhikaraṇahaitu ārādhakau hauti ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ kusala’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamaasappurisasuttaṃ

25. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘asappurisañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, sappurisañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, micchāsaṅkappau, micchāvācau, micchākammantau, micchāājīvau, micchāvāyāmau, micchāsati, micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisau’’|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācau, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaasappurisasuttaṃ

26. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘asappurisañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, asappurisaina asappurisatarañ‍ca| sappurisañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi sappurisaina sappurisatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti…pai॰… micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisau’’|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti…pai॰… micchāsamādhi, micchāñāṇī, micchāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau  hauti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau hauti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi, sammāñāṇī, sammāvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kumbhasuttaṃ

27. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kumbhau anādhārau suppavattiyau hauti, sādhārau duppavattiyau hauti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ anādhāraṃ suppavattiyaṃ hauti, sādhāraṃ duppavattiyaṃ hauti| kau  ca, bhikkhavai, cittassa ādhārau? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ cittassa ādhārau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kumbhau anādhārau suppavattiyau hauti, sādhārau duppavattiyau hauti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ anādhāraṃ suppavattiyaṃ hauti, sādhāraṃ duppavattiyaṃ hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. samādhisuttaṃ

28. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘ariyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, sammāsamādhiṃ daisaissāmi saupanisaṃ saparikkhāraṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, ariyau sammāsamādhi saupanisau saparikkhārau? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsati sammāsamādhi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| yā  khau, bhikkhavai, imaihi sattahaṅgaihi cittassa aikaggatā saparikkhāratā saparikkhatā (sī॰ pī॰) – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyau sammāsamādhi saupanisau itipi saparikkhārau itipī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vaidanāsuttaṃ

29. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vaidanā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. uttiyasuttaṃ

30. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā uttiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā uttiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau  udapādi – ‘pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā vuttā bhagavatā| katamai nu khau pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā vuttā bhagavatā’’’ti? ‘‘sādhu sādhu, uttiya! pañ‍cimai khau, uttiya, kāmaguṇā vuttā mayā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā  phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā – imai khau, uttiya, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā vuttā mayā| imaisaṃ khau, uttiya, pañ‍can‍naṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ pahānāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| imaisaṃ khau, uttiya, pañ‍can‍naṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ pahānāya ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

micchattavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

micchattaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ, duvai paṭipadāpi ca|

asappurisaina dvai kumbhau, samādhi vaidanuttiyaināti||

4. paṭipattivaggau

1. paṭhamapaṭipattisuttaṃ

31. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘micchāpaṭipattiñ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, sammāpaṭipattiñ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipatti? saiyyathidaṃ – micchādiṭṭhi…pai॰… micchāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipatti| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sammāpaṭipatti? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāpaṭipattī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyapaṭipattisuttaṃ

32. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘micchāpaṭipan‍nañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, sammāpaṭipan‍nañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti…pai॰… micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipan‍nau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sammāpaṭipan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau hauti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāpaṭipan‍nau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. viraddhasuttaṃ

33. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau viraddhau, viraddhau taisaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau āraddhau, āraddhau taisaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau viraddhau, viraddhau taisaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau āraddhau, āraddhau taisaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. pāraṅgamasuttaṃ

34. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya  saṃvattanti| katamai aṭṭha? saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya saṃvattantī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘appakā tai manussaisu, yai janā pāragāminau|

athāyaṃ itarā pajā, tīramaivānudhāvati||

‘‘yai ca khau sammadakkhātai, dhammai dhammānuvattinau|

tai janā pāramaissanti, mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttaraṃ||

‘‘kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ vippahāya, suk‍kaṃ bhāvaitha paṇḍitau|

aukā anaukamāgamma, vivaikai yattha dūramaṃ||

‘‘tatrābhiratimicchaiyya, hitvā kāmai akiñ‍canau|

pariyaudapaiyya attānaṃ, cittak‍laisaihi paṇḍitau||

‘‘yaisaṃ  sambaudhiyaṅgaisu, sammā cittaṃ subhāvitaṃ|

ādānapaṭinissaggai, anupādāya yai ratā|

khīṇāsavā jutimantau, tai laukai parinibbutā’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamasāmañ‍ñasuttaṃ

35. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sāmañ‍ñañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, sāmañ‍ñaphalāni ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sāmañ‍ñaṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sāmañ‍ñaṃ| katamāni ca, bhikkhavai, sāmañ‍ñaphalāni? sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattaphalaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, sāmañ‍ñaphalānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyasāmañ‍ñasuttaṃ

36. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sāmañ‍ñañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, sāmañ‍ñaṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sāmañ‍ñaṃ| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sāmañ‍ñatthau? yau khau, bhikkhavai, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sāmañ‍ñatthau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamabrahmañ‍ñasuttaṃ

37. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘brahmañ‍ñañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, brahmañ‍ñaphalāni ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, brahmañ‍ñaṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, brahmañ‍ñaṃ| katamāni ca, bhikkhavai, brahmañ‍ñaphalāni? sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattaphalaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, brahmañ‍ñaphalānī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyabrahmañ‍ñasuttaṃ

38. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘brahmañ‍ñañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, brahmañ‍ñaṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, brahmañ‍ñaṃ| katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, brahmañ‍ñatthau? yau khau, bhikkhavai, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, brahmañ‍ñatthau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamabrahmacariyasuttaṃ

39. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘brahmacariyañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, brahmacariyaphalāni ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ  – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ| katamāni  ca, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaphalāni? sautāpattiphalaṃ, sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, anāgāmiphalaṃ, arahattaphalaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaphalānī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyabrahmacariyasuttaṃ

40. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘brahmacariyañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, brahmacariyatthañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ| katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyatthau? yau khau, bhikkhavai, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyatthau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

paṭipattivaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

paṭipatti paṭipan‍nau ca, viraddhañ‍ca pāraṃgamā|

sāmañ‍ñaina ca dvai vuttā, brahmañ‍ñā aparai duvai|

brahmacariyaina dvai vuttā, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

5. añ‍ñatitthiyapaiyyālavaggau

1. rāgavirāgasuttaṃ

41. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sacai vau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kimatthiyaṃ, āvusau, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, aivaṃ  puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘rāgavirāgatthaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti| sacai pana vau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā  aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘atthi panāvusau, maggau, atthi paṭipadā rāgavirāgāyā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau, atthi paṭipadā rāgavirāgāyā’ti| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, maggau, katamā ca paṭipadā rāgavirāgāya? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau, ayaṃ paṭipadā rāgavirāgāyāti| aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyāthā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-7. saṃyaujanappahānādisuttachak‍kaṃ

42-47. ‘‘sacai vau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kimatthiyaṃ, āvusau, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ  paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘saṃyaujanappahānatthaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti…pai॰… ‘anusayasamugghātanatthaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti…pai॰… ‘addhānapariñ‍ñatthaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti…pai॰… ‘āsavānaṃ khayatthaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti…pai॰… ‘vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyatthaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti…pai॰… ‘ñāṇadassanatthaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. anupādāparinibbānasuttaṃ

48. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sacai  vau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kimatthiyaṃ, āvusau, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘anupādāparinibbānatthaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti| sacai pana vau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘atthi panāvusau, maggau, atthi paṭipadā anupādāparinibbānāyā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ  paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha  – ‘atthi khau, āvusau, maggau, atthi paṭipadā anupādāparinibbānāyā’ti| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, maggau, katamā ca paṭipadā anupādāparinibbānāya? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau, ayaṃ paṭipadā anupādāparinibbānāyāti| aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyāthā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

añ‍ñatitthiyapaiyyālavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

virāgasaṃyaujanaṃ anusayaṃ, addhānaṃ āsavā khayā|

vij‍jāvimuttiñāṇañ‍ca, anupādāya aṭṭhamī||

6. sūriyapaiyyālavaggau

1. kalyāṇamittasuttaṃ

49. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sūriyassa, bhikkhavai, udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – aruṇuggaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa uppādāya aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰…  sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. sīlasampadādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

50-54. ‘‘sūriyassa, bhikkhavai, udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – aruṇuggaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa uppādāya  aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – sīlasampadā| sīlasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ… yadidaṃ  – chandasampadā… yadidaṃ – attasampadā… yadidaṃ – diṭṭhisampadā… yadidaṃ – appamādasampadā…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. yaunisaumanasikārasampadāsuttaṃ

55. ‘‘sūriyassa, bhikkhavai, udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – aruṇuggaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa uppādāya aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimmittaṃ, yadidaṃ – yaunisaumanasikārasampadā| yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

1. kalyāṇamittasuttaṃ

56. ‘‘sūriyassa, bhikkhavai, udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – aruṇuggaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa uppādāya aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. sīlasampadādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

57-61. ‘‘sūriyassa, bhikkhavai, udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – aruṇuggaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa uppādāya aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – sīlasampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ  – chandasampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ – attasampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ – diṭṭhisampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ – appamādasampadā…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. yaunisaumanasikārasampadāsuttaṃ

62. ‘‘yadidaṃ  – yaunisaumanasikārasampadā| yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ  maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

sūriyapaiyyālavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kalyāṇamittaṃ sīlañ‍ca, chandau ca attasampadā|

diṭṭhi ca appamādau ca, yaunisau bhavati sattamaṃ||

7. aikadhammapaiyyālavaggau

1. kalyāṇamittasuttaṃ

63. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘aikadhammau, bhikkhavai, bahūpakārau ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa uppādāya| katamau aikadhammau? yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ  virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. sīlasampadādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

64-68. ‘‘aikadhammau, bhikkhavai, bahūpakārau ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa uppādāya| katamau aikadhammau? yadidaṃ – sīlasampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ – chandasampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ – attasampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ – diṭṭhisampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ  – appamādasampadā…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. yaunisaumanasikārasampadāsuttaṃ

69. ‘‘yadidaṃ – yaunisaumanasikārasampadā| yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

1. kalyāṇamittasuttaṃ

70. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘aikadhammau, bhikkhavai, bahūpakārau ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa uppādāya| katamau aikadhammau? yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ  aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ  mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. sīlasampadādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

71-75. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘aikadhammau, bhikkhavai, bahūpakārau ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa uppādāya| katamau aikadhammau? yadidaṃ – sīlasampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ – chandasampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ – attasampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ – diṭṭhisampadā…pai॰… yadidaṃ  – appamādasampadā…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. yaunisaumanasikārasampadāsuttaṃ

76. ‘‘yadidaṃ – yaunisaumanasikārasampadā| yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

aikadhammapaiyyālavaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kalyāṇamittaṃ  sīlañ‍ca, chandau ca attasampadā|

diṭṭhi ca appamādau ca, yaunisau bhavati sattamaṃ||

8. dutiyaaikadhammapaiyyālavaggau

1. kalyāṇamittasuttaṃ

77. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikadhammampi samanupassāmi, yaina anuppan‍nau vā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau vā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati, yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇamittatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. sīlasampadādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

78-82. ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikadhammampi samanupassāmi, yaina anuppan‍nau vā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau vā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati, yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadā…pai॰… yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandasampadā…pai॰… yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attasampadā…pai॰… yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampadā…pai॰… yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, appamādasampadā…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. yaunisaumanasikārasampadāsuttaṃ

83. ‘‘yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisaumanasikārasampadā| yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

1. kalyāṇamittasuttaṃ

84. ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikadhammampi samanupassāmi, yaina anuppan‍nau vā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau vā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati, yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇamittatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. sīlasampadādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

85-89. ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikadhammampi samanupassāmi, yaina anuppan‍nau vā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau vā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati, yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadā…pai॰… yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandasampadā…pai॰… yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attasampadā…pai॰… yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampadā…pai॰… yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, appamādasampadā…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. yaunisaumanasikārasampadāsuttaṃ

90. ‘‘yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisaumanasikārasampadā| yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ  dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

dutiyaaikadhammapaiyyālavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kalyāṇamittaṃ sīlañ‍ca, chandau ca attasampadā|

diṭṭhi ca appamādau ca, yaunisau bhavati sattamaṃ||

1. gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1. paṭhamapācīnanin‍nasuttaṃ

91. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-5. dutiyādipācīnanin‍nasuttacatuk‍kaṃ

92-95. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yamunā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aciravatī nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sarabhū nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahī nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. chaṭṭhapācīnanin‍nasuttaṃ

96. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kācimā mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, sabbā tā pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ  aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

1. paṭhamasamuddanin‍nasuttaṃ

97. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. dutiyādisamuddanin‍nasuttapañ‍cakaṃ

98-102. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yamunā nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aciravatī nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sarabhū nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahī  nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kācimā mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, sabbā tā samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ  bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ  maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

aitai dvai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti|

gaṅgāpaiyyālī pācīnanin‍navācanamaggī, vivaikanissitaṃ dvādasakī paṭhamakī||

2. dutiyagaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1. paṭhamapācīnanin‍nasuttaṃ

103. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ  dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. dutiyādipācīnanin‍nasuttapañ‍cakaṃ

104. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yamunā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… dutiyaṃ|

105. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aciravatī nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… tatiyaṃ|

106. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sarabhū nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰…  catutthaṃ|

107. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahī nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ|

108. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kācimā mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, sabbā tā  pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… chaṭṭhaṃ|

1. paṭhamasamuddanin‍nasuttaṃ

109. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau  nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. dutiyādisamuddanin‍nasuttapañ‍cakaṃ

110. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yamunā nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… dutiyaṃ|

111. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aciravatī  nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… tatiyaṃ|

112. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sarabhū nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰…  catutthaṃ|

113. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahī nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ|

114. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kācimā mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, sabbā tā samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰…  sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

(rāgavinayadvādasakī dutiyakī samuddanin‍nanti)|

1. paṭhamapācīnanin‍nasuttaṃ

115. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ  aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. dutiyādipācīnanin‍nasuttapañ‍cakaṃ

116. saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yamunā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… dutiyaṃ|

117. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aciravatī nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… tatiyaṃ|

118. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sarabhū nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… catutthaṃ|

119. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahī nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ|

120. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kācimā mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, sabbā tā pācīnanin‍nā  pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰…  chaṭṭhaṃ|

1. paṭhamasamuddanin‍nasuttaṃ

121. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. dutiyādisamuddanin‍nasuttapañ‍cakaṃ

122-126. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yamunā nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aciravatī nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sarabhū nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahī nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā  samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kācimā mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, sabbā tā samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ  aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

(amataugadhadvādasakī tatiyakī)|

1. paṭhamapācīnanin‍nasuttaṃ

127. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau  nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. dutiyādipācīnanin‍nasuttapañ‍cakaṃ

128-132. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yamunā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aciravatī nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sarabhū nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahī nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā  pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kācimā mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, sabbā tā pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau  nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

1. paṭhamasamuddanin‍nasuttaṃ

133. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2-6. dutiyādisamuddanin‍nasuttapañ‍cakaṃ

134-138. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yamunā nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aciravatī nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sarabhū nadī samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahī nadī samuddanin‍nā  samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kācimā mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī sarabhū, mahī, sabbā tā samuddanin‍nā samuddapauṇā samuddapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

(gaṅgāpaiyyālī)|

dutiyagaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha  pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

aitai dvai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti|

nibbānanin‍nau dvādasakī, catutthakī chaṭṭhā navakī||

5. appamādapaiyyālavaggau

1. tathāgatasuttaṃ

139. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattā apadā vā dvipadā dipadā (sī॰) vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā bahupadā (?) vā rūpinau vā arūpinau vā sañ‍ñinau vā asañ‍ñinau  vā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñinau vā, tathāgatau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā; appamādau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyati| appamattassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ  bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti|

‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattā apadā vā dvipadā vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vā rūpinau vā arūpinau vā sañ‍ñinau vā asañ‍ñinau vā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñinau vā, tathāgatau  taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā; appamādau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyati| appamattassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ  maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti|

‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattā apadā vā dvipadā vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vā rūpinau vā arūpinau vā sañ‍ñinau vā asañ‍ñinau vā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñinau vā, tathāgatau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā; appamādau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyati| appamattassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti|

‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattā apadā vā dvipadā vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vā rūpinau vā arūpinau vā sañ‍ñinau vā asañ‍ñinau vā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñinau vā, tathāgatau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā; appamādau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyati| appamattassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. padasuttaṃ

140. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici jaṅgalānaṃ pāṇānaṃ padajātāni, sabbāni tāni hatthipadai samaudhānaṃ gacchanti; hatthipadaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – mahantattaina; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā; appamādau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyati| appamattassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3-7. kūṭādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

141. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgārassa yā kāci gaupānasiyau sabbā tā kūṭaṅgamā kūṭanin‍nā kūṭasamausaraṇā; kūṭaṃ tāsaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… tatiyaṃ|

142. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici mūlagandhā, kāḷānusāriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… catutthaṃ|

143. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici sāragandhā, lauhitacandanaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…  pañ‍camaṃ|

144. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici pupphagandhā, vassikaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… chaṭṭhaṃ|

145. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kuṭṭarājānau, sabbai tai rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa anuyantā bhavanti, rājā taisaṃ cak‍kavatti aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sattamaṃ|

8-10. candimādisuttatatiyakaṃ

146. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kāci tārakarūpānaṃ pabhā, sabbā tā candimappabhāya candimāpabhāya (syā॰ ka॰) kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ, candappabhā tāsaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… aṭṭhamaṃ|

147. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, saradasamayai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai ādic‍cau nabhaṃ abbhussak‍kamānau sabbaṃ ākāsagataṃ tamagataṃ abhivihac‍ca bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucati ca; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… navamaṃ|

148. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici tantāvutānaṃ vatthānaṃ, kāsikavatthaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā; appamādau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyati| appamattassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissati ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

(yadapi tathāgataṃ, tadapi vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

appamādapaiyyālavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

tathāgataṃ  padaṃ kūṭaṃ, mūlaṃ sārau ca vassikaṃ|

rājā candimasūriyā ca, vatthaina dasamaṃ padaṃ||

6. balakaraṇīyavaggau

1. balasuttaṃ

149. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyanti, sabbai tai pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya  aivamaitai balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti|

(paragaṅgāpaiyyālīvaṇṇiyatau paripuṇṇasuttanti vitthāramaggī)|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyanti, sabbai tai pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya aivamaitai balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyanti, sabbai tai pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya aivamaitai balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici balakaraṇīyā kammantā  karīyanti, sabbai tai pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya aivamaitai balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. bījasuttaṃ
