150. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaicimai bījagāmabhūtagāmā vuḍḍhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti, sabbai tai pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya aivamaitai bījagāmabhūtagāmā vuḍḍhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau vuḍḍhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisu| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau vuḍḍhiṃ  virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisu? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ  vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau vuḍḍhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisū’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. nāgasuttaṃ

151. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, himavantaṃ pabbatarājaṃ nissāya nāgā kāyaṃ vaḍḍhainti, balaṃ gāhainti; tai tattha kāyaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā balaṃ gāhaitvā kusaubbhai autaranti, kusaubbhai kussubbhai (sī॰ syā॰), kusubbhai (pī॰ ka॰) autaritvā mahāsaubbhai autaranti, mahāsaubbhai autaritvā kun‍nadiyau autaranti, kun‍nadiyau autaritvā mahānadiyau autaranti, mahānadiyau autaritvā mahāsamuddaṃ mahāsamuddasāgaraṃ (sabbattha) saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.23 autaranti, tai tattha mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ āpaj‍janti kāyaina; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisu| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisu? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ  niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisū’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. rukkhasuttaṃ

152. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau pācīnanin‍nau pācīnapauṇau pācīnapabbhārau| sau mūlacchin‍nau mūlacchindai katai (syā॰) katamaina papataiyyā’’ti? ‘‘yaina, bhantai, nin‍nau yaina pauṇau yaina pabbhārau’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau  hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. kumbhasuttaṃ

153. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kumbhau nik‍kuj‍jau vamataiva udakaṃ, nau pac‍cāvamati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau vamataiva  pāpakai akusalai dhammai, nau pac‍cāvamati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau vamataiva pāpakai akusalai dhammai, nau pac‍cāvamati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau vamataiva pāpakai akusalai dhammai, nau pac‍cāvamatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sūkasuttaṃ

154. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sālisūkaṃ vā yavasūkaṃ vā sammāpaṇihitaṃ hatthaina vā pādaina vā ak‍kantaṃ hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā bhindissati lauhitaṃ vā uppādaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? sammāpaṇihitattā, bhikkhavai, sūkassa| aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammāpaṇihitāya diṭṭhiyā sammāpaṇihitāya maggabhāvanāya avij‍jaṃ bhindissati, vij‍jaṃ uppādaissati, nibbānaṃ sacchikarissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? sammāpaṇihitattā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiyā| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammāpaṇihitāya diṭṭhiyā sammāpaṇihitāya maggabhāvanāya avij‍jaṃ bhindati, vij‍jaṃ uppādaiti, nibbānaṃ sacchikarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰…  sammāsamādhiṃ  bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammāpaṇihitāya diṭṭhiyā sammāpaṇihitāya maggabhāvanāya avij‍jaṃ bhindati, vij‍jaṃ uppādaiti, nibbānaṃ sacchikarautī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ākāsasuttaṃ

155. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ākāsai vividhā vātā vāyanti – puratthimāpi vātā vāyanti, pacchimāpi vātā vāyanti, uttarāpi vātā vāyanti, dakkhiṇāpi vātā vāyanti, sarajāpi vātā vāyanti, arajāpi vātā vāyanti, sītāpi vātā vāyanti, uṇhāpi vātā vāyanti, parittāpi vātā vāyanti, adhimattāpi vātā vāyanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvayatau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautau cattāraupi satipaṭṭhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, cattāraupi sammappadhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, cattāraupi iddhipādā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍capi indriyāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍capi balāni bhāvanāparipūriṃ gacchanti, sattapi baujjhaṅgā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvayatau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautau cattāraupi satipaṭṭhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, cattāraupi sammappadhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, cattāraupi iddhipādā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍capi indriyāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ  gacchanti, pañ‍capi balāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, sattapi baujjhaṅgā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ  bhāvayatau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautau cattāraupi satipaṭṭhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, cattāraupi sammappadhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, cattāraupi iddhipādā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍capi indriyāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍capi balāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, sattapi baujjhaṅgā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭhamamaighasuttaṃ

156. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai ūhataṃ rajaujal‍laṃ, tamainaṃ mahāakālamaighau ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti; aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau  ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ  niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaitī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyamaighasuttaṃ

157. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uppan‍naṃ mahāmaighaṃ, tamainaṃ mahāvātau antarāyaiva antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai antarāyaiva antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai antarāyaiva antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ  vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai antarāyaiva antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaitī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. nāvāsuttaṃ

158. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sāmuddikāya nāvāya vaittabandhanabandhāya cha māsāni udakai pariyādāya pariyātāya (ka॰), pariyāhatāya (?) haimantikaina thalaṃ ukkhittāya vātātapaparaitāni bandhanāni tāni pāvussakaina maighaina abhippavuṭṭhāni  appakasirainaiva paṭippassambhanti, pūtikāni bhavanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvayatau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautau appakasirainaiva saṃyaujanāni paṭippassambhanti, pūtikāni bhavanti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvayatau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautau appakasirainaiva saṃyaujanāni paṭippassambhanti, pūtikāni bhavanti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ  bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvayatau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautau appakasirainaiva saṃyaujanāni paṭippassambhanti, pūtikāni bhavantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. āgantukasuttaṃ

159. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, āgantukāgāraṃ| tattha puratthimāyapi disāya āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, pacchimāyapi disāya āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, uttarāyapi disāya āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, dakkhiṇāyapi disāya āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, khattiyāpi āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, brāhmaṇāpi  āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, vaissāpi āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti, suddāpi āgantvā vāsaṃ kappainti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā, tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā parijānāti, yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā, tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā pajahati, yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā, tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā sacchikarauti, yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā, tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaiti|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? pañ‍cupādānakkhandhātissa vacanīyaṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imai, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā? avij‍jā ca bhavataṇhā ca – imai, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā? vij‍jā ca vimutti ca – imai, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā? samathau ca vipassanā ca – imai, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau, yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā parijānāti…pai॰… yai  dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā, tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ  niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā, tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā parijānāti, yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā, tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā pajahati, yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā, tai dhammai  abhiñ‍ñā sacchikarauti, yai dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā, tai dhammai abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. nadīsuttaṃ

160. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā| atha mahājanakāyau āgacchaiyya kuddāla-piṭakaṃ ādāya – ‘mayaṃ imaṃ gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ pacchānin‍naṃ karissāma pacchāpauṇaṃ pacchāpabbhāra’nti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau mahājanakāyau gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ pacchānin‍naṃ karaiyya pacchāpauṇaṃ pacchāpabbhāra’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘gaṅgā, bhantai, nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā| sā na sukarā pacchānin‍naṃ kātuṃ pacchāpauṇaṃ pacchāpabbhāraṃ| yāvadaiva pana sau mahājanakāyau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintaṃ ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntaṃ rājānau vā rājamahāmattā vā mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā ñātisālauhitā vā bhaugaihi abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyyuṃ – ‘aihambhau purisa, kiṃ tai imai kāsāvā anudahanti, kiṃ muṇḍau kapālamanusaṃcarasi! aihi, hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jassu, puñ‍ñāni ca karauhī’ti| sau  vata, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaintau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauntau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? yañhi taṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ vivaikanin‍naṃ vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāraṃ taṃ vata hīnāyāvattissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰…  sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti, ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bahulīkarautī’’ti| (yadapi balakaraṇīyaṃ, tadapi vitthāraitabbaṃ|) dvādasamaṃ|

balakaraṇīyavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

balaṃ bījañ‍ca nāgau ca, rukkhau kumbhaina sūkiyā|

ākāsaina ca dvai maighā, nāvā āgantukā nadīti||

7. aisanāvaggau

1. aisanāsuttaṃ

161. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, aisanā| katamā tissau? kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau aisanā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti|

‘‘tissau imā khau, bhikkhavai, aisanā| katamā tissau? kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau aisanā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰…  sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti|

‘‘tissau  imā khau, bhikkhavai, aisanā| katamā tissau? kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau aisanā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti|

‘‘tissau imā khau, bhikkhavai, aisanā| katamā tissau? kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau aisanā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰…  sammāsamādhiṃ  bhāvaiti nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti|

‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, aisanā| katamā tissau? kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau  aisanā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| (yadapi abhiñ‍ñā, tadapi pariñ‍ñāya vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, aisanā| katamā tissau? kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau aisanā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ parikkhayāya…pai॰…  ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| (yadapi abhiñ‍ñā, tadapi parikkhayāya vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, aisanā| katamā tissau? kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau aisanā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ pahānāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ pahānāya ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau  bhāvaitabbau’’ti| (yadapi abhiñ‍ñā, tadapi pahānāya vitthāraitabbaṃ|) paṭhamaṃ|

2. vidhāsuttaṃ

162. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, vidhā| katamā tissau? ‘saiyyauhamasmī’ti vidhā, ‘sadisauhamasmī’ti vidhā, ‘hīnauhamasmī’ti vidhā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vidhā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ vidhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰…  sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai tissan‍naṃ vidhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| (yathā aisanā, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)| dutiyaṃ|

3. āsavasuttaṃ

163. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, āsavā| katamai tayau? kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, avij‍jāsavau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau āsavā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇan‍naṃ āsavānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bhavasuttaṃ

164. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, bhavā| katamai tayau? kāmabhavau, rūpabhavau, arūpabhavau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau bhavā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇan‍naṃ bhavānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰…  ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dukkhatāsuttaṃ

165. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, dukkhatā| katamā tissau? dukkhadukkhatā, saṅkhāradukkhatā, vipariṇāmadukkhatā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau dukkhatā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ dukkhatānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. khilasuttaṃ

166. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, khilā| katamai tayau? rāgau khilau, dausau khilau, mauhau khilau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau khilā| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇan‍naṃ khilānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. malasuttaṃ

167. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, malāni| katamāni tīṇi? rāgau malaṃ, dausau malaṃ, mauhau malaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi malāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇan‍naṃ malānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya  parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰…  ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. nīghasuttaṃ

168. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, nīghā| katamai tayau? rāgau nīghau, dausau nīghau, mauhau nīghau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau nīghā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇan‍naṃ nīghānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vaidanāsuttaṃ

169. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vaidanā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰…  ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. taṇhāsuttaṃ

170. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, taṇhā| katamā tissau? kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau taṇhā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ taṇhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ taṇhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. tasināsuttaṃ

171. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, tasinā| katamā tissau? kāmatasinā, bhavatasinā, vibhavatasinā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ tasinānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰…  rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ…pai॰…  nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ tasinānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

aisanāvaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aisanā vidhā āsavau, bhavau ca dukkhatā khilā|

malaṃ nīghau ca vaidanā, dvai taṇhā tasināya cāti||

8. aughavaggau

1. aughasuttaṃ

172. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, aughā| katamai cattārau? kāmaughau, bhavaughau, diṭṭhaughau, avij‍jaughau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau aughā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ aughānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| (yathā aisanā, aivaṃ sabbaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|) paṭhamaṃ|

2. yaugasuttaṃ

173. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, yaugā| katamai cattārau? kāmayaugau, bhavayaugau, diṭṭhiyaugau avij‍jāyaugau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau yaugā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ yaugānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰…  ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. upādānasuttaṃ

174. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, upādānāni| katamāni cattāri? kāmupādānaṃ, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, sīlabbatupādānaṃ, attavādupādānaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri upādānāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ upādānānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. ganthasuttaṃ

175. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, ganthā| katamai cattārau? abhijjhā kāyaganthau, byāpādau kāyaganthau, sīlabbataparāmāsau kāyaganthau, idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisau kāyaganthau – imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau ganthā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ganthānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. anusayasuttaṃ

176. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, anusayā| katamai satta? kāmarāgānusayau, paṭighānusayau, diṭṭhānusayau, vicikicchānusayau, mānānusayau, bhavarāgānusayau, avij‍jānusayau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, sattānusayā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sattan‍naṃ anusayānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kāmaguṇasuttaṃ

177. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. nīvaraṇasuttaṃ

178. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, nīvaraṇāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ, thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ, vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ nīvaraṇānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. upādānakkhandhasuttaṃ

179. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. aurambhāgiyasuttaṃ

180. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāsau, kāmacchandau, byāpādau – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni  saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ khau bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya…pai॰… ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. uddhambhāgiyasuttaṃ

181. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya  parikkhayāya pahānāya ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti|

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ  saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau| katamau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau  maggau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ… amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ… nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvaitabbau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

aughavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aughau yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthaṃ anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇaṃ, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyāti||

vagguddānaṃ –

avij‍jāvaggau paṭhamau, dutiyaṃ vihāraṃ vuc‍cati|

micchattaṃ tatiyau vaggau, catutthaṃ paṭipan‍nainaiva||

titthiyaṃ  pañ‍camau vaggau, chaṭṭhau sūriyaina ca|

bahukatai sattamau vaggau, uppādau aṭṭhamaina ca||

divasavaggau navamau, dasamau appamādaina ca|

aikādasabalavaggau, dvādasa aisanā pāḷiyaṃ|

aughavaggau bhavati tairasāti||

maggasaṃyuttaṃ paṭhamaṃ|



2. baujjhaṅgasaṃyuttaṃ

1. pabbatavaggau

1. himavantasuttaṃ

182. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, himavantaṃ  pabbatarājānaṃ nissāya nāgā kāyaṃ vaḍḍhainti, balaṃ gāhainti; tai tattha kāyaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā balaṃ gāhaitvā kusaubbhai autaranti, kusaubbhai autaritvā mahāsaubbhai autaranti, mahāsaubbhai autaritvā kun‍nadiyau autaranti, kun‍nadiyau autaritvā mahānadiyau autaranti, mahānadiyau autaritvā mahāsamuddasāgaraṃ autaranti; tai tattha mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ āpaj‍janti kāyaina; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaintau satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauntau mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisu| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaintau satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauntau mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisūti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ; dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ  bhāvaiti…pai॰… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ  bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaintau satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauntau mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti dhammaisū’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. kāyasuttaṃ

183. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ kāyau āhāraṭṭhitikau, āhāraṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhati, anāhārau nau tiṭṭhati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā āhāraṭṭhitikā, āhāraṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhanti, anāhārā nau tiṭṭhanti|

‘‘kau  ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, subhanimittaṃ| tattha ayaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, paṭighanimittaṃ| tattha ayaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, arati tandi vijambhitā bhattasammadau caitasau ca līnattaṃ| tattha ayaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau  – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, caitasau avūpasamau| tattha ayaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya uppādāya, uppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, vicikicchāṭṭhānīyā dhammā| tattha ayaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau  – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya uppādāya, uppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ kāyau āhāraṭṭhitikau, āhāraṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhati, anāhārau nau tiṭṭhati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā āhāraṭṭhitikā, āhāraṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhanti, anāhārā nau tiṭṭhanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ kāyau āhāraṭṭhitikau, āhāraṃ paṭic‍ca  tiṭṭhati, anāhārau nau tiṭṭhati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā āhāraṭṭhitikā, āhāraṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhanti, anāhārā nau tiṭṭhanti|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, satisambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhānīyā dhammā| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau  ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, kusalākusalā dhammā, sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jā dhammā, hīnapaṇītā dhammā, kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgā dhammā| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, ārambhadhātu ārabbhadhātu (syā॰ ka॰) nik‍kamadhātu parak‍kamadhātu| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa  vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau  ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, pītisambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhānīyā dhammā| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, kāyapassaddhi, cittapassaddhi| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, samathanimittaṃ samādhinimittaṃ (syā॰) abyagganimittaṃ| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau  ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhānīyā dhammā| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ kāyau āhāraṭṭhitikau, āhāraṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhati, anāhārau nau tiṭṭhati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imai satta baujjhaṅgā āhāraṭṭhitikā, āhāraṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhanti, anāhārā nau tiṭṭhantī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sīlasuttaṃ

184. ‘‘yai tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū sīlasampan‍nā samādhisampan‍nā ñāṇasampan‍nā vimuttisampan‍nā  vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nā, dassanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahukāraṃ bahūpakāraṃ (syā॰) vadāmi; savanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahukāraṃ vadāmi; upasaṅkamanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahukāraṃ vadāmi; payirupāsanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahukāraṃ vadāmi; anussatimpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahukāraṃ vadāmi; anupabbaj‍jampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahukāraṃ vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? tathārūpānaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ sutvā dvayaina vūpakāsaina vūpakaṭṭhau dvayaina vūpakaṭṭhau (sī॰ syā॰) viharati – kāyavūpakāsaina ca cittavūpakāsaina ca| sau tathā vūpakaṭṭhau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ anussarati anuvitak‍kaiti|

‘‘yasmiṃ  samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathā vūpakaṭṭhau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ anussarati anuvitak‍kaiti, satisambaujjhaṅgau  tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti; satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti; satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| sau tathā satau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicarati parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jati|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathā satau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicarati parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jati, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti; dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti; dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| tassa taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicaratau parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicaratau parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti; vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti; vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti nirāmisā|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti  nirāmisā, pītisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti; pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti; pītisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati, cittampi passambhati|

‘‘yasmiṃ  samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati cittampi passambhati, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti; passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti; passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ  gacchati| passaddhakāyassa sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau passaddhakāyassa sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti; samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti; samādhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| sau tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti; upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti; upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhavai, sattasu sambaujjhaṅgaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu satta phalā sattānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| katamai satta phalā  sattānisaṃsā? diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, atha maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, atha pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti, atha pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai pañ‍can‍naṃ  aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti, nau cai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, atha pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti, nau cai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ  saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, nau cai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, atha  pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti, nau cai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, nau cai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, nau cai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, atha pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī| aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhavai, sattasu baujjhaṅgaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu imai satta phalā sattānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. vatthasuttaṃ

185. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau, bhikkhavau’’ti! ‘‘āvusau’’ti  khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘sattimai, āvusau, baujjhaṅgā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, āvusau, satta baujjhaṅgā| imaisaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ yaina yaina baujjhaṅgaina  ākaṅkhāmi pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, taina taina baujjhaṅgaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharāmi; yaina yaina baujjhaṅgaina ākaṅkhāmi majjhanhikaṃ samayaṃ viharituṃ, taina taina baujjhaṅgaina majjhanhikaṃ samayaṃ viharāmi; yaina yaina baujjhaṅgaina ākaṅkhāmi sāyanhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, taina taina baujjhaṅgaina sāyanhasamayaṃ viharāmi| satisambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai, āvusau, hauti, ‘appamāṇau’ti mai hauti, ‘susamāraddhau’ti mai hauti, tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ ‘tiṭṭhatī’ti pajānāmi| sacaipi mai cavati, ‘idappac‍cayā mai cavatī’ti pajānāmi…pai॰…  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai, āvusau, hauti, ‘appamāṇau’ti mai hauti, ‘susamāraddhau’ti mai hauti, tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ ‘tiṭṭhatī’ti pajānāmi| sacaipi mai cavati, ‘idappac‍cayā mai cavatī’ti pajānāmi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, rañ‍ñau vā rājamahāmattassa vā nānārattānaṃ dussānaṃ dussakaraṇḍakau pūrau assa| sau yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya  pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva dussayugaṃ pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pārupaiyya; yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya majjhanhikaṃ samayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva dussayugaṃ majjhanhikaṃ samayaṃ pārupaiyya; yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya sāyanhasamayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva dussayugaṃ sāyanhasamayaṃ pārupaiyya| aivamaiva  khvāhaṃ, āvusau, imaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ yaina yaina baujjhaṅgaina ākaṅkhāmi pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, taina taina baujjhaṅgaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharāmi;  yaina yaina baujjhaṅgaina ākaṅkhāmi majjhanhikaṃ samayaṃ viharituṃ, taina taina baujjhaṅgaina majjhanhikaṃ samayaṃ viharāmi; yaina yaina baujjhaṅgaina ākaṅkhāmi sāyanhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, taina taina baujjhaṅgaina sāyanhasamayaṃ viharāmi| satisambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai, āvusau, hauti, ‘appamāṇau’ti mai hauti, ‘susamāraddhau’ti mai hauti, tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ ‘tiṭṭhatī’ti pajānāmi| sacaipi mai cavati, ‘idappac‍cayā mai cavatī’ti pajānāmi…pai॰…  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau iti cai mai, āvusau, hauti, ‘appamāṇau’ti mai hauti, ‘susamāraddhau’ti mai hauti, tiṭṭhantañ‍ca naṃ ‘tiṭṭhatī’ti pajānāmi| sacaipi mai cavati, ‘idappac‍cayā mai cavatī’ti pajānāmī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. bhikkhusuttaṃ

186. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘baujjhaṅgā, baujjhaṅgā’ti, bhantai, vuc‍canti| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, ‘baujjhaṅgā’ti vuc‍cantī’’ti? ‘‘baudhāya saṃvattantīti khau, bhikkhu, tasmā ‘baujjhaṅgā’ti vuc‍canti| idha, bhikkhu, satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| tassimai satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvayatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| baudhāya saṃvattantīti, bhikkhu, tasmā ‘baujjhaṅgā’ti vuc‍cantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kuṇḍaliyasuttaṃ

187. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sākaitai viharati añ‍janavanai migadāyai| atha khau kuṇḍaliyau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kuṇḍaliyau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahamasmi, bhau gautama, ārāmanissayī ārāmanisādī (sī॰), ārāmaniyādī (syā॰) parisāvacarau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhau gautama, pacchābhattaṃ bhuttapātarāsassa ayamācārau ayamāhārau (syā॰ ka॰) hauti – ārāmaina ārāmaṃ uyyānaina uyyānaṃ anucaṅkamāmi anuvicarāmi| sau tattha passāmi aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇai itivādappamaukkhānisaṃsañ‍caiva kathaṃ kathaintai upārambhānisaṃsañ‍ca – ‘bhavaṃ pana gautamau kimānisaṃsau viharatī’’’ti? ‘‘vij‍jāvimuttiphalānisaṃsau khau, kuṇḍaliya, tathāgatau viharatī’’ti|

‘‘katamai pana, bhau gautama, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintī’’ti? ‘‘satta khau, kuṇḍaliya, baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintī’’ti| ‘‘katamai pana, bhau gautama, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūraintī’’ti? ‘‘cattārau khau, kuṇḍaliya, satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūraintī’’ti| ‘‘katamai pana, bhau gautama, dhammā bhāvitā, bahulīkatā cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraintī’’ti? ‘‘tīṇi khau, kuṇḍaliya, sucaritāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraintī’’ti| ‘‘katamai pana, bhau gautama, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā tīṇi sucaritāni paripūraintī’’ti? ‘‘indriyasaṃvarau  khau, kuṇḍaliya, bhāvitau bahulīkatau tīṇi sucaritāni paripūraitī’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitau ca, kuṇḍaliya, indriyasaṃvarau kathaṃ bahulīkatau tīṇi sucaritāni paripūraitīti? idha, kuṇḍaliya, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā manāpaṃ nābhijjhati nābhihaṃsati, na rāgaṃ janaiti| tassa ṭhitau ca kāyau hauti, ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ ajjhattaṃ susaṇṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ| cakkhunā khau panaiva rūpaṃ disvā amanāpaṃ na maṅku hauti appatiṭṭhitacittau adīnamānasau abyāpan‍nacaitasau| tassa ṭhitau ca kāyau hauti ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ ajjhattaṃ susaṇṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, kuṇḍaliya, bhikkhu sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰…  ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya manāpaṃ nābhijjhati nābhihaṃsati, na rāgaṃ janaiti| tassa ṭhitau ca kāyau hauti, ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ ajjhattaṃ susaṇṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ| manasā khau panaiva dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya amanāpaṃ na maṅku hauti appatiṭṭhitacittau adīnamānasau abyāpan‍nacaitasau| tassa ṭhitau ca kāyau hauti, ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ ajjhattaṃ susaṇṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ|

‘‘yatau khau, kuṇḍaliya, bhikkhunau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā manāpāmanāpaisu rūpaisu  ṭhitau ca kāyau hauti, ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ ajjhattaṃ susaṇṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya manāpāmanāpaisu dhammaisu ṭhitau ca kāyau hauti, ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ ajjhattaṃ susaṇṭhitaṃ suvimuttaṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitau khau, kuṇḍaliya, indriyasaṃvarau aivaṃ bahulīkatau tīṇi sucaritāni paripūraiti|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitāni ca, kuṇḍaliya, tīṇi sucaritāni kathaṃ bahulīkatāni cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūrainti? idha, kuṇḍaliya, bhikkhu kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, vacīduc‍caritaṃ  pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, manauduc‍caritaṃ pahāya manausucaritaṃ bhāvaiti| aivaṃ bhāvitāni khau, kuṇḍaliya, tīṇi sucaritāni aivaṃ bahulīkatāni cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūrainti|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, kuṇḍaliya, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā kathaṃ bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti? idha, kuṇḍaliya, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, kuṇḍaliya, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā aivaṃ bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti|

‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitā ca, kuṇḍaliya, satta baujjhaṅgā kathaṃ bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūrainti? idha, kuṇḍaliya, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ  bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, kuṇḍaliya, satta baujjhaṅgā aivaṃ bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai kuṇḍaliyau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya, cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaiva bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kūṭāgārasuttaṃ

188. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgārassa yā kāci gaupānasiyau, sabbā tā kūṭanin‍nā kūṭapauṇā kūṭapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaintau satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaintau satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaintau satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. upavānasuttaṃ

189. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca upavānau āyasmā ca sāriputtau kausambiyaṃ viharanti ghausitārāmai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā upavānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā upavānaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ upavānaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘jānaiyya  nu khau, āvusau upavāna, bhikkhu ‘pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā aivaṃ susamāraddhā mai satta baujjhaṅgā phāsuvihārāya saṃvattantī’’’ti? ‘‘jānaiyya khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu ‘pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā aivaṃ susamāraddhā mai satta baujjhaṅgā phāsuvihārāya saṃvattantī’’’ti|

‘‘satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu ārabbhamānau pajānāti ‘cittañ‍ca mai suvimuttaṃ, thinamiddhañ‍ca mai susamūhataṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cañ‍ca mai suppaṭivinītaṃ, āraddhañ‍ca mai vīriyaṃ, aṭṭhiṃkatvā manasi karaumi, nau  ca līna’nti…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ āvusau, bhikkhu ārabbhamānau pajānāti ‘cittañ‍ca  mai suvimuttaṃ, thinamiddhañ‍ca mai susamūhataṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cañ‍ca mai suppaṭivinītaṃ, āraddhañ‍ca mai vīriyaṃ, aṭṭhiṃkatvā manasi karaumi, nau ca līna’nti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu jānaiyya ‘pac‍cattaṃ yaunisaumanasikārā aivaṃ susamāraddhā mai satta baujjhaṅgā phāsuvihārāya saṃvattantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamauppan‍nasuttaṃ

190. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra tathāgatassa pātubhāvā arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰…  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra tathāgatassa pātubhāvā arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyauppan‍nasuttaṃ

191. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuppan‍nā uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

pabbatavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

himavantaṃ  kāyaṃ sīlaṃ, vatthaṃ bhikkhu ca kuṇḍali|

kūṭañ‍ca upavānañ‍ca, uppan‍nā aparai duvaiti||

2. gilānavaggau

1. pāṇasuttaṃ

192. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici pāṇā cattārau iriyāpathai kappainti – kālaina gamanaṃ, kālaina ṭhānaṃ, kālaina nisaj‍jaṃ, kālaina saiyyaṃ, sabbai tai pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya aivamaitai cattārau iriyāpathai kappainti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭhamasūriyūpamasuttaṃ

193. ‘‘sūriyassa, bhikkhavai, udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – aruṇuggaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ uppādāya aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaissati, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarissati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  kalyāṇamittau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyasūriyūpamasuttaṃ

194. ‘‘sūriyassa, bhikkhavai, udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – aruṇuggaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ uppādāya aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ  pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – yaunisaumanasikārau| yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaissati, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarissati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarautī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamagilānasuttaṃ

195. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahākassapau pippaliguhāyaṃ vipphaliguhāyaṃ (sī॰) viharati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha  khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā mahākassapau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kac‍ci tai, kassapa, khamanīyaṃ kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ? kac‍ci dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti, nau abhik‍kamanti; paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau abhik‍kamau’’ti? ‘‘na  mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti|

‘‘sattimai, kassapa, baujjhaṅgā mayā sammadakkhātā bhāvitā bahulīkatā abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau khau, kassapa, mayā sammadakkhātau bhāvitau bahulīkatau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati…pai॰…  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau khau, kassapa, mayā sammadakkhātau bhāvitau bahulīkatau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya  nibbānāya saṃvattati| imai khau, kassapa, satta baujjhaṅgā mayā sammadakkhātā bhāvitā bahulīkatā abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| ‘‘taggha, bhagavā, baujjhaṅgā; taggha, sugata, baujjhaṅgā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā mahākassapau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandi| vuṭṭhahi cāyasmā mahākassapau tamhā ābādhā| tathāpahīnau cāyasmatau mahākassapassa sau ābādhau ahausīti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyagilānasuttaṃ

196. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau gijjhakūṭai pabbatai viharati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kac‍ci tai, mauggal‍lāna, khamanīyaṃ kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ? kac‍ci dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti, nau abhik‍kamanti; paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau abhik‍kamau’’ti? ‘‘na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti|

‘‘sattimai, mauggal‍lāna, baujjhaṅgā mayā sammadakkhātā bhāvitā bahulīkatā abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau khau, mauggal‍lāna, mayā sammadakkhātau bhāvitau bahulīkatau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati…pai॰…  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau khau, mauggal‍lāna, mayā sammadakkhātau bhāvitau bahulīkatau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| imai khau, mauggal‍lāna, satta baujjhaṅgā mayā sammadakkhātā bhāvitā bahulīkatā abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| ‘‘taggha, bhagavā, baujjhaṅgā; taggha, sugata, baujjhaṅgā’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandi| vuṭṭhahi cāyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tamhā ābādhā| tathāpahīnau cāyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa sau ābādhau ahausīti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. tatiyagilānasuttaṃ

197. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau āyasmā mahācundau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ mahācundaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhantu taṃ, cunda, baujjhaṅgā’’ti|

‘‘sattimai, bhantai, baujjhaṅgā bhagavatā sammadakkhātā bhāvitā bahulīkatā abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau khau, bhantai, bhagavatā sammadakkhātau bhāvitau bahulīkatau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati…pai॰…  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau khau, bhantai, bhagavatā sammadakkhātau bhāvitau bahulīkatau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhantai, satta baujjhaṅgā bhagavatā sammadakkhātā bhāvitā bahulīkatā abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| ‘‘taggha, cunda, baujjhaṅgā; taggha, cunda, baujjhaṅgā’’ti|

idamavaucāyasmā cundau| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| vuṭṭhahi ca bhagavā tamhā ābādhā| tathāpahīnau ca bhagavatau sau ābādhau ahausīti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. pāraṅgamasuttaṃ

198. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya saṃvattantī’’ti|

‘‘appakā  tai manussaisu, yai janā pāragāminau|

athāyaṃ itarā pajā, tīramaivānudhāvati||

‘‘yai ca khau sammadakkhātai, dhammai dhammānuvattinau|

tai janā pāramaissanti, mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttaraṃ||

‘‘kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ vippahāya, suk‍kaṃ bhāvaitha paṇḍitau|

aukā anaukamāgamma, vivaikai yattha dūramaṃ||

‘‘tatrābhiratimicchaiyya, hitvā kāmai akiñ‍canau|

pariyaudapaiyya attānaṃ, cittak‍laisaihi paṇḍitau||

‘‘yaisaṃ sambaudhiyaṅgaisu, sammā cittaṃ subhāvitaṃ|

ādānappaṭinissaggai, anupādāya yai ratā|

khīṇāsavā jutimantau, tai laukai parinibbutā’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. viraddhasuttaṃ

199. ‘‘yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā viraddhā, viraddhau taisaṃ  ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā āraddhā, āraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, imai satta baujjhaṅgā viraddhā, viraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, imai satta baujjhaṅgā āraddhā, āraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ariyasuttaṃ

200. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ariyā niyyānikā nīyanti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ariyā niyyānikā nīyanti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. nibbidāsuttaṃ

201. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā  aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

gilānavaggau  dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

pāṇā  sūriyūpamā dvai, gilānā aparai tayau|

pāraṅgāmī viraddhau ca, ariyau nibbidāya cāti||

3. udāyivaggau

1. baudhāyasuttaṃ

202. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘baujjhaṅgā, baujjhaṅgā’ti, bhantai, vuc‍canti| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, ‘baujjhaṅgā’ti vuc‍cantī’’ti? ‘‘‘baudhāya saṃvattantī’ti khau, bhikkhu, tasmā baujjhaṅgāti vuc‍canti| idha, bhikkhu, satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| ‘baudhāya saṃvattantī’ti khau, bhikkhu, tasmā ‘baujjhaṅgā’ti vuc‍cantī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. baujjhaṅgadaisanāsuttaṃ

203. ‘‘satta  vau, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgai daisaissāmi; taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. ṭhāniyasuttaṃ

204. ‘‘kāmarāgaṭṭhāniyānaṃ, kāmarāgaṭṭhānīyānaṃ (sī॰) bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ manasikārabahulīkārā anuppan‍nau caiva kāmacchandau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca kāmacchandau bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati| byāpādaṭṭhāniyānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ manasikārabahulīkārā anuppan‍nau caiva byāpādau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca byāpādau bhiyyaubhāvāya  vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati| thinamiddhaṭṭhāniyānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ manasikārabahulīkārā anuppan‍nañ‍caiva thinamiddhaṃ uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nañ‍ca thinamiddhaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāyaṃ saṃvattati| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṭṭhāniyānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ manasikārabahulīkārā anuppan‍nañ‍caiva uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nañ‍ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati| vicikicchāṭṭhāniyānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ manasikārabahulīkārā anuppan‍nā caiva vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nā ca vicikicchā bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati|

‘‘satisambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhāniyānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ manasikārabahulīkārā anuppan‍nau caiva satisambaujjhaṅgau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca satisambaujjhaṅgau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati…pai॰…  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhāniyānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ manasikārabahulīkārā anuppan‍nau caiva upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. ayaunisaumanasikārasuttaṃ

205. ‘‘ayaunisau, bhikkhavai, manasikarautau anuppan‍nau caiva kāmacchandau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca kāmacchandau bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati; anuppan‍nau  caiva byāpādau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca byāpādau bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati; anuppan‍nañ‍caiva  thinamiddhaṃ uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nañ‍ca thinamiddhaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati; anuppan‍nañ‍caiva uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nañ‍ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati; anuppan‍nā caiva vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nā ca vicikicchā bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati; anuppan‍nau caiva satisambaujjhaṅgau nuppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca satisambaujjhaṅgau nirujjhati…pai॰… anuppan‍nau caiva upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau nuppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau nirujjhati|

yaunisau  ca khau, bhikkhavai, manasikarautau anuppan‍nau caiva kāmacchandau nuppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca kāmacchandau pahīyati; anuppan‍nau caiva byāpādau nuppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca byāpādau pahīyati; anuppan‍nañ‍caiva thinamiddhaṃ nuppaj‍jati, uppan‍nañ‍ca thinamiddhaṃ pahīyati; anuppan‍nañ‍caiva uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ  nuppaj‍jati, uppan‍nañ‍ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahīyati; anuppan‍nā caiva vicikicchā nuppaj‍jati, uppan‍nā ca vicikicchā pahīyati|

‘‘anuppan‍nau caiva satisambaujjhaṅgau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca satisambaujjhaṅgau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati…pai॰… anuppan‍nau caiva upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. aparihāniyasuttaṃ

206. ‘‘satta  vau, bhikkhavai, aparihāniyai dhammai daisaissāmi; taṃ suṇātha| katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, satta aparihāniyā dhammā? yadidaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta aparihāniyā dhammā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. taṇhakkhayasuttaṃ

207. ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, maggau yā paṭipadā taṇhakkhayāya saṃvattati, taṃ maggaṃ taṃ paṭipadaṃ bhāvaitha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, maggau katamā ca paṭipadā taṇhakkhayāya saṃvattati? yadidaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā udāyī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā nu khau, bhantai, satta baujjhaṅgā, kathaṃ bahulīkatā taṇhakkhayāya saṃvattantī’’ti?

‘‘idha, udāyi, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ vipulaṃ mahaggataṃ appamāṇaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ abyāpajjhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)| tassa satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvayatau vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ vipulaṃ mahaggataṃ appamāṇaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ taṇhā pahīyati| taṇhāya pahānā kammaṃ pahīyati| kammassa pahānā dukkhaṃ pahīyati…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ  niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ vipulaṃ mahaggataṃ appamāṇaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ| tassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvayatau vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ vipulaṃ mahaggataṃ appamāṇaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ taṇhā pahīyati  taṇhāya pahānā kammaṃ pahīyati| kammassa pahānā dukkhaṃ pahīyati| iti khau, udāyi, taṇhakkhayā kammakkhayau, kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. taṇhāniraudhasuttaṃ

208. ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, maggau yā paṭipadā taṇhāniraudhāya saṃvattati, taṃ maggaṃ taṃ paṭipadaṃ bhāvaitha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, maggau katamā ca paṭipadā taṇhāniraudhāya saṃvattati? yadidaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau| kathaṃ bhāvitā, ca bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā kathaṃ bahulīkatā taṇhāniraudhāya saṃvattanti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā aivaṃ bahulīkatā taṇhāniraudhāya saṃvattantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. nibbaidhabhāgiyasuttaṃ

209. ‘‘nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, maggaṃ daisaissāmi; taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, nibbaidhabhāgiyau maggau? yadidaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā udāyī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā nu khau, bhantai, satta baujjhaṅgā kathaṃ bahulīkatā nibbaidhāya saṃvattantī’’ti?

‘‘idha, udāyi, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ vipulaṃ mahaggataṃ appamāṇaṃ  abyāpaj‍jaṃ| sau satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvitaina cittaina anibbiddhapubbaṃ appadālitapubbaṃ laubhakkhandhaṃ nibbijjhati padālaiti; anibbiddhapubbaṃ appadālitapubbaṃ  dausakkhandhaṃ nibbijjhati padālaiti; anibbiddhapubbaṃ appadālitapubbaṃ mauhakkhandhaṃ nibbijjhati padālaiti…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ  vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ vipulaṃ mahaggataṃ appamāṇaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ| sau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvitaina cittaina anibbiddhapubbaṃ appadālitapubbaṃ laubhakkhandhaṃ nibbijjhati padālaiti; anibbiddhapubbaṃ appadālitapubbaṃ dausakkhandhaṃ nibbijjhati padālaiti; anibbiddhapubbaṃ appadālitapubbaṃ mauhakkhandhaṃ nibbijjhati padālaiti| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, udāyi, satta baujjhaṅgā aivaṃ bahulīkatā nibbaidhāya saṃvattantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. aikadhammasuttaṃ

210. ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikadhammampi samanupassāmi, yau aivaṃ bhāvitau bahulīkatau saṃyaujanīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya saṃvattati, yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā kathaṃ bahulīkatā saṃyaujanīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya saṃvattanti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā aivaṃ bahulīkatā saṃyaujanīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujanīyā dhammā? cakkhu, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujanīyau dhammau| aitthaitai uppaj‍janti saṃyaujanavinibandhā ajjhausānā…pai॰… jivhā saṃyaujanīyā dhammā| aitthaitai uppaj‍janti saṃyaujanavinibandhā ajjhausānā…pai॰… manau saṃyaujanīyau dhammau| aitthaitai uppaj‍janti saṃyaujanavinibandhā ajjhausānā| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujanīyā dhammā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. udāyisuttaṃ

211. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sumbhaisu viharati saitakaṃ nāma sumbhānaṃ nigamau| atha khau āyasmā udāyī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā udāyī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva bahukatañ‍ca mai, bhantai, bhagavati paimañ‍ca gāravau  ca hirī ca auttappañ‍ca| ahañhi, bhantai, pubbai agārikabhūtau samānau  abahukatau ahausiṃ dhammaina dhammai (?) abahukatau saṅghaina| sau khvāhaṃ bhagavati paimañ‍ca gāravañ‍ca hiriñ‍ca auttappañ‍ca sampassamānau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| tassa mai bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaisi – ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā…pai॰… iti sañ‍ñā… iti saṅkhārā… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti|

‘‘sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, suñ‍ñāgāragatau imaisaṃ pañ‍cupādānakkhandhānaṃ  uk‍kuj‍jāvakuj‍jaṃ samparivattaintau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti  yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| dhammau ca mai, bhantai, abhisamitau, maggau ca mai paṭiladdhau; yau mai bhāvitau bahulīkatau tathā tathā viharantaṃ tathattāya upanaissati yathāhaṃ – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānissāmi|

‘‘satisambaujjhaṅgau mai, bhantai, paṭiladdhau, yau mai bhāvitau bahulīkatau tathā tathā viharantaṃ tathattāya upanaissati yathāhaṃ – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānissāmi…pai॰…  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau mai, bhantai, paṭiladdhau, yau mai bhāvitau bahulīkatau tathā tathā viharantaṃ tathattāya upanaissati yathāhaṃ – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānissāmi| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, maggau paṭiladdhau, yau mai bhāvitau bahulīkatau tathā tathā viharantaṃ tathattāya upanaissati yathāhaṃ – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānissāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, udāyi! aisau hi tai, udāyi, maggau paṭiladdhau, yau tai bhāvitau bahulīkatau tathā tathā viharantaṃ tathattāya upanaissati yathā tvaṃ – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ  karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānissasī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

udāyivaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

baudhāya  daisanā ṭhānā, ayaunisau cāparihānī|

khayau niraudhau nibbaidhau, aikadhammau udāyināti||

4. nīvaraṇavaggau

1. paṭhamakusalasuttaṃ

212. ‘‘yai  kaici, bhikkhavai, dhammā kusalā kusalabhāgiyā kusalapakkhikā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā; appamādau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyati| appamattassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaissati, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarissati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appamattau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyakusalasuttaṃ

213. ‘‘yai kaici, bhikkhavai, dhammā kusalā kusalabhāgiyā kusalapakkhikā, sabbai tai yaunisaumanasikāramūlakā yaunisaumanasikārasamausaraṇā; yaunisaumanasikārau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyati| yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaissati, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarissati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau satta baujjhaṅgai  bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. upak‍kilaisasuttaṃ

214. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, jātarūpassa upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ jātarūpaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ, na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca, na ca sammā upaiti kammāya| katamai pañ‍ca? ayau, bhikkhavai, jātarūpassa upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ jātarūpaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ, na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca, na ca sammā upaiti kammāya| lauhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jātarūpassa upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ jātarūpaṃ…pai॰… tipu, bhikkhavai, jātarūpassa upak‍kilaisau…pai॰…  sīsaṃ, bhikkhavai, jātarūpassa upak‍kilaisau…pai॰… sajjhu, bhikkhavai, jātarūpassa upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ jātarūpaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ, na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca, na ca sammā upaiti kammāya| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca jātarūpassa upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ jātarūpaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ, na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca, na ca sammā upaiti kammāya|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai cittassa upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ cittaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ, na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca, na ca sammā samādhiyati āsavānaṃ khayāya| katamai pañ‍ca? kāmacchandau, bhikkhavai, cittassa upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ cittaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ, na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca, na ca sammā samādhiyati āsavānaṃ khayāya…pai॰… imai  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca cittassa upaik‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhaṃ cittaṃ na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ, na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca, na ca sammā samādhiyati āsavānaṃ khayāyā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. anupak‍kilaisasuttaṃ

215. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā anāvaraṇā anīvaraṇā caitasau anupak‍kilaisā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau, bhikkhavai, anāvaraṇau anīvaraṇau  caitasau anupak‍kilaisau bhāvitau bahulīkatau vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattati…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau, bhikkhavai, anāvaraṇau anīvaraṇau caitasau anupak‍kilaisau bhāvitau bahulīkatau vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā anāvaraṇā anīvaraṇā caitasau anupak‍kilaisā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. ayaunisaumanasikārasuttaṃ

216. ‘‘ayaunisau, bhikkhavai, manasikarautau anuppan‍nau caiva kāmacchandau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca kāmacchandau bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati; anuppan‍nau caiva byāpādau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca byāpādau bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati; anuppan‍nañ‍caiva thinamiddhaṃ uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nañ‍ca thinamiddhaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati; anuppan‍nañ‍caiva uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nañ‍ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattati; anuppan‍nā  caiva vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nā ca vicikicchā  bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. yaunisaumanasikārasuttaṃ

217. ‘‘yaunisau  ca khau, bhikkhavai, manasikarautau anuppan‍nau caiva satisambaujjhaṅgau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca satisambaujjhaṅgau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati…pai॰… anuppan‍nau caiva upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. buddhisuttaṃ

218. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā buddhiyā aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā buddhiyā aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. āvaraṇanīvaraṇasuttaṃ

219. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇā nīvaraṇā caitasau upak‍kilaisā pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? kāmacchandau, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇau nīvaraṇau  caitasau upak‍kilaisau pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇau| byāpādau, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇau nīvaraṇau caitasau upak‍kilaisau pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇau| thinamiddhaṃ, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ caitasau upak‍kilaisaṃ pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇaṃ| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ caitasau upak‍kilaisaṃ pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇaṃ| vicikicchā, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇā nīvaraṇā caitasau upak‍kilaisā  pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca āvaraṇā nīvaraṇā caitasau upak‍kilaisā pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇā|

‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā anāvaraṇā anīvaraṇā caitasau anupak‍kilaisā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau, bhikkhavai, anāvaraṇau anīvaraṇau caitasau anupak‍kilaisau bhāvitau bahulīkatau vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattati…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau, bhikkhavai, anāvaraṇau anīvaraṇau caitasau anupak‍kilaisau bhāvitau bahulīkatau vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā anāvaraṇā anīvaraṇā caitasau anupak‍kilaisā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattantīti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbaṃ caitasau saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti, imassa pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā tasmiṃ samayai na haunti| satta baujjhaṅgā tasmiṃ samayai bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

‘‘katamai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā tasmiṃ samayai na haunti? kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ tasmiṃ samayai na hauti, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ tasmiṃ samayai na hauti, thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ tasmiṃ samayai na hauti, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ tasmiṃ samayai na hauti, vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ tasmiṃ samayai na hauti| imassa pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā tasmiṃ samayai na haunti|

‘‘katamai satta baujjhaṅgā tasmiṃ samayai bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti? satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati…pai॰…  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| imai satta baujjhaṅgā tasmiṃ samayai bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbaṃ caitasau saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautau  dhammaṃ suṇāti, imassa pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā tasmiṃ samayai na haunti| imai satta baujjhaṅgā tasmiṃ samayai bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. rukkhasuttaṃ

220. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhā aṇubījā mahākāyā rukkhānaṃ ajjhāruhā, yaihi rukkhā ajjhārūḷhā aubhaggavibhaggā vipatitā sainti| katamai ca tai, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhā aṇubījā mahākāyā rukkhānaṃ ajjhāruhā, yaihi rukkhā ajjhārūḷhā aubhaggavibhaggā vipatitā sainti sainti| saiyyathidaṃ (katthaci)? assatthau, nigraudhau, pilakkhau, udumbarau, kacchakau, kapitthanau – imai khau tai, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhā aṇubījā mahākāyā rukkhānaṃ ajjhāruhā, yaihi rukkhā ajjhārūḷhā aubhaggavibhaggā vipatitā sainti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau kulaputtau yādisakai kāmai auhāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau tādisakaihi kāmaihi tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraihi aubhaggavibhaggau vipatitau saiti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇā nīvaraṇā caitasau ajjhāruhā pañ‍ñāya  dubbalīkaraṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? kāmacchandau, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇau nīvaraṇau caitasau ajjhāruhau pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇau| byāpādau, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇau nīvaraṇau caitasau ajjhāruhau pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇau| thinamiddhaṃ, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ caitasau ajjhāruhaṃ pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇaṃ| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ caitasau ajjhāruhaṃ pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇaṃ| vicikicchā, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇā nīvaraṇā caitasau ajjhāruhā pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca āvaraṇā nīvaraṇā caitasau ajjhāruhā pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇā|

‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā anāvaraṇā anīvaraṇā caitasau anajjhāruhā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau, bhikkhavai, anāvaraṇau anīvaraṇau caitasau anajjhāruhau bhāvitau bahulīkatau vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattati…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau, bhikkhavai, anāvaraṇau  anīvaraṇau caitasau anajjhāruhau bhāvitau bahulīkatau vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā anāvaraṇā anīvaraṇā caitasau anajjhāruhā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. nīvaraṇasuttaṃ
