221. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, nīvaraṇā andhakaraṇā acakkhukaraṇā añ‍ñāṇakaraṇā pañ‍ñāniraudhikā vighātapakkhiyā anibbānasaṃvattanikā| katamai pañ‍ca? kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, andhakaraṇaṃ acakkhukaraṇaṃ añ‍ñāṇakaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāniraudhikaṃ vighātapakkhiyaṃ anibbānasaṃvattanikaṃ| byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai… uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai… vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, andhakaraṇaṃ acakkhukaraṇaṃ añ‍ñāṇakaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāniraudhikaṃ vighātapakkhiyaṃ anibbānasaṃvattanikaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā andhakaraṇā acakkhukaraṇā añ‍ñāṇakaraṇā pañ‍ñāniraudhikā vighātapakkhiyā anibbānasaṃvattanikā|

‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā cakkhukaraṇā ñāṇakaraṇā pañ‍ñābuddhiyā avighātapakkhiyā nibbānasaṃvattanikā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau, bhikkhavai, cakkhukaraṇau ñāṇakaraṇau pañ‍ñābuddhiyau avighātapakkhiyau nibbānasaṃvattanikau…pai॰…  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau, bhikkhavai, cakkhukaraṇau ñāṇakaraṇau pañ‍ñābuddhiyau avighātapakkhiyau nibbānasaṃvattanikau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā cakkhukaraṇā ñāṇakaraṇā pañ‍ñābuddhiyā avighātapakkhiyā nibbānasaṃvattanikā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

nīvaraṇavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dvai kusalā kilaisā ca, dvai yaunisau ca buddhi ca|

āvaraṇā nīvaraṇā rukkhaṃ, nīvaraṇañ‍ca tai dasāti||

5. cak‍kavattivaggau

1. vidhāsuttaṃ

222. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā tissau vidhā pajahiṃsu, sabbai tai sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā tissau vidhā pajahissanti, sabbai tai sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā tissau vidhā pajahanti, sabbai tai sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| katamaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ? satisambaujjhaṅgassa…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā tissau vidhā pajahiṃsu…pai॰… pajahissanti…pai॰… pajahanti, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. cak‍kavattisuttaṃ

223. ‘‘rañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattissa pātubhāvā sattan‍naṃ ratanānaṃ pātubhāvau hauti| katamaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ? cak‍karatanassa pātubhāvau hauti, hatthiratanassa pātubhāvau hauti, assaratanassa pātubhāvau hauti, maṇiratanassa pātubhāvau hauti, itthiratanassa pātubhāvau hauti, gahapatiratanassa pātubhāvau hauti, pariṇāyakaratanassa pātubhāvau hauti| rañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, cak‍kavattissa pātubhāvā imaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ ratanānaṃ pātubhāvau hauti|

‘‘tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, pātubhāvā arahatau sammāsambuddhassa sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgaratanānaṃ pātubhāvau hauti| katamaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ? satisambaujjhaṅgassa ratanassa pātubhāvau hauti…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa ratanassa pātubhāvau hauti| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, pātubhāvā arahatau sammāsambuddhassa imaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgaratanānaṃ pātubhāvau hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. mārasuttaṃ

224. ‘‘mārasainappamaddanaṃ  vau, bhikkhavai, maggaṃ daisaissāmi; taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, mārasainappamaddanau maggau? yadidaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mārasainappamaddanau maggau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. duppañ‍ñasuttaṃ

225. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘duppañ‍ñau aiḷamūgau, duppañ‍ñau aiḷamūgau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, ‘duppañ‍ñau aiḷamūgau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti? ‘‘sattan‍naṃ khau, bhikkhu, baujjhaṅgānaṃ abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā ‘duppañ‍ñau aiḷamūgau’ti vuc‍cati| katamaisaṃ  sattan‍naṃ? satisambaujjhaṅgassa…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā ‘duppañ‍ñau aiḷamūgau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. pañ‍ñavantasuttaṃ

226. ‘‘‘pañ‍ñavā anaiḷamūgau, pañ‍ñavā anaiḷamūgau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, ‘pañ‍ñavā anaiḷamūgau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti? ‘‘sattan‍naṃ khau, bhikkhu, baujjhaṅgānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā ‘pañ‍ñavā anaiḷamūgau’ti vuc‍cati| katamaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ? satisambaujjhaṅgassa…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā ‘pañ‍ñavā anaiḷamūgau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. daliddasuttaṃ

227. ‘‘‘daliddau, daliddau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, ‘daliddau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti? ‘‘sattan‍naṃ khau, bhikkhu, baujjhaṅgānaṃ abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā ‘daliddau’ti vuc‍cati| katamaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ? satisambaujjhaṅgassa…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā ‘daliddau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. adaliddasuttaṃ

228. ‘‘‘adaliddau, adaliddau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, ‘adaliddau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti? ‘‘sattan‍naṃ khau, bhikkhu, baujjhaṅgānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā ‘adaliddau’ti vuc‍cati| katamaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ? satisambaujjhaṅgassa  …pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa – imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhu, sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā ‘adaliddau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. ādic‍casuttaṃ

229. ‘‘ādic‍cassa, bhikkhavai, udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – aruṇuggaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ uppādāya aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – kalyāṇamittatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaissati, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ajjhattikaṅgasuttaṃ

230. ‘‘ajjhattikaṃ, bhikkhavai, aṅganti karitvā nāñ‍ñaṃ aikaṅgampi samanupassāmi sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ uppādāya, yathayidaṃ – bhikkhavai, yaunisaumanasikārau| yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaissati, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau satta baujjhaṅgai  bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti  vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisaumanasikārasampan‍nau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. bāhiraṅgasuttaṃ

231. ‘‘bāhiraṃ, bhikkhavai, aṅganti karitvā nāñ‍ñaṃ aikaṅgampi samanupassāmi sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ uppādāya, yathayidaṃ – bhikkhavai, kalyāṇamittatā| kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaissati, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarissati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaiti, satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

cak‍kavattivaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

vidhā cak‍kavatti mārau, duppañ‍ñau pañ‍ñavaina ca|

daliddau adaliddau ca, ādic‍caṅgaina tai dasāti||

6. sākacchavaggau

1. āhārasuttaṃ

232. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍can‍nañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, nīvaraṇānaṃ sattan‍nañ‍ca baujjhaṅgānaṃ āhārañ‍ca anāhārañ‍ca daisaissāmi; taṃ suṇātha| kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa bhiyyaubhāvāya  vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, subhanimittaṃ| tattha ayaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, paṭighanimittaṃ| tattha ayaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, arati tandi vijambhitā bhattasammadau caitasau ca līnattaṃ| tattha ayaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa bhiyyaubhāvāya  vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, caitasau avūpasamau| tattha ayaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya uppādāya, uppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, vicikicchāṭṭhānīyā dhammā| tattha ayaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya uppādāya, uppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, satisambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhānīyā  dhammā| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau  ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya  pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, kusalākusalā dhammā sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jā dhammā hīnapaṇītā dhammā kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgā dhammā| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau  ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, ārambhadhātu nik‍kamadhātu parak‍kamadhātu| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, pītisambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhānīyā dhammā| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, kāyappassaddhi cittappassaddhi| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau  ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, samathanimittaṃ abyagganimittaṃ| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, āhārau anuppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhānīyā  dhammā| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, asubhanimittaṃ| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā kāmacchandassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau  ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, maittācaitauvimutti| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā byāpādassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, ārambhadhātu nik‍kamadhātu parak‍kamadhātu| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā thinamiddhassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, caitasau vūpasamau| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya uppādāya, uppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya? atthi, bhikkhavai, kusalākusalā dhammā sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jā dhammā hīnapaṇītā dhammā kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgā dhammā| tattha yaunisaumanasikārabahulīkārau  – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya uppādāya, uppan‍nāya vā vicikicchāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, satisambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhānīyā dhammā| tattha amanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā satisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau  ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, kusalākusalā dhammā sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jā dhammā hīnapaṇītā dhammā kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgā dhammā| tattha amanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa  bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, ārambhadhātu nik‍kamadhātu parak‍kamadhātu| tattha amanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa  bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, pītisambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhānīyā dhammā| tattha amanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, kāyappassaddhi cittappassaddhi| tattha amanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau  ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, samathanimittaṃ abyagganimittaṃ| tattha amanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, anāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa  uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā? atthi, bhikkhavai, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṭṭhānīyā dhammā| tattha amanasikārabahulīkārau – ayamanāhārau anuppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūriyā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pariyāyasuttaṃ

233. atha  khau sambahulā bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pavisiṃsu| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘samaṇau, āvusau, gautamau sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai satta baujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaithā’ti| mayampi khau, āvusau, sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaima – ‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai satta baujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaithā’ti| idha nau, āvusau, kau visaisau, kau  adhippayāsau, kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ samaṇassa vā  gautamassa amhākaṃ vā, yadidaṃ – dhammadaisanāya vā dhammadaisanaṃ, anusāsaniyā vā anusāsani’’nti?

atha khau tai bhikkhū taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃsu nappaṭik‍kausiṃsu; anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu – ‘‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmā’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘idha  mayaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pavisimha| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘atippagau khau tāva sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ, yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāmā’ti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamimha; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudimha| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdimha| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau amhai, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘samaṇau, āvusau, gautamau sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ‘aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya  dubbalīkaraṇai satta baujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaithā’ti| mayampi khau, āvusau, sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaima – ‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai satta baujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaithā’ti| idha nau, āvusau, kau visaisau, kau adhippayāsau, kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ samaṇassa vā gautamassa amhākaṃ vā, yadidaṃ – dhammadaisanāya vā dhammadaisanaṃ, anusāsaniyā vā anusāsani’’nti?

‘‘atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandimha nappaṭik‍kausimha, anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamimha – ‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmā’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃvādinau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘atthi panāvusau, pariyāyau, yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā dasa haunti, satta baujjhaṅgā catuddasā’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhā, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā na caiva sampāyissanti, uttariñ‍ca vighātaṃ āpaj‍jissanti| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā taṃ, bhikkhavai, avisayasmiṃ| ‘‘nāhaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya, yau imaisaṃ pañhānaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaiyya, añ‍ñatra tathāgataina vā tathāgatasāvakaina vā itau vā pana sutvā’’|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau, yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā dasa haunti? yadapi, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandau tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ, yadapi bahiddhā kāmacchandau tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ| ‘kāmacchandanīvaraṇa’nti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti| yadapi, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ byāpādau tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ, yadapi bahiddhā byāpādau tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ| ‘byāpādanīvaraṇa’nti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti| yadapi, bhikkhavai, thinaṃ tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ, yadapi middhaṃ tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ| ‘thinamiddhanīvaraṇa’nti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti| yadapi, bhikkhavai, uddhac‍caṃ tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ, yadapi kuk‍kuc‍caṃ tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ| ‘uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇa’nti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti| yadapi, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu vicikicchā tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ, yadapi bahiddhā dhammaisu vicikicchā tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ| ‘vicikicchānīvaraṇa’nti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau, yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā dasa haunti|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau, yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma satta baujjhaṅgā catuddasa haunti? yadapi, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu sati tadapi satisambaujjhaṅgau, yadapi bahiddhā dhammaisu sati tadapi satisambaujjhaṅgau| ‘satisambaujjhaṅgau’ti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti|

‘‘yadapi, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicināti (ka॰) pavicarati parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jati  tadapi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, yadapi bahiddhā dhammaisu pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicarati parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jati tadapi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau| ‘dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau’ti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti|

‘‘yadapi, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ vīriyaṃ tadapi vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, yadapi caitasikaṃ vīriyaṃ tadapi vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau| ‘vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau’ti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti|

‘‘yadapi, bhikkhavai, savitak‍kasavicārā pīti tadapi pītisambaujjhaṅgau, yadapi avitak‍kaavicārā pīti tadapi pītisambaujjhaṅgau| ‘pītisambaujjhaṅgau’ti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti|

‘‘yadapi, bhikkhavai, kāyappassaddhi tadapi passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, yadapi cittappassaddhi tadapi passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau| ‘passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau’ti  iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti|

‘‘yadapi, bhikkhavai, savitak‍kau savicārau samādhi tadapi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, yadapi avitak‍kaavicārau samādhi tadapi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau| ‘samādhisambaujjhaṅgau’ti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti|

‘‘yadapi, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu upaikkhā tadapi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau, yadapi bahiddhā dhammaisu upaikkhā tadapi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau| ‘upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’ti iti hidaṃ uddaisaṃ gacchati| tadamināpaitaṃ pariyāyaina dvayaṃ hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau, yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma satta baujjhaṅgā catuddasā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. aggisuttaṃ

234. atha  khau sambahulā bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiyaṃ  piṇḍāya pavisiṃsu| (pariyāyasuttasadisaṃ)|

‘‘aivaṃvādinau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai līnaṃ cittaṃ hauti, katamaisaṃ tasmiṃ samayai baujjhaṅgānaṃ akālau bhāvanāya, katamaisaṃ tasmiṃ samayai baujjhaṅgānaṃ kālau bhāvanāya? yasmiṃ panāvusau, samayai uddhataṃ cittaṃ hauti, katamaisaṃ tasmiṃ samayai baujjhaṅgānaṃ akālau bhāvanāya, katamaisaṃ tasmiṃ samayai baujjhaṅgānaṃ kālau bhāvanāyā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā na caiva sampāyissanti, uttariñ‍ca vighātaṃ āpaj‍jissanti| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā taṃ, bhikkhavai, avisayasmiṃ|

‘‘nāhaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya yau imaisaṃ pañhānaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaina  cittaṃ ārādhaiyya, añ‍ñatra tathāgataina vā tathāgatasāvakaina vā itau vā pana sutvā|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai līnaṃ cittaṃ hauti, akālau tasmiṃ samayai passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, akālau samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, akālau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? līnaṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ taṃ aitaihi dhammaihi dussamuṭṭhāpayaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau parittaṃ aggiṃ uj‍jālaitukāmau assa| sau tattha al‍lāni  caiva tiṇāni pakkhipaiyya, al‍lāni ca gaumayāni pakkhipaiyya, al‍lāni ca kaṭṭhāni pakkhipaiyya, udakavātañ‍ca dadaiyya, paṃsukaina ca aukiraiyya; bhabbau nu khau sau purisau parittaṃ aggiṃ uj‍jālitu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ samayai līnaṃ cittaṃ hauti, akālau tasmiṃ samayai passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, akālau samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, akālau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? līnaṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ taṃ aitaihi dhammaihi dussamuṭṭhāpayaṃ hauti|

‘‘yasmiñ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, samayai līnaṃ cittaṃ hauti, kālau tasmiṃ  samayai dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? līnaṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ taṃ aitaihi dhammaihi susamuṭṭhāpayaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau parittaṃ aggiṃ uj‍jālaitukāmau assa| sau tattha sukkhāni caiva tiṇāni pakkhipaiyya, sukkhāni gaumayāni pakkhipaiyya, sukkhāni kaṭṭhāni pakkhipaiyya, mukhavātañ‍ca dadaiyya, na ca paṃsukaina aukiraiyya; bhabbau nu khau sau purisau parittaṃ aggiṃ uj‍jālitu’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ samayai līnaṃ cittaṃ hauti, kālau tasmiṃ samayai dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? līnaṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ taṃ aitaihi dhammaihi susamuṭṭhāpayaṃ hauti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai uddhattaṃ cittaṃ hauti, akālau tasmiṃ samayai dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, akālau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, akālau pītisambaujjhaṅgassa  bhāvanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? uddhataṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ taṃ aitaihi dhammaihi duvūpasamayaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ nibbāpaitukāmau assa| sau tattha sukkhāni caiva tiṇāni pakkhipaiyya, sukkhāni ca gaumayāni pakkhipaiyya, sukkhāni ca kaṭṭhāni pakkhipaiyya, mukhavātañ‍ca dadaiyya, na ca paṃsukaina aukiraiyya; bhabbau nu khau sau purisau  mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ nibbāpaitu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ samayai uddhataṃ cittaṃ hauti, akālau tasmiṃ samayai dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, akālau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, akālau pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? uddhataṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ taṃ aitaihi dhammaihi duvūpasamayaṃ hauti|

‘‘yasmiñ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, samayai uddhataṃ cittaṃ hauti, kālau tasmiṃ samayai passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? uddhataṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ taṃ aitaihi dhammaihi suvūpasamayaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ nibbāpaitukāmau assa| sau tattha al‍lāni caiva tiṇāni pakkhipaiyya, al‍lāni ca gaumayāni pakkhipaiyya, al‍lāni ca kaṭṭhāni pakkhipaiyya, udakavātañ‍ca dadaiyya, paṃsukaina ca aukiraiyya; bhabbau nu khau sau purisau mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ nibbāpaitu’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ samayai uddhataṃ cittaṃ  hauti, kālau tasmiṃ samayai passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? uddhataṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ taṃ aitaihi dhammaihi suvūpasamayaṃ hauti| satiñ‍ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbatthikaṃ vadāmī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. maittāsahagatasuttaṃ

235. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kauliyaisu viharati haliddavasanaṃ nāma kauliyānaṃ nigamau| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya haliddavasanaṃ piṇḍāya pavisiṃsu| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva haliddavasanai piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘samaṇau, āvusau, gautamau sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ; iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā  vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharatha| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ; iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya  sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ karuṇāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharatha| muditāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ; iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ muditāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharatha| upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ; iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharathā’’’ti|

‘‘mayampi  khau, āvusau, sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaima – ‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha…pai॰… karuṇāsahagataina caitasā… muditāsahagataina caitasā… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā  aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ; iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharathā’ti| idha nau, āvusau, kau visaisau, kau adhippayāsau, kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ samaṇassa vā gautamassa amhākaṃ vā, yadidaṃ – dhammadaisanāya  vā  dhammadaisanaṃ, anusāsaniyā vā anusāsani’’nti?

atha khau tai bhikkhū taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃsu nappaṭik‍kausiṃsu| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu – ‘‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmā’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū haliddavasanai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya haliddavasanai piṇḍāya pavisimha| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘atippagau khau tāva haliddavasanai piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāmā’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamimha, upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudimha| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdimha| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau amhai, bhantai, tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘samaṇau, āvusau, gautamau sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘aitha  tumhai, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā  viharatha…pai॰… karuṇāsahagataina caitasā … muditāsahagataina caitasā… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ; iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina  appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharathā’’’ti|

‘‘mayampi khau, āvusau, sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaima – ‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha…pai॰… karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ; iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā  viharathā’ti| idha nau, āvusau, kau visaisau, kau adhippayāsau, kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ samaṇassa vā gautamassa amhākaṃ vā, yadidaṃ, dhammadaisanāya vā dhammadaisanaṃ, anusāsaniyā vā anusāsani’’nti?

atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandimha nappaṭik‍kausimha, anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamimha – ‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmā’ti|

‘‘aivaṃvādinau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘kathaṃ bhāvitā panāvusau, maittācaitauvimutti, kiṃgatikā hauti, kiṃparamā, kiṃphalā, kiṃpariyausānā? kathaṃ bhāvitā panāvusau, karuṇācaitauvimutti, kiṃgatikā hauti, kiṃparamā, kiṃphalā, kiṃpariyausānā? kathaṃ bhāvitā panāvusau, muditācaitauvimutti, kiṃgatikā hauti, kiṃparamā, kiṃphalā, kiṃpariyausānā? kathaṃ bhāvitā panāvusau, upaikkhācaitauvimutti, kiṃgatikā hauti, kiṃparamā, kiṃphalā, kiṃpariyausānā’’’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā  paribbājakā na caiva sampāyissanti, uttariñ‍ca vighātaṃ āpaj‍jissanti| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā taṃ, bhikkhavai, avisayasmiṃ| ‘‘nāhaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, passāmi sadaivakai  laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya, yau imaisaṃ pañhānaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaiyya, añ‍ñatra tathāgataina vā tathāgatasāvakaina  vā itau vā pana sutvā’’|

‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, maittācaitauvimutti, kiṃgatikā hauti, kiṃparamā, kiṃphalā, kiṃpariyausānā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu maittāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… maittāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| sau sacai ākaṅkhati ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati ‘paṭikūlai appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati ‘appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlai ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati ‘paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati ‘appaṭikūlañ‍ca paṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyyaṃ satau sampajānau’ti, upaikkhakau ca tattha viharati satau sampajānau, subhaṃ vā khau pana vimaukkhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| subhaparamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, maittācaitauvimuttiṃ vadāmi, idhapañ‍ñassa bhikkhunau uttarivimuttiṃ appaṭivijjhatau|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, karuṇācaitauvimutti, kiṃgatikā hauti, kiṃparamā, kiṃphalā, kiṃpariyausānā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu karuṇāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰…  karuṇāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| sau sacai ākaṅkhati ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati…pai॰… sacai ākaṅkhati ‘appaṭikūlañ‍ca paṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyyaṃ satau sampajānau’ti, upaikkhakau tattha viharati satau sampajānau| sabbasau vā pana rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaparamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, karuṇācaitauvimuttiṃ  vadāmi, idhapañ‍ñassa bhikkhunau uttarivimuttiṃ appaṭivijjhatau|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, muditācaitauvimutti, kiṃgatikā hauti, kiṃparamā, kiṃphalā, kiṃpariyausānā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu muditāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… muditāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| sau sacai ākaṅkhati ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati  …pai॰… sacai ākaṅkhati ‘appaṭikūlañ‍ca paṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyyaṃ satau sampajānau’ti, upaikkhakau tattha viharati satau sampajānau| sabbasau vā pana ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaparamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, muditācaitauvimuttiṃ vadāmi, idhapañ‍ñassa bhikkhunau uttarivimuttiṃ appaṭivijjhatau|

‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, upaikkhācaitauvimutti, kiṃgatikā hauti, kiṃparamā, kiṃphalā, kiṃpariyausānā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu upaikkhāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| sau sacai ākaṅkhati ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati ‘paṭikūlai appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati ‘appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlai ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati ‘paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati| sacai ākaṅkhati ‘appaṭikūlañ‍ca paṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyyaṃ satau sampajānau’ti, upaikkhakau tattha viharati satau sampajānau| sabbasau  vā pana viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaparamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, upaikkhācaitauvimuttiṃ vadāmi, idhapañ‍ñassa bhikkhunau uttarivimuttiṃ appaṭivijjhatau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. saṅgāravasuttaṃ

236. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kau  nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yainaikadā dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā? kau pana, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yainaikadā dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā’’ti?

‘‘yasmiṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, samayai kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, paratthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati; dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau saṃsaṭṭhau lākhāya vā haliddiyā vā nīliyā vā mañ‍jiṭṭhāya vā| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ na jānaiyya na passaiyya| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ  samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati; dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati byāpādaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰…  ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati; dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau agginā santattau pak‍kuthitau pak‍kudhitau (ka॰), uk‍kaṭṭhitau (sī॰), uk‍kuṭṭhitau (syā॰) usmudakajātau  ussadakajātau (sī॰), usmādakajātau (syā॰)| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ na jānaiyya na passaiyya| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati byāpādaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, paratthampi tasmiṃ samayai…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati; dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati thinamiddhaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca thinamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai  yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati; dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau saivālapaṇakapariyaunaddhau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau  yathābhūtaṃ na jānaiyya na passaiyya| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati thinamiddhaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca thinamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati; dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰…  ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati; dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau vātairitau calitau bhantau ūmijātau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ  na jānaiyya na passaiyya| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati; dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi… dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau āvilau luḷitau kalalībhūtau andhakārai nikkhittau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ na jānaiyya na  passaiyya| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati, paratthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti  na passati, ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati; dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yainaikadā dīgharattaṃ sajjhāyakatāpi mantā nappaṭibhanti, pagaiva asajjhāyakatā|

‘‘yasmiñ‍ca khau, brāhmaṇa, samayai na kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai  yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, paratthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati; dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau asaṃsaṭṭhau lākhāya vā haliddiyā vā nīliyā vā mañ‍jiṭṭhāya vā| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ jānaiyya passaiyya| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na byāpādaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca  byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi… dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau na agginā santattau na pak‍kuthitau na usmudakajātau, tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ jānaiyya passaiyya| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na byāpādaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca byāpādassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi… dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na thinamiddhaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca thinamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi … dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau na saivālapaṇakapariyaunaddhau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ  jānaiyya passaiyya| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na thinamiddhaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca thinamiddhassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi… dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi… dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau na vātairitau na calitau na bhantau na ūmijātau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ jānaiyya passaiyya| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, paratthampi…pai॰… ubhayatthampi… dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti pajānāti passati (syā॰), attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, paratthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati; ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati; dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udapattau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau ālaukai nikkhittau| tattha cakkhumā purisau sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau yathābhūtaṃ jānaiyya passaiyya| aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yasmiṃ samayai na vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati na vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, attatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, paratthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati, ubhayatthampi tasmiṃ samayai yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati; dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā| ayaṃ  khau, brāhmaṇa, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yainaikadā dīgharattaṃ asajjhāyakatāpi mantā paṭibhanti, pagaiva sajjhāyakatā|

‘‘sattimai, brāhmaṇa, baujjhaṅgā anāvaraṇā anīvaraṇā caitasau anupak‍kilaisā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau khau, brāhmaṇa, anāvaraṇau  anīvaraṇau caitasau anupak‍kilaisau bhāvitau bahulīkatau vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattati…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau khau, brāhmaṇa, anāvaraṇau anīvaraṇau caitasau anupak‍kilaisau bhāvitau bahulīkatau vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattati| imai khau, brāhmaṇa, satta baujjhaṅgā anāvaraṇā anīvaraṇā caitasau anupak‍kilaisā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattantī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰…  upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. abhayasuttaṃ

237. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau abhayau rājakumārau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau abhayau rājakumārau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pūraṇau, bhantai, kassapau aivamāha – ‘natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau añ‍ñāṇāya adassanāya| ahaitu, appac‍cayau appac‍cayā (sī॰), appac‍cayaṃ (?) añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ hauti| natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau ñāṇāya dassanāya| ahaitu, appac‍cayau ñāṇaṃ dassanaṃ hautī’ti| idha bhagavā kimāhā’’ti? ‘‘atthi, rājakumāra, haitu, atthi pac‍cayau añ‍ñāṇāya adassanāya| sahaitu, sappac‍cayau sappac‍cayā (sī॰), sappac‍cayaṃ (?) añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ hauti| atthi, rājakumāra, haitu, atthi pac‍cayau ñāṇāya dassanāya| sahaitu, sappac‍cayau ñāṇaṃ  dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, haitu, katamau pac‍cayau añ‍ñāṇāya adassanāya? kathaṃ sahaitu, sappac‍cayau añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘yasmiṃ khau, rājakumāra, samayai kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati kāmarāgaparaitaina, uppan‍nassa ca kāmarāgassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati – ayampi khau, rājakumāra, haitu, ayaṃ  pac‍cayau añ‍ñāṇāya adassanāya| aivampi sahaitu sappac‍cayau añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, rājakumāra, yasmiṃ samayai byāpādapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati byāpādaparaitaina…pai॰… thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhitaina… uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitaina… vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā viharati vicikicchāparaitaina, uppan‍nāya ca vicikicchāya nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati – ayampi khau, rājakumāra, haitu, ayaṃ  pac‍cayau añ‍ñāṇāya adassanāya| aivampi sahaitu sappac‍cayau añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

‘‘kau  nāmāyaṃ, bhantai, dhammapariyāyau’’ti? ‘‘nīvaraṇā nāmaitai, rājakumārā’’ti| ‘‘taggha, bhagavā, nīvaraṇā; taggha, sugata, nīvaraṇā! aikamaikainapi khau, bhantai, nīvaraṇaina abhibhūtau yathābhūtaṃ na jānaiyya na passaiyya, kau pana vādau pañ‍cahi nīvaraṇaihi?

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, haitu, katamau pac‍cayau ñāṇāya dassanāya? kathaṃ sahaitu, sappac‍cayau ñāṇaṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, rājakumāra, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| sau satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvitaina cittaina yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati – ayampi khau, rājakumāra, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau ñāṇāya dassanāya| aivampi sahaitu, sappac‍cayau ñāṇaṃ dassanaṃ hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, rājakumāra, bhikkhu…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| sau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvitaina cittaina yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati – ayampi khau, rājakumāra, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau ñāṇāya dassanāya| aivaṃ sahaitu, sappac‍cayau ñāṇaṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

‘‘kau nāmāyaṃ, bhantai, dhammapariyāyau’’ti? ‘‘baujjhaṅgā nāmaitai, rājakumārā’’ti| ‘‘taggha, bhagavā, baujjhaṅgā; taggha, sugata, baujjhaṅgā! aikamaikainapi khau, bhantai, baujjhaṅgaina saman‍nāgatau yathābhūtaṃ  jānaiyya passaiyya, kau pana vādau sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi? yaupi mai, bhantai, gijjhakūṭaṃ pabbataṃ ārauhantassa  kāyakilamathau cittakilamathau, saupi mai paṭippassaddhau, dhammau ca mai abhisamitau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

sākacchavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

āhārā  pariyāyamaggi, maittaṃ saṅgāravaina ca|

abhayau pucchitau pañhaṃ, gijjhakūṭamhi pabbataiti||

7. ānāpānavaggau

1. aṭṭhikamahapphalasuttaṃ

238. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā’’ti|

añ‍ñataraphalasuttaṃ

‘‘aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāya, bhikkhavai, bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā| kathaṃ bhāvitāya ca khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāya kathaṃ bahulīkatāya dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitāya khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāya aivaṃ bahulīkatāya  dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ  pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti|

mahatthasuttaṃ

‘‘aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahatau atthāya saṃvattati| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahatau atthāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahatau atthāya saṃvattatī’’ti|

yaugakkhaimasuttaṃ

‘‘aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahatau yaugakkhaimāya saṃvattati| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahatau yaugakkhaimāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahatau yaugakkhaimāya saṃvattatī’’ti|

saṃvaigasuttaṃ

‘‘aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahatau saṃvaigāya saṃvattati| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahatau saṃvaigāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahatau saṃvaigāya saṃvattatī’’ti|

phāsuvihārasuttaṃ

‘‘aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahatau phāsuvihārāya saṃvattati| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahatau phāsuvihārāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… aṭṭhikasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhikasañ‍ñā aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahatau phāsuvihārāya saṃvattatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. puḷavakasuttaṃ

239. ‘‘puḷavakasañ‍ñā puḷuvakasañ‍ñā (ka॰), bhikkhavai, bhāvitā…pai॰…  dutiyaṃ|

3. vinīlakasuttaṃ

240. ‘‘vinīlakasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰… tatiyaṃ|

4. vicchiddakasuttaṃ

241. ‘‘vicchiddakasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰…  catutthaṃ|

5. uddhumātakasuttaṃ

242. ‘‘uddhumātakasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ|

6. maittāsuttaṃ

243. ‘‘maittā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā…pai॰…  chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. karuṇāsuttaṃ

244. ‘‘karuṇā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā…pai॰… sattamaṃ|

8. muditāsuttaṃ

245. ‘‘muditā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā…pai॰…  aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. upaikkhāsuttaṃ

246. ‘‘upaikkhā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā…pai॰… navamaṃ|

10. ānāpānasuttaṃ

247. ‘‘ānāpānassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā…pai॰… dasamaṃ|

ānāpānavaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aṭṭhikapuḷavakaṃ vinīlakaṃ, vicchiddakaṃ uddhumātaina pañ‍camaṃ|

maittā karuṇā muditā upaikkhā, ānāpānaina tai dasāti||

8. niraudhavaggau

1. asubhasuttaṃ

248. ‘‘asubhasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ|

2. maraṇasuttaṃ

249. ‘‘maraṇasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰…  dutiyaṃ|

3. āhāraipaṭikūlasuttaṃ

250. ‘‘āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰… tatiyaṃ|

4. anabhiratisuttaṃ

251. ‘‘sabbalaukai anabhiratisañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰…  catutthaṃ|

5. anic‍casuttaṃ

252. ‘‘anic‍casañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dukkhasuttaṃ

253. ‘‘anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰…  chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. anattasuttaṃ

254. ‘‘dukkhai  anattasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰…  sattamaṃ|

8. pahānasuttaṃ

255. ‘‘pahānasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰… aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. virāgasuttaṃ

256. ‘‘virāgasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai…pai॰…  navamaṃ|

10. niraudhasuttaṃ

257. ‘‘niraudhasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, niraudhasañ‍ñā kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu niraudhasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… niraudhasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, niraudhasañ‍ñā aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsāti|

‘‘niraudhasañ‍ñāya, bhikkhavai, bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā| kathaṃ bhāvitāya, bhikkhavai, niraudhasañ‍ñāya kathaṃ bahulīkatāya dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  niraudhasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… niraudhasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitāya khau, bhikkhavai, niraudhasañ‍ñāya aivaṃ bahulīkatāya dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti|

‘‘niraudhasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahatau atthāya saṃvattati, mahatau yaugakkhaimāya saṃvattati, mahatau saṃvaigāya saṃvattati, mahatau phāsuvihārāya saṃvattati| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, niraudhasañ‍ñā kathaṃ bahulīkatā  mahatau atthāya saṃvattati, mahatau yaugakkhaimāya  saṃvattati, mahatau saṃvaigāya saṃvattati, mahatau phāsuvihārāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu niraudhasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… niraudhasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, niraudhasañ‍ñā aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahatau atthāya saṃvattati, mahatau yaugakkhaimāya saṃvattati, mahatau saṃvaigāya saṃvattati, mahatau phāsuvihārāya saṃvattatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

niraudhavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

asubhamaraṇaāhārai, paṭikūlaanabhirataina paṭikūlaina ca sabbalaukai (syā॰)|

anic‍cadukkhaanattapahānaṃ, virāganiraudhaina tai dasāti||

9. gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1-12. gaṅgānadīādisuttaṃ

258-269. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaintau satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau  nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaintau satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvaintau satta baujjhaṅgai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| (yāva aisanā pāḷi vitthāraitabbā)|

gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau navamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha  pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

dvaitai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

10. appamādavaggau

1-10. tathāgatādisuttaṃ

270. ‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattā apadā vā dvipadā vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vāti vitthāraitabbaṃ|

appamādavaggau dasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

tathāgataṃ padaṃ kūṭaṃ, mūlaṃ sāraina vassikaṃ|

rājā candimasūriyā ca, vatthaina dasamaṃ padanti||

(appamādavaggau baujjhaṅgasaṃyuttassa baujjhaṅgavasaina vitthāraitabbā)|

11. balakaraṇīyavaggau

1-12. balādisuttaṃ

280. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyanti…pai॰…|

balakaraṇīyavaggau aikādasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

balaṃ  bījañ‍ca nāgau ca, rukkhau kumbhaina sūkiyā|

ākāsaina ca dvai maighā, nāvā āgantukā nadīti||

(balakaraṇīyavaggau baujjhaṅgasaṃyuttassa baujjhaṅgavasaina vitthāraitabbā)|

12. aisanāvaggau

1-10. aisanādisuttaṃ

292. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, aisanā| katamā tissau? kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanāti vitthāraitabbaṃ|

aisanāvaggau dvādasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aisanā vidhā āsavau, bhavau ca dukkhatā tissau|

khilaṃ malañ‍ca nīghau ca, vaidanā taṇhā tasināya cāti||

(baujjhaṅgasaṃyuttassa aisanāpaiyyālaṃ vivaikanissitatau vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

13. aughavaggau

1-8. aughādisuttaṃ

302. ‘‘cattāraumai bhikkhavai, aughā| katamai cattārau? kāmaughau, bhavaughau, diṭṭhaughau, avij‍jaughauti vitthāraitabbaṃ|

10. uddhambhāgiyasuttaṃ

311. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā  – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvaitabbā| katamai satta? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ… amataugadhaṃ amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ… nibbānanin‍naṃ nibbānapauṇaṃ nibbānapabbhāraṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya imai satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

aughavaggau tairasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aughau  yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthā anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇā, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyānīti||

14. punagaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

312-323

 punagaṅgānadīādisuttaṃ

vaggau cuddasamau|

uddānaṃ –

cha  pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

dvaitai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

(baujjhaṅgasaṃyuttassa gaṅgāpaiyyālaṃ rāgavasaina vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

15. punaappamādavaggau

324-333

 tathāgatādisuttaṃ

pan‍narasamau|

uddānaṃ –

tathāgataṃ padaṃ kūṭaṃ, mūlaṃ sāraina vassikaṃ|

rājā candimasūriyā ca, vatthaina dasamaṃ padanti||

(appamādavaggau rāgavasaina vitthāraitabbau)|

16. punabalakaraṇīyavaggau

334-345

 punabalādisuttaṃ

sauḷasamau|

uddānaṃ –

balaṃ  bījañ‍ca nāgau ca, rukkhau kumbhaina sūkiyā|

ākāsaina ca dvai maighā, nāvā āgantukā nadīti||

(baujjhaṅgasaṃyuttassa balakaraṇīyavaggau rāgavasaina vitthāraitabbau)|

17. punaaisanāvaggau

346-356

 punaaisanādisuttaṃ

punaaisanāvaggau sattarasamau|

uddānaṃ –

aisanā vidhā āsavau, bhavau ca dukkhatā tissau|

khilaṃ malañ‍ca nīghau ca, vaidanātaṇhā tasināya cāti||

18. punaaughavaggau

357-366

 punaaughādisuttaṃ

baujjhaṅgasaṃyutassa punaaughavaggau aṭṭhārasamau|

uddānaṃ –

aughau yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthā anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇā, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyānīti||

(rāgavinayapariyausāna-dausavinayapariyausāna-mauhavinayapariyausānavaggau vitthāraitabbau)| (yadapi maggasaṃyuttaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ, tadapi baujjhaṅgasaṃyuttaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

baujjhaṅgasaṃyuttaṃ dutiyaṃ|



3. satipaṭṭhānasaṃyuttaṃ

1. ambapālivaggau

1. ambapālisuttaṃ

367. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati ambapālivanai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘aikāyanau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; cittai cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aikāyanau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. satisuttaṃ

368. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati ambapālivanai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘satau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu viharaiyya sampajānau| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya  laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānakārī hauti| satau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu viharaiyya sampajānau| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. bhikkhusuttaṃ

369. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau  appamattau ātāpī  pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva panidhaikac‍cai maughapurisā mañ‍caiva mamaiva (sī॰) ajjhaisanti, dhammai ca bhāsitai mamaiva anubandhitabbaṃ mañ‍ñantī’’ti| ‘‘daisaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ, daisaitu sugatau saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ| appaiva nāmāhaṃ bhagavatau bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānaiyyaṃ, appaiva nāmāhaṃ bhagavatau bhāsitassa dāyādau assa’’nti| ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, ādimaiva visaudhaihi kusalaisu dhammaisu| kau cādi kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ? sīlañ‍ca suvisuddhaṃ, diṭṭhi ca ujukā| yatau khau tai, bhikkhu, sīlañ‍ca suvisuddhaṃ bhavissati diṭṭhi ca ujukā, tatau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānai tividhaina bhāvaiyyāsi|

katamai  cattārau? idha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, ajjhattaṃ vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; bahiddhā vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; ajjhattabahiddhā  vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ajjhattaṃ vā vaidanāsu…pai॰… bahiddhā vā vaidanāsu…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā vā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ajjhattaṃ vā cittai…pai॰… bahiddhā vā cittai…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā vā cittai cittānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ajjhattaṃ vā dhammaisu…pai॰… bahiddhā vā dhammaisu…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| yatau khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānai aivaṃ tividhaina bhāvaissasi, tatau tuyhaṃ, bhikkhu, yā ratti vā divasau vā āgamissati vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihānī’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā  anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sau bhikkhu aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| tatiyaṃ|

4. sālasuttaṃ

370. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu viharati sālāya brāhmaṇagāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… aitadavauca –

‘‘yai tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, tai vau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanāya samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? aitha tumhai, āvusau, kāyai  kāyānupassinau viharatha ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, kāyassa yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassinau viharatha ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, vaidanānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya; cittai cittānupassinau viharatha ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, cittassa yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya; dhammaisu dhammānupassinau viharatha ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, dhammānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya| yaipi  tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū saikhā appattamānasā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānā viharanti, taipi kāyai kāyānupassinau viharanti ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, kāyassa pariñ‍ñāya; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassinau viharanti ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya; cittai cittānupassinau viharanti ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, cittassa pariñ‍ñāya; dhammaisu dhammānupassinau viharanti ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, dhammānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya|

‘‘yaipi  tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā, taipi kāyai kāyānupassinau viharanti ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, kāyaina visaṃyuttā; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassinau viharanti ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, vaidanāhi visaṃyuttā; cittai cittānupassinau viharanti ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, cittaina visaṃyuttā; dhammaisu dhammānupassinau viharanti ātāpinau sampajānā aikaudibhūtā vippasan‍nacittā samāhitā aikaggacittā, dhammaihi visaṃyuttā|

‘‘yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, tai vau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanāya  samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. akusalarāsisuttaṃ

371. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘‘akusalarāsī’ti, bhikkhavai, vadamānau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| kaivalau hāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, akusalarāsi, yadidaṃ – pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ, thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ, vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ| ‘akusalarāsī’ti, bhikkhavai, vadamānau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| kaivalau hāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, akusalarāsi, yadidaṃ – pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā|

‘‘‘kusalarāsī’ti, bhikkhavai, vadamānau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| kaivalau hāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kusalarāsi, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ‘kusalarāsī’ti, bhikkhavai, vadamānau imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānai sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| kaivalau hāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kusalarāsi, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sakuṇagghisuttaṃ

372. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sakuṇagghi lāpaṃ sakuṇaṃ sahasā ajjhappattā aggahaisi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, lāpau sakuṇau sakuṇagghiyā hariyamānau aivaṃ paridaivasi – ‘mayamaivamha mayamaivāmha (ka॰) alakkhikā, mayaṃ  appapuñ‍ñā, yai mayaṃ agaucarai carimha paravisayai| sacaij‍ja mayaṃ gaucarai caraiyyāma sakai paittikai visayai, na myāyaṃ na cāyaṃ (sī॰), sakuṇagghi, alaṃ abhavissa, yadidaṃ – yuddhāyā’ti| ‘kau pana tai, lāpa, gaucarau sakau paittikau visayau’ti? ‘yadidaṃ – naṅgalakaṭṭhakaraṇaṃ laiḍḍuṭṭhāna’’’nti| ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, sakuṇagghi sakai balai apatthaddhā sakai balai asaṃvadamānā avacamānā (sī॰) lāpaṃ sakuṇaṃ pamuñ‍ci – ‘gaccha khau tvaṃ, lāpa, tatrapi mai gantvā na maukkhasī’’’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, lāpau sakuṇau naṅgalakaṭṭhakaraṇaṃ laiḍḍuṭṭhānaṃ gantvā mahantaṃ laiḍḍuṃ abhiruhitvā sakuṇagghiṃ vadamānau aṭṭhāsi – ‘aihi khau dāni mai, sakuṇagghi, aihi khau dāni mai, sakuṇagghī’ti| atha khau sā, bhikkhavai, sakuṇagghi sakai balai apatthaddhā sakai balai asaṃvadamānā ubhau pakkhai san‍nayha sandhāya (sī॰ syā॰) lāpaṃ sakuṇaṃ sahasā ajjhappattā| yadā khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñāsi lāpau sakuṇau ‘bahuāgatau khau myāyaṃ sakuṇagghī’ti, atha tassaiva laiḍḍussa antaraṃ pac‍cupādi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sakuṇagghi tatthaiva uraṃ pac‍catāḷaisi| aivañhi taṃ aivaṃ haitaṃ (sī॰), bhikkhavai, hauti yau agaucarai carati paravisayai|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, mā agaucarai carittha paravisayai| agaucarai, bhikkhavai, carataṃ paravisayai lacchati mārau autāraṃ, lacchati mārau ārammaṇaṃ| kau ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau agaucarau paravisayau? yadidaṃ – pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā  saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā  gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau agaucarau paravisayau|

‘‘gaucarai, bhikkhavai, caratha sakai paittikai visayai| gaucarai, bhikkhavai, carataṃ sakai paittikai visayai na lacchati mārau autāraṃ, na lacchati mārau ārammaṇaṃ| kau  ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau gaucarau sakau paittikau visayau? yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau gaucarau sakau paittikau visayau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mak‍kaṭasuttaṃ

373. ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, himavatau pabbatarājassa duggā visamā daisā, yattha naiva mak‍kaṭānaṃ cārī na manussānaṃ| atthi, bhikkhavai, himavatau pabbatarājassa  duggā visamā daisā, yattha mak‍kaṭānañhi khau cārī, na manussānaṃ| atthi, bhikkhavai, himavatau pabbatarājassa samā bhūmibhāgā ramaṇīyā, yattha mak‍kaṭānañ‍caiva cārī manussānañ‍ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, luddā mak‍kaṭavīthīsu laipaṃ auḍḍainti mak‍kaṭānaṃ bādhanāya|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai mak‍kaṭā abālajātikā alaulajātikā, tai taṃ laipaṃ disvā ārakā parivaj‍janti| yau pana sau hauti  mak‍kaṭau bālajātikau laulajātikau, sau taṃ laipaṃ upasaṅkamitvā hatthaina gaṇhāti| sau tattha bajjhati| ‘hatthaṃ maucaissāmī’ti dutiyaina hatthaina gaṇhāti| sau tattha bajjhati| ‘ubhau hatthai maucaissāmī’ti pādaina gaṇhāti| sau tattha bajjhati| ‘ubhau hatthai maucaissāmi pādañ‍cā’ti dutiyaina pādaina gaṇhāti| sau tattha bajjhati| ‘ubhau hatthai maucaissāmi pādai cā’ti tuṇḍaina gaṇhāti| sau tattha bajjhati| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, mak‍kaṭau pañ‍cauḍḍitau thunaṃ saiti anayaṃ āpan‍nau byasanaṃ āpan‍nau yathākāmakaraṇīyau  luddassa| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, luddau vijjhitvā tasmiṃyaiva kaṭṭhakataṅgārai tasmiṃyaiva mak‍kaṭaṃ uddharitvā (sī॰ syā॰) avassaj‍jaitvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamati| aivaṃ sau taṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yau agaucarai carati paravisayai|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, mā agaucarai carittha paravisayai| agaucarai, bhikkhavai, carataṃ paravisayai lacchati mārau autāraṃ, lacchati mārau ārammaṇaṃ| kau ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau agaucarau paravisayau? yadidaṃ – pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau agaucarau paravisayau|

‘‘gaucarai, bhikkhavai, caratha sakai paittikai visayai| gaucarai, bhikkhavai, carataṃ sakai paittikai visayai na lacchati mārau autāraṃ, na lacchati mārau ārammaṇaṃ| kau ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau gaucarau sakau paittikau visayau? yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau gaucarau sakau paittikau visayau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. sūdasuttaṃ

374. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, bālau abyattau akusalau sūdau rājānaṃ vā rājamahāmattaṃ vā rājamahāmattānaṃ vā (sī॰) nānac‍cayaihi sūpaihi pac‍cupaṭṭhitau assa – ambilaggaihipi, tittakaggaihipi, kaṭukaggaihipi, madhuraggaihipi, khārikaihipi, akhārikaihipi, lauṇikaihipi, alauṇikaihipi|

‘‘sa  khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau abyattau akusalau sūdau sakassa bhattu nimittaṃ na uggaṇhāti – ‘idaṃ vā mai aj‍ja bhattu sūpaiyyaṃ ruc‍cati, imassa vā abhiharati, imassa vā bahuṃ gaṇhāti, imassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| ambilaggaṃ vā mai aj‍ja bhattu sūpaiyyaṃ ruc‍cati, ambilaggassa vā abhiharati, ambilaggassa vā bahuṃ gaṇhāti, ambilaggassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| tittakaggaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… kaṭukaggaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… madhuraggaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… khārikaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… akhārikaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… lauṇikaṃ  vā mai aj‍ja… alauṇikaṃ vā mai aj‍ja bhattu sūpaiyyaṃ ruc‍cati, alauṇikassa vā abhiharati, alauṇikassa vā bahuṃ gaṇhāti, alauṇikassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsatī’’’ti|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau abyattau akusalau sūdau na caiva lābhī hauti acchādanassa, na lābhī vaitanassa, na lābhī abhihārānaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau, bhikkhavai, bālau abyattau akusalau sūdau sakassa bhattu nimittaṃ na uggaṇhāti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bālau abyattau akusalau bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa kāyai kāyānupassinau viharatau cittaṃ na samādhiyati, upak‍kilaisā na pahīyanti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ na uggaṇhāti| vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati…pai॰… cittai cittānupassī viharati  …pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa dhammaisu dhammānupassinau viharatau cittaṃ na samādhiyati, upak‍kilaisā na pahīyanti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ na uggaṇhāti|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bālau abyattau akusalau bhikkhu na caiva lābhī hauti diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhavihārānaṃ, na lābhī satisampajañ‍ñassa| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau, bhikkhavai, bālau abyattau akusalau bhikkhu sakassa cittassa nimittaṃ na uggaṇhāti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau byattau kusalau sūdau rājānaṃ  vā rājamahāmattaṃ vā nānac‍cayaihi sūpaihi pac‍cupaṭṭhitau assa – ambilaggaihipi, tittakaggaihipi, kaṭukaggaihipi, madhuraggaihipi, khārikaihipi, akhārikaihipi, lauṇikaihipi, alauṇikaihipi|
