‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau byattau kusalau sūdau sakassa bhattu nimittaṃ uggaṇhāti – ‘idaṃ vā mai aj‍ja bhattu sūpaiyyaṃ ruc‍cati, imassa vā abhiharati, imassa vā bahuṃ gaṇhāti, imassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| ambilaggaṃ vā mai aj‍ja bhattu sūpaiyyaṃ ruc‍cati, ambilaggassa vā abhiharati, ambilaggassa vā bahuṃ gaṇhāti, ambilaggassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| tittakaggaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… kaṭukaggaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… madhuraggaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… khārikaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… akhārikaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… lauṇikaṃ vā mai aj‍ja… alauṇikaṃ vā mai aj‍ja bhattu sūpaiyyaṃ ruc‍cati, alauṇikassa vā abhiharati, alauṇikassa vā bahuṃ gaṇhāti, alauṇikassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsatī’’’ti|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau byattau kusalau sūdau lābhī caiva hauti acchādanassa, lābhī vaitanassa, lābhī abhihārānaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau byattau kusalau sūdau sakassa bhattu nimittaṃ uggaṇhāti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau paṇḍitau byattau kusalau bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa kāyai kāyānupassinau viharatau  cittaṃ samādhiyati, upak‍kilaisā pahīyanti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ uggaṇhāti| vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati…pai॰… cittai cittānupassī viharati…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati  ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa dhammaisu dhammānupassinau viharatau cittaṃ samādhiyati, upak‍kilaisā pahīyanti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ uggaṇhāti|

‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau byattau kusalau bhikkhu lābhī caiva hauti diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhavihārānaṃ, lābhī hauti satisampajañ‍ñassa| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau byattau kusalau bhikkhu sakassa cittassa nimittaṃ uggaṇhātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. gilānasuttaṃ

375. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati vaiḷuvagāmakai bailuvagāmakai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, samantā vaisāliyā yathāmittaṃ yathāsandiṭṭhaṃ yathāsambhattaṃ vassaṃ upaitha| idhaivāhaṃ vaiḷuvagāmakai vassaṃ upagacchāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti  khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissutvā samantā vaisāliyā yathāmittaṃ yathāsandiṭṭhaṃ yathāsambhattaṃ vassaṃ upagacchuṃ| bhagavā pana tatthaiva vaiḷuvagāmakai vassaṃ upagacchi upagañchi (sī॰ pī॰)|

atha khau bhagavatau vassūpagatassa kharau ābādhau uppaj‍ji, bāḷhā vaidanā vattanti māraṇantikā| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā satau sampajānau adhivāsaisi avihañ‍ñamānau| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘na khau  mai taṃ patirūpaṃ, yauhaṃ anāmantaitvā upaṭṭhākai anapalaukaitvā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ parinibbāyaiyyaṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ ābādhaṃ vīriyaina paṭipaṇāmaitvā jīvitasaṅkhāraṃ adhiṭṭhāya  viharaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā taṃ ābādhaṃ vīriyaina paṭipaṇāmaitvā jīvitasaṅkhāraṃ adhiṭṭhāya vihāsi| (atha khau bhagavatau sau ābādhau paṭippassambhi) ( ) dī॰ ni॰ 2.164 dissati|

atha khau bhagavā gilānā vuṭṭhitau gilānavuṭṭhitau (saddanīti) aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā vihārā nikkhamitvā vihārapacchāyāyaṃ vihārapacchāchāyāyaṃ (bahūsu) pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘diṭṭhau mai, bhantai, bhagavatau phāsu; diṭṭhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau khamanīyaṃ; diṭṭhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau yāpanīyaṃ| api ca mai, bhantai, madhurakajātau viya kāyau, disāpi mai na pakkhāyanti, dhammāpi maṃ nappaṭibhanti bhagavatau gailañ‍ñaina| api ca mai, bhantai, ahausi kācidaiva assāsamattā – ‘na tāva bhagavā parinibbāyissati, na yāva bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ ārabbha kiñ‍cidaiva udāharatī’’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ pana dāni, ānanda, bhikkhusaṅghau mayi pac‍cāsīsati pac‍cāsiṃsati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)? daisitau, ānanda, mayā dhammau anantaraṃ abāhiraṃ karitvā| natthānanda, tathāgatassa dhammaisu ācariyamuṭṭhi| yassa nūna, ānanda, aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmī’ti vā, ‘mamuddaisikau bhikkhusaṅghau’ti vā, sau nūna, ānanda, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ ārabbha kiñ‍cidaiva udāharaiyya| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmī’ti vā, ‘mamuddaisikau bhikkhusaṅghau’ti vā| sa kiṃ sau nūna (sī॰ pī॰), ānanda, tathāgatau bhikkhusaṅghaṃ ārabbha kiñ‍cidaiva udāharissati! aitarahi khau panāhaṃ, ānanda, jiṇṇau vuddhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| āsītikau mai vayau vattati| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, jaj‍jarasakaṭaṃ jarasakaṭaṃ (sabbattha) vaiḷamissakaina vaigamissakaina (sī॰), vaiḷumissakaina (syā॰ kaṃ॰), vaidhamissakaina (pī॰ ka॰), vaikhamissakaina (ka॰) yāpaiti; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, vaidhamissakaina mañ‍ñai tathāgatassa kāyau yāpaiti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, ānanda, samayai tathāgatau sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā aikac‍cānaṃ vaidanānaṃ niraudhā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, phāsutarau phāsutaraṃ (sabbattha), ānanda, tasmiṃ samayai tathāgatassa kāyau hauti tathāgatassa hauti (bahūsu)| tasmātihānanda, attadīpā viharatha attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau, dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau? idhānanda, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau  satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau, dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau| yai hi kaici, ānanda, aitarahi vā mamac‍cayai vā attadīpā viharissanti attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā; tamataggai maitai, ānanda, bhikkhū bhavissanti yai kaici sikkhākāmā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. bhikkhunupassayasuttaṃ

376. atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhunupassayau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhuniyau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tā bhikkhuniyau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘idha, bhantai ānanda, sambahulā bhikkhuniyau catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittā supaṭṭhitacittā (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) viharantiyau uḷāraṃ pubbaināparaṃ visaisaṃ sañ‍jānantī’’ti sampajānantīti (ka॰)| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, aivamaitaṃ, bhaginiyau! yau hi kauci, bhaginiyau, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau viharati, tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘uḷāraṃ pubbaināparaṃ visaisaṃ sañ‍jānissatī’’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau tā bhikkhuniyau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā ānandau sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhunupassayau  tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdiṃ| atha khau, bhantai, sambahulā bhikkhuniyau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nā khau, bhantai, tā bhikkhuniyau maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘idha, bhantai ānanda, sambahulā bhikkhuniyau catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittā viharantiyau uḷāraṃ pubbaināparaṃ visaisaṃ sañ‍jānantī’ti| aivaṃ vuttāhaṃ, bhantai, tā bhikkhuniyau aitadavaucaṃ – ‘aivamaitaṃ, bhaginiyau, aivamaitaṃ, bhaginiyau! yau hi kauci, bhaginiyau, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau viharati, tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – uḷāraṃ pubbaināparaṃ visaisaṃ sañ‍jānissatī’’’ti|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ, ānanda, aivamaitaṃ, ānanda! yau hi kauci, ānanda, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau viharati, tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘uḷāraṃ pubbaināparaṃ visaisaṃ sañ‍jānissati’’’ sañ‍jānissatīti (bahūsu)|

‘‘katamaisu catūsu? idhānanda, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa  kāyai kāyānupassinau viharatau kāyārammaṇau vā uppaj‍jati kāyasmiṃ pariḷāhau, caitasau vā līnattaṃ, bahiddhā vā cittaṃ vikkhipati| tainānanda tainahānanda (sī॰), bhikkhunā kismiñ‍cidaiva pasādanīyai nimittai cittaṃ paṇidahitabbaṃ| tassa kismiñ‍cidaiva pasādanīyai nimittai cittaṃ paṇidahatau  pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidayati vaidiyati (sī॰)| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yassa khvāhaṃ atthāya cittaṃ paṇidahiṃ, sau mai atthau  abhinipphan‍nau| handa, dāni paṭisaṃharāmī’ti| sau paṭisaṃharati caiva na ca vitak‍kaiti na ca vicāraiti| ‘avitak‍kaumhi avicārau, ajjhattaṃ satimā sukhamasmī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, bhikkhu vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa dhammaisu dhammānupassinau viharatau dhammārammaṇau vā uppaj‍jati kāyasmiṃ pariḷāhau, caitasau vā līnattaṃ, bahiddhā vā cittaṃ vikkhipati| tainānanda, bhikkhunā kismiñ‍cidaiva pasādanīyai nimittai cittaṃ paṇidahitabbaṃ| tassa kismiñ‍cidaiva pasādanīyai nimittai cittaṃ paṇidahatau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidayati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yassa khvāhaṃ atthāya cittaṃ paṇidahiṃ, sau mai atthau abhinipphan‍nau| handa, dāni paṭisaṃharāmī’ti| sau paṭisaṃharati caiva na ca vitak‍kaiti na ca vicāraiti| ‘avitak‍kaumhi avicārau, ajjhattaṃ satimā sukhamasmī’ti pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, paṇidhāya bhāvanā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, appaṇidhāya bhāvanā hauti? bahiddhā, ānanda, bhikkhu cittaṃ appaṇidhāya ‘appaṇihitaṃ mai bahiddhā citta’nti pajānāti| atha pacchāpurai ‘asaṃkhittaṃ vimuttaṃ appaṇihita’nti pajānāti| atha ca pana ‘kāyai kāyānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā sukhamasmī’ti pajānāti| bahiddhā, ānanda, bhikkhu cittaṃ appaṇidhāya ‘appaṇihitaṃ mai bahiddhā citta’nti pajānāti| atha pacchāpurai ‘asaṃkhittaṃ vimuttaṃ appaṇihita’nti pajānāti| atha ca pana ‘vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā sukhamasmī’ti pajānāti| bahiddhā, ānanda, bhikkhu cittaṃ appaṇidhāya ‘appaṇihitaṃ mai bahiddhā citta’nti pajānāti| atha pacchāpurai ‘asaṃkhittaṃ vimuttaṃ appaṇihita’nti pajānāti| atha ca pana ‘cittai cittānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā sukhamasmī’ti pajānāti| bahiddhā, ānanda, bhikkhu cittaṃ appaṇidhāya ‘appaṇihitaṃ mai bahiddhā citta’nti pajānāti| atha pacchāpurai ‘asaṃkhittaṃ vimuttaṃ appaṇihita’nti pajānāti| atha ca pana ‘dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā sukhamasmī’ti pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, appaṇidhāya bhāvanā hauti|

‘‘iti  khau, ānanda, daisitā mayā paṇidhāya bhāvanā, daisitā appaṇidhāya bhāvanā| yaṃ, ānanda, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā  anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, ānanda, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni! jhāyathānanda, mā pamādattha; mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha! ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti| dasamaṃ|

ambapālivaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

ambapāli  satau bhikkhu, sālā kusalarāsi ca|

sakuṇagdhi mak‍kaṭau sūdau, gilānau bhikkhunupassayauti||

2. nālandavaggau

1. mahāpurisasuttaṃ

377. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘mahāpurisau, mahāpurisau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, mahāpurisau hautī’’ti? ‘‘vimuttacittattā khvāhaṃ, sāriputta, ‘mahāpurisau’ti vadāmi| avimuttacittattā ‘nau mahāpurisau’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, sāriputta, vimuttacittau hauti? idha, sāriputta, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa kāyai kāyānupassinau viharatau cittaṃ viraj‍jati, vimuc‍cati anupādāya āsavaihi| vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰…  dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa dhammaisu dhammānupassinau viharatau cittaṃ viraj‍jati, vimuc‍cati anupādāya āsavaihi| aivaṃ khau, sāriputta, vimuttacittau hauti| vimuttacittattā khvāhaṃ, sāriputta, ‘mahāpurisau’ti vadāmi| avimuttacittattā ‘nau mahāpurisau’ti vadāmī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. nālandasuttaṃ

378. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā nālandāyaṃ viharati pāvārikambavanai| atha  khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivaṃpasan‍nau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati! na cāhu, na  ca bhavissati, na caitarahi vij‍jati añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarau, yadidaṃ – sambaudhiya’’nti| ‘‘uḷārā khau tyāyaṃ, sāriputta, āsabhī vācā bhāsitā, aikaṃsau gahitau, sīhanādau naditau – ‘aivaṃpasan‍nau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati! na cāhu, na ca bhavissati na caitarahi vij‍jati añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarau, yadidaṃ – sambaudhiya’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ nu tai, sāriputta, yai tai ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, sabbai tai bhagavantau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditā – ‘aivaṃsīlā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃdhammā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃpañ‍ñā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃvihārinau tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau ahaisuṃ’ iti vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’!

‘‘kiṃ pana tai, sāriputta, yai tai bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, sabbai tai bhagavantau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditā – ‘aivaṃsīlā tai bhagavantau bhavissanti’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃdhammā tai bhagavantau bhavissanti’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃpañ‍ñā tai bhagavantau bhavissanti’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃvihārinau tai bhagavantau bhavissanti’ iti  vā, ‘aivaṃvimuttā tai bhagavantau bhavissanti’ iti vā’’ti? ‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘kiṃ  pana tyāhaṃ kiṃ pana tai (sī॰), sāriputta, aitarahi, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau – ‘aivaṃsīlau bhagavā’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃdhammau bhagavā’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃpañ‍ñau bhagavā’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃvihārī bhagavā’ iti vā, ‘aivaṃvimuttau bhagavā’ iti vā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aittha ca tai, sāriputta, atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naisu arahantaisu sammāsambuddhaisu caitaupariyañāṇaṃ caitaupariyāyañāṇaṃ (bahūsu) natthi| atha kiñ‍carahi tyāyaṃ, sāriputta, uḷārā āsabhī vācā bhāsitā, aikaṃsau gahitau, sīhanādau naditau – ‘aivaṃpasan‍nau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati! na cāhu, na ca bhavissati, na caitarahi vij‍jati añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā’ bhiyyaubhiñ‍ñatarau, yadidaṃ – sambaudhiya’’nti?

‘‘na  khau mai na khau mai taṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), bhantai, atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naisu arahantaisu sammāsambuddhaisu caitaupariyañāṇaṃ atthi, api ca mai dhammanvayau viditau| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ daḷhuddhāpaṃ daḷhuddāpaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), daḷhaddhāpaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) daḷhapākāratauraṇaṃ aikadvāraṃ| tatrassa dauvārikau paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī añ‍ñātānaṃ nivāraitā ñātānaṃ pavaisaitā| sau tassa nagarassa samantā anupariyāyapathaṃ anuk‍kamamānau na passaiyya pākārasandhiṃ  vā pākāravivaraṃ vā, antamasau biḷāranikkhamanamattampi| tassa aivamassa – ‘yai khau kaici auḷārikā pāṇā imaṃ nagaraṃ pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā, sabbai tai imināva dvāraina pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā’ti| aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai, dhammanvayau viditau – ‘yaipi tai, bhantai, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, sabbai tai bhagavantau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya, caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittā, satta baujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaitvā, anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhiṃsu| yaipi tai, bhantai, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, sabbai tai bhagavantau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya, caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittā, satta baujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ  bhāvaitvā, anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhissanti| bhagavāpi, bhantai, aitarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya, caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau, satta baujjhaṅgai yathābhūtaṃ bhāvaitvā, anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’’’ti|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, sāriputta! tasmātiha tvaṃ, sāriputta, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ abhikkhaṇaṃ bhāsaiyyāsi bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ| yaisampi hi, sāriputta, maughapurisānaṃ bhavissati tathāgatai kaṅkhā  vā vimati vā, taisampimaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ sutvā yā tathāgatai kaṅkhā vā vimati vā sā pahīyissatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. cundasuttaṃ

379. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sāriputtau magadhaisu  viharati nālakagāmakai ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| cundau ca samaṇuddaisau āyasmatau sāriputtassa upaṭṭhākau hauti|

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau tainaiva ābādhaina parinibbāyi| atha khau cundau samaṇuddaisau āyasmatau sāriputtassa pattacīvaramādāya yaina sāvatthi jaitavanaṃ anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau cundau samaṇuddaisau  āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, sāriputtau parinibbutau| idamassa pattacīvara’’nti|

‘‘atthi khau idaṃ, āvusau cunda, kathāpābhataṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| āyāmāvusau cunda, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau cundau samaṇuddaisau āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausi|

atha khau āyasmā ca ānandau cundau ca samaṇuddaisau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, cundau samaṇuddaisau aivamāha – ‘āyasmā, bhantai, sāriputtau parinibbutau; idamassa pattacīvara’nti| api ca mai, bhantai, madhurakajātau viya kāyau, disāpi mai na pakkhāyanti, dhammāpi maṃ nappaṭibhanti ‘āyasmā sāriputtau parinibbutau’ti sutvā’’|

‘‘kiṃ  nu khau tai, ānanda, sāriputtau sīlakkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbutau, samādhikkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbutau, pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbutau, vimuttikkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbutau, vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbutau’’ti? ‘‘na ca khau mai, bhantai, āyasmā sāriputtau sīlakkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbutau, samādhikkhandhaṃ vā…pai॰… pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ vā… vimuttikkhandhaṃ vā… vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaṃ vā ādāya parinibbutau| api ca mai, bhantai, āyasmā sāriputtau auvādakau ahausi autiṇṇau viñ‍ñāpakau sandassakau samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau, akilāsu dhammadaisanāya, anuggāhakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| taṃ mayaṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa dhammaujaṃ dhammabhaugaṃ dhammānuggahaṃ anussarāmā’’ti|

‘‘nanu  taṃ, ānanda, mayā paṭikac‍caiva paṭigac‍caiva (sī॰ pī॰) akkhātaṃ – ‘sabbaihi piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau añ‍ñathābhāvau| taṃ kutaittha, ānanda, labbhā! yaṃ taṃ jātaṃ bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ palaukadhammaṃ, taṃ vata mā paluj‍jīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau yau  mahantatarau khandhau sau paluj‍jaiyya; aivamaiva khau ānanda, mahatau bhikkhusaṅghassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau sāriputtau parinibbutau| taṃ kutaittha, ānanda, labbhā! yaṃ taṃ jātaṃ bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ palaukadhammaṃ, taṃ vata mā paluj‍jī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| tasmātihānanda, attadīpā viharatha attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā|

‘‘kathañ‍cānanda, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau, dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau? idhānanda, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau, dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau| yai hi kaici, ānanda, aitarahi vā mamac‍cayai vā attadīpā viharissanti attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā; tamataggai maitai, ānanda, bhikkhū bhavissanti yai kaici sikkhākāmā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. uk‍kacailasuttaṃ

380. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaj‍jīsu viharati uk‍kacailāyaṃ gaṅgāya nadiyā tīrai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aciraparinibbutaisu sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau ajjhaukāsai  nisin‍nau hauti|

atha khau bhagavā tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘api  myāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā suñ‍ñā viya khāyati parinibbutaisu sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu| asuñ‍ñā mai, bhikkhavai, parisā hauti, anapaikkhā tassaṃ disāyaṃ hauti, yassaṃ disāyaṃ sāriputtamauggal‍lānā viharanti| yai hi tai, bhikkhavai, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, taisampi bhagavantānaṃ aitapparamaṃyaiva sāvakayugaṃ aitaparamaṃyaiva (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ahausi – saiyyathāpi mayhaṃ sāriputtamauggal‍lānā| yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, taisampi bhagavantānaṃ aitapparamaṃyaiva sāvakayugaṃ bhavissati – saiyyathāpi mayhaṃ sāriputtamauggal‍lānā| acchariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāvakānaṃ! abbhutaṃ, bhikkhavai, sāvakānaṃ! satthu ca nāma sāsanakarā bhavissanti auvādappaṭikarā, catun‍nañ‍ca parisānaṃ piyā bhavissanti manāpā garubhāvanīyā ca! acchariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa, abbhutaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa! aivarūpaipi nāma sāvakayugai parinibbutai natthi tathāgatassa saukau vā paridaivau vā! taṃ kutaittha, bhikkhavai, labbhā! yaṃ taṃ jātaṃ bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ palaukadhammaṃ, taṃ vata mā paluj‍jīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau yai mahantatarā khandhā tai paluj‍jaiyyuṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, mahatau  bhikkhusaṅghassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau sāriputtamauggal‍lānā parinibbutā| taṃ kutaittha, bhikkhavai, labbhā! yaṃ taṃ jātaṃ bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ palaukadhammaṃ, taṃ vata mā paluj‍jīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, attadīpā viharatha attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau, dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī  viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attadīpau viharati attasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau, dhammadīpau dhammasaraṇau anañ‍ñasaraṇau| yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi vā mamac‍cayai vā attadīpā viharissanti attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā; tamataggai maitai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhavissanti yai kaici sikkhākāmā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. bāhiyasuttaṃ

381. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā bāhiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā bāhiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ  sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, bāhiya, ādimaiva visaudhaihi kusalaisu dhammaisu| kau cādi kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ? sīlañ‍ca suvisuddhaṃ, diṭṭhi ca ujukā| yatau ca khau tai, bāhiya, sīlañ‍ca suvisuddhaṃ bhavissati, diṭṭhi ca ujukā, tatau tvaṃ, bāhiya, sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaiyyāsi’’|

‘‘katamai cattārau? idha, tvaṃ, bāhiya, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| yatau khau tvaṃ, bāhiya, sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānai aivaṃ bhāvaissasi, tatau tuyhaṃ, bāhiya, yā ratti vā divasau vā āgamissati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihānī’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā bāhiyau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā bāhiyau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva  agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā bāhiyau arahataṃ ahausīti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. uttiyasuttaṃ

382. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā uttiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā uttiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau  dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, uttiya, ādimaiva visaudhaihi kusalaisu dhammaisu| kau cādi kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ? sīlañ‍ca suvisuddhaṃ, diṭṭhi ca ujukā| yatau ca khau tai, uttiya, sīlañ‍ca suvisuddhaṃ bhavissati, diṭṭhi ca ujukā, tatau tvaṃ, uttiya, sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaiyyāsi’’|

‘‘katamai cattārau? idha tvaṃ, uttiya, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| yatau khau tvaṃ, uttiya, sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānai aivaṃ bhāvaissasi, tatau tvaṃ, uttiya, gamissasi mac‍cudhaiyyassa pāra’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā uttiyau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā  uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā uttiyau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā uttiyau arahataṃ ahausīti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ariyasuttaṃ

383. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ariyā niyyānikā niyyanti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ariyā niyyānikā niyyanti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. brahmasuttaṃ

384. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai ajapālanigraudhai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘aikāyanau ayaṃ maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ  atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’|

‘‘katamai cattārau? kāyai vā bhikkhu kāyānupassī viharaiyya ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vā bhikkhu…pai॰… cittai vā bhikkhu…pai॰… dhammaisu vā bhikkhu dhammānupassī viharaiyya ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aikāyanau ayaṃ maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’ti|

atha khau brahmā sahampati bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā  purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – brahmalaukai antarahitau bhagavatau puratau pāturahausi| atha khau brahmā sahampati aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ, sugata! aikāyanau ayaṃ, bhantai, maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’|

‘‘katamai cattārau? kāyai vā, bhantai, bhikkhu kāyānupassī viharaiyya ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vā, bhantai, bhikkhu…pai॰… cittai vā, bhantai, bhikkhu…pai॰… dhammaisu vā, bhantai, bhikkhu dhammānupassī viharaiyya ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aikāyanau ayaṃ, bhantai, maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya  ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’ti|

idamavauca brahmā sahampati| idaṃ vatvā athāparaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘aikāyanaṃ jātikhayantadassī, maggaṃ pajānāti hitānukampī|

aitaina maggaina tariṃsu pubbai, tarissanti yai ca taranti augha’’nti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. saidakasuttaṃ

385. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sumbhaisu viharati saidakaṃ nāma sumbhānaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, caṇḍālavaṃsikau caṇḍālavaṃsaṃ ussāpaitvā maidakathālikaṃ antaivāsiṃ āmantaisi – ‘aihi tvaṃ, samma maidakathālikai, caṇḍālavaṃsaṃ abhiruhitvā mama uparikhandhai tiṭṭhāhī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, ācariyā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, maidakathālikā antaivāsī caṇḍālavaṃsikassa paṭissutvā caṇḍālavaṃsaṃ abhiruhitvā ācariyassa uparikhandhai aṭṭhāsi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, caṇḍālavaṃsikau maidakathālikaṃ antaivāsiṃ aitadavauca – ‘tvaṃ, samma  maidakathālikai, mamaṃ rakkha, ahaṃ taṃ rakkhissāmi| aivaṃ mayaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ  guttā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ rakkhitā  sippāni caiva dassaissāma, lābhañ‍ca lābhai ca (sī॰) lacchāma, sautthinā ca caṇḍālavaṃsā aurauhissāmā’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, maidakathālikā antaivāsī caṇḍālavaṃsikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘na khau panaitaṃ, ācariya, aivaṃ bhavissati| tvaṃ, ācariya, attānaṃ rakkha, ahaṃ attānaṃ rakkhissāmi| aivaṃ mayaṃ attaguttā attarakkhitā sippāni caiva dassaissāma, lābhañ‍ca lacchāma, sautthinā ca caṇḍālavaṃsā aurauhissāmā’’’ti| ‘‘sau tattha ñāyau’’ti bhagavā aitadavauca, ‘‘yathā maidakathālikā antaivāsī ācariyaṃ avauca| attānaṃ, bhikkhavai, rakkhissāmīti satipaṭṭhānaṃ saivitabbaṃ; paraṃ rakkhissāmīti satipaṭṭhānaṃ saivitabbaṃ| attānaṃ, bhikkhavai, rakkhantau paraṃ rakkhati, paraṃ rakkhantau attānaṃ rakkhati’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, attānaṃ rakkhantau paraṃ rakkhati? āsaivanāya, bhāvanāya, bahulīkammaina – aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, attānaṃ rakkhantau paraṃ rakkhati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paraṃ rakkhantau attānaṃ rakkhati? khantiyā, avihiṃsāya, maittacittatāya, anudayatāya – aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, paraṃ rakkhantau attānaṃ rakkhati| attānaṃ, bhikkhavai, rakkhissāmīti satipaṭṭhānaṃ saivitabbaṃ; paraṃ rakkhissāmīti satipaṭṭhānaṃ saivitabbaṃ| attānaṃ, bhikkhavai, rakkhantau paraṃ rakkhati, paraṃ rakkhantau attānaṃ rakkhatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. janapadakalyāṇīsuttaṃ

386. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sumbhaisu viharati saidakaṃ nāma sumbhānaṃ nigamau| tatra  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ‘janapadakalyāṇī, janapadakalyāṇī’ti khau, bhikkhavai, mahājanakāyau san‍nipataiyya| ‘sā khau panassa janapadakalyāṇī paramapāsāvinī nac‍cai, paramapāsāvinī gītai| janapadakalyāṇī nac‍cati gāyatī’ti khau, bhikkhavai, bhiyyausaumattāya mahājanakāyau  san‍nipataiyya| atha purisau āgacchaiyya jīvitukāmau amaritukāmau sukhakāmau dukkhappaṭikūlau| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ayaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, samatittikau tailapattau antaraina ca mahāsamaj‍jaṃ antaraina ca janapadakalyāṇiyā pariharitabbau| purisau ca tai ukkhittāsikau piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhissati| yatthaiva naṃ thaukampi chaḍḍaissati tatthaiva tai sirau pātaissatī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau amuṃ tailapattaṃ amanasikaritvā bahiddhā pamādaṃ āharaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘upamā khau myāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, katā atthassa viñ‍ñāpanāya| ayaṃ caivaittha atthau – samatittikau tailapattauti khau, bhikkhavai, kāyagatāya aitaṃ satiyā adhivacanaṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kāyagatā sati nau bhāvitā  bhavissati bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā  anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā’ti| aivañhi khau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dasamaṃ|

nālandavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

mahāpurisau  nālandaṃ, cundau cailañ‍ca bāhiyau|

uttiyau ariyau brahmā, saidakaṃ janapadaina cāti||

3. sīlaṭṭhitivaggau

1. sīlasuttaṃ

387. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca ānandau āyasmā ca bhaddau pāṭaliputtai viharanti kuk‍kuṭārāmai| atha khau āyasmā bhaddau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā bhaddau  āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yānimāni, āvusau ānanda, kusalāni sīlāni vuttāni bhagavatā, imāni kusalāni sīlāni kimatthiyāni vuttāni bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘sādhu sādhu, āvusau bhadda! bhaddakau khau tai, āvusau bhadda, ummaṅgau ummaggau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhaddakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ, kalyāṇī paripucchā| aivañhi tvaṃ, āvusau bhadda, pucchasi – ‘yānimāni āvusau ānanda, kusalāni sīlāni vuttāni bhagavatā, imāni kusalāni sīlāni kimatthiyāni vuttāni bhagavatā’’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘yānimāni, āvusau bhadda, kusalāni sīlāni vuttāni bhagavatā, imāni kusalāni sīlāni yāvadaiva catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanāya vuttāni bhagavatā’’|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰…  cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| yānimāni, āvusau bhadda, kusalāni sīlāni vuttāni bhagavatā, imāni kusalāni sīlāni yāvadaiva imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanāya vuttāni bhagavatā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ciraṭṭhitisuttaṃ

388. taṃyaiva nidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā bhaddau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau ānanda, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hauti? kau panāvusau ānanda, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti?

‘‘sādhu sādhu, āvusau bhadda! bhaddakau khau tai, āvusau bhadda, ummaṅgau, bhaddakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ, kalyāṇī paripucchā| aivañhi tvaṃ, āvusau bhadda, pucchasi – ‘kau nu khau, āvusau ānanda, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hauti? kau panāvusau ānanda, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau  hautī’’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘catun‍naṃ khau, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hauti| catun‍nañ‍ca khau, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hauti’’|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hauti| imaisañ‍ca khau, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. parihānasuttaṃ

389. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ca ānandau āyasmā ca bhaddau pāṭaliputtai  viharanti kuk‍kuṭārāmai| atha khau āyasmā bhaddau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā bhaddau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau ānanda, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina saddhammaparihānaṃ hauti? kau nu khau, āvusau ānanda, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina saddhammaaparihānaṃ hautī’’ti?

‘‘sādhu sādhu, āvusau bhadda! bhaddakau khau tai, āvusau bhadda, ummaṅgau, bhaddakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ, kalyāṇī paripucchā| aivañhi tvaṃ, āvusau bhadda, pucchasi – ‘kau nu khau, āvusau ānanda, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina saddhammaparihānaṃ  hauti? kau panāvusau ānanda, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina saddhammaaparihānaṃ hautī’’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘catun‍naṃ khau, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā saddhammaparihānaṃ hauti| catun‍nañ‍ca khau, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā saddhammaaparihānaṃ hauti’’|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā saddhammaparihānaṃ hauti| imaisañ‍ca khau, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā saddhammaaparihānaṃ hautī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. suddhasuttaṃ

390. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu  …pai॰… cittai  …pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. añ‍ñatarabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

391. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau yaina  bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hauti? kau pana, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti?

‘‘catun‍naṃ  khau, brāhmaṇa, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hauti| catun‍nañ‍ca khau, brāhmaṇa, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hauti|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idha, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ abhāvitattā abahulīkatattā tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hauti| imaisañ‍ca khau, brāhmaṇa, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. padaisasuttaṃ

392. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmā ca anuruddhau sākaitai viharanti kaṇḍakīvanai kaṇṭakīvanai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| atha khau āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitā yaināyasmā aniruddhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā anuruddhaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ  sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘saikhau, saikhau’ti saikkhau saikkhauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰), āvusau anuruddha, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, saikhau hautī’’ti? ‘‘catun‍naṃ khau, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ padaisaṃ bhāvitattā saikhau hauti’’|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰…  cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ padaisaṃ bhāvitattā saikhau hautī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. samattasuttaṃ

393. taṃyaiva nidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘asaikhau, asaikhau’ti, āvusau anuruddha, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, asaikhau hautī’’ti? ‘‘catun‍naṃ khau, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ samattaṃ bhāvitattā asaikhau hauti’’|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ samattaṃ bhāvitattā asaikhau hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. laukasuttaṃ

394. taṃyaiva nidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katamaisaṃ, āvusau anuruddha, dhammānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ mahābhiñ‍ñātaṃ (pī॰) pattau’’ti? ‘‘catun‍naṃ, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idhāhaṃ, āvusau, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau| imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā sahassaṃ laukaṃ  abhijānāmī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sirivaḍḍhasuttaṃ

395. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina sirivaḍḍhau sirīvaḍḍhau (ka॰) gahapati ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau sirivaḍḍhau gahapati añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina āyasmatau ānandassa pādai sirasā vanda – ‘sirivaḍḍhau, bhantai, gahapati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau āyasmatau ānandassa pādai sirasā vandatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau yaina sirivaḍḍhassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau purisau sirivaḍḍhassa gahapatissa paṭissutvā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau  sau purisau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sirivaḍḍhau, bhantai, gahapati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau, sau āyasmatau ānandassa pādai sirasā vandati| aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau yaina sirivaḍḍhassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| adhivāsaisi  khau āyasmā ānandau tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya  yaina sirivaḍḍhassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau āyasmā ānandau sirivaḍḍhaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, gahapati, khamanīyaṃ kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti, nau abhik‍kamanti; paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau abhik‍kamau’’ti? ‘‘na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha tai, gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kāyai kāyānupassī viharissāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰…  cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharissāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’nti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabba’’nti|

‘‘yaimai, bhantai, bhagavatā cattārau satipaṭṭhānā daisitā saṃvij‍janti, tai dhammā saṃvij‍jantai ratanadhammā (sī॰) mayi, ahañ‍ca taisu dhammaisu sandissāmi| ahañhi, bhantai, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| yāni cimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni  daisitāni, nāhaṃ, bhantai, taisaṃ kiñ‍ci attani appahīnaṃ samanupassāmī’’ti| ‘‘lābhā tai, gahapati, suladdhaṃ tai, gahapati! anāgāmiphalaṃ tayā, gahapati, byākata’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. mānadin‍nasuttaṃ

396. taṃyaiva  nidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina mānadin‍nau gahapati ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau mānadin‍nau gahapati añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa…pai॰… na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamauti| aivarūpāya cāhaṃ, bhantai, dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau kāyai kāyānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| yāni cimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni daisitāni, nāhaṃ, bhantai, taisaṃ kiñ‍ci attani appahīnaṃ samanupassāmī’’ti| ‘‘lābhā tai, gahapati, suladdhaṃ tai, gahapati! anāgāmiphalaṃ tayā, gahapati, byākata’’nti| dasamaṃ|

sīlaṭṭhitivaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sīlaṃ  ṭhiti parihānaṃ, suddhaṃ brāhmaṇapadaisaṃ|

samattaṃ laukau sirivaḍḍhau, mānadin‍naina tai dasāti||

4. ananussutavaggau

1. ananussutasuttaṃ

397. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘‘ayaṃ kāyai kāyānupassanā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu  dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘sā khau panāyaṃ kāyai kāyānupassanā bhāvaitabbā’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… bhāvitā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi’’|

‘‘‘ayaṃ  vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘sā khau panāyaṃ vaidanāsu vaidanānupassanā bhāvaitabbā’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… bhāvitā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘ayaṃ cittai cittānupassanā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘sā khau panāyaṃ cittai cittānupassanā bhāvaitabbā’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… bhāvitā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘ayaṃ  dhammaisu dhammānupassanā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘sā khau panāyaṃ dhammaisu dhammānupassanā bhāvaitabbā’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… bhāvitā’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. virāgasuttaṃ

398. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. viraddhasuttaṃ

399. ‘‘yaisaṃ  kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā viraddhā, viraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau ariyau aṭṭhaṅkikau maggau (ka॰) imasmiṃ yaiva suttai dissati aṭṭhaṅgikautipadaṃ, na panāñ‍ñattha iddhipāda anuruddhādīsu sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| yaisaṃ  kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā, āraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī|

‘‘katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau  satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā viraddhā, viraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā, āraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bhāvitasuttaṃ

400. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya saṃvattantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. satisuttaṃ

401. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘satau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu viharaiyya sampajānau| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| viditā vitak‍kā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| viditā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajānau hauti| satau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu viharaiyya sampajānau| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. añ‍ñāsuttaṃ

402. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. chandasuttaṃ

403. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa kāyai kāyānupassinau viharatau yau kāyasmiṃ chandau sau pahīyati| chandassa pahānā amataṃ sacchikataṃ hauti|

‘‘vaidanāsu  vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa vaidanāsu vaidanānupassinau viharatau yau vaidanāsu chandau sau pahīyati| chandassa pahānā amataṃ sacchikataṃ hauti|

‘‘cittai  cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa cittai cittānupassinau viharatau yau cittamhi chandau sau pahīyati| chandassa pahānā amataṃ sacchikataṃ hauti|

‘‘dhammaisu  dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa dhammaisu dhammānupassinau viharatau yau dhammaisu chandau sau pahīyati| chandassa pahānā amataṃ sacchikataṃ hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. pariñ‍ñātasuttaṃ

404. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa kāyai kāyānupassinau viharatau kāyau pariñ‍ñātau hauti| kāyassa pariñ‍ñātattā amataṃ sacchikataṃ hauti|

‘‘vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa vaidanāsu vaidanānupassinau viharatau vaidanā pariñ‍ñātā haunti| vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñātattā  amataṃ sacchikataṃ hauti|

‘‘cittai cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa cittai cittānupassinau viharatau cittaṃ pariñ‍ñātaṃ hauti| cittassa pariñ‍ñātattā amataṃ sacchikataṃ hauti|

‘‘dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| tassa dhammaisu dhammānupassinau viharatau dhammā pariñ‍ñātā haunti| dhammānaṃ pariñ‍ñātattā amataṃ sacchikataṃ hautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. bhāvanāsuttaṃ

405. ‘‘catun‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha’’| ‘‘katamā, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī  viharati ātāpī  sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. vibhaṅgasuttaṃ

406. ‘‘satipaṭṭhānañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi satipaṭṭhānabhāvanañ‍ca satipaṭṭhānabhāvanāgāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ| taṃ suṇātha’’| ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati…pai॰… cittai cittānupassī viharati…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī  sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānabhāvanā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samudayadhammānupassī kāyasmiṃ viharati, vayadhammānupassī kāyasmiṃ viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī kāyasmiṃ viharati, ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| samudayadhammānupassī vaidanāsu viharati…pai॰… samudayadhammānupassī cittai viharati… samudayadhammānupassī dhammaisu viharati, vayadhammānupassī dhammaisu viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī dhammaisu viharati, ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānabhāvanā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

ananussutavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

ananussutaṃ  virāgau, viraddhau bhāvanā sati|

añ‍ñā chandaṃ pariñ‍ñāya, bhāvanā vibhaṅgaina cāti||

5. amatavaggau

1. amatasuttaṃ

407. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘catūsu, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittā viharatha| mā vau amataṃ panassa| katamaisu catūsu? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisu, bhikkhavai, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittā viharatha| mā vau amataṃ panassā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. samudayasuttaṃ

408. ‘‘catun‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| kau ca, bhikkhavai, kāyassa samudayau? āhārasamudayā kāyassa samudayau; āhāraniraudhā kāyassa atthaṅgamau| phassasamudayā vaidanānaṃ samudayau; phassaniraudhā vaidanānaṃ atthaṅgamau| nāmarūpasamudayā cittassa samudayau; nāmarūpaniraudhā cittassa atthaṅgamau| manasikārasamudayā dhammānaṃ samudayau; manasikāraniraudhā dhammānaṃ atthaṅgamau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. maggasuttaṃ

409. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, samayaṃ uruvailāyaṃ viharāmi naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai ajapālanigraudhai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘aikāyanau  ayaṃ maggau sattānaṃ  visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’’|

‘‘katamai cattārau? kāyai vā bhikkhu kāyānupassī viharaiyya ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vā bhikkhu vaidanānupassī viharaiyya…pai॰… cittai vā bhikkhu cittānupassī viharaiyya…pai॰… dhammaisu vā bhikkhu dhammānupassī viharaiyya ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aikāyanau ayaṃ maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati mama caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – brahmalaukai antarahitau mama puratau pāturahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaināhaṃ tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘aivamaitaṃ, bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ, sugata! aikāyanau ayaṃ, bhantai, maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ – cattārau  satipaṭṭhānā’’’|

‘‘katamai cattārau? kāyai  vā, bhantai, bhikkhu kāyānupassī viharaiyya ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vā…pai॰… cittai vā  …pai॰… dhammaisu vā, bhantai, bhikkhu dhammānupassī viharaiyya ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aikāyanau ayaṃ, bhantai, maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’ti|

‘‘idamavauca, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati| idaṃ vatvā athāparaṃ aitadavauca –

‘aikāyanaṃ jātikhayantadassī, maggaṃ pajānāti hitānukampī|

aitaina maggaina tariṃsu pubbai, tarissanti yai ca taranti augha’’’nti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. satisuttaṃ

410. ‘‘satau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu viharaiyya| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satau hauti| satau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu viharaiyya| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. kusalarāsisuttaṃ

411. ‘‘‘kusalarāsī’ti, bhikkhavai, vadamānau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| kaivalau hāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kusalarāsi, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā|

‘‘katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… cittānupassī…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī  viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ‘kusalarāsī’ti, bhikkhavai, vadamānau imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānai sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| kaivalau hāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kusalarāsi, yadidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pātimaukkhasaṃvarasuttaṃ

412. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, ādimaiva visaudhaihi kusalaisu dhammaisu| kau cādi kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ? idha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharāhi ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhassu sikkhāpadaisu| yatau khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharissasi ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu  bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhissu sikkhāpadaisu; tatau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaiyyāsi’’|

‘‘katamai cattārau? idha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| yatau khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānai aivaṃ bhāvaissasi, tatau tuyhaṃ, bhikkhu, yā ratti vā divasau vā āgamissati vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihānī’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha  khau sau bhikkhu aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ  pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. duc‍caritasuttaṃ

413. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, ādimaiva visaudhaihi kusalaisu dhammaisu| kau cādi kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ? idha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaissasi| vacīduc‍caritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ  bhāvaissasi| manauduc‍caritaṃ pahāya manausucaritaṃ bhāvaissasi| yatau khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaissasi, vacīduc‍caritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaissasi, manauduc‍caritaṃ pahāya manausucaritaṃ bhāvaissasi, tatau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaiyyāsi’’|

‘‘katamai  cattārau? idha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| yatau khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānai aivaṃ bhāvaissasi, tatau tuyhaṃ, bhikkhu, yā ratti vā divasau vā āgamissati vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihānī’’ti…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| sattamaṃ|

8. mittasuttaṃ

414. ‘‘yai, bhikkhavai, anukampaiyyātha, yai ca khau sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyuṃ mittā vā amac‍cā  vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā, tai vau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanāya samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā|

‘‘katamaisaṃ, catun‍naṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| yai, bhikkhavai, anukampaiyyātha, yai ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyuṃ mittā vā  amac‍cā vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā, tai vau, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanāya samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vaidanāsuttaṃ

415. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vaidanā| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā|

‘‘katamai cattārau? idha bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. āsavasuttaṃ

416. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai āsavā| katamai tayau? kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, avij‍jāsavau  – imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau āsavā| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇan‍naṃ āsavānaṃ pahānāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā|

‘‘katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai  abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇan‍naṃ āsavānaṃ pahānāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

amatavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

amataṃ samudayau maggau, sati kusalarāsi ca|

pātimaukkhaṃ duc‍caritaṃ, mittavaidanā āsavaina cāti||

6. gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1-12. gaṅgānadīādisuttadvādasakaṃ

417-428. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaintau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaintau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu  …pai॰… cittai  …pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaintau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti vitthāraitabbaṃ|

gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

aitai dvai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

7. appamādavaggau

1-10. tathāgatādisuttadasakaṃ

429-438. yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattā apadā vā dvipadā vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vāti vitthāraitabbaṃ|

appamādavaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

tathāgataṃ  padaṃ kūṭaṃ, mūlaṃ sārau ca vassikaṃ|

rājā candimasūriyā, vatthaina dasamaṃ padanti||

8. balakaraṇīyavaggau

1-12. balādisuttadvādasakaṃ

439-450. saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyantīti vitthāraitabbaṃ|

balakaraṇīyavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

balaṃ  bījañ‍ca nāgau ca, rukkhau kumbhaina sūkiyā|

ākāsaina ca dvai maighā, nāvā āgantukā nadīti||

9. aisanāvaggau

1-10. aisanādisuttadasakaṃ

451-460. tissau imā, bhikkhavai, aisanā| katamā tissau? kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanāti vitthāraitabbaṃ|

aisanāvaggau navamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aisanā vidhā āsavau, bhavau ca dukkhatā tissau|

khilaṃ malañ‍ca nīghau ca, vaidanā taṇhā tasināya cāti||

10. aughavaggau

1-10. uddhambhāgiyādisuttadasakaṃ

461-470. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā  – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā|

‘‘katamai  cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai  kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

(yathā maggasaṃyuttaṃ tathā satipaṭṭhānasaṃyuttaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

aughavaggau dasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aughau yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthā anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇā, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyāti||

satipaṭṭhānasaṃyuttaṃ tatiyaṃ|



4. indriyasaṃyuttaṃ

1. suddhikavaggau

1. suddhikasuttaṃ

471. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyānī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭhamasautāpan‍nasuttaṃ

472. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādañ‍ca samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.175 ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyasautāpan‍nasuttaṃ

473. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca  nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau  niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamaarahantasuttaṃ

474. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādañ‍ca samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.175 ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā  anupādāvimuttau hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyaarahantasuttaṃ

475. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

476. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā  brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca panaitai  āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti|

‘‘yai  ca khau kaici yai ca khau tai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.174, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā; tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

477. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā saddhindriyaṃ nappajānanti, saddhindriyasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, saddhindriyaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, saddhindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti; vīriyindriyaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… satindriyaṃ nappajānanti  …pai॰… samādhindriyaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ nappajānanti, pañ‍ñindriyasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, pañ‍ñindriyaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, pañ‍ñindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca panaitai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ  vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā saddhindriyaṃ pajānanti, saddhindriyasamudayaṃ pajānanti, saddhindriyaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, saddhindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti; vīriyindriyaṃ pajānanti, vīriyindriyasamudayaṃ pajānanti, vīriyindriyaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, vīriyindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti; satindriyaṃ pajānanti…pai॰… samādhindriyaṃ pajānanti…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ  pajānanti, pañ‍ñindriyasamudayaṃ pajānanti, pañ‍ñindriyaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, pañ‍ñindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti, tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā, tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. daṭṭhabbasuttaṃ

478. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu – aittha saddhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu sammappadhānaisu – aittha vīriyindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha  ca, bhikkhavai, satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu – aittha satindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu jhānaisu – aittha samādhindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| kattha ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu ariyasac‍caisu – aittha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamavibhaṅgasuttaṃ

479. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saddhindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ  – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau  sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saddhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vīriyindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vīriyindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, satindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau satimā hauti paramaina  satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā  anussaritā – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, satindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, samādhindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vaussaggārammaṇaṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ, labhati cittassa aikaggataṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, samādhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya, sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyānī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyavibhaṅgasuttaṃ

480. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saddhindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saddhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vīriyindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| sau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ  pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ  ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā samāpattiyā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vīriyindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, satindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| sau kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, satindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, samādhindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vaussaggārammaṇaṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ, labhati cittassa aikaggataṃ| sau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti  yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, samādhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya, sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyānī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

suddhikavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

suddhikañ‍caiva  dvai sautā, arahantā aparai duvai|

samaṇabrāhmaṇā daṭṭhabbaṃ, vibhaṅgā aparai duvaiti||

2. mudutaravaggau

1. paṭilābhasuttaṃ

481. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰…| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saddhindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saddhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vīriyindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau sammappadhānai ārabbha vīriyaṃ paṭilabhati – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vīriyindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, satindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānai ārabbha satiṃ paṭilabhati – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, satindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, samādhindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vaussaggārammaṇaṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ, labhati cittassa aikaggataṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, samādhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā  pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya  nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyānī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭhamasaṃkhittasuttaṃ

482. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samattā paripūrattā arahaṃ hauti, tatau mudutaraihi anāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sakadāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sautāpan‍nau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi dhammānusārī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi saddhānusārī hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyasaṃkhittasuttaṃ

483. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samattā paripūrattā arahaṃ hauti, tatau mudutaraihi anāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sakadāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sautāpan‍nau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi dhammānusārī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi saddhānusārī hauti| iti khau, bhikkhavai, indriyavaimattatā phalavaimattatā hauti, phalavaimattatā puggalavaimattatā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. tatiyasaṃkhittasuttaṃ

484. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samattā paripūrattā arahaṃ hauti, tatau mudutaraihi anāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sakadāgāmī  hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sautāpan‍nau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi dhammānusārī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi saddhānusārī hauti| iti khau, bhikkhavai, paripūraṃ paripūrakārī ārādhaiti, padaisaṃ  padaisakārī ārādhaiti| ‘avañjhāni tvaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyānī’ti vadāmī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamavitthārasuttaṃ
