485. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samattā  paripūrattā arahaṃ hauti, tatau mudutaraihi antarāparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī, tatau mudutaraihi sakadāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sautāpan‍nau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi dhammānusārī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi saddhānusārī hautī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyavitthārasuttaṃ

486. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samattā paripūrattā arahaṃ hauti, tatau mudutaraihi antarāparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī, tatau mudutaraihi sakadāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sautāpan‍nau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi dhammānusārī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi saddhānusārī hauti| iti khau, bhikkhavai, indriyavaimattatā phalavaimattatā hauti, phalavaimattatā puggalavaimattatā hautī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatiyavitthārasuttaṃ

487. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ  samattā paripūrattā arahaṃ hauti, tatau mudutaraihi antarāparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī, tatau mudutaraihi sakadāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sautāpan‍nau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi dhammānusārī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi saddhānusārī hauti| iti khau, bhikkhavai, paripūraṃ  paripūrakārī ārādhaiti, padaisaṃ padaisakārī ārādhaiti| ‘avañjhāni tvaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyānī’ti vadāmī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭipan‍nasuttaṃ

488. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samattā paripūrattā arahaṃ hauti, tatau mudutaraihi arahattaphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi anāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya  paṭipan‍nau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sakadāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sautāpan‍nau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| yassa khau, bhikkhavai, imāni pañ‍cindriyāni sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ natthi, tamahaṃ ‘bāhirau puthuj‍janapakkhai ṭhitau’ti vadāmī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sampan‍nasuttaṃ

489. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘indriyasampan‍nau, indriyasampan‍nau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, indriyasampan‍nau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, bhikkhu saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ bhāvaiti upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ, satindriyaṃ bhāvaiti upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ, samādhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaiti upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ| aittāvatā khau, bhikkhu, bhikkhu indriyasampan‍nau hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. āsavakkhayasuttaṃ

490. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā  bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva  dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

mudutaravaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

paṭilābhau tayau saṃkhittā, vitthārā aparai tayau|

paṭipan‍nau ca sampan‍nau paṭipan‍nau cūpasamau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), dasamaṃ āsavakkhayanti||

3. chaḷindriyavaggau

1. punabbhavasuttaṃ

491. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva  tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ abhisambuddhau pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| yatau ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya  ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti caitauvimutti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), ayamantimā jāti, natthidāni punabbhavau’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. jīvitindriyasuttaṃ

492. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni tīṇi? itthindriyaṃ, purisindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi indriyānī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. añ‍ñindriyasuttaṃ

493. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni tīṇi? anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ, añ‍ñindriyaṃ, añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi indriyānī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. aikabījīsuttaṃ

494. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samattā paripūrattā arahaṃ hauti, tatau mudutaraihi antarāparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi  sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī, tatau mudutaraihi sakadāgāmī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi aikabījī aikabīji (ka॰) hauti, tatau mudutaraihi kaulaṃkaulau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi sattakkhattuparamau hauti, tatau mudutaraihi dhammānusārī hauti, tatau mudutaraihi saddhānusārī hautī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. suddhakasuttaṃ

495. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni cha? cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cha indriyānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sautāpan‍nasuttaṃ

496. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni cha? cakkhundriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. arahantasuttaṃ

497. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni cha? cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau’’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. sambuddhasuttaṃ

498. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni cha? cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ| yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassa maṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| yatau ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca  yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti  pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthidāni punabbhavau’’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

499. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni cha? cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca panaitai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti’’| ‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā, tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

500. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā cakkhundriyaṃ nappajānanti, cakkhundriyasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, cakkhundriyaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, cakkhundriyaniraudhagāminiṃ  paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti; sautindriyaṃ…pai॰… ghānindriyaṃ…pai॰… jivhindriyaṃ…pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ nappajānanti, manindriyasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, manindriyaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, manindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti| na mai tai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti|

‘‘yai  ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā cakkhundriyaṃ pajānanti, cakkhundriyasamudayaṃ pajānanti, cakkhundriyaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, cakkhundriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti, sautindriyaṃ…pai॰… ghānindriyaṃ…pai॰… jivhindriyaṃ…pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ pajānanti, manindriyasamudayaṃ pajānanti, manindriyaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, manindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti, tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā, tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

chaḷindriyavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

punabbhavau jīvitañ‍ñāya, aikabījī ca suddhakaṃ|

sautau arahasambuddhau, dvai ca samaṇabrāhmaṇāti||

4. sukhindriyavaggau

1. suddhikasuttaṃ

501. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyānī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sautāpan‍nasuttaṃ

502. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. arahantasuttaṃ

503. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

504. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca panaitai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti|

‘‘yai  ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā, tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

505. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā sukhindriyaṃ nappajānanti, sukhindriyasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, sukhindriyaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, sukhindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti; dukkhindriyaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… saumanassindriyaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… daumanassindriyaṃ nappajānanti  …pai॰… upaikkhindriyaṃ nappajānanti, upaikkhindriyasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, upaikkhindriyaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, upaikkhindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ  paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti; na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca panaitai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā sukhindriyaṃ pajānanti, sukhindriyasamudayaṃ pajānanti, sukhindriyaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, sukhindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti; dukkhindriyaṃ pajānanti…pai॰… saumanassindriyaṃ pajānanti… daumanassindriyaṃ pajānanti… upaikkhindriyaṃ pajānanti, upaikkhindriyasamudayaṃ pajānanti, upaikkhindriyaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, upaikkhindriyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti, tai ca khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā, tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamavibhaṅgasuttaṃ

506. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sukhindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ, kāyikaṃ sātaṃ, kāyasamphassajaṃ sukhaṃ sātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sukhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ, kāyikaṃ asātaṃ, kāyasamphassajaṃ dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saumanassindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ, caitasikaṃ sātaṃ, manausamphassajaṃ sukhaṃ sātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saumanassindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daumanassindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, caitasikaṃ asātaṃ, manausamphassajaṃ dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, daumanassindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, upaikkhindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ vā caitasikaṃ vā naivasātaṃ nāsātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, upaikkhindriyaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyānī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyavibhaṅgasuttaṃ

507. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sukhindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ, kāyikaṃ sātaṃ, kāyasamphassajaṃ sukhaṃ sātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sukhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ, kāyikaṃ asātaṃ, kāyasamphassajaṃ dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saumanassindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ, caitasikaṃ sātaṃ, manausamphassajaṃ sukhaṃ sātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saumanassindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daumanassindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, caitasikaṃ asātaṃ, manausamphassajaṃ dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, daumanassindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, upaikkhindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ vā caitasikaṃ vā naivasātaṃ nāsātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, upaikkhindriyaṃ|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yañ‍ca sukhindriyaṃ yañ‍ca saumanassindriyaṃ, sukhā sā vaidanā daṭṭhabbā| tatra, bhikkhavai, yañ‍ca dukkhindriyaṃ yañ‍ca daumanassindriyaṃ, dukkhā sā vaidanā daṭṭhabbā| tatra, bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ, adukkhamasukhā sā vaidanā daṭṭhabbā| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyānī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. tatiyavibhaṅgasuttaṃ

508. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sukhindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ, kāyikaṃ sātaṃ, kāyasamphassajaṃ sukhaṃ sātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sukhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ, kāyikaṃ asātaṃ, kāyasamphassajaṃ dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saumanassindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ, caitasikaṃ sātaṃ, manausamphassajaṃ sukhaṃ sātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saumanassindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daumanassindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, caitasikaṃ asātaṃ, manausamphassajaṃ dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, daumanassindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, upaikkhindriyaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ vā caitasikaṃ vā naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, upaikkhindriyaṃ|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yañ‍ca sukhindriyaṃ yañ‍ca saumanassindriyaṃ, sukhā sā vaidanā daṭṭhabbā| tatra, bhikkhavai, yañ‍ca dukkhindriyaṃ yañ‍ca daumanassindriyaṃ, dukkhā sā vaidanā daṭṭhabbā| tatra, bhikkhavai, yadidaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ, adukkhamasukhā sā vaidanā daṭṭhabbā| iti khau, bhikkhavai, imāni pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca hutvā  tīṇi haunti, tīṇi hutvā pañ‍ca haunti pariyāyainā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kaṭṭhaupamasuttaṃ

509. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| sukhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhindriyaṃ| sau sukhitauva samānau ‘sukhitausmī’ti pajānāti| tassaiva sukhavaidaniyassa  phassassa niraudhā ‘yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ sukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ sukhindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati, taṃ vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘dukkhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhindriyaṃ| sau dukkhitauva samānau ‘dukkhitausmī’ti pajānāti| tassaiva dukkhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā ‘yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ dukkhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ  paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ dukkhindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati, taṃ vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘saumanassavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyaṃ| sau sumanauva samānau ‘sumanausmī’ti pajānāti| tassaiva saumanassavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā ‘yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ saumanassavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ saumanassindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati, taṃ vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘daumanassavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati daumanassindriyaṃ| sau dummanauva samānau ‘dummanausmī’ti pajānāti| tassaiva daumanassavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā ‘yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ daumanassavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ daumanassindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati, taṃ  vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘upaikkhāvaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyaṃ| sau upaikkhakauva samānau ‘upaikkhakausmī’ti pajānāti| tassaiva upaikkhāvaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā ‘yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ upaikkhāvaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati, taṃ vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dvin‍naṃ kaṭṭhānaṃ saṅghaṭṭanasamaudhānā saṃghaṭṭanāsamaudhānā (pī॰ ka॰), saṃghaṭanasamaudhānā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) usmā jāyati, taijau abhinibbattati; taisaṃyaiva kaṭṭhānaṃ nānābhāvāvinikkhaipā yā  nānābhāvanikkhaipā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) taj‍jā usmā sā nirujjhati sā vūpasammati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, sukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhindriyaṃ| sau sukhitauva samānau ‘sukhitausmī’ti pajānāti| tassaiva sukhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā ‘yaṃ taj‍jaṃ  vaidayitaṃ sukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati, taṃ vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘dukkhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… saumanassavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… daumanassavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… upaikkhāvaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyaṃ| sau upaikkhakauva samānau ‘upaikkhakausmī’ti pajānāti| tassaiva upaikkhāvaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā ‘yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ upaikkhāvaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyaṃ taṃ nirujjhati, taṃ vūpasammatī’ti pajānāti’’| navamaṃ|

10. uppaṭipāṭikasuttaṃ

510. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? dukkhindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, sukhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati dukkhindriyaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ dukkhindriyaṃ, tañ‍ca khau sanimittaṃ sanidānaṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ sappac‍cayaṃ| tañ‍ca animittaṃ anidānaṃ asaṅkhāraṃ appac‍cayaṃ dukkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’| sau dukkhindriyañ‍ca pajānāti, dukkhindriyasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, dukkhindriyaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, yattha cuppan‍naṃ dukkhindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati tañ‍ca pajānāti| kattha cuppan‍naṃ dukkhindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, aittha cuppan‍naṃ dukkhindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu añ‍ñāsi dukkhindriyassa niraudhaṃ, tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati’’’|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati daumanassindriyaṃ| sau  aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ daumanassindriyaṃ, tañ‍ca khau sanimittaṃ sanidānaṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ sappac‍cayaṃ| tañ‍ca animittaṃ anidānaṃ asaṅkhāraṃ appac‍cayaṃ daumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’| sau daumanassindriyañ‍ca pajānāti, daumanassindriyasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, daumanassindriyaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, yattha cuppan‍naṃ  daumanassindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ  nirujjhati tañ‍ca pajānāti| kattha cuppan‍naṃ daumanassindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, aittha cuppan‍naṃ daumanassindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu añ‍ñāsi daumanassindriyassa niraudhaṃ, tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati’’’|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati sukhindriyaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ sukhindriyaṃ, tañ‍ca khau sanimittaṃ sanidānaṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ sappac‍cayaṃ| tañ‍ca animittaṃ anidānaṃ asaṅkhāraṃ appac‍cayaṃ sukhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’| sau sukhindriyañ‍ca pajānāti, sukhindriyasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, sukhindriyaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, yattha cuppan‍naṃ sukhindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati tañ‍ca pajānāti| kattha cuppan‍naṃ sukhindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, aittha  cuppan‍naṃ sukhindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu añ‍ñāsi sukhindriyassa niraudhaṃ, tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati’’’|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ, tañ‍ca khau sanimittaṃ sanidānaṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ sappac‍cayaṃ| tañ‍ca animittaṃ anidānaṃ asaṅkhāraṃ appac‍cayaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’| sau saumanassindriyañ‍ca pajānāti, saumanassindriyasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, saumanassindriyaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, yattha cuppan‍naṃ saumanassindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati tañ‍ca pajānāti| kattha cuppan‍naṃ saumanassindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, aittha cuppan‍naṃ saumanassindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu añ‍ñāsi saumanassindriyassa niraudhaṃ, tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharati’’’|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ, tañ‍ca khau sanimittaṃ sanidānaṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ  sappac‍cayaṃ| tañ‍ca animittaṃ anidānaṃ asaṅkhāraṃ appac‍cayaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’| sau upaikkhindriyañ‍ca pajānāti, upaikkhindriyasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, upaikkhindriyaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, yattha cuppan‍naṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati tañ‍ca pajānāti| kattha cuppan‍naṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, aittha cuppan‍naṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ aparisaisaṃ nirujjhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu añ‍ñāsi  upaikkhindriyassa niraudhaṃ, tadatthāya cittaṃ upasaṃharatī’’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sukhindriyavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

suddhikañ‍ca sautau arahā, duvai samaṇabrāhmaṇā|

vibhaṅgaina tayau vuttā, kaṭṭhau uppaṭipāṭikanti||

5. jarāvaggau

1. jarādhammasuttaṃ

511. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau pacchātapai nisin‍nau hauti piṭṭhiṃ autāpayamānau|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā bhagavatau gattāni pāṇinā anaumaj‍jantau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! na caivaṃ dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau tāva parisuddhau chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau, sithilāni ca gattāni sabbāni valiyajātāni, puratau pabbhārau  ca kāyau, dissati ca indriyānaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ – cakkhundriyassa sautindriyassa ghānindriyassa jivhindriyassa kāyindriyassā’’ti|

‘‘aivañhaitaṃ, ānanda, hauti – jarādhammau yaubbañ‍ñai, byādhidhammau āraugyai, maraṇadhammau jīvitai| na caiva tāva parisuddhau hauti chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau, sithilāni ca haunti gattāni sabbāni valiyajātāni, puratau pabbhārau ca kāyau, dissati ca indriyānaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ – cakkhundriyassa sautindriyassa ghānindriyassa jivhindriyassa kāyindriyassā’’ti|

‘‘idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvā ca sugatau  athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘dhī taṃ jammi jarai atthu, dubbaṇṇakaraṇī jarai|

tāva manauramaṃ bimbaṃ, jarāya abhimadditaṃ||

‘‘yaupi vassasataṃ jīvai, saupi mac‍cuparāyaṇau sabbai mac‍cuparāyanā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)|

na kiñ‍ci parivaj‍jaiti, sabbamaivābhimaddatī’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. uṇṇābhabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

512. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau uṇṇābhau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uṇṇābhau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhau gautama, indriyāni nānāvisayāni nānāgaucarāni, na añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhaunti| katamāni pañ‍ca? cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ| imaisaṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nānāvisayānaṃ  nānāgaucarānaṃ na añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhauntānaṃ kiṃ paṭisaraṇaṃ, kau ca naisaṃ gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhautī’’ti?

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, brāhmaṇa, indriyāni nānāvisayāni nānāgaucarāni na añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhaunti| katamāni pañ‍ca? cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nānāvisayānaṃ nānāgaucarānaṃ  na añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhauntānaṃ manau paṭisaraṇaṃ, manauva naisaṃ gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhautī’’ti|

‘‘manassa pana, bhau gautama, kiṃ paṭisaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘manassa khau, brāhmaṇa, sati paṭisaraṇa’’nti| ‘‘satiyā pana, bhau gautama, kiṃ paṭisaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘satiyā khau, brāhmaṇa, vimutti paṭisaraṇa’’nti| ‘‘vimuttiyā pana, bhau gautama, kiṃ paṭisaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘vimuttiyā khau, brāhmaṇa, nibbānaṃ paṭisaraṇa’’nti| ‘‘nibbānassa pana, bhau gautama, kiṃ paṭisaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘ac‍cayāsi ac‍casarā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), ajjhaparaṃ (pī॰ ka॰), brāhmaṇa, pañhaṃ, nāsakkhi pañhassa pariyantaṃ gahaituṃ| nibbānaugadhañhi, brāhmaṇa, brahmacariyaṃ vussati nibbānaparāyaṇaṃ nibbānapariyausāna’’nti|

atha khau uṇṇābhau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantai uṇṇābhai brāhmaṇai bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgārai vā kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ vā rasmiyau (syā॰ ka॰) pācīnavātapānā sūriyai uggacchantai vātapānaina rasmi kūṭāgāraṃ vā kūṭāgārasālaṃ vā uttarāya (ka॰ sī॰) pavisitvā k‍vāssa kāya (syā॰ ka॰) patiṭṭhitā’’ti? ‘‘pacchimāyaṃ, bhantai, bhittiya’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, uṇṇābhassa brāhmaṇassa tathāgatai saddhā  niviṭṭhā mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā daḷhā asaṃhāriyā samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ| imamhi cai, bhikkhavai, samayai uṇṇābhau brāhmaṇau kālaṅkaraiyya, natthi saṃyaujanaṃ yaina saṃyaujanaina saṃyuttau uṇṇābhau brāhmaṇau puna imaṃ laukaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sākaitasuttaṃ

513. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sākaitai viharati añ‍janavanai migadāyai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma yāni pañ‍cindriyāni tāni pañ‍ca balāni haunti, yāni pañ‍ca balāni tāni pañ‍cindriyāni hauntī’’ti?

‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma yāni pañ‍cindriyāni tāni pañ‍ca balāni haunti, yāni pañ‍ca balāni tāni pañ‍cindriyāni haunti’’|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma yāni pañ‍cindriyāni tāni pañ‍ca balāni haunti, yāni pañ‍ca balāni tāni pañ‍cindriyāni haunti? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, saddhindriyaṃ taṃ saddhābalaṃ, yaṃ saddhābalaṃ taṃ saddhindriyaṃ; yaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ taṃ vīriyabalaṃ, yaṃ vīriyabalaṃ taṃ vīriyindriyaṃ; yaṃ satindriyaṃ taṃ satibalaṃ, yaṃ satibalaṃ taṃ satindriyaṃ; yaṃ samādhindriyaṃ taṃ  samādhibalaṃ, yaṃ samādhibalaṃ taṃ samādhindriyaṃ; yaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ, yaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ taṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā, tassa majjhai dīpau| atthi, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma tassā nadiyā aikau sautau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati saṅkhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| atthi pana, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma tassā nadiyā dvai sautāni tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchanti|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma tassā nadiyā aikau sautau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati? yañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, tassa dīpassa purimantai puratthimantai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) udakaṃ, yañ‍ca pacchimantai udakaṃ – ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma tassā nadiyā aikau sautau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma tassā nadiyā dvai sautāni tvaiva  saṅkhyaṃ gacchanti? yañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, tassa dīpassa uttarantai udakaṃ, yañ‍ca dakkhiṇantai udakaṃ – ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma tassā nadiyā dvai sautāni tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchanti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ saddhindriyaṃ taṃ saddhābalaṃ, yaṃ saddhābalaṃ taṃ saddhindriyaṃ; yaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ taṃ vīriyabalaṃ, yaṃ vīriyabalaṃ taṃ vīriyindriyaṃ; yaṃ satindriyaṃ taṃ satibalaṃ, yaṃ satibalaṃ taṃ satindriyaṃ; yaṃ  samādhindriyaṃ taṃ samādhibalaṃ, yaṃ samādhibalaṃ taṃ samādhindriyaṃ; yaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ, yaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ taṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| pañ‍can‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. pubbakauṭṭhakasuttaṃ

514. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbakauṭṭhakai| tatra khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘saddahasi saddahāsi (sī॰ pī॰) tvaṃ, sāriputta – saddhindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausāna’’nti?

‘‘na  khvāhaṃ aittha, bhantai, bhagavatau saddhāya gacchāmi – saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| yaisañhaitaṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñātaṃ assa adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphassitaṃ apassitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), aphusitaṃ (pī॰) pañ‍ñāya, tai tattha paraisaṃ saddhāya gacchaiyyuṃ – saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| yaisañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhantai, ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya, nik‍kaṅkhā tai tattha nibbicikicchā – saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| mayhañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhantai, ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya| nik‍kaṅkhavāhaṃ  tattha nibbicikicchau saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausāna’’nti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! yaisañhaitaṃ, sāriputta, añ‍ñātaṃ assa adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ  aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya, tai tattha paraisaṃ saddhāya gacchaiyyuṃ – saddhindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| yaisañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, sāriputta, ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya, nik‍kaṅkhā tai tattha nibbicikicchā  – saddhindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ  amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausāna’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamapubbārāmasuttaṃ

515. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘katinaṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti?

bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘aikassa khau, bhikkhavai, indriyassa bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmīti| katamassa aikassa pañ‍ñindriyassa pañ‍ñavatau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa tadanvayā saddhā saṇṭhāti, tadanvayaṃ vīriyaṃ saṇṭhāti, tadanvayā sati saṇṭhāti, tadanvayau samādhi saṇṭhāti| imassa khau, bhikkhavai, aikassa indriyassa bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyapubbārāmasuttaṃ

516. taṃyaiva nidānaṃ| ‘‘katinaṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘dvin‍naṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti  – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmīti| katamaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ? ariyāya ca pañ‍ñāya, ariyāya ca vimuttiyā| yā hissa, bhikkhavai, ariyā pañ‍ñā tadassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| yā hissa, bhikkhavai, ariyā vimutti tadassa samādhindriyaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dvin‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatiyapubbārāmasuttaṃ

517. taṃyaiva nidānaṃ| ‘‘katinaṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā  bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘catun‍naṃ khau, bhikkhavai, indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmīti| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? vīriyindriyassa, satindriyassa, samādhindriyassa, pañ‍ñindriyassa – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. catutthapubbārāmasuttaṃ

518. taṃyaiva nidānaṃ| ‘‘katinaṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ khau, bhikkhavai, indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmīti| katamaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ? saddhindriyassa, vīriyindriyassa, satindriyassa, samādhindriyassa, pañ‍ñindriyassa – imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā  khīṇāsavau bhikkhu añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. piṇḍaulabhāradvājasuttaṃ

519. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatā piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina añ‍ñā byākatā hauti – ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’’ti| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘āyasmatā, bhantai, piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina añ‍ñā byākatā – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmīti| kiṃ nu khau, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānaina āyasmatā piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina añ‍ñā byākatā – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti?

‘‘tiṇṇan‍naṃ khau, bhikkhavai, indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina bhikkhunā añ‍ñā byākatā – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmīti| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ? satindriyassa, samādhindriyassa, pañ‍ñindriyassa – imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina bhikkhunā añ‍ñā byākatā – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmīti| imāni ca, bhikkhavai, tīṇindriyāni kimantāni? khayantāni| kissa khayantāni? jātijarāmaraṇassa| ‘jātijarāmaraṇaṃ khaya’nti khau, bhikkhavai, sampassamānaina piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina  bhikkhunā añ‍ñā byākatā – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. āpaṇasuttaṃ

520. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā aṅgaisu viharati āpaṇaṃ nāma aṅgānaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘yau sau, sāriputta, ariyasāvakau tathāgatai aikantagatau aikantigatau (sī॰) abhippasan‍nau, na sau tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā kaṅkhaiyya vā vicikicchaiyya vā’’ti?

‘‘yau sau, bhantai, ariyasāvakau tathāgatai aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau, na sau tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā kaṅkhaiyya vā vicikicchaiyya vā| saddhassa hi, bhantai, ariyasāvakassa aivaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ yaṃ  āraddhavīriyau viharissati – akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| yaṃ hissa, bhantai, vīriyaṃ tadassa vīriyindriyaṃ|

‘‘saddhassa hi, bhantai, ariyasāvakassa āraddhavīriyassa aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ yaṃ satimā bhavissati, paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| yā hissa, bhantai, sati tadassa satindriyaṃ|

‘‘saddhassa hi, bhantai, ariyasāvakassa āraddhavīriyassa upaṭṭhitassatinau aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ yaṃ vaussaggārammaṇaṃ karitvā labhissati samādhiṃ, labhissati cittassa aikaggataṃ| yau hissa, bhantai, samādhi tadassa samādhindriyaṃ|

‘‘saddhassa hi, bhantai, ariyasāvakassa āraddhavīriyassa upaṭṭhitassatinau  samāhitacittassa aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ yaṃ aivaṃ pajānissati – anamataggau khau saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| avij‍jāya tvaiva tamaukāyassa asaisavirāganiraudhau santamaitaṃ padaṃ paṇītamaitaṃ padaṃ, yadidaṃ – sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ nibbānanti (?)| yā hissa, bhantai, pañ‍ñā tadassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ|

‘‘saddhau  sau sa khau sau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhantai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ padahitvā padahitvā aivaṃ saritvā saritvā aivaṃ samādahitvā samādahitvā aivaṃ pajānitvā pajānitvā aivaṃ abhisaddahati – ‘imai khau tai dhammā yai mai pubbai sutavā ahaisuṃ| taināhaṃ  aitarahi kāyaina ca phusitvā viharāmi, pañ‍ñāya ca ativijjha paṭivijjha (sī॰ ka॰) tadaṭṭhakathāsu pana ativijjhitvāti vaṇṇitaṃ passāmī’ti| yā hissa, bhantai, saddhā tadassa saddhindriya’’nti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! yau sau, sāriputta, ariyasāvakau tathāgatai aikantagatau abhippasan‍nau, na sau tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā kaṅkhaiyya vā vicikicchaiyya vā| saddhassa hi, sāriputta, ariyasāvakassa aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ yaṃ āraddhavīriyau viharissati – akusalānaṃ  dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| yaṃ hissa, sāriputta, vīriyaṃ tadassa vīriyindriyaṃ|

‘‘saddhassa hi, sāriputta, ariyasāvakassa āraddhavīriyassa aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ yaṃ satimā bhavissati, paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| yā hissa, sāriputta, sati tadassa satindriyaṃ|

‘‘saddhassa hi, sāriputta, ariyasāvakassa āraddhavīriyassa upaṭṭhitassatinau aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ yaṃ vaussaggārammaṇaṃ karitvā labhissati samādhiṃ, labhissati cittassa aikaggataṃ| yau hissa, sāriputta, samādhi tadassa samādhindriyaṃ|

‘‘saddhassa hi, sāriputta, ariyasāvakassa āraddhavīriyassa upaṭṭhitassatinau samāhitacittassa  aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ yaṃ aivaṃ pajānissati – anamataggau khau saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| avij‍jāya tvaiva tamaukāyassa asaisavirāganiraudhau santamaitaṃ padaṃ paṇītamaitaṃ padaṃ, yadidaṃ – sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| yā hissa, sāriputta, pañ‍ñā tadassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ|

‘‘saddhau sau sa khau sau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sāriputta, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ padahitvā padahitvā aivaṃ saritvā saritvā aivaṃ samādahitvā samādahitvā aivaṃ pajānitvā pajānitvā aivaṃ abhisaddahati – ‘imai khau tai  dhammā yai mai pubbai sutavā ahaisuṃ| taināhaṃ aitarahi  kāyaina ca phusitvā viharāmi, pañ‍ñāya ca ativijjha paṭivijjha (ka॰ sī॰ ka॰) passāmī’ti| yā hissa, sāriputta, saddhā tadassa saddhindriya’’nti| dasamaṃ|

jarāvaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

jarā uṇṇābhau brāhmaṇau, sākaitau pubbakauṭṭhakau|

pubbārāmai ca cattāri, piṇḍaulau āpaṇaina cāti saddhaina tai dasāti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)||

6. sūkarakhatavaggau

1. sālasuttaṃ

521. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu viharati sālāya brāhmaṇagāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici tiracchānagatā pāṇā, sīhau migarājā taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – thāmaina javaina sūraina sūriyaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰); aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāya’’|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyā dhammā? saddhindriyaṃ, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati; vīriyindriyaṃ baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati; satindriyaṃ baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati; samādhindriyaṃ baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati; pañ‍ñindriyaṃ baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici tiracchānagatā pāṇā, sīhau migarājā taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – thāmaina javaina sūraina; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāyā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. mal‍likasuttaṃ

522. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā mal‍laisu mal‍lakaisu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), mal‍likaisu (ka॰) viharati uruvailakappaṃ  nāma mal‍lānaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa ariyañāṇaṃ na uppan‍naṃ hauti naiva tāva catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ saṇṭhiti hauti, naiva tāva catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ avaṭṭhiti hauti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa ariyañāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti, atha catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ saṇṭhiti hauti, atha catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ avaṭṭhiti hauti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāvakīvañ‍ca kūṭāgārassa kūṭaṃ na ussitaṃ hauti, naiva tāva gaupānasīnaṃ saṇṭhiti hauti, naiva tāva gaupānasīnaṃ avaṭṭhiti hauti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgārassa kūṭaṃ ussitaṃ  hauti, atha gaupānasīnaṃ saṇṭhiti hauti, atha gaupānasīnaṃ avaṭṭhiti hauti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yāvakīvañ‍ca ariyasāvakassa ariyañāṇaṃ na uppan‍naṃ hauti, naiva tāva catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ saṇṭhiti hauti, naiva tāva catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ avaṭṭhiti hauti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa ariyañāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti, atha catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ…pai॰… avaṭṭhiti hauti|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? saddhindriyassa, vīriyindriyassa, satindriyassa, samādhindriyassa| pañ‍ñavatau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa tadanvayā saddhā saṇṭhāti, tadanvayaṃ vīriyaṃ saṇṭhāti, tadanvayā sati saṇṭhāti, tadanvayau samādhi saṇṭhātī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saikhasuttaṃ

523. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma saikhau bhikkhu saikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘saikhausmī’ti pajānaiyya, asaikhau bhikkhu asaikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘asaikhausmī’ti pajānaiyyā’’ti?

bhagavaṃmūlakā  nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma saikhau bhikkhu saikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘saikhausmī’ti pajānaiyya, asaikhau bhikkhu asaikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘asaikhausmī’ti pajānaiyya’’|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma saikhau bhikkhu saikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘saikhausmī’ti pajānāti? idha, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – ayampi khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma saikhau bhikkhu saikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘saikhausmī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘atthi nu khau itau bahiddhā añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā yau aivaṃ bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ tathaṃ dhammaṃ  daisaiti  yathā bhagavā’ti? sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘natthi khau itau bahiddhā añ‍ñau samaṇau vā  brāhmaṇau vā yau aivaṃ bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ tathaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti yathā bhagavā’ti| ayampi khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma saikhau bhikkhu saikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘saikhausmī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, saikhau bhikkhu pañ‍cindriyāni pajānāti – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – yaṃgatikāni yaṃparamāni yaṃphalāni yaṃpariyausānāni| na haiva khau kāyaina phusitvā viharati; pañ‍ñāya ca ativijjha passati| ayampi khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma saikhau bhikkhu saikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘saikhausmī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma asaikhau bhikkhu asaikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘asaikhausmī’ti pajānāti? idha, bhikkhavai, asaikhau bhikkhu pañ‍cindriyāni pajānāti – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – yaṃgatikāni yaṃparamāni yaṃphalāni yaṃpariyausānāni| kāyaina ca phusitvā viharati; pañ‍ñāya ca ativijjha  passati| ayampi khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma asaikhau bhikkhu asaikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘asaikhausmī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asaikhau bhikkhu cha indriyāni pajānāti| ‘cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ – imāni khau cha indriyāni sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ  aparisaisaṃ nirujjhissanti, añ‍ñāni ca cha indriyāni na kuhiñ‍ci kismiñ‍ci uppaj‍jissantī’ti pajānāti| ayampi khau, bhikkhavai, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma asaikhau bhikkhu asaikhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau ‘asaikhausmī’ti pajānātī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. padasuttaṃ

524. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici jaṅgalānaṃ jaṅgamānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) pāṇānaṃ padajātāni sabbāni tāni hatthipadai samaudhānaṃ gacchanti, hatthipadaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – mahantattaina; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici padāni baudhāya saṃvattanti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ padaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāya| katamāni ca, bhikkhavai, padāni baudhāya saṃvattanti? saddhindriyaṃ, bhikkhavai, padaṃ, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati; vīriyindriyaṃ padaṃ, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati; satindriyaṃ padaṃ, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati; samādhindriyaṃ padaṃ, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati; pañ‍ñindriyaṃ padaṃ, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici jaṅgalānaṃ pāṇānaṃ padajātāni sabbāni tāni hatthipadai samaudhānaṃ gacchanti, hatthipadaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – mahantattaina; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici padāni baudhāya saṃvattanti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ padaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. sārasuttaṃ

525. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici sāragandhā, lauhitacandanaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāya| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyā dhammā? saddhindriyaṃ, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati| vīriyindriyaṃ…pai॰… satindriyaṃ  …pai॰… samādhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici sāragandhā, lauhitacandanaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāyā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. patiṭṭhitasuttaṃ

526. ‘‘aikadhammai  patiṭṭhitassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitāni| katamasmiṃ aikadhammai? appamādai| katamau ca bhikkhavai, appamādau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cittaṃ rakkhati āsavaisu ca sāsavaisu ca dhammaisu| tassa cittaṃ rakkhatau āsavaisu ca sāsavaisu ca dhammaisu saddhindriyampi bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| vīriyindriyampi bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| satindriyampi bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| samādhindriyampi bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| pañ‍ñindriyampi bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, aikadhammai patiṭṭhitassa bhikkhunau pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitānī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sahampatibrahmasuttaṃ

527. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai ajapālanigraudhai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| atha khau  bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni amataugadhāni haunti amataparāyaṇāni amatapariyausānāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| vīriyindriyaṃ…pai॰… satindriyaṃ…pai॰… samādhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| imāni pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni amataugadhāni haunti amataparāyaṇāni amatapariyausānānī’’ti|

atha khau brahmā sahampati bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā  purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya; aivamaiva brahmalaukai antarahitau bhagavatau puratau pāturahausi| atha  khau brahmā sahampati aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ sugata! pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni amataugadhāni haunti amataparāyaṇāni amatapariyausānāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amataugadhaṃ hauti amataparāyaṇaṃ amatapariyausānaṃ| imāni pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni amataugadhāni haunti amataparāyaṇāni amatapariyausānāni’’|

‘‘bhūtapubbāhaṃ, bhantai, kassapai sammāsambuddhai brahmacariyaṃ acariṃ| tatrapi maṃ aivaṃ jānanti – ‘sahakau bhikkhu, sahakau bhikkhū’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, imaisaṃyaiva pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā kāmaisu kāmacchandaṃ virājaitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ brahmalaukaṃ upapan‍nau| tatrapi maṃ aivaṃ jānanti – ‘brahmā sahampati, brahmā sahampatī’’’ti| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ sugata! ahamaitaṃ jānāmi, ahamaitaṃ passāmi yathā imāni pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni amataugadhāni haunti amataparāyaṇāni amatapariyausānānī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. sūkarakhatasuttaṃ

528. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai sūkarakhatāyaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, sāriputta, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā paramanipac‍cakāraṃ pavattamānau pavattatī’’ti pavattaitīti (sī॰)? ‘‘anuttarañhi, bhantai, yaugakkhaimaṃ sampassamānau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā paramanipac‍cakāraṃ pavattamānau pavattatī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! anuttarañhi, sāriputta, yaugakkhaimaṃ sampassamānau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā paramanipac‍cakāraṃ pavattamānau pavattatī’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, sāriputta, anuttarau yaugakkhaimau yaṃ sampassamānau  khīṇāsavau bhikkhu tathāgatai  vā tathāgatasāsanai vā paramanipac‍cakāraṃ pavattamānau pavattatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhantai, khīṇāsavau bhikkhu saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… satindriyaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… samādhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaiti upasamagāmiṃ sambaudhagāmiṃ| ayaṃ khau, bhantai, anuttarau yaugakkhaimau yaṃ sampassamānau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā paramanipac‍cakāraṃ pavattamānau pavattatī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! aisau hi, sāriputta, anuttarau yaugakkhaimau yaṃ sampassamānau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā paramanipac‍cakāraṃ pavattamānau pavattatī’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, sāriputta, paramanipac‍cakārau yaṃ khīṇāsavau bhikkhu tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā paramanipac‍cakāraṃ pavattamānau pavattatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhantai, khīṇāsavau bhikkhu satthari sagāravau viharati sappatissau sappaṭissau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), dhammai sagāravau viharati sappatissau, saṅghai sagāravau viharati sappatissau, sikkhāya sagāravau viharati sappatissau, samādhismiṃ sagāravau viharati sappatissau| ayaṃ khau, bhantai, paramanipac‍cakārau yaṃ khīṇāsavau bhikkhu tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā paramanipac‍cakāraṃ pavattamānau pavattatī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu  sādhu, sāriputta! aisau hi, sāriputta, paramanipac‍cakārau yaṃ khīṇāsavau bhikkhu tathāgatai vā tathāgatasāsanai vā paramanipac‍cakāraṃ pavattamānau pavattatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamauppādasuttaṃ

529. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni anuppan‍nāni uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra tathāgatassa pātubhāvā arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni anuppan‍nāni uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra tathāgatassa pātubhāvā arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyauppādasuttaṃ

530. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni anuppan‍nāni uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni anuppan‍nāni uppaj‍janti, nāñ‍ñatra sugatavinayā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sūkarakhatavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sālaṃ mal‍likaṃ saikhau ca, padaṃ sāraṃ patiṭṭhitaṃ|

brahmasūkarakhatāyau, uppādā aparai duvaiti||

7. baudhipakkhiyavaggau

1. saṃyaujanasuttaṃ

531. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni saṃyaujanappahānāya saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya (syā॰ ka॰) saṃvattanti| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni saṃyaujanappahānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. anusayasuttaṃ

532. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni anusayasamugghātāya saṃvattanti| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni anusayasamugghātāya saṃvattantī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. pariñ‍ñāsuttaṃ

533. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni addhānapariñ‍ñāya saṃvattanti| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni addhānapariñ‍ñāya saṃvattantī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. āsavakkhayasuttaṃ

534. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattanti| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattantī’’ti|

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni saṃyaujanappahānāya saṃvattanti, anusayasamugghātāya saṃvattanti, addhānapariñ‍ñāya  saṃvattanti, āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattanti| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni bahulīkatāni saṃyaujanappahānāya saṃvattanti, anusayasamugghātāya saṃvattanti, addhānapariñ‍ñāya saṃvattanti, āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamaphalasuttaṃ

535. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaphalasuttaṃ

536. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā satta phalā sattānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| katamai satta phalā sattānisaṃsā? diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, atha maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, atha pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti, upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī  hauti, uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā imai satta phalā sattānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamarukkhasuttaṃ

537. ‘‘saiyyathāpi bhikkhavai, yai kaici jambudīpakā rukkhā, jambū taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāya| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyā dhammā? saddhindriyaṃ, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati| vīriyindriyaṃ…pai॰… satindriyaṃ…pai॰… samādhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici jambudīpakā rukkhā, jambū taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyarukkhasuttaṃ

538. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ rukkhā, pārichattakau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāya| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyā dhammā? saddhindriyaṃ, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati| vīriyindriyaṃ…pai॰… satindriyaṃ…pai॰…  samādhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ rukkhā, pārichattakau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāyā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tatiyarukkhasuttaṃ

539. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici asurānaṃ rukkhā, cittapāṭali taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāya| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyā dhammā? saddhindriyaṃ, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati| saiyyathāpi bhikkhavai, yai kaici asurānaṃ rukkhā, cittapāṭali taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāyā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. catuttharukkhasuttaṃ

540. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici supaṇṇānaṃ rukkhā, kūṭasimbalī kauṭasimbali (syā॰ kaṃ॰) taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāya| katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyā dhammā? saddhindriyaṃ, bhikkhavai, baudhipakkhiyau dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ baudhipakkhiyau  dhammau, taṃ baudhāya saṃvattati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici supaṇṇānaṃ rukkhā, kūṭasimbalī taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici baudhipakkhiyā dhammā, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ – baudhāyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

baudhipakkhiyavaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

saṃyaujanā  anusayā, pariñ‍ñā āsavakkhayā|

dvai phalā caturau rukkhā, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

8. gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1-12. pācīnādisuttadvādasakaṃ

541-552. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaintau pañ‍cindriyāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaintau pañ‍cindriyāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ…pai॰… satindriyaṃ… samādhindriyaṃ… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaintau pañ‍cindriyāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha  pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

dvaitai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

appamādavaggau  vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

tathāgataṃ padaṃ kūṭaṃ, mūlaṃ sāraina vassikaṃ|

rājā candimasūriyā, vatthaina dasamaṃ padanti||

balakaraṇīyavaggau vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

balaṃ bījañ‍ca nāgau ca, rukkhau kumbhaina sūkiyā|

ākāsaina ca dvai maighā, nāvā āgantukā nadīti||

aisanāvaggau vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aisanā  vidhā āsavau, bhavau ca dukkhatā tissau|

khilaṃ malañ‍ca nīghau ca, vaidanā taṇhā tasinā cāti||

12. aughavaggau

1-10. aughādisuttadasakaṃ

587-596. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaitabbāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti  vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya imāni pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaitabbānī’’ti| dasamaṃ| (yathā maggasaṃyuttaṃ, tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

aughavaggau dvādasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aughau yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthā anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇā, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyāti||

13. gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1-12. pācīnādisuttadvādasakaṃ

597-608. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaintau pañ‍cindriyāni bahulīkarauntau  nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaintau pañ‍cindriyāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaintau pañ‍cindriyāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau tairasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha  pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

dvaitai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

appamādavagga-balakaraṇīyavagga-aisanāvaggā vitthāraitabbā|

17. aughavaggau

1-10. aughādisuttadasakaṃ

641-650. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaitabbāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| vīriyindriyaṃ  …pai॰… satindriyaṃ… samādhindriyaṃ  … pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya imāni pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaitabbānī’’ti|

aughau yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthā anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇā, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyāti||

indriyasaṃyuttaṃ catutthaṃ|



5. sammappadhānasaṃyuttaṃ

1. gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1-12. pācīnādisuttadvādasakaṃ

651-662. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, sammappadhānā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ  dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau sammappadhānāti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau sammappadhānai bhāvaintau cattārau sammappadhānai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau sammappadhānai bhāvaintau cattārau sammappadhānai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya  vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau sammappadhānai bhāvaintau cattārau sammappadhānai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| dvādasamaṃ| (sammappadhānasaṃyuttassa gaṅgāpaiyyālī sammappadhānavasaina vitthāraitabbā)|

gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha  pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

dvaitai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

2. appamādavaggau

(appamādavaggau sammappadhānavasaina vitthāraitabbau)|

tassuddānaṃ –

tathāgataṃ padaṃ kūṭaṃ, mūlaṃ sāraina vassikaṃ|

rājā candimasūriyā, vatthaina dasamaṃ padanti||

3. balakaraṇīyavaggau

1-12. balakaraṇīyādisuttadvādasakaṃ

673-684. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici balakaraṇīyā kammantā kayiranti, sabbai  tai pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya aivamaitai balakaraṇīyā kammantā kayiranti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ  nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya cattārau sammappadhānai bhāvaiti, cattārau sammappadhānai bahulīkarauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya sīlai patiṭṭhāya cattārau sammappadhānai bhāvaiti, cattārau sammappadhānai bahulīkarauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaṃ nissāya  sīlai patiṭṭhāya cattārau sammappadhānai bhāvaiti, cattārau sammappadhānai bahulīkarautī’’ti| (aivaṃ balakaraṇīyavaggau sammappadhānavasaina vitthāraitabbau)| dvādasamaṃ|

balakaraṇīyavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

balaṃ bījañ‍ca nāgau ca, rukkhau kumbhaina sūkiyā|

ākāsaina ca dvai maighā, nāvā āgantukā nadīti||

4. aisanāvaggau

1-10. aisanādisuttadasakaṃ

685-694. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, aisanā| katamā tissau? kāmaisanā, bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau aisanā| imāsaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati  cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tissan‍naṃ aisanānaṃ  abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya imai cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| (vitthāraitabbaṃ)| dasamaṃ|

aisanāvaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aisanā vidhā āsavau, bhavau ca dukkhatā tissau|

khilaṃ malañ‍ca nīghau ca, vaidanā taṇhā tasinā cāti||

5. aughavaggau

1-10. aughādisuttadasakaṃ

695-704. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti  vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya imai cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| (vitthāraitabbā)| dasamaṃ|

aughavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aughau yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthā anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇā, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyāti||

sammappadhānasaṃyuttaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|



6. balasaṃyuttaṃ

1. gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1-12. balādisuttadvādasakaṃ

705-716. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca balānīti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ca balāni bhāvaintau pañ‍cabalāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ca balāni bhāvaintau pañ‍ca balāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhābalaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ, vīriyabalaṃ…pai॰… satibalaṃ… samādhibalaṃ… pañ‍ñābalaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ca balāni bhāvaintau pañ‍ca balāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha  pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

dvaitai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

2. appamādavaggau

appamādavaggau  vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

tathāgataṃ padaṃ kūṭaṃ, mūlaṃ sāraina vassikaṃ|

rājā candimasūriyā, vatthaina dasamaṃ padanti||

balakaraṇīyavaggau vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

balaṃ  bījañ‍ca nāgau ca, rukkhau kumbhaina sūkiyā|

ākāsaina ca dvai maighā, nāvā āgantukā nadīti||

aisanāvaggau vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aisanā vidhā āsavau, bhavau ca dukkhatā tissau|

khilaṃ malañ‍ca nīghau ca, vaidanā taṇhā tasinā cāti||

5. aughavaggau

1-10. aughādisuttadasakaṃ

749-758. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya pañ‍ca balāni bhāvaitabbāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu, saddhābalaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ, vīriyabalaṃ…pai॰… satibalaṃ…pai॰… samādhibalaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñābalaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya imāni pañ‍ca balāni bhāvaitabbānī’’ti| (aivaṃ vitthāraitabbā)| dasamaṃ|

aughavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aughau yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthā anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇā, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyāti||

6. gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1-12. pācīnādisuttadvādasakaṃ

759-770. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ca balāni bhāvaintau pañ‍ca balāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ca balāni bhāvaintau pañ‍ca balāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu, saddhābalaṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ca balāni bhāvaintau pañ‍ca balāni bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| (vitthāraitabbā) dvādasamaṃ|

gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

dvaitai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

appamāda-balakaraṇīyavaggā vitthāraitabbā|

9. aisanāvaggau

1-12. aisanādisuttadvādasakaṃ

792-802. aivaṃ aisanāpāḷi vitthāraitabbā – rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ|

aisanāvaggau navamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aisanā vidhā āsavau, bhavau ca dukkhatā tissau|

khilaṃ malañ‍ca nīghau ca, vaidanā taṇhā tasinā cāti||

10. aughavaggau

1-10. aughādisuttadasakaṃ

803-812. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ avij‍jā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya pañ‍ca balāni bhāvaitabbāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhābalaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… pañ‍ñābalaṃ bhāvaiti rāgavinayapariyausānaṃ dausavinayapariyausānaṃ mauhavinayapariyausānaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya imāni pañ‍ca balāni bhāvaitabbānī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

aughavaggau dasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aughau yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthā anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇā, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyāti||

balasaṃyuttaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|



7. iddhipādasaṃyuttaṃ

1. cāpālavaggau

1. apārasuttaṃ

813. ‘‘cattāraumai  bhikkhavai, iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, cittasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya saṃvattantī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. viraddhasuttaṃ

814. ‘‘yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā viraddhā, viraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā āraddhā, āraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| yaisaṃ  kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, imai cattārau iddhipādā viraddhā, viraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci, bhikkhavai, imai cattārau iddhipādā āraddhā, āraddhau taisaṃ ariyau  maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. ariyasuttaṃ

815. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ariyā niyyānikā niyyanti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā  bahulīkatā ariyā niyyānikā niyyanti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. nibbidāsuttaṃ

816. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. iddhipadaisasuttaṃ

817. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā iddhipadaisaṃ abhinipphādaisuṃ sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā iddhipadaisaṃ abhinipphādaissanti sabbai tai  catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā iddhipadaisaṃ abhinipphādainti sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā|
