‘‘katamaisaṃ  catun‍naṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā iddhipadaisaṃ abhinipphādaisuṃ, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā iddhipadaisaṃ abhinipphādaissanti, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā iddhipadaisaṃ abhinipphādainti, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. samattasuttaṃ

818. ‘‘yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samattaṃ iddhiṃ abhinipphādaisuṃ, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samattaṃ iddhiṃ abhinipphādaissanti, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samattaṃ iddhiṃ abhinipphādainti, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samattaṃ iddhiṃ abhinipphādaisuṃ, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samattaṃ iddhiṃ abhinipphādaissanti, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samattaṃ iddhiṃ abhinipphādainti, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. bhikkhusuttaṃ

819. ‘‘yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ bhikkhū āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihariṃsu, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ bhikkhū āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissanti, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi bhikkhū āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā|

‘‘katamaisaṃ  catun‍naṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ bhikkhū āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihariṃsu sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ bhikkhū āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissanti, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi bhikkhū āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. buddhasuttaṃ

820. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, iddhipādā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi …pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgatau ‘arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ñāṇasuttaṃ

821. ‘‘‘ayaṃ  chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau’ti mai bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘sau khau panāyaṃ chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau bhāvaitabbau’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘bhāvitau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘ayaṃ  vīriyasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘sau khau panāyaṃ vīriyasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau bhāvaitabbau’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘bhāvitau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘ayaṃ cittasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘sau khau panāyaṃ cittasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau bhāvaitabbau’ti  mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘bhāvitau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘ayaṃ vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘sau khau panāyaṃ vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau bhāvaitabbau’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘bhāvitau’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ  udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. caitiyasuttaṃ

822. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gaṇhāhi, ānanda, nisīdanaṃ| yaina cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ pāvālacaitiyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) tainupasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā nisīdanaṃ ādāya bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi| atha khau bhagavā yaina cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| āyasmāpi khau ānandau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘ramaṇīyā, ānanda, vaisālī, ramaṇīyaṃ udainaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ gautamakaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sattambaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ bahuputtaṃ caitiyaṃ bahuputtakacaitiyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), ramaṇīyaṃ sārandadaṃ caitiyaṃ ānandacaitiyaṃ (ka॰), sānandaraṃ (ka॰), ramaṇīyaṃ cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ| yassa kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā| ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā’’ti|

aivampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatā auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ; na bhagavantaṃ yāci – ‘‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ sugatau kappāvasaisaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 2.167 bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti yathā taṃ māraina pariyuṭṭhitacittau|

dutiyampi  khau bhagavā…pai॰… tatiyampi  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ramaṇīyā, ānanda, vaisālī, ramaṇīyaṃ udainaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ gautamakaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sattambaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ  bahuputtaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sārandadaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ| yassa kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, sau ākaṅkhamānau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā| ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā’’ti|

aivampi  khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatā auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ; na bhagavantaṃ yāci – ‘‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti yathā taṃ māraina pariyuṭṭhitacittau|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gaccha khau tvaṃ, ānanda, yassa dāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi| atha khau mārau pāpimā, acirapak‍kantai āyasmantai ānandai, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘parinibbātu  dāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu dāni sugatau parinibbātu sugatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivamuparipi! parinibbānakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau| bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi yāva mai bhikkhū na sāvakā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti uttāniṃ karissanti (ka॰), uttānikarissanti (pī॰), uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissantī’’’ti|

santi khau pana, bhantai, aitarahi bhikkhū bhagavatau sāvakā viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā  ācikkhanti daisainti pañ‍ñapainti paṭṭhapainti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaraunti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisainti| parinibbātu dāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu dāni, sugatau! parinibbānakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau|

‘‘bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi yāva mai bhikkhuniyau na sāvikā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā  visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti  uttānīkarissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissantī’’’ti|

‘‘santi khau pana, bhantai, aitarahi bhikkhuniyau bhagavatau sāvikā viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhanti daisainti pañ‍ñapainti paṭṭhapainti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaraunti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisainti| parinibbātu dāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu dāni, sugatau! parinibbānakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau|

‘‘bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi yāva mai upāsakā…pai॰… yāva mai upāsikā na sāvikā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissantī’’’ti|

‘‘santi khau pana, bhantai, aitarahi upāsakā…pai॰… upāsikā bhagavatau sāvikā viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā  sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhanti daisainti pañ‍ñapainti paṭṭhapainti vivaranti vibhajanti  uttānīkaraunti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisainti| parinibbātu dāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu dāni, sugatau! parinibbānakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau|

‘‘bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi yāva mai idaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na iddhañ‍caiva bhavissati phītañ‍ca vitthāritaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsita’nti| tayidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau brahmacariyaṃ iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca vitthāritaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ  yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ| parinibbātu dāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu dāni sugatau| parinibbānakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘appaussuk‍kau tvaṃ, pāpima, hauhi| na ciraṃ nacirassaiva (ka॰) tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati| itau tiṇṇaṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina tathāgatau parinibbāyissatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā cāpālai caitiyai satau sampajānau āyusaṅkhāraṃ aussaji| aussaṭṭhai ca ausaj‍jai pana (ka॰) bhagavatā āyusaṅkhārai mahābhūmicālau ahausi bhiṃsanakau laumahaṃsau, daivadundubhiyau daivadudrabhiyau (ka॰) ca phaliṃsu| atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘tulamatulañ‍ca  sambhavaṃ, bhavasaṅkhāramavassaji muni|

ajjhattaratau samāhitau, abhindi kavacamivattasambhava’’nti|| dasamaṃ|

cāpālavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

apārāpi viraddhau ca, ariyā nibbidāpi ca|

padaisaṃ samattaṃ bhikkhu, buddhaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca caitiyanti||

2. pāsādakampanavaggau

1. pubbasuttaṃ

823. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pubbaiva mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu, kau pac‍cayau iddhipādabhāvanāyā’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘idha bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – iti mai chandau na ca atilīnau bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitau bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittau bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittau bhavissati| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā  adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti’’’|

‘‘vīriyasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ  iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – iti mai vīriyaṃ na ca atilīnaṃ bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitaṃ bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittaṃ bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ bhavissati| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘cittasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ  iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – iti mai cittaṃ na ca atilīnaṃ bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitaṃ bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittaṃ bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ bhavissati| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – iti mai vīmaṃsā na ca atilīnā bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitā bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittā bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhu, catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu, anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai  abhij‍jamānau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) gacchati, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina  kamati, saiyyathāpi  pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasati parāmasati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) parimaj‍jati; yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhu, catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti – dibbai ca mānusai ca, dūrai santikai cāti|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhu, catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu, parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti| sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītamauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘saṃkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vikkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘mahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti; amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘amahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti; sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sauttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti; anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘anuttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti; samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘asamāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti’’|

‘‘aivaṃ  bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhu, catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu, anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi  jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ uppādiṃ (sī॰); tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau  aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau bhikkhu, catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu, dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata, bhauntau, sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana, bhauntau, sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ  anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhu, catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. mahapphalasuttaṃ

824. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti  mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ‘iti mai chandau na ca atilīnau bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitau bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittau bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittau bhavissati’| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā  divā| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ‘iti mai vīmaṃsā na ca atilīnā bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitā  bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittā bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati’| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti…pai॰…|

‘‘aivaṃ  bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. chandasamādhisuttaṃ

825. ‘‘chandaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nissāya labhati samādhiṃ, labhati cittassa aikaggataṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati chandasamādhi| sau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ  akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| uppan‍nānaṃ  kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imai vuc‍canti ‘padhānasaṅkhārā’ti| iti ayañ‍ca chandau, ayañ‍ca chandasamādhi, imai ca padhānasaṅkhārā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau’’’|

‘‘vīriyaṃ  cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nissāya labhati samādhiṃ, labhati cittassa aikaggataṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘vīriyasamādhi’| sau anuppan‍nānaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imai vuc‍canti ‘padhānasaṅkhārā’ti| iti idañ‍ca vīriyaṃ, ayañ‍ca vīriyasamādhi, imai ca padhānasaṅkhārā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘vīriyasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau’’’|

‘‘cittaṃ  cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nissāya labhati samādhiṃ, labhati cittassa aikaggataṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘cittasamādhi’| sau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imai vuc‍canti ‘padhānasaṅkhārā’ti| iti idañ‍ca cittaṃ, ayañ‍ca cittasamādhi, imai ca padhānasaṅkhārā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘cittasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau  iddhipādau’’’|

‘‘vīmaṃsaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nissāya labhati samādhiṃ, labhati cittassa aikaggataṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘vīmaṃsāsamādhi’| sau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imai vuc‍canti ‘padhānasaṅkhārā’ti| iti ayañ‍ca vīmaṃsā, ayañ‍ca vīmaṃsāsamādhi, imai ca padhānasaṅkhārā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau’’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. mauggal‍lānasuttaṃ

826. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū haiṭṭhāmigāramātupāsādai viharanti uddhatā un‍naḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā muṭṭhassatinau asampajānā asamāhitā bhantacittā vibbhantacittā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) pākatindriyā|

atha  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aitai khau, mauggal‍lāna, sabrahmacārinau haiṭṭhāmigāramātupāsādai  viharanti uddhatā un‍naḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā muṭṭhassatinau asampajānā asamāhitā bhantacittā pākatindriyā| gaccha, mauggal‍lāna, tai bhikkhū saṃvaijaihī’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau  bhagavatau paṭissutvā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāraisi abhisaṅkharaisi (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) yathā pādaṅguṭṭhakaina migāramātupāsādaṃ saṅkampaisi sampakampaisi sampacālaisi| atha khau tai bhikkhū saṃviggā laumahaṭṭhajātā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! nivātañ‍ca vata ayañ‍ca migāramātupāsādau gambhīranaimau sunikhātau acalau asampakampī, atha ca pana saṅkampitau sampakampitau sampacālitau’’ti!

atha khau bhagavā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu tumhai, bhikkhavai, saṃviggā laumahaṭṭhajātā aikamantaṃ ṭhitā’’ti? ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ bhantai! nivātañ‍ca vata ayañ‍ca migāramātupāsādau gambhīranaimau sunikhātau acalau asampakampī, atha ca pana saṅkampitau sampakampitau sampacālitau’’ti! ‘‘tumhaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃvaijaitukāmaina mauggal‍lānaina bhikkhunā pādaṅguṭṭhakaina migāramātupāsādau, saṅkampitau sampakampitau sampacālitau| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā, bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

‘‘taina  hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha| catun‍naṃ khau, bhikkhavai, iddhipādānaṃ  bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ‘iti mai vīmaṃsā na ca  atilīnā bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitā bhavissati; na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittā bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati’| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau| imaisañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti…pai॰… imaisañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. uṇṇābhabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

827. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha  khau uṇṇābhau brāhmaṇau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uṇṇābhau brāhmaṇau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kimatthiyaṃ nu khau, bhau ānanda, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘chandappahānatthaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti|

‘‘atthi  pana, bhau ānanda, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitassa chandassa pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, brāhmaṇa, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitassa chandassa pahānāyā’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhau ānanda, maggau katamā paṭipadā aitassa chandassa pahānāyā’’ti? ‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā aitassa chandassa pahānāyā’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ  santai, bhau ānanda, santakaṃ hauti nau asantakaṃ| chandainaiva chandaṃ pajahissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’’| ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya tathā taṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, ahausi tai pubbai chandau ‘ārāmaṃ gamissāmī’ti? tassa tai ārāmagatassa yau taj‍jau chandau sau paṭippassaddhau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’| ‘‘ahausi tai pubbai vīriyaṃ ‘ārāmaṃ gamissāmī’ti? tassa tai ārāmagatassa yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vīriyaṃ taṃ paṭippassaddha’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’| ‘‘ahausi tai pubbai cittaṃ ‘ārāmaṃ gamissāmī’ti? tassa tai ārāmagatassa yaṃ taj‍jaṃ cittaṃ taṃ paṭippassaddha’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’| ‘‘ahausi tai pubbai vīmaṃsā ‘ārāmaṃ gamissāmī’ti? tassa tai ārāmagatassa yā taj‍jā vīmaṃsā sā paṭippassaddhā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yau sau bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau, tassa yau pubbai chandau ahausi arahattappattiyā, arahattappattai arahattai pattai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) yau taj‍jau chandau sau paṭippassaddhau; yaṃ pubbai vīriyaṃ ahausi arahattappattiyā, arahattappattai yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vīriyaṃ taṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ; yaṃ pubbai cittaṃ ahausi arahattappattiyā, arahattappattai yaṃ taj‍jaṃ cittaṃ taṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ; yā pubbai vīmaṃsā ahausi arahattappattiyā, arahattappattai yā taj‍jā vīmaṃsā sā paṭippassaddhā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, iti aivaṃ santai, santakaṃ vā hauti nau asantakaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘addhā, bhau ānanda, aivaṃ santai, santakaṃ hauti  nau asantakaṃ| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau ānanda, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau ānanda! saiyyathāpi, bhau ānanda, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ  vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā ānandaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhau ānanda, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ ānandau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

828. ‘‘yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā mahiddhikā ahaisuṃ mahānubhāvā, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā mahiddhikā bhavissanti mahānubhāvā, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā mahiddhikā mahānubhāvā, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā  mahiddhikā ahaisuṃ mahānubhāvā, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā mahiddhikā bhavissanti mahānubhāvā, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā mahiddhikā mahānubhāvā, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

829. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhausuṃ – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā ahaisuṃ, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau ahaisuṃ; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānā agamaṃsu, saiyyathāpi  ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ akaṃsu, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai abhij‍jamānā (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) agamaṃsu, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamiṃsu, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasiṃsu parāmasiṃsu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) parimaj‍jiṃsu; yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaisuṃ, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā|

‘‘yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhaussanti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā bhavissanti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau bhavissanti; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānā gamissanti, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karissanti, saiyyathāpi  udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gamissanti, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamissanti, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasissanti parimaj‍jissanti; yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattissanti, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā|

‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhaunti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā haunti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau haunti; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānā gacchanti, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karaunti, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchanti, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamanti, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasanti parāmasanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) parimaj‍janti; yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattainti, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattāti|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhausuṃ – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā ahaisuṃ…pai॰…  yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaisuṃ, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā|

‘‘yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhaussanti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā bhavissanti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattissanti, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā|

‘‘yai  hi kaici bhikkhavai aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhaunti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā haunti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattainti, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. bhikkhusuttaṃ

830. ‘‘catun‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā  anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. iddhādidaisanāsuttaṃ

831. ‘‘iddhiṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi iddhipādañ‍ca iddhipādabhāvanañ‍ca  iddhipādabhāvanāgāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ| taṃ suṇātha|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, iddhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhi|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, iddhipādau? yau sau, bhikkhavai, maggau yā paṭipadā iddhilābhāya iddhipaṭilābhāya saṃvattati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhipādau|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi …pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. vibhaṅgasuttaṃ

832. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā’’|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ‘iti mai chandau na  ca atilīnau bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitau bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittau bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittau bhavissati’| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – ‘yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā  tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā’| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ‘iti mai vīmaṃsā na ca atilīnā bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitā bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittā bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati’| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – ‘yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā’| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, atilīnau chandau? yau, bhikkhavai, chandau kausaj‍jasahagatau kausaj‍jasampayuttau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atilīnau chandau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, atippaggahitau chandau? yau, bhikkhavai, chandau uddhac‍casahagatau uddhac‍casampayuttau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atippaggahitau chandau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittau chandau? yau, bhikkhavai, chandau thinamiddhasahagatau thinamiddhasampayuttau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittau chandau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, bahiddhā vikkhittau chandau? yau, bhikkhavai, chandau bahiddhā pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai ārabbha anuvikkhittau anuvisaṭau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bahiddhā vikkhittau chandau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pacchāpuraisañ‍ñā suggahitā hauti sumanasikatā sūpadhāritā suppaṭividdhā pañ‍ñāya| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’nti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau viharati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaihi ākāraihi yaihi liṅgaihi yaihi nimittaihi divā chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, sau taihi ākāraihi taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi rattiṃ chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti; yaihi vā pana ākāraihi yaihi liṅgaihi yaihi nimittaihi rattiṃ chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, sau taihi ākāraihi taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi divā chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā viharati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ālaukasañ‍ñā suggahitā hauti divāsañ‍ñā svādhiṭṭhitā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atilīnaṃ vīriyaṃ? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, vīriyaṃ kausaj‍jasahagataṃ kausaj‍jasampayuttaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atilīnaṃ vīriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atippaggahitaṃ vīriyaṃ? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, vīriyaṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ uddhac‍casampayuttaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atippaggahitaṃ vīriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittaṃ vīriyaṃ? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, vīriyaṃ  thinamiddhasahagataṃ thinamiddhasampayuttaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittaṃ vīriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ vīriyaṃ? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, vīriyaṃ bahiddhā pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai ārabbha anuvikkhittaṃ anuvisaṭaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ vīriyaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ālaukasañ‍ñā suggahitā hauti divāsañ‍ñā svādhiṭṭhitā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atilīnaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ kausaj‍jasahagataṃ kausaj‍jasampayuttaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atilīnaṃ cittaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atippaggahitaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ uddhac‍casampayuttaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atippaggahitaṃ cittaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ thinamiddhasahagataṃ thinamiddhasampayuttaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittaṃ cittaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ bahiddhā pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai ārabbha anuvikkhittaṃ anuvisaṭaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, atilīnā vīmaṃsā? yā, bhikkhavai, vīmaṃsā kausaj‍jasahagatā kausaj‍jasampayuttā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atilīnā vīmaṃsā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, atippaggahitā vīmaṃsā? yā, bhikkhavai, vīmaṃsā uddhac‍casahagatā uddhac‍casampayuttā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atippaggahitā vīmaṃsā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittā vīmaṃsā? yā, bhikkhavai, vīmaṃsā thinamiddhasahagatā thinamiddhasampayuttā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittā vīmaṃsā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, bahiddhā vikkhittā vīmaṃsā? yā, bhikkhavai, vīmaṃsā bahiddhā pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai ārabbha anuvikkhittā anuvisaṭā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bahiddhā  vikkhittā vīmaṃsā…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu, anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi  kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti| aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ  pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

pāsādakampanavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

pubbaṃ mahapphalaṃ chandaṃ, mauggal‍lānañ‍ca uṇṇābhaṃ|

dvai samaṇabrāhmaṇā bhikkhu, daisanā vibhaṅgaina cāti||

3. ayauguḷavaggau

1. maggasuttaṃ

833. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pubbaiva mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau maggau, kā paṭipadā iddhipādabhāvanāyā’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘idha bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – iti mai chandau na ca atilīnau bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitau bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittau bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittau bhavissati| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – iti mai vīmaṃsā na ca atilīnā bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitā bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittā bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā’ – iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘aivaṃ  bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ  bahulīkataisu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti| aivaṃ bhāvitaisu khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu catūsu iddhipādaisu aivaṃ bahulīkataisu, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

(chapi abhiñ‍ñāyau vitthāraitabbā)|

2. ayauguḷasuttaṃ

834. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhijānāti nu khau, bhantai, bhagavā iddhiyā manaumayaina kāyaina brahmalaukaṃ upasaṅkamitā’’ti? ‘‘abhijānāmi khvāhaṃ, ānanda, iddhiyā manaumayaina kāyaina brahmalaukaṃ upasaṅkamitā’’ti| ‘‘abhijānāti pana, bhantai, bhagavā iminā cātumahābhūtikaina kāyaina iddhiyā brahmalaukaṃ upasaṅkamitā’’ti? ‘‘abhijānāmi khvāhaṃ, ānanda, iminā cātumahābhūtikaina cātummahābhūtikaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) kāyaina iddhiyā brahmalaukaṃ upasaṅkamitā’’ti|

‘‘yañ‍ca khau, aumāti, bhantai, bhagavā iddhiyā manaumayaina kāyaina brahmalaukaṃ upasaṅkamituṃ, yañ‍ca  khau abhijānāti, bhantai, bhagavā iminā cātumahābhūtikaina kāyaina iddhiyā brahmalaukaṃ upasaṅkamitā, tayidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau acchariyañ‍caiva abbhutañ‍cā’’ti| ‘‘acchariyā  caiva, ānanda, tathāgatā acchariyadhammasaman‍nāgatā ca, abbhutā caiva, ānanda, tathāgatā abbhutadhammasaman‍nāgatā ca’’|

‘‘yasmiṃ, ānanda, samayai tathāgatau kāyampi cittai samaudahati samādahati (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) cittampi kāyai samaudahati, sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca kāyai auk‍kamitvā viharati; tasmiṃ, ānanda, samayai tathāgatassa kāyau lahutarau caiva hauti mudutarau ca kammaniyatarau ca pabhassaratarau ca|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, ayauguḷau divasaṃ santattau lahutarau caiva hauti mudutarau ca kammaniyatarau ca pabhassaratarau ca; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yasmiṃ samayai tathāgatau kāyampi cittai samaudahati, cittampi kāyai samaudahati, sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca kāyai auk‍kamitvā viharati; tasmiṃ, ānanda, samayai tathāgatassa kāyau lahutarau caiva hauti mudutarau ca kammaniyatarau ca pabhassaratarau ca|

‘‘yasmiṃ, ānanda, samayai tathāgatau kāyampi cittai samaudahati, cittampi kāyai samaudahati, sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca kāyai auk‍kamitvā viharati; tasmiṃ, ānanda, samayai tathāgatassa kāyau appakasirainaiva pathaviyā vaihāsaṃ abbhuggacchati, sau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, tūlapicu vā kappāsapicu vā lahukau vātūpādānau appakasirainaiva pathaviyā vaihāsaṃ abbhuggacchati; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yasmiṃ samayai tathāgatau kāyampi cittai samaudahati, cittampi  kāyai samaudahati, sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca kāyai auk‍kamitvā viharati; tasmiṃ, ānanda, samayai tathāgatassa kāyau appakasirainaiva pathaviyā vaihāsaṃ abbhuggacchati, sau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaitī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. bhikkhusuttaṃ

835. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, iddhipādā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi …pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. suddhikasuttaṃ

836. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, iddhipādā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ  iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamaphalasuttaṃ

837. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, iddhipādā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi …pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā bhikkhunā dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaphalasuttaṃ

838. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, iddhipādā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ  iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi …pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau iddhipādā| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā satta phalā sattānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā|

‘‘katamai satta phalā sattānisaṃsā? diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca paṭigac‍ca (sī॰), paṭihac‍ca (pī॰) añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti; atha maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti; atha pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti, upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā imai satta phalā sattānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamaānandasuttaṃ

839. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘katamā nu khau, bhantai, iddhi, katamau iddhipādau, katamā iddhipādabhāvanā, katamā iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā’’ti? ‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, iddhi’’|

‘‘katamau cānanda, iddhipādau? yau, ānanda, maggau yā paṭipadā iddhilābhāya iddhipaṭilābhāya saṃvattati – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, iddhipādau|

‘‘katamā  cānanda, iddhipādabhāvanā? idhānanda, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ  bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, iddhipādabhāvanā|

‘‘katamā cānanda, iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyaānandasuttaṃ

840. aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘katamā nu khau, ānanda, iddhi, katamau iddhipādau, katamā  iddhipādabhāvanā, katamā iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā…pai॰…|

‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, iddhi|

‘‘katamau cānanda, iddhipādau? yau, ānanda, maggau yā paṭipadā iddhilābhāya iddhipaṭilābhāya saṃvattati – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, iddhipādau|

‘‘katamā cānanda, iddhipādabhāvanā? idhānanda, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, iddhipādabhāvanā|

‘‘katamā cānanda, iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamabhikkhusuttaṃ

841. atha  khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau  tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘katamā nu khau, bhantai, iddhi, katamau iddhipādau, katamā iddhipādabhāvanā, katamā iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī  paṭipadā’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhi|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, iddhipādau? yau, bhikkhavai, maggau, yā paṭipadā iddhilābhāya iddhipaṭilābhāya saṃvattati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhipādau|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyabhikkhusuttaṃ

842. atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘katamā nu khau, bhikkhavai, iddhi, katamau iddhipādau, katamā iddhipādabhāvanā, katamā iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā…pai॰…|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, iddhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhi|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, iddhipādau? yau, bhikkhavai, maggau, yā paṭipadā iddhilābhāya iddhipaṭilābhāya saṃvattati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhipādau|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ  iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, iddhipādabhāvanāgāminī paṭipadā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. mauggal‍lānasuttaṃ

843. tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā…pai॰… ‘‘catun‍naṃ khau, bhikkhavai, iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā  mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau’’|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ‘iti mai chandau na ca atilīnau bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitau bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittau bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittau bhavissati’| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – ‘yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā’| iti  vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ‘iti mai vīmaṃsā na ca atilīnā bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitā bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittā bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati’…pai॰… iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau|

‘‘imaisañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mauggalānau bhikkhu aivaṃ anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti…pai॰… yāva  brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti| imaisañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā  mauggal‍lānau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. tathāgatasuttaṃ

844. tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgatau aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘catun‍naṃ khau, bhikkhavai, iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgatau aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau’’|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ‘iti mai chandau na ca atilīnau bhavissati, na ca atippaggahitau bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittau bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittau bhavissati’| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – ‘yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā’| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti – ‘iti mai vīmaṃsā  na ca atilīnā bhavissati, na ca atipaggahitā bhavissati, na ca ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittā bhavissati, na ca bahiddhā vikkhittā bhavissati’| pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī ca viharati – ‘yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai; yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau; yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā’| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgatau aivaṃmahiddhikau  aivaṃmahānubhāvau|

‘‘imaisañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgatau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti…pai॰…  yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti| imaisañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā tathāgatau āsavānaṃ  khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

(chapi abhiñ‍ñāyau vitthāraitabbā)|

ayauguḷavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

maggau ayauguḷau bhikkhu, suddhikañ‍cāpi dvai phalā|

dvai cānandā duvai bhikkhū, mauggal‍lānau tathāgatauti||

4. gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1-12. gaṅgānadīādisuttadvādasakaṃ

845-856. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau iddhipādai bhāvaintau cattārau iddhipādai bahulīkarauntau  nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau iddhipādai bhāvaintau cattārau iddhipādai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti|

‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau iddhipādai  bhāvaintau cattārau iddhipādai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

dvaitai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

appamādavaggau vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

tathāgataṃ  padaṃ kūṭaṃ, mūlaṃ sārau ca vassikaṃ|

rājā candimasūriyā, vatthaina dasamaṃ padanti||

balakaraṇīyavaggau  vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

balaṃ bījañ‍ca nāgau ca, rukkhau kumbhaina sūkiyā|

ākāsaina ca dvai maighā, nāvā āgantukā nadīti||

aisanāvaggau vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aisanā  vidhā āsavau, bhavau ca dukkhatā tissau|

khilaṃ malañ‍ca nīghau ca, vaidanā taṇhā tasinā cāti||

8. aughavaggau

1-10. aughādisuttadasakaṃ

889-898. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya cattārau iddhipādā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ  iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi  …pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya imai cattārau iddhipādā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

(yathā  maggasaṃyuttaṃ tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

aughavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aughau yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthā anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇā, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyāti||

iddhipādasaṃyuttaṃ sattamaṃ|



8. anuruddhasaṃyuttaṃ

1. rahaugatavaggau

1. paṭhamarahaugatasuttaṃ

899. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā anuruddhau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmatau anuruddhassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci cattārau satipaṭṭhānā viraddhā, viraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci cattārau satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā, āraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmatau anuruddhassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – āyasmatau anuruddhassa sammukhai pāturahausi| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau anuruddha, bhikkhunau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā hauntī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ kāyai samudayadhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattaṃ kāyai vayadhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattaṃ kāyai  samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati  ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| bahiddhā kāyai samudayadhammānupassī viharati, bahiddhā kāyai vayadhammānupassī viharati, bahiddhā kāyai samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ajjhattabahiddhā kāyai samudayadhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā kāyai vayadhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā  kāyai samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘sau  sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati; sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati; sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlai ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati; sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati; sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlañ‍ca paṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyyaṃ satau sampajānau’ti, upaikkhakau tattha viharati satau sampajānau|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ vaidanāsu samudayadhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattaṃ vaidanāsu vayadhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattaṃ vaidanāsu samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| bahiddhā vaidanāsu samudayadhammānupassī viharati, bahiddhā vaidanāsu vayadhammānupassī viharati, bahiddhā vaidanāsu samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ajjhattabahiddhā vaidanāsu samudayadhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vaidanāsu vayadhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vaidanāsu samudayavayadhammānupassī  viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati; sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati; sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlai ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati; sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati; sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlañ‍ca paṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyyaṃ satau sampajānau’ti, upaikkhakau tattha viharati satau sampajānau|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ cittai…pai॰… bahiddhā cittai…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā cittai samudayadhammānupassī  viharati… ajjhattabahiddhā cittai vayadhammānupassī viharati… ajjhattabahiddhā cittai samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati ātāpī…pai॰… abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘sau  sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati…pai॰… upaikkhakau tattha viharati satau sampajānau|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu…pai॰… bahiddhā dhammaisu…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā dhammaisu samudayadhammānupassī viharati… ajjhattabahiddhā dhammaisu vayadhammānupassī viharati… ajjhattabahiddhā dhammaisu samudayavayadhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, paṭikūlasañ‍ñī tattha viharati…pai॰… upaikkhakau tattha viharati satau sampajānau| aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā hauntī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyarahaugatasuttaṃ

900. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmatau anuruddhassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci cattārau satipaṭṭhānā viraddhā, viraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī; yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci cattārau satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā, āraddhau taisaṃ ariyau maggau sammā dukkhakkhayagāmī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmatau anuruddhassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma  balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – āyasmatau anuruddhassa sammukhai pāturahausi|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau anuruddha, bhikkhunau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā hauntī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| bahiddhā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| bahiddhā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī  viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ajjhattabahiddhā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ cittai…pai॰… bahiddhā cittai…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā cittai cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu…pai॰… bahiddhā dhammaisu…pai॰… ajjhattabahiddhā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aittāvatā khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau  cattārau satipaṭṭhānā āraddhā hauntī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sutanusuttaṃ

901. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā anuruddhau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati sutanutīrai| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaināyasmā anuruddhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā anuruddhaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘katamaisaṃ āyasmā anuruddhau dhammānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’ti?

‘‘catun‍naṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idhāhaṃ, āvusau, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰…  cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – imaisaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau| imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā hīnaṃ dhammaṃ hīnatau abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, majjhimaṃ dhammaṃ majjhimatau abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, paṇītaṃ dhammaṃ paṇītatau abbhañ‍ñāsi’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamakaṇḍakīsuttaṃ

902. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca anuruddhau āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau sākaitai viharanti kaṇḍakīvanai| atha  khau āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitā yaināyasmā anuruddhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā anuruddhaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saikhaināvusau anuruddha, bhikkhunā katamai dhammā upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā’’ti?

‘‘saikhaināvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā cattārau satipaṭṭhānā upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā| katamai cattārau? idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu  …pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – saikhaināvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyakaṇḍakīsuttaṃ

903. sākaitanidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘asaikhaināvusau anuruddha, bhikkhunā katamai dhammā upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā’’ti? ‘‘asaikhaināvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā cattārau satipaṭṭhānā upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā| katamai cattārau? idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – asaikhaināvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. tatiyakaṇḍakīsuttaṃ

904. sākaitanidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katamaisaṃ āyasmā anuruddhau dhammānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’ti? ‘‘catun‍naṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idhāhaṃ, āvusau, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – imaisaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau| imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā sahassaṃ laukaṃ abhijānāmī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. taṇhakkhayasuttaṃ

905. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau āyasmā anuruddhau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau anuruddhassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā anuruddhau aitadavauca –

‘‘cattāraumai, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā taṇhakkhayāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati…pai॰…  vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – imai khau, āvusau, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā taṇhakkhayāya saṃvattantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. salaḷāgārasuttaṃ

906. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā anuruddhau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati salaḷāgārai| tatra khau āyasmā anuruddhau bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā| atha mahājanakāyau āgacchaiyya kuddālapiṭakaṃ kuddālapiṭakaṃ (bahūsu) ādāya – ‘mayaṃ imaṃ gaṅgānadiṃ pacchānin‍naṃ karissāma pacchāpauṇaṃ pacchāpabbhāra’nti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñathāvusau, api nu sau mahājanakāyau gaṅgānadiṃ pacchānin‍naṃ karaiyya pacchāpauṇaṃ pacchāpabbhāra’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘gaṅgā, āvusau, nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā| sā na sukarā pacchānin‍naṃ kātuṃ pacchāpauṇaṃ pacchāpabbhāraṃ| yāvadaiva ca pana sau mahājanakāyau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva  khau, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaintaṃ cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bahulīkarauntaṃ rājānau vā rājamahāmattā vā mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā  bhaugaihi abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraiyyuṃ – ‘aihambhau purisa, kiṃ tai imai kāsāvā anudahanti? kiṃ muṇḍau kapālamanusañ‍carasi? aihi hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jassu puñ‍ñāni ca karauhī’’’ti|

‘‘sau vata, āvusau, bhikkhu cattārau satipaṭṭhānai  bhāvaintau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bahulīkarauntau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? yañhi taṃ, āvusau, cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ vivaikanin‍naṃ vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāraṃ taṃ vata hīnāyāvattissatīti  – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| kathañ‍cāvusau, bhikkhu cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaiti, cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bahulīkarautīti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati…pai॰… vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bhāvaiti, cattārau satipaṭṭhānai bahulīkarautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ambapālivanasuttaṃ

907. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca anuruddhau āyasmā ca sāriputtau vaisāliyaṃ viharanti ambapālivanai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘vippasan‍nāni khau tai, āvusau anuruddha, indriyāni, parisuddhau mukhavaṇṇau pariyaudātau| katamaināyasmā anuruddhau vihāraina aitarahi bahulaṃ viharatī’’ti? ‘‘catūsu khvāhaṃ, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau aitarahi bahulaṃ viharāmi| katamaisu catūsu? idhāhaṃ, āvusau, kāyai kāyānupassī  viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī  sampajānau  satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – imaisu khvāhaṃ, āvusau, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau aitarahi bahulaṃ viharāmi| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau, sau imaisu catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittau bahulaṃ viharatī’’ti|

‘‘lābhā vata nau, āvusau, suladdhaṃ vata nau, āvusau! yai mayaṃ āyasmatau anuruddhassa sammukhāva assumha āsabhiṃ vācaṃ bhāsamānassā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. bāḷhagilānasuttaṃ

908. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā anuruddhau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati andhavanasmiṃ ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaināyasmā anuruddhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘katamaināyasmatau anuruddhassa vihāraina viharatau uppan‍nā sārīrikā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhantī’’ti? ‘‘catūsu khau mai, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittassa viharatau uppan‍nā sārīrikā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| katamaisu catūsu? idhāhaṃ, āvusau, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāmi…pai॰… vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – imaisu  khau mai, āvusau, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittassa viharatau uppan‍nā sārīrikā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

rahaugatavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

rahaugataina  dvai vuttā, sutanu kaṇḍakī tayau|

taṇhakkhayasalaḷāgāraṃ, ambapāli ca gilānanti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. kappasahassasuttaṃ

909. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā anuruddhau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaināyasmā anuruddhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā  anuruddhaina saddhiṃ…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘katamaisaṃ āyasmā anuruddhau dhammānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’ti? ‘‘catun‍naṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idhāhaṃ, āvusau, kāyai kāyānupassī viharāmi…pai॰… vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – imaisaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau| imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā kappasahassaṃ anussarāmī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. iddhividhasuttaṃ

910. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhaumi – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā haumi…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaimī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dibbasautasuttaṃ

911. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca  panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāmi dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai cā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. caitaupariyasuttaṃ

912. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi  – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ  citta’nti pajānāmi…pai॰… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāmī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. ṭhānasuttaṃ

913. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānatau aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāmī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kammasamādānasuttaṃ

914. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ ṭhānasau haitusau vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāmī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sabbatthagāminisuttaṃ

915. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā sabbatthagāminippaṭipadaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāmī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. nānādhātusuttaṃ

916. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā anaikadhātunānādhātulaukaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāmī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. nānādhimuttisuttaṃ

917. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca  panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikataṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāmī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. indriyaparaupariyattasuttaṃ

918. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca  panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ indriyaparaupariyattaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāmī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. jhānādisuttaṃ

919. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca  panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāmī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. pubbainivāsasuttaṃ

920. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. dibbacakkhusuttaṃ

921. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai…pai॰… iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmī’’ti| tairasamaṃ|

14. āsavakkhayasuttaṃ

922. ‘‘imaisañ‍ca panāhaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhai  dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’’ti| cuddasamaṃ|

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

mahābhiñ‍ñaṃ  iddhi dibbaṃ, caitaupariyaṃ ṭhānaṃ kammaṃ|

sabbatthadhātudhimutti, indriyaṃ jhānaṃ tissau vij‍jāti||

anuruddhasaṃyuttaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|



9. jhānasaṃyuttaṃ

1. gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau

1-12. jhānādisuttadvādasakaṃ
