923-934. sāvatthinidānaṃ  ‘‘cattārau mai, bhikkhavai, jhānā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau jhānā’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṅgā nadī pācīnanin‍nā pācīnapauṇā pācīnapabbhārā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau  jhānai bhāvaintau cattārau jhānai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau jhānai bhāvaintau cattārau jhānai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰…  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau jhānai bhāvaintau cattārau jhānai bahulīkarauntau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

gaṅgāpaiyyālavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cha  pācīnatau nin‍nā, cha nin‍nā ca samuddatau|

dvaitai cha dvādasa haunti, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

appamādavaggau vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

tathāgataṃ padaṃ kūṭaṃ, mūlaṃ sārau ca vassikaṃ|

rājā candimasūriyā, vatthaina dasamaṃ padanti||

balakaraṇīyavaggau vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

balaṃ  bījañ‍ca nāgau ca, rukkhau kumbhaina sūkiyā|

ākāsaina ca dvai maighā, nāvā āgantukā nadīti||

aisanāvaggau vitthāraitabbau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aisanā vidhā āsavau, bhavau ca dukkhatā tissau|

khilaṃ malañ‍ca nīghau ca, vaidanā taṇhā tasinā cāti||

5. aughavaggau

1-10. aughādisuttaṃ

967-976. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni  saṃyaujanāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya cattārau jhānā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰…  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya pahānāya imai cattārau jhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti vitthāraitabbaṃ| dasamaṃ| (yathā maggasaṃyuttaṃ tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

aughavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aughau yaugau upādānaṃ, ganthā anusayaina ca|

kāmaguṇā nīvaraṇā, khandhā auruddhambhāgiyāti||

jhānasaṃyuttaṃ navamaṃ|



10. ānāpānasaṃyuttaṃ

1. aikadhammavaggau

1. aikadhammasuttaṃ

977. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘aikadhammau, bhikkhavai, bhāvitau bahulīkatau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau| katamau aikadhammau? ānāpānassati ānāpānasati (sī॰ pī॰)| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva satau (bahūsu) tatiyapārājikaipi passasati| dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā passasantau ‘rassaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; ‘sabbakāyappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘sabbakāyappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘pītippaṭisaṃvaidī  assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘pītippaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘cittasaṅkhārappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘cittasaṅkhārappaṭisaṃvaidī  passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘cittappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘cittappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘vimaucayaṃ  cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘anic‍cānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘anic‍cānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘virāgānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘virāgānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘niraudhānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘niraudhānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. baujjhaṅgasuttaṃ

978. ‘‘ānāpānassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ānāpānassatisahagataṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ, ānāpānassatisahagataṃ dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… ānāpānassatisahagataṃ  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ  bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. suddhikasuttaṃ

979. ‘‘ānāpānassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva passasati…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamaphalasuttaṃ

980. ‘‘ānāpānassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva passasati…pai॰…  ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| aivaṃ  bhāvitāya khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatiyā aivaṃ bahulīkatāya dvin‍naṃ phalānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ phalaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – diṭṭhaiva dhammai añ‍ñā, sati vā upādisaisai anāgāmitā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyaphalasuttaṃ

981. ‘‘ānāpānassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā| kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva passasati…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitāya khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatiyā aivaṃ bahulīkatāya satta phalā sattānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| katamai satta phalā sattānisaṃsā? diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti; nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti| atha maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti; nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai paṭikac‍ca añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti, nau cai maraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiti| atha pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti… upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti… asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti… sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti… uddhaṃsautau  hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī – aivaṃ bhāvitāya  khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatiyā aivaṃ bahulīkatāya imai satta phalā sattānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ariṭṭhasuttaṃ

982. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘bhāvaitha nau tumhai bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati’’nti? aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā ariṭṭhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi ānāpānassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā  kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, ariṭṭha, bhāvaisi ānāpānassati’’nti? ‘‘atītaisu mai, bhantai, kāmaisu kāmacchandau pahīnau, anāgataisu mai kāmaisu kāmacchandau vigatau, ajjhattabahiddhā ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) ca mai dhammaisu paṭighasañ‍ñā suppaṭivinītā| sau sauhaṃ (?) satauva assasissāmi, satauva passasissāmi| aivaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi ānāpānassati’’nti|

‘‘‘atthaisā, ariṭṭha, ānāpānassati, naisā natthī’ti vadāmi| api ca, ariṭṭha, yathā ānāpānassati vitthāraina paripuṇṇā hauti taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ariṭṭhau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘kathañ‍ca, ariṭṭha, ānāpānassati vitthāraina paripuṇṇā hauti? idha, ariṭṭha, bhikkhu  arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva passasati| dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ khau, ariṭṭha, ānāpānassati vitthāraina paripuṇṇā hautī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mahākappinasuttaṃ

983. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahākappinau bhagavatau avidūrai  nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| addasā khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahākappinaṃ avidūrai nisin‍naṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘passatha  nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitassa bhikkhunau kāyassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā phanditattaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘yadāpi mayaṃ, bhantai, taṃ āyasmantaṃ passāma saṅghamajjhai vā nisin‍naṃ aikaṃ vā rahau nisin‍naṃ, tadāpi  mayaṃ tassa āyasmatau na passāma kāyassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā phanditattaṃ vā’’ti|

‘‘yassa, bhikkhavai, samādhissa bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā naiva kāyassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā hauti phanditattaṃ vā, na cittassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā hauti phanditattaṃ vā, tassa sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samādhissa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| katamassa  ca, bhikkhavai, samādhissa bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā naiva kāyassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā hauti phanditattaṃ vā, na cittassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā hauti phanditattaṃ vā?

‘‘ānāpānassatisamādhissa, bhikkhavai, bhāvitattā bahulīkatattā naiva kāyassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā hauti phanditattaṃ vā, na cittassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā hauti phanditattaṃ vā| kathaṃ bhāvitai ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhimhi kathaṃ bahulīkatai naiva kāyassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā hauti phanditattaṃ vā, na cittassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā hauti phanditattaṃ vā?

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva passasati…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ bhāvitai ca khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhimhi aivaṃ bahulīkatai naiva kāyassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā hauti phanditattaṃ vā, na cittassa iñ‍jitattaṃ vā hauti phanditattaṃ vā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. padīpaupamasuttaṃ

984. ‘‘ānāpānassatisamādhi, bhikkhavai, bhāvitau bahulīkatau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau| kathaṃ bhāvitau ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi kathaṃ bahulīkatau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau?

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ  kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ  satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva passasati| dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ bhāvitau khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi aivaṃ bahulīkatau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau|

‘‘ahampi sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhau baudhisattauva samānau iminā vihāraina bahulaṃ viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, iminā vihāraina bahulaṃ viharatau naiva kāyau kilamati na cakkhūni; anupādāya ca mai āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍ci|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘naiva mai kāyau kilamaiyya na cakkhūni, anupādāya ca mai āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍caiyyā’ti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘yai mai gaihasitā sarasaṅkappā tai pahīyaiyyu’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘appaṭikūlai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘paṭikūlai appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī  viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘paṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlai ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘paṭikūlai  ca appaṭikūlai ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘appaṭikūlañ‍ca paṭikūlañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā upaikkhakau viharaiyyaṃ satau sampajānau’ti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘vitak‍kavicārānaṃ  vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘pītiyā ca  virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharaiyyaṃ satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiyyaṃ, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārīti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā  pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā anantau ākāsauti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanti  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma natthi kiñ‍cīti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu caipi ākaṅkhaiyya – ‘sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, ayamaiva ānāpānassatisamādhi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitai khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhimhi aivaṃ bahulīkatai, sukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sā ‘anic‍cā’ti pajānāti, ‘anajjhausitā’ti pajānāti, ‘anabhinanditā’ti pajānāti; dukkhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, ‘sā anic‍cā’ti pajānāti, ‘anajjhausitā’ti pajānāti, ‘anabhinanditā’ti  pajānāti; adukkhamasukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, ‘sā anic‍cā’ti pajānāti, ‘anajjhausitā’ti pajānāti, ‘anabhinanditā’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘sukhaṃ sau sukhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.364 aṭṭhakathāṭīkā aulaukaitabbā cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidayati; dukkhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidayati; adukkhamasukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidayati| sau kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti, jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti, ‘kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissantī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tailañ‍ca paṭic‍ca, vaṭṭiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tailappadīpau  jhāyaiyya, tassaiva tailassa ca vaṭṭiyā ca pariyādānā anāhārau nibbāyaiyya; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyapariyantikaṃ  vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti, jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti, ‘kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissantī’ti pajānātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vaisālīsuttaṃ

985. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ anaikapariyāyaina asubhakathaṃ kathaiti, asubhāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, asubhabhāvanāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati|

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aḍḍhamāsaṃ paṭisal‍līyituṃ| nāmhi kainaci upasaṅkamitabbau, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakainā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissutvā nāssudha kauci bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamati, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina|

atha  khau tai bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavā anaikapariyāyaina asubhakathaṃ kathaiti, asubhāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, asubhabhāvanāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsatī’’ti anaikākāravaukāraṃ asubhabhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti| tai iminā kāyaina aṭṭīyamānā aṭṭiyamānā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) harāyamānā jigucchamānā satthahārakaṃ pariyaisanti| dasapi bhikkhū aikāhaina satthaṃ āharanti, vīsampi…pai॰… tiṃsampi bhikkhū aikāhaina satthaṃ āharanti|

atha  khau bhagavā tassa aḍḍhamāsassa ac‍cayaina paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, ānanda, tanubhūtau viya bhikkhusaṅghau’’ti? ‘‘tathā hi pana, bhantai, ‘bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ anaikapariyāyaina asubhakathaṃ kathaiti, asubhāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, asubhabhāvanāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsatī’ti anaikākāravaukāraṃ asubhabhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti| tai iminā kāyaina aṭṭīyamānā harāyamānā jigucchamānā satthahārakaṃ pariyaisanti| dasapi bhikkhū aikāhaina satthaṃ āharanti, vīsampi bhikkhū… tiṃsampi bhikkhū aikāhaina satthaṃ āharanti| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā añ‍ñaṃ pariyāyaṃ ācikkhatu yathāyaṃ bhikkhusaṅghau añ‍ñāya saṇṭhahaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘tainahānanda, yāvatikā bhikkhū vaisāliṃ upanissāya viharanti tai sabbai upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nipātaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā yāvatikā bhikkhū vaisāliṃ upanissāya viharanti tai sabbai upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nipātaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘san‍nipatitau san‍nipātitau (sī॰), bhantai, bhikkhusaṅghau| yassa dāni, bhantai, bhagavā kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā yaina upaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘ayampi khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau santau caiva paṇītau ca asaicanakau ca sukhau ca vihārau uppan‍nuppan‍nai ca pāpakai akusalai dhammai ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai ūhataṃ rajaujal‍laṃ, tamainaṃ mahāakālamaighau ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau santau caiva paṇītau ca  asaicanakau ca sukhau ca  vihārau uppan‍nuppan‍nai ca pāpakai akusalai dhammai ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti| kathaṃ bhāvitau ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi kathaṃ bahulīkatau santau caiva paṇītau ca asaicanakau ca sukhau ca vihārau uppan‍nuppan‍nai ca pāpakai akusalai dhammai ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva passasati…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ bhāvitau khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi aivaṃ bahulīkatau santau caiva paṇītau ca asaicanakau ca  sukhau ca vihārau uppan‍nuppan‍nai ca pāpakai akusalai dhammai ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaitī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. kimilasuttaṃ

986. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kimilāyaṃ kimbilāyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) viharati vaiḷuvanai| tatra khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ kimilaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitau nu khau, kimila, ānāpānassatisamādhi kathaṃ bahulīkatau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau’’ti?

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā kimilau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau bhagavā…pai॰… tatiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ kimilaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitau nu khau, kimila, ānāpānassatisamādhi kathaṃ bahulīkatau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau’’ti? tatiyampi khau āyasmā kimilau tuṇhī ahausi|

aivaṃ  vuttai āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau; aitassa, sugata, kālau! yaṃ bhagavā ānāpānassatisamādhiṃ bhāsaiyya| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

‘‘tainahānanda, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau  āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitau ca, ānanda, ānāpānassatisamādhi kathaṃ bahulīkatau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau? idhānanda, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva passasati…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī  passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| aivaṃ bhāvitau khau, ānanda, ānāpānassatisamādhi aivaṃ bahulīkatau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau’’|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā passasantau ‘rassaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; ‘sabbakāyappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘sabbakāyappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – kāyai kāyānupassī, ānanda, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? kāyañ‍ñatarāhaṃ, ānanda, aitaṃ vadāmi yadidaṃ – assāsapassāsaṃ| tasmātihānanda, kāyai kāyānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu ‘pītippaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘pītippaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘cittasaṅkhārappaṭisaṃvaidī  assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘cittasaṅkhārappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ  cittasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī, ānanda, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau  satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? vaidanāñ‍ñatarāhaṃ, ānanda, aitaṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ – assāsapassāsānaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.145 sādhukaṃ manasikāraṃ| tasmātihānanda, vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ  samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu ‘cittappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘cittappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… samādahaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – cittai cittānupassī, ānanda, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? nāhaṃ, ānanda, muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa ānāpānassatisamādhibhāvanaṃ vadāmi| tasmātihānanda, cittai cittānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu ‘anic‍cānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati…pai॰… virāgānupassī…pai॰… niraudhānupassī…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – dhammaisu  dhammānupassī, ānanda, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| sau yaṃ taṃ hauti abhijjhādaumanassānaṃ pahānaṃ taṃ pañ‍ñāya disvā sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti| tasmātihānanda, dhammaisu dhammānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, catumahāpathai cātummahāpathai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) mahāpaṃsupuñ‍jau| puratthimāya caipi disāyaṃ āgacchaiyya sakaṭaṃ vā rathau vā, upahanataiva taṃ paṃsupuñ‍jaṃ; pacchimāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya…pai॰… uttarāya caipi disāya…pai॰… dakkhiṇāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya sakaṭaṃ vā rathau vā, upahanataiva taṃ paṃsupuñ‍jaṃ| aivamaiva khau, ānanda, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharantaupi upahanataiva  pāpakai akusalai dhammai; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharantaupi upahanataiva pāpakai akusalai dhammai’’ti| dasamaṃ|

aikadhammavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aikadhammau  ca baujjhaṅgau, suddhikañ‍ca duvai phalā|

ariṭṭhau kappinau dīpau, vaisālī kimilaina cāti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. icchānaṅgalasuttaṃ

987. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhikkhavai, taimāsaṃ paṭisal‍līyituṃ| nāmhi kainaci upasaṅkamitabbau, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakainā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissutvā nāssudha kauci bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamati, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina|

atha  khau bhagavā tassa taimāsassa ac‍cayaina paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘sacai khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamaināvusau, vihāraina samaṇau gautamau vassāvāsaṃ bahulaṃ vihāsī’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘ānāpānassatisamādhinā khau, āvusau, bhagavā vassāvāsaṃ bahulaṃ vihāsī’ti| idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, satau assasāmi, satau passasāmi| dīghaṃ assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāmi, dīghaṃ passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāmi; rassaṃ assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāmi, rassaṃ passasantau ‘rassaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāmi; ‘sabbakāyappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti pajānāmi…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti pajānāmi, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti  pajānāmi’’|

‘‘yañhi  taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘ariyavihārau’ itipi, ‘brahmavihārau’  itipi, ‘tathāgatavihārau’ itipi| ānāpānassatisamādhiṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘ariyavihārau’ itipi, ‘brahmavihārau’ itipi, ‘tathāgatavihārau’ itipi| yai tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū saikhā appattamānasā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānā viharanti taisaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattati| yai ca khau tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā taisaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya caiva saṃvattati satisampajañ‍ñāya ca|

‘‘yañhi taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘ariyavihārau’ itipi, ‘brahmavihārau’ itipi, ‘tathāgatavihārau’ itipi| ānāpānassatisamādhiṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘ariyavihārau’ itipi, ‘brahmavihārau’ itipi, ‘tathāgatavihārau’ itipī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. kaṅkhaiyyasuttaṃ

988. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā laumasakaṃbhiyau laumasavaṅgisau (sī॰ pī॰) sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaināyasmā laumasakaṃbhiyau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ laumasakaṃbhiyaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau āyasmantaṃ laumasakaṃbhiyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sau aiva nu khau, bhantai, saikhau  vihārau sau tathāgatavihārau, udāhu añ‍ñauva añ‍ñau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) saikhau vihārau añ‍ñau tathāgatavihārau’’ti?

‘‘na khau, āvusau mahānāma, svaiva saikhau vihārau, sau tathāgatavihārau| añ‍ñau khau, āvusau mahānāma, saikhau vihārau, añ‍ñau tathāgatavihārau| yai tai, āvusau mahānāma, bhikkhū saikhā appattamānasā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānā viharanti, tai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya viharanti| katamai pañ‍ca? kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ pahāya viharanti, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ…pai॰… thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ…pai॰… uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ…pai॰… vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ pahāya viharanti|

‘‘yaipi  tai, āvusau mahānāma, bhikkhū saikhā appattamānasā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānā viharanti, tai imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya viharanti|

‘‘yai ca khau tai, āvusau mahānāma, bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā, taisaṃ pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā anabhāvakatā (sī॰ pī॰) āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| katamai pañ‍ca? kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ pahīnaṃ ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ; byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ pahīnaṃ…pai॰… thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ…pai॰… uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ…pai॰… vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ pahīnaṃ ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ  anuppādadhammaṃ|

‘‘yai tai, āvusau mahānāma, bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau  katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā, taisaṃ imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tadamināpaitaṃ, āvusau mahānāma, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā – añ‍ñauva saikhau vihārau, añ‍ñau tathāgatavihārau|

‘‘aikamidaṃ, āvusau mahānāma, samayaṃ bhagavā icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| tatra khau, āvusau mahānāma, bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘icchāmahaṃ, bhikkhavai, taimāsaṃ paṭisal‍līyituṃ| nāmhi kainaci upasaṅkamitabbau, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakainā’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhantai’ti khau, āvusau mahānāma, tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissutvā nāssudha kauci bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamati, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina|

‘‘atha khau, āvusau, bhagavā tassa taimāsassa ac‍cayaina paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘sacai khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – katamaināvusau, vihāraina samaṇau gautamau vassāvāsaṃ bahulaṃ vihāsīti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ  añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ānāpānassatisamādhinā khau, āvusau, bhagavā vassāvāsaṃ bahulaṃ vihāsīti| idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, satau assasāmi, satau passasāmi| dīghaṃ assasantau dīghaṃ assasāmīti pajānāmi, dīghaṃ passasantau dīghaṃ passasāmīti pajānāmi…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassī  assasissāmīti pajānāmi, paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti pajānāmi’’|

‘‘yañhi taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ariyavihārau itipi, brahmavihārau itipi, tathāgatavihārau itipi| ānāpānassatisamādhiṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ariyavihārau itipi, brahmavihārau itipi, tathāgatavihārau itipi|

‘‘yai tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū saikhā appattamānasā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānā viharanti, taisaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattati|

‘‘yai ca khau tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā, taisaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau diṭṭhaiva dhammai sukhavihārāya caiva saṃvattati satisampajañ‍ñāya ca|

‘‘yañhi taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ariyavihārau itipi, brahmavihārau itipi, tathāgatavihārau itipi| ānāpānassatisamādhiṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ariyavihārau itipi, brahmavihārau itipi, tathāgatavihārau itipī’’ti| ‘‘iminā khau aitaṃ, āvusau mahānāma, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ, yathā – añ‍ñauva saikhau vihārau, añ‍ñau tathāgatavihārau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamaānandasuttaṃ

989. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi  nu khau, bhantai, aikadhammau aikau dhammau (sī॰) bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau  dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūraintī’’ti?

‘‘atthi khau, ānanda, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūraintī’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, aikadhammau aikau dhammau (sī॰) bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūraintī’’ti? ‘‘ānāpānassatisamādhi khau, ānanda, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūrainti’’|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitau, ānanda, ānāpānassatisamādhi kathaṃ bahulīkatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti? idhānanda, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva passasati| dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti  pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati’’| ‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; rassaṃ vā…pai॰… ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – kāyai kāyānupassī, ānanda, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? kāyañ‍ñatarāhaṃ, ānanda, aitaṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ – assāsapassāsaṃ| tasmātihānanda, kāyai kāyānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ’’|

‘‘yasmiṃ  samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu ‘pītippaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati  …pai॰… sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī…pai॰… cittasaṅkhārappaṭisaṃvaidī…pai॰… ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī, ānanda, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? vaidanāñ‍ñatarāhaṃ, ānanda, aitaṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ – assāsapassāsānaṃ sādhukaṃ manasikāraṃ| tasmātihānanda, vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau  satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu ‘cittappaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘cittappaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… samādahaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – cittai cittānupassī, ānanda, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? nāhaṃ, ānanda, muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa ānāpānassatisamādhibhāvanaṃ vadāmi| tasmātihānanda, cittai cittānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu anic‍cānupassī…pai॰… virāgānupassī…pai॰… niraudhānupassī…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – dhammaisu dhammānupassī, ānanda, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| sau yaṃ taṃ hauti abhijjhādaumanassānaṃ pahānaṃ taṃ pañ‍ñāya disvā sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā  hauti| tasmātihānanda, dhammaisu dhammānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitau khau, ānanda, ānāpānassatisamādhi aivaṃ bahulīkatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti|

‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitā cānanda, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā kathaṃ bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti? yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī  viharati – upaṭṭhitāssa upaṭṭhitassati (pī॰ ka॰) tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau tasmiṃ samayai ānanda bhikkhunau (pī॰ ka॰), tasmiṃ samayai (ma॰ ni॰ 3.149) sati hauti asammuṭṭhā| yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhunau upaṭṭhitā sati hauti asammuṭṭhā – satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘sau tathā satau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicarati parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jati| yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu tathā satau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicarati parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jati – dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘tassa taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicaratau parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ| yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhunau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicaratau parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ  hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ – vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai  bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti nirāmisā| yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhunau āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti nirāmisā – pītisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, pītisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati, cittampi passambhati| yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhunau pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati, cittampi passambhati – passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘passaddhakāyassa  sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhunau passaddhakāyassa sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati – samādhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘sau tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti| yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti – upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati – upaṭṭhitāssa tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau sati hauti asammuṭṭhā| yasmiṃ  samayai, ānanda, bhikkhunau upaṭṭhitā sati hauti asammuṭṭhā – satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| (yathā paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

‘‘sau tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti| yasmiṃ samayai, ānanda, bhikkhu tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti – upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, ānanda, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā aivaṃ bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā, ānanda, satta baujjhaṅgā kathaṃ bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūrainti? idhānanda, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti  …pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ  virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, ānanda, satta baujjhaṅgā aivaṃ bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyaānandasuttaṃ

990. atha  khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, ānanda, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā, satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūraintī’’ti| bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘atthānanda, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai  paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūrainti|

‘‘katamau cānanda, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūrainti? ānāpānassatisamādhi, ānanda, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintī’’ti| ‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitau cānanda, ānāpānassatisamādhi, kathaṃ bahulīkatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti? idhānanda, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā…pai॰… aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, ānanda, satta baujjhaṅgā aivaṃ bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamabhikkhusuttaṃ

991. atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhantai, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā  bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūraintī’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, bhikkhavai, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai  paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūraintī’’ti|

‘‘katamau  pana, bhantai, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūraintī’’ti? ‘‘ānāpānassatisamādhi khau, bhikkhavai, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintī’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitau ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi kathaṃ bahulīkatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā…pai॰… aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā aivaṃ bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyabhikkhusuttaṃ

992. atha  khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhikkhavai, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūraintī’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūrainti’’|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau dhammai paripūraiti, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhammai paripūrainti, satta dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dvai dhammai paripūrainti? ānāpānassatisamādhi, bhikkhavai, aikadhammau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cattārau  satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti, satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintīti|

‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitau ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi kathaṃ bahulīkatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai  paripūraiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva passasati…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati’’|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti…pai॰… sabbakāyappaṭisaṃvaidī…pai॰… ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – kāyai kāyānupassī, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? kāyañ‍ñatarāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ – assāsapassāsaṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, kāyai kāyānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pītippaṭisaṃvaidī…pai॰… sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī…pai॰… cittasaṅkhārappaṭisaṃvaidī…pai॰… ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? vaidanāñ‍ñatarāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ – assāsapassāsānaṃ sādhukaṃ manasikāraṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cittappaṭisaṃvaidī…pai॰… abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… ‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘samādahaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti  sikkhati; ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – cittai cittānupassī, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa ānāpānassatisamādhibhāvanaṃ vadāmi| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, cittai cittānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anic‍cānupassī…pai॰… virāgānupassī…pai॰… niraudhānupassī…pai॰… ‘paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati – dhammaisu dhammānupassī, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| sau yaṃ taṃ hauti abhijjhādaumanassānaṃ pahānaṃ taṃ pañ‍ñāya disvā sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu dhammānupassī bhikkhu tasmiṃ samayai viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

‘‘aivaṃ bhāvitau khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi aivaṃ bahulīkatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānai paripūraiti|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā kathaṃ bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti? yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati – upaṭṭhitāssa tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau sati hauti asammuṭṭhā| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau upaṭṭhitā  sati hauti asammuṭṭhā – satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘sau tathā satau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicarati parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jati| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathā satau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicarati parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jati – dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘tassa  taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicaratau parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ  asal‍līnaṃ| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicaratau parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ – vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti nirāmisā| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti nirāmisā – pītisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, pītisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati, cittampi passambhati| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati, cittampi passambhati – passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘passaddhakāyassa sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau passaddhakāyassa sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati – samādhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘sau tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti – upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati|

‘‘yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati – upaṭṭhitāssa tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau sati hauti  asammuṭṭhā| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau upaṭṭhitā sati hauti asammuṭṭhā – satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau  āraddhau hauti, satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti – satisambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati…pai॰…|

sau tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti| yasmiṃ samayai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāsamāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti – upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau āraddhau hauti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu bhāvaiti, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhunau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā aivaṃ bahulīkatā satta baujjhaṅgai paripūrainti|

‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā kathaṃ bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūrainti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ; dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, satta baujjhaṅgā aivaṃ bahulīkatā vij‍jāvimuttiṃ paripūraintī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. saṃyaujanappahānasuttaṃ

993. ānāpānassatisamādhi, bhikkhavai, bhāvitau bahulīkatau saṃyaujanappahānāya  saṃvattati…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. anusayasamugghātasuttaṃ

994. …anusayasamugghātāya saṃvattati…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. addhānapariñ‍ñāsuttaṃ

995. …addhānapariñ‍ñāya saṃvattati…| navamaṃ|

10. āsavakkhayasuttaṃ

996. āsavānaṃ  khayāya saṃvattati| kathaṃ bhāvitau ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi kathaṃ bahulīkatau saṃyaujanappahānāya saṃvattati… anusayasamugghātāya saṃvattati… addhānapariñ‍ñāya saṃvattati… āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā…pai॰ … paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmīti sikkhati, paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmīti sikkhati| aivaṃ bhāvitau khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi aivaṃ bahulīkatau saṃyaujanappahānāya saṃvattati…pai॰… anusayasamugghātāya saṃvattati…pai॰… addhānapariñ‍ñāya saṃvattati…pai॰… āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattatīti| dasamaṃ|

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

icchānaṅgalaṃ  kaṅkhaiyyaṃ, ānandā aparai duvai|

bhikkhū saṃyaujanānusayā, addhānaṃ āsavakkhayanti||

ānāpānasaṃyuttaṃ dasamaṃ|



11. sautāpattisaṃyuttaṃ

1. vaiḷudvāravaggau

1. cak‍kavattirājasuttaṃ

997. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī cak‍kavatti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) catun‍naṃ dīpānaṃ issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ sahabyataṃ, sau tattha nandanai vanai accharāsaṅghaparivutau dibbaihi ca pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti, sau catūhi dhammaihi asaman‍nāgatau, atha khau sau aparimuttauva aparimuttau ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) nirayā aparimuttau tiracchānayauniyā aparimuttau paittivisayā aparimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā| kiñ‍cāpi, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau piṇḍiyālaupaina yāpaiti, nantakāni ca dhāraiti, sau catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau, atha khau sau parimuttau parimuttau ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā’’|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ – cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha  purisapuggalā, aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi  saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasatthaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti| yau ca, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ dīpānaṃ paṭilābhau, yau catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhau catun‍naṃ dīpānaṃ paṭilābhau catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhassa kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasi’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. brahmacariyaugadhasuttaṃ

998. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi  saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna vatvā (sī॰ pī॰) aivamīdisaisu ṭhānaisu sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘yaisaṃ saddhā ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādau dhammadassanaṃ|

tai vai kālaina pac‍cainti, brahmacariyaugadhaṃ sukha’’nti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. dīghāvuupāsakasuttaṃ

999. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina dīghāvu upāsakau ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau dīghāvu upāsakau  pitaraṃ jautikaṃ gahapatiṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, gahapati, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vanda – ‘dīghāvu, bhantai, upāsakau ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, bhagavā yaina dīghāvussa upāsakassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, tātā’’ti khau jautikau gahapati dīghāvussa upāsakassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jautikau gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘dīghāvu, bhantai, upāsakau ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati| aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, bhagavā yaina dīghāvussa upāsakassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha khau bhagavā nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina dīghāvussa upāsakassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā dīghāvuṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, dīghāvu, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ? kac‍ci dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti, nau abhik‍kamanti; paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau abhik‍kamau’’ti? ‘‘na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti| ‘‘tasmātiha tai, dīghāvu, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau bhavissāmi – itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau bhavissāmi akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi’| aivañhi tai, dīghāvu, sikkhitabba’’nti|

‘‘yānimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā cattāri sautāpattiyaṅgāni daisitāni, saṃvij‍jantai tai dhammā mayi, ahañ‍ca taisu dhammaisu sandissāmi| ahañhi, bhantai, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau – itipi  sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihī’’ti| ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, dīghāvu, imaisu catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu patiṭṭhāya cha vij‍jābhāgiyai dhammai uttari bhāvaiyyāsi| idha tvaṃ, dīghāvu, sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī  viharāhi, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñī, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñī pahānasañ‍ñī virāgasañ‍ñī niraudhasañ‍ñīti| aivañhi tai, dīghāvu, sikkhitabba’’nti|

‘‘yaimai, bhantai, bhagavatā cha vij‍jābhāgiyā dhammā daisitā, saṃvij‍jantai tai dhammā mayi, ahañ‍ca taisu dhammaisu sandissāmi| ahañhi, bhantai, sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī viharāmi, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñī, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñī pahānasañ‍ñī virāgasañ‍ñī niraudhasañ‍ñī| api ca mai, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘mā haivāyaṃ jautikau gahapati mamac‍cayaina vighātaṃ āpaj‍jī’’’ti āpaj‍jati (ka॰)| ‘‘mā  tvaṃ, tāta dīghāvu, aivaṃ manasākāsi| iṅgha tvaṃ, tāta dīghāvu, yadaiva tai bhagavā āha, tadaiva tvaṃ sādhukaṃ manasi karauhī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā dīghāvuṃ upāsakaṃ iminā auvādaina auvaditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau dīghāvu upāsakau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau kālamakāsi| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā  khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, dīghāvu nāma upāsakau bhagavatā saṃkhittaina auvādaina auvaditau sau kālaṅkatau| tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti? ‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, dīghāvu upāsakau, pac‍capādi ahausi sac‍cavādī (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) dhammassānudhammaṃ, na ca maṃ dhammādhikaraṇaṃ na ca dhammādhikaraṇaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) vihaisaisi vihaiṭhaisi (itipi añ‍ñattha)| dīghāvu, bhikkhavai, upāsakau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamasāriputtasuttaṃ

1000. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca ānandau sāvatthiyaṃ viharanti jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katinaṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, dhammānaṃ saman‍nāgamanahaitu aivamayaṃ pajā  bhagavatā byākatā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’ti? ‘‘catun‍naṃ  khau, āvusau, dhammānaṃ saman‍nāgamanahaitu aivamayaṃ pajā bhagavatā byākatā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? idhāvusau, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi  sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ saman‍nāgamanahaitu aivamayaṃ pajā bhagavatā byākatā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyasāriputtasuttaṃ

1001. atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘‘sautāpattiyaṅgaṃ, sautāpattiyaṅga’nti hidaṃ, sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau sāriputta, sautāpattiyaṅga’’nti? ‘‘sappurisasaṃsaivau hi, bhantai, sautāpattiyaṅgaṃ, saddhammassavanaṃ sautāpattiyaṅgaṃ, yaunisaumanasikārau sautāpattiyaṅgaṃ, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti sautāpattiyaṅga’’nti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! sappurisasaṃsaivau hi, sāriputta, sautāpattiyaṅgaṃ, saddhammassavanaṃ sautāpattiyaṅgaṃ, yaunisaumanasikārau sautāpattiyaṅgaṃ, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti sautāpattiyaṅgaṃ’’|

‘‘‘sautau, sautau’ti hidaṃ, sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, sāriputta, sautau’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva hi, bhantai, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sautau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu  sādhu, sāriputta! ayamaiva hi, sāriputta, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sautau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi’’|

‘‘‘sautāpan‍nau, sautāpan‍nau’ti hidaṃ, sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, sāriputta, sautāpan‍nau’’ti? ‘‘yau hi, bhantai, iminā ariyaina aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina saman‍nāgatau ayaṃ vuc‍cati sautāpan‍nau, svāyaṃ āyasmā  aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! yau hi, sāriputta, iminā ariyaina aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina saman‍nāgatau ayaṃ vuc‍cati sautāpan‍nau, svāyaṃ āyasmā aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. thapatisuttaṃ

1002. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū bhagavatau cīvarakammaṃ karaunti – ‘‘niṭṭhitacīvarau bhagavā taimāsac‍cayaina cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina isidattapurāṇā thapatayau sādhukai paṭivasanti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| assausuṃ khau isidattapurāṇā thapatayau – ‘‘sambahulā kira bhikkhū bhagavatau cīvarakammaṃ karaunti – ‘niṭṭhitacīvarau bhagavā taimāsac‍cayaina cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’’’ti|

atha khau isidattapurāṇā thapatayau maggai purisaṃ ṭhapaisuṃ – ‘‘yadā tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, passaiyyāsi bhagavantaṃ āgacchantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ, atha amhākaṃ āraucaiyyāsī’’ti| dvīhatīhaṃ ṭhitau khau sau  purisau addasa bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna yaina isidattapurāṇā thapatayau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā isidattapurāṇai thapatayau aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ sau, bhantai, bhagavā āgacchati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| yassa dāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñathā’’ti|

atha khau isidattapurāṇā thapatayau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā maggā auk‍kamma yaina añ‍ñataraṃ rukkhamūlaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai  nisīdi| isidattapurāṇā thapatayau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai isidattapurāṇā thapatayau bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘yadā mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘sāvatthiyā kausalaisu cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ – ‘dūrai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘sāvatthiyā kausalaisu cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ – ‘dūrai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘yadā  pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘kausalaihi mal‍laisu cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ – ‘dūrai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘kausalaihi mal‍laisu cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ – ‘dūrai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘mal‍laihi vaj‍jīsu cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ – ‘dūrai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘mal‍laihi vaj‍jīsu cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ – ‘dūrai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘vaj‍jīhi kāsīsu cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ – ‘dūrai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘vaj‍jīhi kāsīsu cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ – ‘dūrai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘kāsīhi māgadhai cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā  hauti daumanassaṃ – ‘dūrai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘kāsīhi māgadhai cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti anappakā nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti anappakaṃ daumanassaṃ – ‘dūrai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘yadā  pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘māgadhaihi kāsīsu cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ – ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘māgadhaihi kāsīsu cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ – ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘kāsīhi vaj‍jīsu cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ  – ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘kāsīhi vaj‍jīsu cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ – ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘vaj‍jīhi mal‍laisu cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ – ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘vaj‍jīhi mal‍laisu cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ – ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘mal‍laihi kausalai cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā  hauti saumanassaṃ – ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘mal‍laihi kausalai cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ – ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘kausalaihi sāvatthiṃ cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ – ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ suṇāma – ‘sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai’ti, hauti anappakā nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti anappakaṃ saumanassaṃ – ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha, thapatayau, sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| alañ‍ca pana  vau, thapatayau, appamādāyā’’ti| ‘‘atthi khau nau, bhantai, aitamhā sambādhā añ‍ñau sambādhau sambādhatarau caiva sambādhasaṅkhātatarau cā’’ti| ‘‘katamau  pana vau, thapatayau, aitamhā sambādhā añ‍ñau sambādhau sambādhatarau caiva sambādhasaṅkhātatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, yadā rājā pasainadi kausalau uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyātukāmau hauti, yai tai rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa nāgā aupavayhā tai kappaitvā, yā tā rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa pajāpatiyau piyā manāpā tā aikaṃ puratau aikaṃ  pacchatau  nisīdāpaima| tāsaṃ khau pana, bhantai, bhaginīnaṃ aivarūpau gandhau hauti, saiyyathāpi nāma gandhakaraṇḍakassa tāvadaiva vivariyamānassa, yathā taṃ rājakañ‍ñānaṃ gandhaina vibhūsitānaṃ| tāsaṃ khau pana, bhantai, bhaginīnaṃ aivarūpau kāyasamphassau hauti, saiyyathāpi nāma tūlapicunau vā kappāsapicunau vā, yathā taṃ rājakañ‍ñānaṃ sukhaidhitānaṃ| tasmiṃ khau pana, bhantai, samayai nāgaupi rakkhitabbau hauti, tāpi bhaginiyau rakkhitabbā haunti, attāpi rakkhitabbau hauti| na khau pana mayaṃ, bhantai, abhijānāma tāsu bhaginīsu pāpakaṃ cittaṃ uppādaitā| ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, aitamhā sambādhā añ‍ñau sambādhau sambādhatarau caiva sambādhasaṅkhātatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha, thapatayau, sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| alañ‍ca pana vau, thapatayau, appamādāya| catūhi khau, thapatayau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, thapatayau, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā ajjhāgāraṃ vasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| imaihi khau, thapatayau, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  ariyasāvakau  sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau|

‘‘tumhai khau, thapatayau, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci kulai daiyyadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ appaṭivibhattaṃ sīlavantaihi kalyāṇadhammaihi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, thapatayau, katividhā  tai kausalaisu manussā yai tumhākaṃ samasamā, yadidaṃ – dānasaṃvibhāgai’’ti? ‘‘lābhā nau, bhantai, suladdhaṃ nau, bhantai! yaisaṃ nau bhagavā aivaṃ pajānātī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vaiḷudvāraiyyasuttaṃ

1003. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina vaiḷudvāraṃ nāma kausalānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmau  tadavasari| assausuṃ khau tai vaiḷudvāraiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ vaiḷudvāraṃ anuppattau| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā bhagavāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau  dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti’| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

atha  khau tai vaiḷudvāraiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā appaikac‍cai bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu; sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai bhagavatau santikai nāmagauttaṃ sāvaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai tuṇhībhūtā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai vaiḷudvāraiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mayaṃ, bhau gautama, aivaṃkāmā aivaṃchandā aivaṃadhippāyā – puttasambādhasayanaṃ ajjhāvasaiyyāma, kāsikacandanaṃ pac‍canubhavaiyyāma, mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ dhāraiyyāma, jātarūparajataṃ sādiyaiyyāma, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyāma| taisaṃ nau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ aivaṃkāmānaṃ aivaṃchandānaṃ aivaṃadhippāyānaṃ tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā mayaṃ puttasambādhasayanaṃ ajjhāvasaiyyāma…pai॰… sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyāmā’’ti|

‘‘attūpanāyikaṃ  vau, gahapatayau, dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau tai  vaiḷudvāraiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau  ca, gahapatayau, attupanāyikau dhammapariyāyau? idha, gahapatayau, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khausmi jīvitukāmau amaritukāmau sukhakāmau dukkhappaṭikūlau| yau khau maṃ jīvitukāmaṃ amaritukāmaṃ sukhakāmaṃ dukkhappaṭikūlaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, na maitaṃ assa piyaṃ manāpaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana paraṃ jīvitukāmaṃ amaritukāmaṃ sukhakāmaṃ dukkhappaṭikūlaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyyaṃ, parassapi taṃ assa appiyaṃ amanāpaṃ| yau khau myāyaṃ dhammau appiyau amanāpau, parassa paisau dhammau  appiyau amanāpau| yau khau myāyaṃ dhammau appiyau amanāpau, kathāhaṃ paraṃ taina saṃyaujaiyya’nti! sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| aivamassāyaṃ kāyasamācārau tikauṭiparisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yau khau mai adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyaiyya, na maitaṃ assa piyaṃ manāpaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana parassa adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyaiyyaṃ, parassapi taṃ assa appiyaṃ amanāpaṃ| yau khau myāyaṃ dhammau appiyau amanāpau, parassa paisau dhammau appiyau amanāpau| yau khau myāyaṃ dhammau appiyau amanāpau, kathāhaṃ paraṃ taina saṃyaujaiyya’nti! sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya attanā ca adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| aivamassāyaṃ kāyasamācārau tikauṭiparisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yau khau mai dāraisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya, na maitaṃ assa piyaṃ manāpaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana parassa dāraisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, parassapi taṃ assa appiyaṃ amanāpaṃ| yau khau myāyaṃ dhammau appiyau amanāpau, parassa paisau dhammau appiyau amanāpau| yau khau myāyaṃ dhammau appiyau amanāpau, kathāhaṃ paraṃ taina saṃyaujaiyya’nti! sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya attanā ca kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau  hauti, parañ‍ca kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ  bhāsati| aivamassāyaṃ kāyasamācārau tikauṭiparisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yau khau mai musāvādaina atthaṃ bhañ‍jaiyya, na maitaṃ assa piyaṃ manāpaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana parassa musāvādaina atthaṃ bhañ‍jaiyyaṃ, parassapi taṃ assa appiyaṃ amanāpaṃ| yau khau myāyaṃ dhammau appiyau amanāpau, parassa paisau dhammau appiyau amanāpau| yau  khau myāyaṃ dhammau appiyau amanāpau, kathāhaṃ paraṃ taina saṃyaujaiyya’nti! sau  iti paṭisaṅkhāya attanā ca musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca musāvādā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, musāvādā vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| aivamassāyaṃ vacīsamācārau tikauṭiparisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – yau khau maṃ pisuṇāya vācāya mittai bhindaiyya mittaihi bhaidaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), na maitaṃ assa piyaṃ manāpaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana paraṃ pisuṇāya vācāya mittai bhindaiyyaṃ, parassapi taṃ assa appiyaṃ amanāpaṃ…pai॰… aivamassāyaṃ vacīsamācārau tikauṭiparisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – yau khau maṃ pharusāya vācāya samudācaraiyya, na maitaṃ assa piyaṃ manāpaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana paraṃ pharusāya vācāya samudācaraiyyaṃ, parassapi taṃ assa appiyaṃ amanāpaṃ| yau khau myāyaṃ dhammau…pai॰… aivamassāyaṃ vacīsamācārau tikauṭiparisuddhau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapatayau, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yau khau maṃ samphabhāsaina samphappalāpabhāsaina samudācaraiyya, na maitaṃ assa piyaṃ manāpaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana paraṃ samphabhāsaina samphappalāpabhāsaina samudācaraiyyaṃ, parassapi taṃ assa appiyaṃ amanāpaṃ| yau khau myāyaṃ dhammau appiyau amanāpau, parassa paisau dhammau appiyau amanāpau| yau khau myāyaṃ dhammau appiyau amanāpau, kathāhaṃ paraṃ taina saṃyaujaiyya’nti! sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya attanā ca samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca samphappalāpā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, samphappalāpā  vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| aivamassāyaṃ vacīsamācārau tikauṭiparisuddhau hauti|

‘‘sau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti; dhammai  …pai॰… saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| yatau khau, gahapatayau, ariyasāvakau imaihi sattahi saddhammaihi dhammaihi (sī॰) saman‍nāgatau hauti imaihi catūhi ākaṅkhiyaihi ṭhānaihi, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇatiracchānayauniyau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), khīṇatiracchānayaunikau (ka॰) khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai vaiḷudvāraiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… aitai mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakai nau bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitai pāṇupaitaṃ (ka॰) saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭhamagiñ‍jakāvasathasuttaṃ

1004. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā ñātikai viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sāḷhau nāma, bhantai, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau; tassa kā gati kau abhisamparāyau? nandā nāma, bhantai, bhikkhunī kālaṅkatā; tassā kā gati kau abhisamparāyau? sudattau nāma, bhantai, upāsakau kālaṅkatau; tassa kā gati kau abhisamparāyau? sujātā nāma, bhantai, upāsikā kālaṅkatā; tassā kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti?

‘‘sāḷhau, ānanda, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| nandā, ānanda, bhikkhunī kālaṅkatā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ  parikkhayā aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyinī anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā| sudattau, ānanda, upāsakau kālaṅkatau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī; sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissati| sujātā, ānanda, upāsikā kālaṅkatā tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā|

‘‘anacchariyaṃ khau panaitaṃ, ānanda, yaṃ manussabhūtau kālaṃ karaiyya; tasmiṃ tasmiṃ cai maṃ kālaṅkatai upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchissatha| vihaisā paisā, ānanda, assa tathāgatassa| tasmātihānanda, dhammādāsaṃ nāma dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaissāmi; yaina saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’|

‘‘katamau ca sau, ānanda, dhammādāsau dhammapariyāyau; yaina saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’?
