‘‘idha, ānanda, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ayaṃ khau sau, ānanda, dhammādāsau dhammapariyāyau; yaina saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

(tīṇipi suttantāni aikanidānāni)|

9. dutiyagiñ‍jakāvasathasuttaṃ

1005. aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘asaukau nāma, bhantai, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau; tassa kā gati, kau  abhisamparāyau? asaukā nāma, bhantai, bhikkhunī kālaṅkatā…pai॰… asaukau nāma, bhantai, upāsakau kālaṅkatau…pai॰… asaukā nāma, bhantai, upāsikā kālaṅkatā; tassā kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti?

‘‘asaukau, ānanda, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi…pai॰… (purimavaiyyākaraṇaina aikanidānaṃ)|

‘‘ayaṃ khau sau, ānanda, dhammādāsau dhammapariyāyau; yaina saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tatiyagiñ‍jakāvasathasuttaṃ

1006. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kak‍kaṭau nāma, bhantai, ñātikai upāsakau kālaṅkatau; tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau? kaḷibhau nāma, bhantai, ñātikai upāsakau…pai॰… nikatau nāma, bhantai, ñātikai upāsakau…pai॰… kaṭissahau nāma, bhantai, ñātikai upāsakau…pai॰… tuṭṭhau nāma, bhantai, ñātikai upāsakau…pai॰… santuṭṭhau nāma, bhantai, ñātikai upāsakau…pai॰… bhaddau nāma, bhantai, ñātikai upāsakau…pai॰… subhaddau nāma, bhantai, ñātikai upāsakau kālaṅkatau; tassa kā gati kau abhisamparāyau’’ti?

‘‘kak‍kaṭau, ānanda, upāsakau kālaṅkatau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| kaḷibhau, ānanda  …pai॰… nikatau, ānanda…pai॰… kaṭissahau, ānanda  …pai॰… tuṭṭhau, ānanda…pai॰… santuṭṭhau, ānanda…pai॰… bhaddau, ānanda…pai॰… subhaddau, ānanda, upāsakau kālaṅkatau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| (sabbai aikagatikā kātabbā)|

‘‘paraupañ‍ñāsa, ānanda, ñātikai upāsakā kālaṅkatā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyinau anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā| sādhikanavuti, ānanda, ñātikai upāsakā kālaṅkatā tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāminau; sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissanti| chātiraikāni khau, ānanda, pañ‍casatāni ñātikai upāsakā kālaṅkatā tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā|

‘‘anacchariyaṃ khau panaitaṃ, ānanda, yaṃ manussabhūtau kālaṃ karaiyya; tasmiṃ tasmiṃ cai maṃ kālaṅkatai upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchissatha| vihaisā paisā, ānanda, assa tathāgatassa| tasmātihānanda, dhammādāsaṃ nāma dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaissāmi; yaina saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi  khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’|

‘‘katamau ca sau, ānanda, dhammādāsau dhammapariyāyau; yaina saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’|

‘‘idhānanda, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ayaṃ khau sau, ānanda, dhammādāsau dhammapariyāyau; yaina saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva  attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau  khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti| dasamaṃ|

vaiḷudvāravaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

rājā augadhadīghāvu, sāriputtāparai duvai|

thapatī vaiḷudvāraiyyā, giñ‍jakāvasathai tayauti||

2. rājakārāmavaggau

1. sahassabhikkhunisaṅghasuttaṃ

1007. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati rājakārāmai| atha khau sahassabhikkhunisaṅghau sahassau bhikkhunisaṃghau (sī॰) yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘catūhi khau, bhikkhuniyau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhuniyau, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai  …pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, bhikkhuniyau, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. brāhmaṇasuttaṃ

1008. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘brāhmaṇā, bhikkhavai, udayagāminiṃ nāma paṭipadaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| tai sāvakaṃ aivaṃ samādapainti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, kālassaiva uṭṭhāya pācīnamukhau yāhi| sau tvaṃ mā saubbhaṃ parivaj‍jaihi, mā  papātaṃ, mā khāṇuṃ, mā kaṇḍakaṭhānaṃ kaṇḍakaṃ ṭhānaṃ (pī॰ ka॰), mā candaniyaṃ, mā auḷigal‍laṃ| yattha yatthaiva (syā॰ kaṃ॰), yāni vā (sī॰) papataiyyāsi tatthaiva maraṇaṃ āgamaiyyāsi| aivaṃ tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissasī’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇānaṃ bālagamanamaitaṃ bālānaṃ gamanamaitaṃ (sī॰) mūḷhagamanamaitaṃ na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati| ahañ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai udayagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi; yā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, udayagāminī paṭipadā; yā aikantanibbidāya…pai॰… nibbānāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti; dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ayaṃ khau sā, bhikkhavai, udayagāminī paṭipadā aikantanibbidāya…pai॰… nibbānāya saṃvattatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. ānandatthairasuttaṃ

1009. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca ānandau āyasmā ca sāriputtau sāvatthiyaṃ viharanti jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ  ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katinaṃ khau, āvusau ānanda, dhammānaṃ pahānā, katinaṃ dhammānaṃ saman‍nāgamanahaitu, aivamayaṃ pajā bhagavatā byākatā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’ti? ‘‘catun‍naṃ khau, āvusau, dhammānaṃ pahānā, catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ saman‍nāgamanahaitu, aivamayaṃ pajā bhagavatā byākatā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’ti|

‘‘katamaisaṃ  catun‍naṃ? yathārūpaina khau, āvusau, buddhai appasādaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tathārūpassa  buddhai appasādau na hauti| yathārūpaina ca khau, āvusau, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau sutavā ariyasāvakau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati tathārūpassa buddhai avaic‍cappasādau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’’ti|

‘‘yathārūpaina ca khau, āvusau, dhammai appasādaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tathārūpassa dhammai appasādau na hauti| yathārūpaina ca khau, āvusau, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau sutavā ariyasāvakau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati tathārūpassa dhammai avaic‍cappasādau  hauti – svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… viñ‍ñūhīti|

‘‘yathārūpaina ca khau, āvusau, saṅghai appasādaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tathārūpassa saṅghai appasādau na hauti| yathārūpaina ca khau, āvusau, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau sutavā ariyasāvakau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati tathārūpassa saṅghai avaic‍cappasādau hauti – suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti|

‘‘yathārūpaina ca khau, āvusau, dussīlyaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati tathārūpassa dussīlyaṃ na hauti| yathārūpaihi ca khau, āvusau, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau sutavā ariyasāvakau  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati tathārūpāni ariyakantāni sīlāni haunti akhaṇḍāni…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikāni| imaisaṃ  khau, āvusau, catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ pahānā imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ  dhammānaṃ saman‍nāgamanahaitu aivamayaṃ pajā bhagavatā byākatā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. duggatibhayasuttaṃ

1010. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sabbaduggatibhayaṃ samatik‍kantau hauti| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau  buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sabbaduggatibhayaṃ samatik‍kantau hautī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. duggativinipātabhayasuttaṃ

1011. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sabbaduggativinipātabhayaṃ samatik‍kantau hauti| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sabbaduggativinipātabhayaṃ samatik‍kantau hautī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamamittāmac‍casuttaṃ

1012. ‘‘yai tai, bhikkhavai, anukampaiyyātha, yai ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyuṃ – mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā – tai, bhikkhavai, catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu samādapaitabbā, nivaisaitabbā, patiṭṭhāpaitabbā| katamaisu catūsu? buddhai  avaic‍cappasādai samādapaitabbā, nivaisaitabbā, patiṭṭhāpaitabbā – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaisu sīlaisu samādapaitabbā, nivaisaitabbā, patiṭṭhāpaitabbā akhaṇḍaisu…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaisu| yai tai, bhikkhavai, anukampaiyyātha, yai ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyuṃ – mittā vā amac‍cā  vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā – tai, bhikkhavai, imaisu catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu samādapaitabbā, nivaisaitabbā, patiṭṭhāpaitabbā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyamittāmac‍casuttaṃ

1013. ‘‘yai tai, bhikkhavai, anukampaiyyātha, yai ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyuṃ – mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā – tai, bhikkhavai, catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu samādapaitabbā, nivaisaitabbā, patiṭṭhāpaitabbā| katamaisu catūsu? buddhai avaic‍cappasādai samādapaitabbā, nivaisaitabbā, patiṭṭhāpaitabbā – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’’ti|

‘‘siyā, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ – pathavīdhātuyā, āpaudhātuyā, taijaudhātuyā, vāyaudhātuyā – na tvaiva buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ| tatridaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ – sau vata buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā paittivisayaṃ vā upapaj‍jissatī’’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ‘‘dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaisu sīlaisu samādapaitabbā, nivaisaitabbā, patiṭṭhāpaitabbā akhaṇḍaisu…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaisu| siyā, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ – pathavīdhātuyā, āpaudhātuyā, taijaudhātuyā, vāyaudhātuyā – na tvaiva ariyakantaihi  sīlaihi saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ| tatridaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ – sau vata ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau  nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā paittivisayaṃ vā upapaj‍jissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yai tai, bhikkhavai, anukampaiyyātha, yai ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyuṃ – mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā – tai, bhikkhavai, imaisu catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu samādapaitabbā, nivaisaitabbā, patiṭṭhāpaitabbā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭhamadaivacārikasuttaṃ

1014. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā  bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – jaitavanai antarahitau daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu pāturahausi| atha khau sambahulā tāvatiṃsakāyikā daivatāyau yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tā daivatāyau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu khau, āvusau, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, āvusau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sādhu khau, āvusau, dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… sādhu khau, āvusau, ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰…  samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi  sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, āvusau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyadaivacārikasuttaṃ

1015. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – jaitavanai antarahitau daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu pāturahausi| atha khau sambahulā tāvatiṃsakāyikā daivatāyau yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau tā daivatāyau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu  khau, āvusau, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, āvusau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā| sādhu khau, āvusau, dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, āvusau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā| sādhu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tatiyadaivacārikasuttaṃ

1016. atha khau bhagavā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – jaitavanai antarahitau daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu pāturahausi| atha  khau sambahulā tāvatiṃsakāyikā daivatāyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tā daivatāyau bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu khau, āvusau, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, āvusau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā| sādhu khau, āvusau, dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, āvusau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’ti|

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa, aivamayaṃ pajā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā| sādhu khau, mārisa, dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanaṃ hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgamanahaitu khau, mārisa, aivamayaṃ pajā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

rājakārāmavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sahassabrāhmaṇānanda, duggati aparai duvai|

mittāmac‍cā duvai vuttā, tayau ca daivacārikāti||

3. saraṇānivaggau

1. paṭhamamahānāmasuttaṃ

1017. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, kapilavatthu iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca bāhujañ‍ñaṃ ākiṇṇamanussaṃ sambādhabyūhaṃ| sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ vā payirupāsitvā manaubhāvanīyai vā bhikkhū sāyanhasamayaṃ kapilavatthuṃ pavisantau; bhantainapi vibbhantainapi (sī॰), bhamantainapi (ka॰) hatthinā samāgacchāmi; bhantainapi assaina samāgacchāmi; bhantainapi rathaina samāgacchāmi; bhantainapi sakaṭaina samāgacchāmi; bhantainapi purisaina samāgacchāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, tasmiṃ samayai mussataiva musataiva (?) bhagavantaṃ ārabbha sati, mussati musati (?) dhammaṃ ārabbha sati, mussati saṅghaṃ ārabbha sati| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘imamhi cāhaṃ samayai kālaṃ karaiyyaṃ, kā mayhaṃ gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’’ti?

‘‘mā bhāyi, mahānāma, mā bhāyi, mahānāma! apāpakaṃ tai maraṇaṃ bhavissati apāpikā kālaṃkiriyā kālakiriyā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| yassa kassaci, mahānāma, dīgharattaṃ saddhāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ sīlaparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ sutaparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ cāgaparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ pañ‍ñāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ, tassa yau hi khvāyaṃ kāyau rūpī cātumahābhūtikau cātummahābhūtikau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) mātāpaittikasambhavau  audanakummāsūpacayau  anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammau| taṃ idhaiva kākā vā khādanti gijjhā vā khādanti kulalā vā khādanti sunakhā vā khādanti siṅgālā sigālā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā khādanti vividhā vā pāṇakajātā khādanti; yañ‍ca khvassa cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ saddhāparibhāvitaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñāparibhāvitaṃ taṃ uddhagāmi hauti visaisagāmi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahānāma, purisau sappikumbhaṃ vā tailakumbhaṃ vā gambhīraṃ udakarahadaṃ augāhitvā bhindaiyya| tatra yā assa sakkharā vā kaṭhalā kathalā (pī॰ ka॰) vā sā adhaugāmī assa, yañ‍ca khvassa tatra sappi vā tailaṃ vā taṃ uddhagāmi assa visaisagāmi| aivamaiva khau, mahānāma, yassa  kassaci dīgharattaṃ saddhāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ tassa yau hi khvāyaṃ kāyau rūpī cātumahābhūtikau mātāpaittikasambhavau audanakummāsūpacayau anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammau taṃ idhaiva kākā vā khādanti gijjhā vā khādanti kulalā vā khādanti sunakhā vā khādanti siṅgālā vā khādanti vividhā vā pāṇakajātā khādanti; yañ‍ca khvassa cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ saddhāparibhāvitaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñāparibhāvitaṃ taṃ uddhagāmi hauti visaisagāmi| tuyhaṃ khau pana, mahānāma, dīgharattaṃ saddhāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñāparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ| mā bhāyi, mahānāma, mā bhāyi, mahānāma! apāpakaṃ tai maraṇaṃ bhavissati, apāpikā kālaṃkiriyā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyamahānāmasuttaṃ

1018. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, kapilavatthu iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca bāhujañ‍ñaṃ ākiṇṇamanussaṃ sambādhabyūhaṃ| sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ vā payirupāsitvā manaubhāvanīyai vā bhikkhū sāyanhasamayaṃ kapilavatthuṃ pavisantau; bhantainapi hatthinā samāgacchāmi; bhantainapi assaina samāgacchāmi; bhantainapi rathaina samāgacchāmi; bhantai, napi sakaṭaina samāgacchāmi; bhantai, napi purisaina samāgacchāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, tasmiṃ samayai mussataiva bhagavantaṃ ārabbha sati, mussati dhammaṃ ārabbha sati, mussati saṅghaṃ ārabbha sati| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘imamhi cāhaṃ samayai kālaṃ karaiyyaṃ, kā mayhaṃ gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’’ti?

‘‘mā bhāyi, mahānāma, mā bhāyi, mahānāma! apāpakaṃ tai maraṇaṃ bhavissati apāpikā kālaṃkiriyā| catūhi khau, mahānāma, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| katamaihi catūhi? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai  …pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahānāma, rukkhau pācīnanin‍nau pācīnapauṇau pācīnapabbhārau, sau mūlacchin‍nau katamaina papataiyyā’’ti? ‘‘yaina, bhantai, nin‍nau yaina pauṇau yaina pabbhārau’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, mahānāma, imaihi catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. gaudhasak‍kasuttaṃ

1019. kapilavatthunidānaṃ| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina gaudhā sak‍kau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā gaudhaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katihi  katīhi (pī॰ ka॰) rūpasiddhi aulaukaitabbā tvaṃ, gaudhai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāsi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇa’’nti?

‘‘tīhi khvāhaṃ, mahānāma, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāmi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇaṃ| katamaihi tīhi? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti| imaihi khvāhaṃ, mahānāma, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāmi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇaṃ|

‘‘tvaṃ pana, mahānāma, katihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ  ājānāsi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇa’’nti? ‘‘catūhi khvāhaṃ, gaudhai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāmi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇaṃ| katamaihi catūhi? idha, gaudhai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khvāhaṃ, gaudhai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāmi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇa’’nti|

‘‘āgamaihi tvaṃ, mahānāma, āgamaihi tvaṃ, mahānāma! bhagavāva aitaṃ jānaiyya aitaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ vā asaman‍nāgataṃ vā’’ti| ‘‘āyāma, gaudhai, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaissāmā’’ti| atha  khau mahānāmau sak‍kau gaudhā ca sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, yaina gaudhā sak‍kau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā gaudhaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘katihi tvaṃ, gaudhai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāsi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇaṃ’? aivaṃ  vuttai, bhantai, gaudhā sak‍kau maṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘tīhi khvāhaṃ, mahānāma, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāmi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇaṃ| katamaihi tīhi? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti| imaihi khvāhaṃ, mahānāma, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāmi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇaṃ| tvaṃ pana, mahānāma, katamaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāsi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇa’’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ vuttāhaṃ, bhantai, gaudhaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘catūhi khvāhaṃ, gaudhai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāmi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇaṃ| katamaihi catūhi? idha, gaudhai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khvāhaṃ, gaudhai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ sautāpan‍napuggalaṃ ājānāmi avinipātadhammaṃ niyataṃ sambaudhiparāyaṇa’’’nti|

‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, bhantai, gaudhā sak‍kau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘āgamaihi tvaṃ, mahānāma, āgamaihi tvaṃ, mahānāma! bhagavāva aitaṃ jānaiyya aitaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ vā asaman‍nāgataṃ vā’’’ti| ‘‘idha, bhantai, kaucidaiva dhammau samuppādau uppaj‍jaiyya, aikatau assa bhagavā aikatau bhikkhusaṅghau  ca| yainaiva bhagavā tainaivāhaṃ assaṃ| aivaṃ pasan‍naṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu| idha, bhantai, kaucidaiva dhammau samuppādau uppaj‍jaiyya, aikatau assa bhagavā aikatau bhikkhusaṅghau bhikkhunisaṅghau ca| yainaiva bhagavā tainaivāhaṃ assaṃ| aivaṃ pasan‍naṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu| idha, bhantai, kaucidaiva dhammau samuppādau uppaj‍jaiyya, aikatau assa bhagavā aikatau bhikkhusaṅghau  bhikkhunisaṅghau ca upāsakā ca| yainaiva bhagavā tainaivāhaṃ assaṃ| aivaṃ pasan‍naṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu| idha, bhantai, kaucidaiva dhammau samuppādau uppaj‍jaiyya, aikatau assa bhagavā aikatau bhikkhusaṅghau bhikkhunisaṅghau upāsakā upāsikāyau ca| yainaiva bhagavā tainaivāhaṃ assaṃ| aivaṃ pasan‍naṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu| idha, bhantai, kaucidaiva dhammau samuppādau uppaj‍jaiyya, aikatau assa bhagavā aikatau bhikkhusaṅghau bhikkhunisaṅghau upāsakā upāsikāyau sadaivakau ca laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī  pajā sadaivamanussā| yainaiva bhagavā tainaivāhaṃ assaṃ| aivaṃ pasan‍naṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitū’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃvādī tvaṃ, gaudhai, mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ kiṃ vadaisī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃvādāhaṃ, bhantai, mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ na kiñ‍ci vadāmi, añ‍ñatra kalyāṇā añ‍ñatra kusalā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamasaraṇānisak‍kasuttaṃ

1020. kapilavatthunidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina saraṇāni sarakāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sak‍kau kālaṅkatau hauti| sau bhagavatā byākatau – ‘‘sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| tatra sudaṃ sambahulā sak‍kā saṅgamma samāgamma ujjhāyanti khīyanti vipācainti – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! aittha dāni kau na sautāpan‍nau bhavissati! yatra hi nāma saraṇāni sak‍kau kālaṅkatau; sau bhagavatā byākatau – ‘sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’ti| saraṇāni sak‍kau sikkhādubbalyamāpādi, maj‍japānaṃ apāyī’’ti|

atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, bhantai, saraṇāni sak‍kau kālaṅkatau| sau bhagavatā byākatau – ‘sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’ti| tatra sudaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā sak‍kā saṅgamma samāgamma ujjhāyanti khīyanti vipācainti – ‘‘acchariyaṃ  vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau! aittha dāni  kau na sautapan‍nau bhavissati! yatra hi nāma saraṇāni sak‍kau kālaṅkatau; sau bhagavatā byākatau  – ‘sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’ti| saraṇāni sak‍kau sikkhādubbalyamāpādi, maj‍japānaṃ apāyī’’ti|

‘‘yau sau, mahānāma, dīgharattaṃ upāsakau buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau, sau kathaṃ vinipātaṃ gacchaiyya! yañhi taṃ, mahānāma, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘dīgharattaṃ upāsakau buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau’ti, saraṇāni sak‍kaṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| saraṇāni, mahānāma, sak‍kau dīgharattaṃ upāsakau buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau| sau kathaṃ vinipātaṃ gacchaiyya!

‘‘idha, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… hāsapañ‍ñau javanapañ‍ñau vimuttiyā ca saman‍nāgatau| sau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina  saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… hāsapañ‍ñau javanapañ‍ñau na ca vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā asmā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) laukā| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘idha  pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… na hāsapañ‍ñau na javanapañ‍ñau na ca vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ  karauti| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti; dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… na hāsapañ‍ñau na javanapañ‍ñau na ca vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇauti| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau na haiva khau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti… na dhammai…pai॰… na saṅghai…pai॰… na hāsapañ‍ñau na javanapañ‍ñau na ca vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau| api cassa imai dhammā haunti – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| tathāgatappavaiditā cassa dhammā pañ‍ñāya mattasau nijjhānaṃ khamanti| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau agantā nirayaṃ agantā tiracchānayauniṃ agantā paittivisayaṃ agantā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau na haiva khau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti… na dhammai…pai॰… na saṅghai…pai॰… na hāsapañ‍ñau na javanapañ‍ñau na ca vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau, api cassa imai dhammā haunti saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| tathāgatai cassa saddhāmattaṃ hauti paimamattaṃ| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau agantā nirayaṃ agantā tiracchānayauniṃ agantā paittivisayaṃ agantā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ| imai caipi, mahānāma, mahāsālā subhāsitaṃ dubbhāsitaṃ ājānaiyyuṃ, imai cāhaṃ imaivāhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), imaisāhaṃ (ka॰) mahāsālai byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā  niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’ti; kimaṅgaṃ kimaṅga (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pana saraṇāniṃ sak‍kaṃ| saraṇāni, mahānāma, sak‍kau maraṇakālai sikkhaṃ samādiyī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyasaraṇānisak‍kasuttaṃ

1021. kapilavatthunidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina saraṇāni sak‍kau kālaṅkatau hauti| sau bhagavatā byākatau – ‘‘sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| tatra sudaṃ sambahulā sak‍kā saṅgamma samāgamma ujjhāyanti khīyanti vipācainti – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! aittha dāni kau na sautāpan‍nau bhavissati! yatra hi nāma saraṇāni sak‍kau kālaṅkatau| sau bhagavatā byākatau – ‘sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’ti| saraṇāni sak‍kau sikkhāya aparipūrakārī ahausī’’ti| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, bhantai, saraṇāni sak‍kau kālaṅkatau| sau bhagavatā byākatau – ‘sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’ti| tatra sudaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā sak‍kā saṅgamma samāgamma ujjhāyanti khīyanti vipācainti – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! aittha dāni kau na sautāpan‍nau bhavissati! yatra hi nāma saraṇāni sak‍kau kālaṅkatau| sau bhagavatā byākatau – sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇauti| saraṇāni sak‍kau sikkhāya aparipūrakārī ahausī’’’ti|

‘‘yau  sau, mahānāma, dīgharattaṃ upāsakau buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau, sau kathaṃ vinipātaṃ gacchaiyya! yañhi taṃ, mahānāma, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘dīgharattaṃ upāsakau buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau’, saraṇāniṃ sak‍kaṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| saraṇāni, mahānāma, sak‍kau dīgharattaṃ upāsakau buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau, sau kathaṃ vinipātaṃ gacchaiyya!

‘‘idha, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau buddhai aikantagatau hauti abhippasan‍nau – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… hāsapañ‍ñau javanapañ‍ñau vimuttiyā ca saman‍nāgatau| sau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau buddhai aikantagatau hauti abhippasan‍nau – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… hāsapañ‍ñau javanapañ‍ñau na ca vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti, upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, uddhaṃsautau hauti  akaniṭṭhagāmī| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau buddhai aikantagatau hauti abhippasan‍nau – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… na hāsapañ‍ñau na javanapañ‍ñau na ca vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau buddhai aikantagatau hauti abhippasan‍nau – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… na hāsapañ‍ñau na javanapañ‍ñau na ca vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau na haiva khau buddhai aikantagatau hauti abhippasan‍nau…pai॰… na dhammai…pai॰… na saṅghai…pai॰… na  hāsapañ‍ñau na javanapañ‍ñau na ca vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau; api cassa imai dhammā haunti – saddhindriyaṃ  …pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| tathāgatappavaiditā  cassa dhammā pañ‍ñāya mattasau nijjhānaṃ khamanti| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau agantā nirayaṃ agantā tiracchānayauniṃ agantā paittivisayaṃ agantā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau puggalau na haiva khau buddhai aikantagatau hauti abhippasan‍nau… na dhammai…pai॰… na saṅghai…pai॰… na hāsapañ‍ñau na javanapañ‍ñau na ca vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau; api cassa imai dhammā haunti – saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| tathāgatai cassa saddhāmattaṃ hauti paimamattaṃ| ayampi khau, mahānāma, puggalau agantā nirayaṃ agantā tiracchānayauniṃ agantā paittivisayaṃ agantā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahānāma, dukkhaittaṃ dubbhūmaṃ avihatakhāṇukaṃ, bījāni cassu khaṇḍāni pūtīni vātātapahatāni asārādāni asukhasayitāni asukhāpassayitāni (ka॰), daivau ca na sammā daivau pana sammā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), daivau na sammā (ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 2.438 dhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| api nu tāni bījāni vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyyu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, mahānāma, idha dhammau durakkhātau dvākkhātau (pī॰ ka॰) hauti duppavaiditau aniyyānikau anupasamasaṃvattanikau asammāsambuddhappavaiditau – idamahaṃ dukkhaittasmiṃ vadāmi| tasmiñ‍ca  dhammai sāvakau viharati dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau  sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī – idamahaṃ dubbījasmiṃ vadāmi’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahānāma, sukhaittaṃ subhūmaṃ suvihatakhāṇukaṃ, bījāni cassu akhaṇḍāni apūtīni avātātapahatāni sārādāni sukhasayitāni; daivau ca daivau cassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| api nu tāni bījāni vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyyu’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, mahānāma, idha dhammau svākkhātau hauti suppavaiditau niyyānikau upasamasaṃvattanikau sammāsambuddhappavaiditau – idamahaṃ sukhaittasmiṃ vadāmi| tasmiñ‍ca dhammai sāvakau viharati dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī – idamahaṃ subījasmiṃ vadāmi| kimaṅgaṃ pana saraṇāniṃ sak‍kaṃ! saraṇāni, mahānāma, sak‍kau maraṇakālai sikkhāya paripūrakārī ahausī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamaanāthapiṇḍikasuttaṃ

1022. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina āyasmatau sāriputtassa pādai sirasā vanda – ‘anāthapiṇḍikau, bhantai, gahapati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau āyasmatau sāriputtassa pādai sirasā vandatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, āyasmā sāriputtau yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau  purisau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa paṭissutvā yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau purisau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘anāthapiṇḍikau, bhantai, gahapati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau āyasmatau sāriputtassa pādai sirasā vandati| aivañ‍ca vadati – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, āyasmā sāriputtau yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| adhivāsaisi khau āyasmā sāriputtau tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya āyasmatā ānandaina pacchāsamaṇaina yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau āyasmā sāriputtau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, gahapati, khamanīyaṃ kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ? kac‍ci dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti, nau abhik‍kamanti; paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau abhik‍kamau’’ti? ‘‘na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti|

‘‘yathārūpaina khau, gahapati, buddhai appasādaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati  tathārūpau tai buddhai appasādau natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, buddhai avaic‍cappasādau – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| tañ‍ca pana tai buddhai avaic‍cappasādaṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpaina khau, gahapati, dhammai appasādaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā  apāyaṃ duggatiṃ  vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpau tai dhammai appasādau natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, dhammai avaic‍cappasādau – svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhīti| tañ‍ca pana tai dhammai avaic‍cappasādaṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpaina khau, gahapati, saṅghai appasādaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpau tai saṅghai appasādau natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, saṅghai avaic‍cappasādau – suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti| tañ‍ca pana tai saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpaina khau, gahapati, dussīlyaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpaṃ tai dussīlyaṃ natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, ariyakantāni sīlāni…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikāni| tāni ca pana tai ariyakantāni sīlāni attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpāya khau, gahapati, micchādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpā tai micchādiṭṭhi natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, sammādiṭṭhi| tañ‍ca pana tai sammādiṭṭhiṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpaina khau, gahapati, micchāsaṅkappaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpau tai micchāsaṅkappau natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, sammāsaṅkappau| tañ‍ca pana tai sammāsaṅkappaṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpāya  khau, gahapati, micchāvācāya saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpā tai micchāvācā natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, sammāvācā| tañ‍ca pana tai sammāvācaṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpaina khau, gahapati, micchākammantaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpau tai micchākammantau natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, sammākammantau| tañ‍ca pana tai sammākammantaṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpaina  khau, gahapati, micchāājīvaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpau tai micchāājīvau natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, sammāājīvau| tañ‍ca pana tai sammāājīvaṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpaina khau, gahapati, micchāvāyāmaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpau tai micchāvāyāmau natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, sammāvāyāmau| tañ‍ca pana tai sammāvāyāmaṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpāya khau, gahapati, micchāsatiyā saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpā tai micchāsati natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, sammāsati| tañ‍ca pana tai sammāsatiṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpaina  khau, gahapati, micchāsamādhinā saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpau tai micchāsamādhi natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, sammāsamādhi| tañ‍ca pana tai sammāsamādhiṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpaina  khau, gahapati, micchāñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpaṃ tai micchāñāṇaṃ natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, sammāñāṇaṃ| tañ‍ca pana tai sammāñāṇaṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyya|

‘‘yathārūpāya khau, gahapati, micchāvimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathārūpā tai micchāvimutti natthi| atthi ca khau tai, gahapati, sammāvimutti| tañ‍ca pana tai sammāvimuttiṃ attani samanupassatau ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhaiyyā’’ti|

atha khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa ṭhānasau vaidanā paṭippassambhiṃsu| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati āyasmantañ‍ca sāriputtaṃ āyasmantañ‍ca ānandaṃ sakainaiva thālipākaina parivisi| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcāsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ āyasmā sāriputtau imāhi gāthāhi anumaudi –

‘‘yassa saddhā tathāgatai, acalā suppatiṭṭhitā|

sīlañ‍ca yassa kalyāṇaṃ, ariyakantaṃ pasaṃsitaṃ||

‘‘saṅghai pasādau yassatthi, ujubhūtañ‍ca dassanaṃ|

adaliddauti adaḷiddauti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) taṃ āhu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

‘‘tasmā  saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ buddhānasāsana’’nti||

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘handa! kutau nu tvaṃ, ānanda, āgacchasi divādivassā’’ti? ‘‘āyasmatā, bhantai, sāriputtaina anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati iminā ca iminā ca auvādaina auvaditau’’ti| ‘‘paṇḍitau, ānanda, sāriputtau; mahāpañ‍ñau, ānanda, sāriputtau, yatra hi nāma cattāri sautāpattiyaṅgāni dasahākāraihi vibhajissatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyaanāthapiṇḍikasuttaṃ

1023. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau  gahapati añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina āyasmatau ānandassa pādai sirasā vanda – ‘anāthapiṇḍikau, bhantai, gahapati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau āyasmatau ānandassa pādai sirasā vandatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau purisau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa paṭissutvā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau purisau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘anāthapiṇḍikau, bhantai, gahapati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau āyasmatau ānandassa pādai sirasā vandati| aivañ‍ca vadati – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa  gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| adhivāsaisi khau āyasmā ānandau tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau āyasmā ānandau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ  gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, gahapati, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ? kac‍ci dukkhā  vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti, nau abhik‍kamanti; paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau abhik‍kamau’’ti? ‘‘na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ| bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti|

‘‘catūhi  khau, gahapati, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa assutavatau puthuj‍janassa hauti uttāsau, hauti chambhitattaṃ, hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ| katamaihi catūhi? idha, gahapati, assutavā puthuj‍janau buddhai appasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| tañ‍ca panassa buddhai appasādaṃ attani samanupassatau hauti uttāsau, hauti chambhitattaṃ, hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, assutavā puthuj‍janau dhammai appasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| tañ‍ca panassa dhammai appasādaṃ attani samanupassatau hauti uttāsau, hauti chambhitattaṃ, hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, assutavā puthuj‍janau saṅghai appasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| tañ‍ca panassa saṅghai appasādaṃ attani samanupassatau hauti uttāsau, hauti chambhitattaṃ, hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, assutavā puthuj‍janau dussīlyaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| tañ‍ca panassa dussīlyaṃ attani samanupassatau hauti uttāsau, hauti chambhitattaṃ, hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ| imaihi khau, gahapati, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa assutavatau  puthuj‍janassa hauti uttāsau, hauti chambhitattaṃ, hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ|

‘‘catūhi  khau, gahapati, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa sutavatau ariyasāvakassa na hauti uttāsau, na hauti chambhitattaṃ, na hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ| katamaihi catūhi? idha, gahapati, sutavā ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| tañ‍ca panassa buddhai avaic‍cappasādaṃ attani samanupassatau na hauti uttāsau, na hauti chambhitattaṃ, na hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, sutavā ariyasāvakau dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau  viñ‍ñūhīti| tañ‍ca panassa dhammai avaic‍cappasādaṃ attani samanupassatau na hauti uttāsau, na hauti chambhitattaṃ, na hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, sutavā ariyasāvakau saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti| tañ‍ca panassa saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaṃ attani samanupassatau na hauti uttāsau, na hauti chambhitattaṃ, na hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, sutavā ariyasāvakau ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| tāni ca panassa ariyakantāni sīlāni  attani samanupassatau na  hauti uttāsau, na hauti chambhitattaṃ, na hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ| imaihi khau, gahapati, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa sutavatau ariyasāvakassa na hauti uttāsau, na hauti chambhitattaṃ, na hauti samparāyikaṃ maraṇabhaya’’nti|

‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhāyāmi| kyāhaṃ bhāyissāmi! ahañhi, bhantai, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau haumi – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau haumi – suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti| yāni cimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā gihisāmīcikāni sikkhāpadāni daisitāni, nāhaṃ taisaṃ kiñ‍ci attani khaṇḍaṃ samanupassāmī’’ti| ‘‘lābhā tai, gahapati, suladdhaṃ tai, gahapati! sautāpattiphalaṃ tayā, gahapati, byākata’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭhamabhayavairūpasantasuttaṃ

1024. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘yatau khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni ca haunti, catūhi ca sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, ariyau cassa ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni  khīṇatiracchānayauniyau (sabbattha) khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau; sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’|

‘‘katamāni  pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti? yaṃ, gahapati, pāṇātipātī pāṇātipātappac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ  vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyati| pāṇātipātā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti| yaṃ, gahapati, adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… yaṃ, gahapati, kāmaisumicchācārī…pai॰… yaṃ, gahapati, musāvādī…pai॰… yaṃ, gahapati, surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhāyī surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānappac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyati| surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti| imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘katamau cassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau  paṭic‍casamuppādañ‍ñaiva sādhukaṃ yaunisau manasi karauti – iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā  idaṃ uppaj‍jati; iti imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati; yadidaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau…pai॰… phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayamassa  ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau|

‘‘yatau khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, imaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, ayañ‍cassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau| sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau; sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyabhayavairūpasantasuttaṃ

1025. sāvatthinidānaṃ…pai॰… ‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, imaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, ayañ‍cassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau; sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau; sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. nandakalicchavisuttaṃ

1026. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau nandakau licchavimahāmattau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau nandakaṃ licchavimahāmattaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘catūhi  khau, nandaka, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau  hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau| katamaihi catūhi? idha, nandaka, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, nandaka, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau|

‘‘imaihi ca pana, nandaka, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau āyunā saṃyuttau hauti dibbainapi mānusainapi; vaṇṇaina saṃyuttau hauti dibbainapi mānusainapi; sukhaina saṃyuttau hauti dibbainapi mānusainapi; yasaina saṃyuttau hauti dibbainapi mānusainapi; ādhipataiyyaina saṃyuttau hauti dibbainapi mānusainapi| taṃ khau panāhaṃ, nandaka, nāñ‍ñassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā sutvā vadāmi| api ca yadaiva mayā sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ, tadaivāhaṃ vadāmī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai añ‍ñatarau purisau nandakaṃ licchavimahāmattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nahānakālau, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ dāni, bhaṇai, aitaina bāhiraina nahānaina| alamidaṃ ajjhattaṃ nahānaṃ bhavissati, yadidaṃ – bhagavati pasādau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

saraṇānivaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

mahānāmaina dvai vuttā, gaudhā ca saraṇā duvai|

duvai anāthapiṇḍikā, duvai vairabhayaina ca|

licchavī dasamau vuttau, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

4. puñ‍ñābhisandavaggau

1. paṭhamapuñ‍ñābhisandasuttaṃ

1027. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| ayaṃ paṭhamau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau…pai॰… pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhīti| ayaṃ dutiyau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti| ayaṃ tatiyau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ayaṃ catutthau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyapuñ‍ñābhisandasuttaṃ

1028. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau  bhagavā…pai॰…  satthā daivamanussānaṃ  buddhau bhagavāti| ayaṃ paṭhamau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| ayaṃ catutthau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. tatiyapuñ‍ñābhisandasuttaṃ

1029. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| ayaṃ paṭhamau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| ayaṃ catutthau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamadaivapadasuttaṃ

1030. sāvatthinidānaṃ| cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ daivapadāni avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyaudātānaṃ sattānaṃ pariyaudapanāya|

katamāni  cattāri? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau  bhagavāti| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ daivānaṃ daivapadaṃ avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyaudātānaṃ sattānaṃ pariyaudapanāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| idaṃ catutthaṃ daivānaṃ daivapadaṃ avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyaudātānaṃ sattānaṃ pariyaudapanāya| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri daivānaṃ daivapadāni avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyaudātānaṃ sattānaṃ pariyaudapanāyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyadaivapadasuttaṃ

1031. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ daivapadāni avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyaudātānaṃ sattānaṃ pariyaudapanāya|

‘‘katamāni cattāri? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘kiṃ nu khau daivānaṃ daivapada’nti? sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘abyābajjhaparamai khvāhaṃ aitarahi daivai  suṇāmi| na ca khau panāhaṃ kiñ‍ci byābādhaimi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā| addhāhaṃ daivapadadhammasaman‍nāgatau viharāmī’’’ti| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ daivānaṃ daivapadaṃ avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyaudātānaṃ sattānaṃ pariyaudapanāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘kiṃ nu khau daivānaṃ daivapada’nti? sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘abyābajjhaparamai  khvāhaṃ aitarahi daivai suṇāmi| na khau panāhaṃ kiñ‍ci byābādhaimi tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā| addhāhaṃ daivapadadhammasaman‍nāgatau viharāmī’ti| idaṃ catutthaṃ  daivānaṃ daivapadaṃ avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyaudātānaṃ sattānaṃ pariyaudapanāya| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri daivānaṃ daivapadāni avisuddhānaṃ sattānaṃ visuddhiyā apariyaudātānaṃ sattānaṃ pariyaudapanāyā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. daivasabhāgasuttaṃ

1032. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ attamanā daivā sabhāgataṃ kathainti| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| yā tā daivatā buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā itau cutā tatrūpapan‍nā tāsaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘yathārūpaina khau mayaṃ buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā tatau cutā idhūpapan‍nā, ariyasāvakaupi tathārūpaina buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau aihīti daivānaṃ santikai’’’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| yā tā daivatā ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatā itau cutā tatrūpapan‍nā tāsaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘yathārūpaihi khau mayaṃ ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatā tatau cutā idhūpapan‍nā, ariyasāvakaupi tathārūpaihi ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau aihīti daivānaṃ santikai’ti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ attamanā daivā sabhāgataṃ kathaintī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mahānāmasuttaṃ

1033. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kittāvatā  nu khau, bhantai, upāsakau hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, mahānāma, buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, saṅghaṃ  saraṇaṃ gatau hauti – aittāvatā khau, mahānāma, upāsakau hautī’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, upāsakau sīlasampan‍nau hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, mahānāma, upāsakau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti, – aittāvatā khau, mahānāma, upāsakau sīlasampan‍nau hautī’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, upāsakau saddhāsampan‍nau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, mahānāma, upāsakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| aittāvatā khau, mahānāma, upāsakau saddhāsampan‍nau hautī’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, upāsakau cāgasampan‍nau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, mahānāma, upāsakau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau  payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau – aittāvatā khau, mahānāma, upāsakau cāgasampan‍nau hautī’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, upāsakau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, mahānāma, upāsakau pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā – aittāvatā khau, mahānāma, upāsakau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. vassasuttaṃ

1034. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uparipabbatai thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai taṃ udakaṃ yathānin‍naṃ pavattamānaṃ pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūraiti, pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūrā kusaubbhai paripūrainti, kusaubbhā paripūrā mahāsaubbhai paripūrainti, mahāsaubbhā paripūrā kun‍nadiyau paripūrainti, kun‍nadiyau paripūrā mahānadiyau paripūrainti, mahānadiyau paripūrā mahāsamuddaṃ mahāsamuddasāgaraṃ (sabbattha) saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.70 paripūrainti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa yau ca buddhai avaic‍cappasādau, yau ca dhammai  avaic‍cappasādau, yau ca saṅghai  avaic‍cappasādau, yāni ca ariyakantāni sīlāni – imai dhammā sandamānā pāraṃ gantvā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kāḷigaudhasuttaṃ

1035. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina kāḷigaudhāya sākiyāniyā nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau kāḷigaudhā sākiyānī  yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kāḷigaudhaṃ sākiyāniṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘catūhi khau, gaudhai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā ariyasāvikā sautāpan‍nā hauti avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā| katamaihi catūhi? idha, gaudhai, ariyasāvikā buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… vigatamalamacchairaina  caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgā payatapāṇinī payatapāṇī (sabbattha) 3.30 mauggal‍lānasuttaṃ aulaukaitabbaṃ vaussaggaratā yācayaugā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā| imaihi khau, gaudhai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā ariyasāvikā sautāpan‍nā hauti avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’ti|

‘‘yānimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā cattāri sautāpattiyaṅgāni daisitāni, saṃvij‍jantai tai dhammā mayi, ahañ‍ca taisu dhammaisu sandissāmi| ahañhi, bhantai, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci kulai daiyyadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ appaṭivibhattaṃ sīlavantaihi kalyāṇadhammaihī’’ti| ‘‘lābhā tai, gaudhai, suladdhaṃ tai, gaudhai! sautāpattiphalaṃ tayā, gaudhai, byākata’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. nandiyasak‍kasuttaṃ

1036. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau nandiyau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau  nandiyau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yassaiva nu khau, bhantai, ariyasāvakassa cattāri sautāpattiyaṅgāni sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ natthi svaiva nu khau, bhantai, ariyasāvakau pamādavihārī’’ti|

‘‘‘yassa khau, nandiya, cattāri sautāpattiyaṅgāni sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ natthi tamahaṃ bāhirau puthuj‍janapakkhai ṭhitau’ti vadāmi| api ca, nandiya, yathā ariyasāvakau pamādavihārī caiva hauti, appamādavihārī ca taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau nandiyau sak‍kau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘kathañ‍ca, nandiya, ariyasāvakau pamādavihārī hauti? idha nandiya, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| sau taina buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina santuṭṭhau na uttari vāyamati divā pavivaikāya, rattiṃ paṭisal‍lānāya| tassa aivaṃ pamattassa viharatau pāmauj‍jaṃ na hauti| pāmauj‍jai asati, pīti na hauti| pītiyā asati, passaddhi na hauti| passaddhiyā asati, dukkhaṃ viharati| dukkhinau cittaṃ na samādhiyati| asamāhitai cittai dhammā na pātubhavanti| dhammānaṃ apātubhāvā pamādavihārī tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nandiya, ariyasāvakau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti  akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| sau taihi ariyakantaihi sīlaihi santuṭṭhau na uttari vāyamati divā pavivaikāya rattiṃ paṭisal‍lānāya| tassa aivaṃ pamattassa viharatau pāmauj‍jaṃ na hauti| pāmauj‍jai asati, pīti na hauti| pītiyā asati, passaddhi na hauti| passaddhiyā asati, dukkhaṃ viharati| dukkhinau cittaṃ na samādhiyati| asamāhitai cittai dhammā na pātubhavanti| dhammānaṃ apātubhāvā pamādavihārī tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| aivaṃ khau, nandiya, ariyasāvakau pamādavihārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, nandiya, ariyasāvakau appamādavihārī hauti? idha, nandiya, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| sau taina buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina asantuṭṭhau uttari vāyamati divā pavivaikāya rattiṃ paṭisal‍lānāya| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa viharatau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa  pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| samāhitai cittai dhammā pātubhavanti| dhammānaṃ pātubhāvā appamādavihārī tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, nandiya, ariyasāvakau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| sau taihi ariyakantaihi sīlaihi asantuṭṭhau uttari vāyamati divā pavivaikāya rattiṃ paṭisal‍lānāya| tassa aivaṃ appamattassa viharatau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati| pamuditassa pīti jāyati| pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati| sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| samāhitai cittai dhammā pātubhavanti| dhammānaṃ pātubhāvā appamādavihārī tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| aivaṃ khau, nandiya, ariyasāvakau appamādavihārī hautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

puñ‍ñābhisandavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

abhisandā tayau vuttā, duvai daivapadāni ca|

sabhāgataṃ mahānāmau, vassaṃ kāḷī ca nandiyāti||

5. sagāthakapuñ‍ñābhisandavaggau

1. paṭhamaabhisandasuttaṃ

1037. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā, kusalābhisandā, sukhassāhārā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| ayaṃ paṭhamau puñ‍ñābhisandau, kusalābhisandau, sukhassāhārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ayaṃ catutthau  puñ‍ñābhisandau, kusalābhisandau, sukhassāhārau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puñ‍ñābhisandā, kusalābhisandā, sukhassāhārā|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi puñ‍ñābhisandaihi kusalābhisandaihi saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇaituṃ – ‘aittakau puñ‍ñābhisandau, kusalābhisandau, sukhassāhārau’ti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai na sukaraṃ udakassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇaituṃ – ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakānī’ti vā ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakasatānī’ti vā ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakasahassānī’ti vā ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakasatasahassānī’ti vāti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau  appamaiyyau mahāudakakkhandhau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imaihi catūhi puñ‍ñābhisandaihi kusalābhisandaihi saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇaituṃ – ‘aittakau puñ‍ñābhisandau, kusalābhisandau, sukhassāhārau’ti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘mahaudadhiṃ aparimitaṃ mahāsaraṃ,

bahubhairavaṃ ratanagaṇānamālayaṃ|

naj‍jau yathā naragaṇasaṅghasaivitā,

puthū savantī upayanti sāgaraṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ naraṃ an‍napānavatthadadaṃ,

saiyyāni pac‍cattharaṇassa saj‍jattharaṇassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dāyakaṃ|

puñ‍ñassa dhārā upayanti paṇḍitaṃ,

naj‍jau yathā vārivahāva sāgara’’nti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyaabhisandasuttaṃ

1038. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā, kusalābhisandā, sukhassāhārā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| ayaṃ paṭhamau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| ayaṃ catutthau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puñ‍ñābhisandā, kusalābhisandā, sukhassāhārā|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi puñ‍ñābhisandaihi kusalābhisandaihi saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇaituṃ – ‘aittakau puñ‍ñābhisandau, kusalābhisandau, sukhassāhārau’ti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yatthimā mahānadiyau saṃsandanti samainti, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, tattha na sukaraṃ udakassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇaituṃ – ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakānī’ti vā ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakasatānī’ti vā ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakasahassānī’ti vā ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakasatasahassānī’ti vāti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāudakakkhandhau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imaihi catūhi puñ‍ñābhisandaihi kusalābhisandaihi saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇaituṃ – ‘aittakau puñ‍ñābhisandau, kusalābhisandau, sukhassāhārau’ti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchatī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā…pai॰… satthā –

‘‘mahaudadhiṃ aparimitaṃ mahāsaraṃ,

bahubhairavaṃ ratanagaṇānamālayaṃ|

naj‍jau yathā naragaṇasaṅghasaivitā,

puthū savantī upayanti sāgaraṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ  naraṃ an‍napānavatthadadaṃ,

saiyyāni pac‍cattharaṇassa dāyakaṃ|

puñ‍ñassa dhārā upayanti paṇḍitaṃ,

naj‍jau yathā vārivahāva sāgara’’nti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. tatiyaabhisandasuttaṃ

1039. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā, kusalābhisandā, sukhassāhārā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| ayaṃ paṭhamau puñ‍ñābhisandau, kusalābhisandau, sukhassāhārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau  ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| ayaṃ catutthau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi puñ‍ñābhisandaihi kusalābhisandaihi saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇaituṃ – ‘aittakau puñ‍ñābhisandau, kusalābhisandau, sukhassāhārau’ti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhau tvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchatī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā…pai॰… satthā –

‘‘yau puñ‍ñakāmau kusalai patiṭṭhitau,

bhāvaiti maggaṃ amatassa pattiyā|

sau dhammasārādhigamau khayai ratau,

na vaidhati mac‍curājāgamanasmi’’nti mac‍curājāgamissatīti (sī॰ pī॰), mac‍cujarākampisminti (ka॰)|| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamamahaddhanasuttaṃ

1040. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ‘aḍḍhau mahaddhanau  mahābhaugau’ti mahābhaugau mahāyasauti (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) vuc‍cati|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti; dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ‘aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyamahaddhanasuttaṃ

1041. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ‘aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau mahāyasau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ‘aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau mahāyasau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. suddhakasuttaṃ

1042. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi  …pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. nandiyasuttaṃ

1043. kapilavatthunidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau nandiyaṃ sak‍kaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘catūhi khau, nandiya, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’|

‘‘katamaihi  catūhi? idha, nandiya, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, nandiya, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. bhaddiyasuttaṃ

1044. kapilavatthunidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau bhaddiyaṃ sak‍kaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘catūhi khau, bhaddiya, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhaddiya, ariyasāvakau buddhai…pai॰… dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, bhaddiya, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. mahānāmasuttaṃ

1045. kapilavatthunidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘catūhi khau, mahānāma, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti…pai॰… sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau buddhai…pai॰… dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, mahānāma, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. aṅgasuttaṃ

1046. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, sautāpattiyaṅgāni| katamāni cattāri? sappurisasaṃsaivau, saddhammassavanaṃ, yaunisaumanasikārau, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri sautāpattiyaṅgānī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sagāthakapuñ‍ñābhisandavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

abhisandā tayau vuttā, duvai mahaddhanaina ca|

suddhaṃ nandiyaṃ bhaddiyaṃ, mahānāmaṅgaina tai dasāti||

6. sappañ‍ñavaggau

1. sagāthakasuttaṃ

1047. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘yassa  saddhā tathāgatai, acalā suppatiṭṭhitā|

sīlañ‍ca yassa kalyāṇaṃ, ariyakantaṃ pasaṃsitaṃ||

‘‘saṅghai pasādau yassatthi, ujubhūtañ‍ca dassanaṃ|

adaliddauti taṃ āhu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||
