‘‘tasmā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ buddhānasāsana’’nti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vassaṃvutthasuttaṃ

1048. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sāvatthiyaṃ vassaṃvutthau kapilavatthuṃ anuppattau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| assausuṃ khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā – ‘‘añ‍ñatarau kira bhikkhu  sāvatthiyaṃ vassaṃvutthau kapilavatthuṃ anuppattau’’ti|

atha khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau kāpilavatthavā sakyā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhantai, bhagavā araugau caiva balavā cā’’ti? ‘‘araugau cāvusau, bhagavā balavā cā’’ti| ‘‘kac‍ci  pana, bhantai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānā araugā caiva balavantau cā’’ti? ‘‘sāriputtamauggal‍lānāpi khau, āvusau, araugā caiva balavantau cā’’ti| ‘‘kac‍ci pana, bhantai, bhikkhusaṅghau araugau ca balavā cā’’ti| ‘‘bhikkhusaṅghaupi khau, āvusau, araugau ca balavā cā’’ti| ‘‘atthi pana tai, bhantai, kiñ‍ci iminā antaravassaina bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahita’’nti? ‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘appakā tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yai āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| atha khau aitaiva bahutarā bhikkhū yai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyinau anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā’’’ti|

‘‘aparampi  khau mai, āvusau, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘appakā tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yai pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyinau anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā| atha khau aitaiva bahutarā bhikkhū  yai tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāminau, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissantī’’’ti|

‘‘aparampi khau mai, āvusau, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘appakā tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yai tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāminau, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā  dukkhassantaṃ karissanti| atha khau aitaiva bahutarā bhikkhū yai tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyaṇā’’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dhammadin‍nasuttaṃ

1049. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| atha  khau dhammadin‍nau upāsakau pañ‍cahi upāsakasataihi saddhiṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau dhammadin‍nau upāsakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘auvadatu nau, bhantai, bhagavā; anusāsatu nau, bhantai, bhagavā yaṃ amhākaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha vau, dhammadin‍naṃ, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yai tai suttantā tathāgatabhāsitā gambhīrā gambhīratthā laukuttarā suñ‍ñatapaṭisaṃyuttā tai kālaina kālaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, dhammadin‍na, sikkhitabba’’nti| ‘‘na khau naitaṃ, bhantai, sukaraṃ amhaihi puttasambādhasayanaṃ ajjhāvasantaihi kāsikacandanaṃ pac‍canubhauntaihi mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ dhārayantaihi jātarūparajataṃ sādiyantaihi – yai tai suttantā tathāgatabhāsitā gambhīrā  gambhīratthā laukuttarā suñ‍ñatapaṭisaṃyuttā tai kālaina kālaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, bhagavā amhākaṃ pañ‍casu sikkhāpadaisu ṭhitānaṃ uttaridhammaṃ daisaitū’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha  vau, dhammadin‍na, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihī’ti| aivañhi vau, dhammadin‍na, sikkhitabba’’nti|

‘‘yānimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā cattāri sautāpattiyaṅgāni daisitāni, saṃvij‍jantai tai dhammā amhaisu, mayañ‍ca taisu dhammaisu sandissāma| mayañhi bhantai, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā – itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau  bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatā akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihī’’ti| ‘‘lābhā vau, dhammadin‍na, suladdhaṃ vau, dhammadin‍na! sautāpattiphalaṃ tumhaihi byākata’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. gilānasuttaṃ

1050. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū bhagavatau cīvarakammaṃ karaunti – ‘‘niṭṭhitacīvarau bhagavā taimāsac‍cayaina cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’’ti| assausi khau mahānāmau sak‍kau – ‘‘sambahulā kira bhikkhū bhagavatau cīvarakammaṃ karaunti – ‘niṭṭhitacīvarau bhagavā taimāsac‍cayaina cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’’’ti| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutamaitaṃ, bhantai – ‘sambahulā kira bhikkhū bhagavatau cīvarakammaṃ karaunti – niṭṭhitacīvarau bhagavā taimāsac‍cayaina cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti| na khau naitaṃ na khau tai aitaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), bhantai, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ sappañ‍ñaina upāsakaina sappañ‍ñau upāsakau ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau auvaditabbau’’ti|

‘‘sappañ‍ñaina mahānāma, upāsakaina sappañ‍ñau upāsakau ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau catūhi assāsanīyaihi dhammaihi assāsaitabbau – ‘assāsatāyasmā – atthāyasmatau buddhai avaic‍cappasādau itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavāti| assāsatāyasmā – atthāyasmatau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikānī’’’ti|

‘‘sappañ‍ñaina, mahānāma, upāsakaina sappañ‍ñau upāsakau ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau imaihi catūhi assāsanīyaihi dhammaihi assāsaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘atthāyasmatau mātāpitūsu apaikkhā’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘atthi mai mātāpitūsu apaikkhā’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmā khau mārisau maraṇadhammau| sacai pāyasmā mātāpitūsu apaikkhaṃ karissati, marissataiva; nau cai  pāyasmā  mātāpitūsu apaikkhaṃ karissati, marissataiva| sādhāyasmā, yā tai mātāpitūsu apaikkhā taṃ pajahā’’’ti|

‘‘sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yā mai mātāpitūsu apaikkhā sā pahīnā’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘atthi panāyasmatau puttadāraisu apaikkhā’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘atthi mai puttadāraisu apaikkhā’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘āyasmā khau mārisau maraṇadhammau| sacai pāyasmā puttadāraisu apaikkhaṃ karissati, marissataiva; nau cai pāyasmā puttadāraisu apaikkhaṃ karissati, marissataiva| sādhāyasmā, yā tai puttadāraisu apaikkhā taṃ pajahā’’’ti|

‘‘sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yā mai puttadāraisu apaikkhā sā pahīnā’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘atthi panāyasmatau mānusakaisu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu apaikkhā’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘atthi mai mānusakaisu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu apaikkhā’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘mānusakaihi khau, āvusau, kāmaihi dibbā kāmā abhik‍kantatarā ca paṇītatarā ca| sādhāyasmā, mānusakaihi kāmaihi cittaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā cātumahārājikaisu cātummahārājikaisu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) daivaisu cittaṃ adhimaucaihī’’’ti|

‘‘sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘mānusakaihi mai kāmaihi cittaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ, cātumahārājikaisu daivaisu cittaṃ adhimaucita’nti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘cātumahārājikaihi khau, āvusau, daivaihi tāvatiṃsā daivā abhik‍kantatarā ca paṇītatarā ca| sādhāyasmā, cātumahārājikaihi  daivaihi cittaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā tāvatiṃsaisu daivaisu cittaṃ adhimaucaihī’’’ti|

‘‘sau  cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘cātumahārājikaihi mai daivaihi cittaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ, tāvatiṃsaisu daivaisu cittaṃ adhimaucita’nti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘tāvatiṃsaihi khau, āvusau, daivaihi yāmā daivā…pai॰… tusitā daivā…pai॰… nimmānaratī daivā…pai॰… paranimmitavasavattī daivā…pai॰… paranimmitavasavattīhi khau, āvusau, daivaihi brahmalaukau abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| sādhāyasmā, paranimmitavasavattīhi daivaihi cittaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā brahmalaukai cittaṃ adhimaucaihī’ti| sau  cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘paranimmitavasavattīhi mai daivaihi cittaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ, brahmalaukai cittaṃ adhimaucita’nti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘brahmalaukaupi khau, āvusau, anic‍cau addhuvau sak‍kāyapariyāpan‍nau| sādhāyasmā, brahmalaukā cittaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā sak‍kāyaniraudhai cittaṃ upasaṃharāhī’’’ti|

‘‘sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘brahmalaukā mai cittaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ, sak‍kāyaniraudhai cittaṃ upasaṃharāmī’ti; aivaṃ vimuttacittassa khau, mahānāma, upāsakassa āsavā vassasata (sī॰ syā॰) vimuttacittaina bhikkhunā na kiñ‍ci nānākaraṇaṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ – vimuttiyā vimutta’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. sautāpattiphalasuttaṃ

1051. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? sappurisasaṃsaivau, saddhammassavanaṃ, yaunisaumanasikārau, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sakadāgāmiphalasuttaṃ

1052. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? …pai॰… saṃvattantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. anāgāmiphalasuttaṃ

1053. …pai॰…  anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya…pai॰… saṃvattantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. arahattaphalasuttaṃ

1054. …pai॰… arahattaphalasacchikiriyāya…pai॰… saṃvattantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pañ‍ñāpaṭilābhasuttaṃ

1055. …pai॰… pañ‍ñāpaṭilābhāya…pai॰… saṃvattantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. pañ‍ñāvuddhisuttaṃ

1056. …pai॰… pañ‍ñāvuddhiyā  …pai॰… saṃvattantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. pañ‍ñāvaipul‍lasuttaṃ

1057. …pai॰…| pañ‍ñāvaipul‍lāya…pai॰… saṃvattantī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

sappañ‍ñavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sagāthakaṃ  vassaṃvutthaṃ, dhammadin‍nañ‍ca gilānaṃ|

caturau phalā paṭilābhau, vuddhi vaipul‍latāya cāti||

7. mahāpañ‍ñavaggau

1. mahāpañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1058. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahāpañ‍ñatāya  saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? sappurisasaṃsaivau, saddhammassavanaṃ, yaunisaumanasikārau, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahāpañ‍ñatā  saṃvattantī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. puthupañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1059. … puthupañ‍ñatā saṃvattantī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. vipulapañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1060. … vipulapañ‍ñatā saṃvattantī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. gambhīrapañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1061. … gambhīrapañ‍ñatā saṃvattantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. appamattapañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1062. …  appamattapañ‍ñatā  saṃvattantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. bhūripañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1063. … bhūripañ‍ñatā saṃvattantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. pañ‍ñābāhul‍lasuttaṃ

1064. … pañ‍ñābāhul‍lā saṃvattantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. sīghapañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1065. … sīghapañ‍ñatā saṃvattantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. lahupañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1066. … lahupañ‍ñatā saṃvattantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. hāsapañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1067. … hāsapañ‍ñatā saṃvattantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. javanapañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1068. … javanapañ‍ñatā  saṃvattantī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. tikkhapañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1069. …  tikkhapañ‍ñatā saṃvattantī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. nibbaidhikapañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1070. … nibbaidhikapañ‍ñatā saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? sappurisasaṃsaivau, saddhammassavanaṃ, yaunisaumanasikārau, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti – imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā nibbaidhikapañ‍ñatāya saṃvattantī’’ti| tairasamaṃ|

mahāpañ‍ñavaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

mahā  puthu vipula-gambhīraṃ, appamatta-bhūri-bāhul‍laṃ|

sīgha-lahu-hāsa-javana, tikkha-nibbaidhikāya cāti||

sautāpattisaṃyuttaṃ aikādasamaṃ|



12. sac‍casaṃyuttaṃ

1. samādhivaggau

1. samādhisuttaṃ

1071. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha| samāhitau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| kiñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha| samāhitau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭisal‍lānasuttaṃ

1072. ‘‘paṭisal‍lānai, bhikkhavai, yaugamāpaj‍jatha| paṭisal‍līnau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| kiñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| paṭisal‍lānai, bhikkhavai, yaugamāpaj‍jatha| paṭisal‍līnau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yaugau  karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamakulaputtasuttaṃ

1073. ‘‘yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ kulaputtā sammā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajiṃsu, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamayāya| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ kulaputtā sammā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissanti, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamayāya| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kulaputtā sammā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, sabbai tai catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamayāya|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa dukkhasamudayassa ariyasac‍cassa dukkhaniraudhassa ariyasac‍cassa dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ kulaputtā sammā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajiṃsu…pai॰… pabbajissanti…pai॰… pabbajanti, sabbai tai imaisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamayāya|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyakulaputtasuttaṃ

1074. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ kulaputtā sammā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaisuṃ, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaisuṃ| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ kulaputtā sammā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā  yathābhūtaṃ  abhisamaissanti, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaissanti| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kulaputtā sammā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā yathābhūtaṃ abhisamainti, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisamainti|

‘‘katamāni cattāri? dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ kulaputtā sammā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaisuṃ  …pai॰… abhisamaissanti…pai॰… abhisamainti, sabbai tai imāni cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisamainti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

1075. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambaujjhiṃsu, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisambaujjhiṃsu| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambaujjhissanti, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisambaujjhissanti| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambaujjhanti, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisambaujjhanti|

‘‘katamāni cattāri? dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā  vā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambaujjhiṃsu…pai॰… abhisambaujjhissanti…pai॰… abhisambaujjhanti, sabbai tai imāni cattāri ariyasac‍cāni  yathābhūtaṃ abhisambaujjhanti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

1076. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ pakāsaisuṃ, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ pakāsaisuṃ| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ pakāsaissanti, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ pakāsaissanti| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ pakāsainti, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ pakāsainti|

‘‘katamāni  cattāri? dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ pakāsaisuṃ…pai॰… pakāsaissanti…pai॰… pakāsainti, sabbai tai imāni cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisambuddhaṃ pakāsainti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vitak‍kasuttaṃ

1077. ‘‘mā, bhikkhavai, pāpakai akusalai vitak‍kai vitak‍kaiyyātha vitak‍kaitha (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), saiyyathidaṃ  – kāmavitak‍kaṃ, byāpādavitak‍kaṃ, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? naitai, bhikkhavai, vitak‍kā atthasaṃhitā nādibrahmacariyakā  na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘vitak‍kaintā ca khau tumhai, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti vitak‍kaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti  vitak‍kaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti vitak‍kaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti vitak‍kaiyyātha| taṃ kissa haitu? aitai, bhikkhavai, vitak‍kā atthasaṃhitā aitai ādibrahmacariyakā aitai nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. cintasuttaṃ

1078. ‘‘mā, bhikkhavai, pāpakaṃ akusalaṃ cittaṃ cintaiyyātha cintaitha (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘antavā laukau’ti vā ‘anantavā laukau’ti vā, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti  tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā| taṃ kissa haitu? naisā, bhikkhavai, cintā atthasaṃhitā nādibrahmacariyakā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya  na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘cintaintā ca khau tumhai, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti cintaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti cintaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti cintaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti cintaiyyātha| taṃ kissa haitu? aisā, bhikkhavai, cintā atthasaṃhitā, aisā ādibrahmacariyakā, aisā nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya  sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. viggāhikakathāsuttaṃ

1079. ‘‘mā, bhikkhavai, viggāhikakathaṃ kathaiyyātha kathaitha (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) – ‘na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi, ahaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāmi| kiṃ tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānissasi! micchāpaṭipan‍nau tvamasi, ahamasmi sammāpaṭipan‍nau| sahitaṃ mai, asahitaṃ tai| puraivacanīyaṃ pacchā avaca, pacchāvacanīyaṃ purai avaca| adhiciṇṇaṃ aciṇṇaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tai viparāvattaṃ| āraupitau tai vādau, cara vādappamaukkhāya| niggahitausi, nibbaiṭhaihi vā sacai pahausī’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? naisā, bhikkhavai, kathā atthasaṃhitā nādibrahmacariyakā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘kathaintā ca khau tumhai, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti kathaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti kathaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti kathaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti kathaiyyātha…pai॰… yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tiracchānakathāsuttaṃ

1080. ‘‘mā, bhikkhavai, anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaiyyātha, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ, bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ, an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ, ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ itthikathaṃ purisakathaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ  kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ, pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ, laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā| taṃ kissa haitu? naisā, bhikkhavai, kathā atthasaṃhitā nādibrahmacariyakā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘kathaintā ca khau tumhai, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti kathaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti kathaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti kathaiyyātha, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti kathaiyyātha| taṃ kissa haitu? aisā, bhikkhavai, kathā atthasaṃhitā, aisā ādibrahmacariyakā, aisā nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

samādhivaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

samādhi  paṭisal‍lānā, kulaputtā aparai duvai|

samaṇabrāhmaṇā vitak‍kaṃ, cintā viggāhikā kathāti||

2. dhammacak‍kappavattanavaggau

1. dhammacak‍kappavattanasuttaṃ

1081. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| tatra  khau bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, antā pabbajitaina  na saivitabbā| katamai dvai? yau cāyaṃ kāmaisu kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugau hīnau gammau pauthuj‍janikau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau, yau cāyaṃ attakilamathānuyaugau dukkhau anariyau anatthasaṃhitau| aitai khau, bhikkhavai, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati’’|

‘‘katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau sā, bhikkhavai, majjhimā paṭipadā tathāgataina abhisambuddhā cakkhukaraṇī ñāṇakaraṇī upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘idaṃ  khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ – jātipi dukkhā, jarāpi  dukkhā, byādhipi dukkhau, maraṇampi dukkhaṃ, appiyaihi sampayaugau dukkhau, piyaihi vippayaugau dukkhau, yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ – saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhāpi (pī॰ ka॰) dukkhā| idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ – yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubbhavikā paunaubhavikā (sī॰ pī॰) nandirāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī, saiyyathidaṃ saiyyathīdaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) – kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā| idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ – yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau| idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā  ariyasac‍caṃ – ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍ca’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñaiyya’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai…pai॰… udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñāta’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘idaṃ  dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍ca’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pahātabba’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai…pai॰…  udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pahīna’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘idaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍ca’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ sacchikātabba’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai…pai॰… udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ sacchikata’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘idaṃ  dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍ca’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ bhāvaitabba’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai…pai॰… udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ bhāvita’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca mai, bhikkhavai, imaisu catūsu ariyasac‍caisu aivaṃ tiparivaṭṭaṃ dvādasākāraṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ na suvisuddhaṃ ahausi, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā  pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ abhisambuddhau pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

‘‘yatau ca khau mai, bhikkhavai, imaisu catūsu ariyasac‍caisu aivaṃ tiparivaṭṭaṃ dvādasākāraṃ yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ ahausi, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti  caitauvimutti (sī॰ pī॰), ayamantimā jāti, natthidāni punabbhavau’’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai āyasmatau kauṇḍañ‍ñassa virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti|

pavattitai ca pana bhagavatā dhammacak‍kai bhummā daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘‘aitaṃ bhagavatā bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti| bhummānaṃ daivānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā cātumahārājikā daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘‘aitaṃ bhagavatā bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ, appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā  daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti| cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā tāvatiṃsā  daivā…pai॰… yāmā daivā…pai॰… tusitā daivā…pai॰… nimmānaratī daivā…pai॰… paranimmitavasavattī daivā…pai॰… brahmakāyikā daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘‘aitaṃ bhagavatā bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ  samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti|

itiha taina khaṇaina (taina layaina) ( ) natthi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) taina muhuttaina yāva brahmalaukā saddau abbhuggacchi| ayañ‍ca dasasahassilaukadhātu saṅkampi sampakampi sampavaidhi, appamāṇau ca uḷārau aubhāsau laukai pāturahausi atik‍kamma daivānaṃ daivānubhāvanti|

atha khau bhagavā imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘añ‍ñāsi vata, bhau, kauṇḍañ‍ñau, añ‍ñāsi vata, bhau, kauṇḍañ‍ñau’’ti! iti hidaṃ āyasmatau kauṇḍañ‍ñassa ‘añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñau’ tvaiva nāmaṃ ahausīti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. tathāgatasuttaṃ

1082. ‘‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍ca’nti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñaiyya’nti  bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai…pai॰… udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñāta’nti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ  udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘idaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍ca’nti bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pahātabba’nti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai…pai॰… udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pahīna’nti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘idaṃ  dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍ca’nti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ sacchikātabba’nti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai…pai॰ … udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ sacchikata’nti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘‘idaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍ca’nti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ bhāvaitabba’nti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai…pai॰… udapādi| ‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ bhāvita’nti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ pubbai  ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. khandhasuttaṃ

1083. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cāni| katamāni cattāri? dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ dukkhasamudayau ariyasac‍caṃ dukkhaniraudhau ariyasac‍caṃ (syā॰) dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? ‘pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā’ tissa vacanīyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ katamai pañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubbhavikā nandirāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ| imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri ariyasac‍cāni|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. ajjhattikāyatanasuttaṃ

1084. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cāni| katamāni cattāri? dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? ‘cha ajjhattikāni āyatanānī’ tissa vacanīyaṃ| katamāni cha? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… manāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubbhavikā nandirāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau  cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ| imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri ariyasac‍cāni|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamadhāraṇasuttaṃ

1085. ‘‘dhāraitha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, mayā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānī’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, dhāraimi bhagavatā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānī’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, dhāraisi mayā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānī’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā paṭhamaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi; dukkhasamudayaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dutiyaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi; dukkhaniraudhaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā tatiyaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi; dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā catutthaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi| aivaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, dhāraimi bhagavatā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, dhāraisi mayā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānīti| dukkhaṃ khau, bhikkhu, mayā paṭhamaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ, tathā naṃ dhāraihi; dukkhasamudayaṃ dukkhasamudayau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) khau, bhikkhu, mayā dutiyaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ, tathā naṃ dhāraihi; dukkhaniraudhaṃ dukkhaniraudhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) khau, bhikkhu, mayā tatiyaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ, tathā naṃ dhāraihi; dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā dukkhaniraudhagāminipaṭipadaṃ (pī॰), dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ (ka॰) khau, bhikkhu, mayā catutthaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ, tathā naṃ dhāraihi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, dhāraihi mayā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānīti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhu, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyadhāraṇasuttaṃ

1086. ‘‘dhāraitha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, mayā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānī’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, dhāraimi bhagavatā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānī’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, dhāraisi mayā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānī’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ  khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā paṭhamaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi| yau hi kauci, bhantai, samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘naitaṃ dukkhaṃ paṭhamaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yaṃ samaṇaina gautamaina daisitaṃ| ahamaitaṃ dukkhaṃ paṭhamaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pac‍cakkhāya añ‍ñaṃ dukkhaṃ paṭhamaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| dukkhasamudayaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā catutthaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ dhāraimi| yau hi kauci, bhantai, samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘naitaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā catutthaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yaṃ samaṇaina gautamaina daisitaṃ| ahamaitaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ catutthaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pac‍cakkhāya añ‍ñaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ catutthaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| aivaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, dhāraimi bhagavatā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, dhāraisi mayā cattāri  ariyasac‍cāni daisitānīti| dukkhaṃ khau, bhikkhu, mayā paṭhamaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ, tathā naṃ dhāraihi| yau hi kauci, bhikkhu, samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘naitaṃ dukkhaṃ paṭhamaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yaṃ samaṇaina gautamaina daisitaṃ| ahamaitaṃ dukkhaṃ paṭhamaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pac‍cakkhāya añ‍ñaṃ dukkhaṃ paṭhamaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| dukkhasamudayaṃ khau, bhikkhu…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhaṃ khau, bhikkhu…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā khau, bhikkhu, mayā catutthaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ daisitaṃ, tathā naṃ dhāraihi| yau hi kauci, bhikkhu, samaṇau vā  brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃ vadaiyya  – ‘naitaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā catutthaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yaṃ samaṇaina gautamaina daisitaṃ| ahamaitaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ catutthaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pac‍cakkhāya añ‍ñaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ catutthaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| aivaṃ khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, dhāraihi mayā cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitānīti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhu, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. avij‍jāsuttaṃ

1087. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘avij‍jā, avij‍jā’ti bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, bhantai, avij‍jā; kittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti? ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, avij‍jā; aittāvatā ca  avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhu, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. vij‍jāsuttaṃ

1088. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘vij‍jā, vij‍jā’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, bhantai, vij‍jā; kittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti? ‘‘yaṃ  khau, bhikkhu, dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, vij‍jā; aittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhu, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. saṅkāsanasuttaṃ

1089. ‘‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍ca’nti bhikkhavai, mayā pañ‍ñattaṃ| tattha aparimāṇā vaṇṇā aparimāṇā byañ‍janā aparimāṇā saṅkāsanā – ‘itipidaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍ca’nti; idaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ…pai॰… ‘idaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍ca’nti, bhikkhavai, mayā pañ‍ñattaṃ| tattha aparimāṇā vaṇṇā aparimāṇā byañ‍janā aparimāṇā saṅkāsanā – ‘itipidaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍ca’nti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tathasuttaṃ

1090. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni| katamāni cattāri? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti, bhikkhavai, tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaitaṃ; ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaitaṃ; ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaitaṃ; ‘ayaṃ  dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti tathamaitaṃ avitathamaitaṃ anañ‍ñathamaitaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

dhammacak‍kappavattanavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dhammacak‍kaṃ  tathāgataṃ, khandhā āyatanaina ca|

dhāraṇā ca dvai avij‍jā, vij‍jā saṅkāsanā tathāti||

3. kauṭigāmavaggau

1. paṭhamakauṭigāmasuttaṃ

1091. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaj‍jīsu viharati kauṭigāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘catun‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cānaṃ ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca’’|

‘‘katamaisaṃ  catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cassa ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| dukkhasamudayassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa ananubaudhā  appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ; ucchin‍nā bhavataṇhā, khīṇā bhavanaitti; natthidāni punabbhavau’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ, yathābhūtaṃ adassanā|

saṃsitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 2.155 dīghamaddhānaṃ, tāsu tāsvaiva jātisu||

‘‘tāni  yāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) aitāni diṭṭhāni, bhavanaitti samūhatā|

ucchin‍naṃ mūlaṃ dukkhassa, natthidāni punabbhavau’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyakauṭigāmasuttaṃ

1092. ‘‘yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca panaitai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā  vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā, tai  ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘yai dukkhaṃ nappajānanti, athau dukkhassa sambhavaṃ|

yattha ca sabbasau dukkhaṃ, asaisaṃ uparujjhati||

‘‘tañ‍ca maggaṃ na jānanti, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ|

caitauvimuttihīnā  tai, athau pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā|

abhabbā tai antakiriyāya, tai vai jātijarūpagā||

‘‘yai  ca dukkhaṃ pajānanti, athau dukkhassa sambhavaṃ|

yattha ca sabbasau dukkhaṃ, asaisaṃ uparujjhati||

‘‘tañ‍ca maggaṃ pajānanti, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ|

caitauvimuttisampan‍nā, athau pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā|

sabbā tai antakiriyāya, na tai jātijarūpagā’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. sammāsambuddhasuttaṃ

1093. sāvatthinidānaṃ| cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cāni| katamāni cattāri? dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri ariyasac‍cāni| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisambuddhattā tathāgatau ‘arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. arahantasuttaṃ

1094. sāvatthinidānaṃ| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambujjhiṃsu, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ  abhisambujjhiṃsu| yai  hi yaipi hi (bahūsu) kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambujjhissanti, sabbai tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisambujjhissanti| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi arahantau sammāsambuddhā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambujjhanti, sabbai  tai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisambujjhanti|

‘‘katamāni cattāri? dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ| yai hi, kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ  arahantau sammāsambuddhā yathābhūtaṃ abhisambujjhiṃsu…pai॰… abhisambujjhissanti…pai॰… abhisambujjhanti, sabbai tai imāni cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathābhūtaṃ abhisambujjhanti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. āsavakkhayasuttaṃ

1095. ‘‘jānatauhaṃ, bhikkhavai, passatau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi, nau ajānatau apassatau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, jānatau passatau āsavānaṃ khayau hauti? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti, bhikkhavai, jānatau passatau āsavānaṃ khayau hauti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti jānatau passatau āsavānaṃ khayau hauti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti jānatau passatau āsavānaṃ khayau hauti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti jānatau passatau āsavānaṃ khayau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau aivaṃ passatau āsavānaṃ khayau hauti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. mittasuttaṃ

1096. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anukampaiyyātha, yai ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyuṃ – mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā  – tai vau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamayāya samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā  patiṭṭhāpaitabbā|

‘‘katamaisaṃ  catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa, dukkhasamudayassa ariyasac‍cassa, dukkhaniraudhassa ariyasac‍cassa, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anukampaiyyātha, yai ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyuṃ – mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā  sālauhitā vā tai vau, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamayāya samādapaitabbā nivaisaitabbā patiṭṭhāpaitabbā|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tathasuttaṃ

1097. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cāni| katamāni cattāri? dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri ariyasac‍cāni tathāni avitathāni anañ‍ñathāni; tasmā ‘ariyasac‍cānī’ti vuc‍canti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. laukasuttaṃ

1098. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cāni| katamāni cattāri? dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ| sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya tathāgatau ariyau; tasmā ‘ariyasac‍cānī’ti vuc‍canti’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau  karaṇīyau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pariñ‍ñaiyyasuttaṃ

1099. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cāni| katamāni cattāri? dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri ariyasac‍cāni| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ atthi ariyasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, atthi ariyasac‍caṃ pahātabbaṃ, atthi ariyasac‍caṃ sacchikātabbaṃ, atthi ariyasac‍caṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ? dukkhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍caṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ pahātabbaṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ sacchikātabbaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. gavampatisuttaṃ

1100. aikaṃ samayaṃ sambahulā thairā bhikkhū caitaisu caitiyaisu (syā॰) viharanti sahañ‍canikai sahajaniyai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulānaṃ thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘yau nu khau, āvusau, dukkhaṃ passati dukkhasamudayampi sau passati, dukkhaniraudhampi passati, dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadampi passatī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā gavampati thairau gavampatitthairau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yau, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ passati dukkhasamudayampi sau passati, dukkhaniraudhampi passati, dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadampi passati| yau dukkhasamudayaṃ passati dukkhampi sau passati, dukkhaniraudhampi passati, dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadampi passati| yau dukkhaniraudhaṃ passati dukkhampi sau passati, dukkhasamudayampi passati, dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadampi  passati| yau dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ passati dukkhampi sau passati, dukkhasamudayampi passati, dukkhaniraudhampi passatī’’’ti| dasamaṃ|

kauṭigāmavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dvai vaj‍jī sammāsambuddhau, arahaṃ āsavakkhayau|

mittaṃ tathā ca laukau ca, pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ gavampatīti||

4. sīsapāvanavaggau

1. sīsapāvanasuttaṃ

1101. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati sīsapāvanai siṃsapāvanai (sī॰ pī॰)| atha khau bhagavā parittāni sīsapāpaṇṇāni pāṇinā gahaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yāni vā mayā parittāni sīsapāpaṇṇāni pāṇinā gahitāni yadidaṃ upari sīsapāvanai’’ti? ‘‘appamattakāni, bhantai, bhagavatā parittāni sīsapāpaṇṇāni pāṇinā gahitāni; atha khau aitānaiva bahutarāni yadidaṃ upari sīsapāvanai’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, aitadaiva bahutaraṃ yaṃ vau mayā abhiñ‍ñāya anakkhātaṃ| kasmā caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā anakkhātaṃ? na haitaṃ, bhikkhavai, atthasaṃhitaṃ nādibrahmacariyakaṃ na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati; tasmā taṃ mayā anakkhātaṃ’’|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mayā akkhātaṃ? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti, bhikkhavai, mayā akkhātaṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti mayā akkhātaṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti mayā akkhātaṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti mayā akkhātaṃ’’|

‘‘kasmā  caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā akkhātaṃ? aitañhi, bhikkhavai, atthasaṃhitaṃ aitaṃ ādibrahmacariyakaṃ aitaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati; tasmā taṃ mayā akkhātaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. khadirapattasuttaṃ

1102. ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ anabhisamaic‍ca, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ anabhisamaic‍ca, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ anabhisamaic‍ca, dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ anabhisamaic‍ca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ khadirapattānaṃ vā saralapattānaṃ palāsapattānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā āmalakapattānaṃ vā puṭaṃ  karitvā udakaṃ vā tālapattaṃ vā āharissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ anabhisamaic‍ca…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ anabhisamaic‍ca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaic‍ca, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaic‍ca, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaic‍ca, dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaic‍ca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’ti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ padumapattānaṃ vā palāsapattānaṃ vā māluvapattānaṃ vā puṭaṃ karitvā udakaṃ vā tālapattaṃ vā āharissāmī’ti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaic‍ca  …pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ  paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaic‍ca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’ti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. daṇḍasuttaṃ

1103. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, daṇḍau uparivaihāsaṃ khittau sakimpi mūlaina nipatati, sakimpi aggaina nipatati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jānīvaraṇā sattā taṇhāsaṃyaujanā sandhāvantā saṃsarantā taṇhāsaṃyaujanabandhā sandhāvatā (ka॰) sakimpi asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ gacchanti, sakimpi parasmā laukā imaṃ laukaṃ āgacchanti| taṃ kissa haitu? adiṭṭhattā, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. cailasuttaṃ

1104. ‘‘ādittai, bhikkhavai, cailai vā sīsai vā kimassa karaṇīya’’nti? ‘‘ādittai, bhantai, cailai vā sīsai vā, tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīya’’nti|

‘‘ādittaṃ, bhikkhavai, cailaṃ vā sīsaṃ vā ajjhupaikkhitvā amanasikaritvā anabhisamaitānaṃ catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamayāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau  ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati  ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. sattisatasuttaṃ

1105. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau vassasatāyukau vassasatajīvī| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aihambhau purisa, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ taṃ sattisataina hanissanti, majjhanhikasamayaṃ sattisataina hanissanti, sāyanhasamayaṃ sattisataina hanissanti| sau khau tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, divasai divasai tīhi tīhi sattisataihi hañ‍ñamānau vassasatāyukau vassasatajīvī vassasatassa ac‍cayaina anabhisamaitāni cattāri ariyasac‍cāni  abhisamaissasī’’’ti|

‘‘atthavasikaina, bhikkhavai, kulaputtaina alaṃ upagantuṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau; pubbā kauṭi nappañ‍ñāyati sattippahārānaṃ asippahārānaṃ usuppahārānaṃ pharasuppahārānaṃ asippahārānaṃ pharasuppahārānaṃ (ka॰)| aivañ‍caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, assa| na khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saha dukkhaina, saha daumanassaina catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ abhisamayaṃ vadāmi; api cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sahāva sukhaina, sahāva saumanassaina catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ abhisamayaṃ vadāmi| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pāṇasuttaṃ

1106. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau yaṃ imasmiṃ jambudīpai tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ  tacchaitvā aikajjhaṃ saṃharaiyya; aikajjhaṃ saṃharitvā sūlaṃ karaiyya| sūlaṃ karitvā yai mahāsamuddai mahantakā  pāṇā tai mahantakaisu sūlaisu āvunaiyya, yai mahāsamuddai majjhimakā pāṇā tai majjhimakaisu sūlaisu āvunaiyya, yai mahāsamuddai sukhumakā pāṇā tai sukhumakaisu sūlaisu āvunaiyya| apariyādin‍nā ca, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai auḷārikā pāṇā assu|

‘‘atha imasmiṃ jambudīpai tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya| itau bahutarā khau, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai sukhumakā pāṇā, yai na sukarā sūlaisu āvunituṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? sukhumattā, bhikkhavai, attabhāvassa| aivaṃ mahā khau, bhikkhavai, apāyau| aivaṃ mahantasmā khau, bhikkhavai, apāyasmā parimuttau diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamasūriyasuttaṃ

1107. ‘‘sūriyassa suriyassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – aruṇuggaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamayāya aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yadidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi| tassaitaṃ bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānissati…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānissati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti  yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyasūriyasuttaṃ

1108. ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, candimasūriyā laukai nuppaj‍janti, naiva tāva mahatau ālaukassa pātubhāvau hauti mahatau aubhāsassa| andhatamaṃ tadā hauti andhakāratimisā| naiva tāva rattindivā rattidivā (ka॰) pañ‍ñāyanti, na māsaddhamāsā pañ‍ñāyanti, na utusaṃvaccharā pañ‍ñāyanti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, candimasūriyā laukai uppaj‍janti, atha mahatau ālaukassa pātubhāvau hauti mahatau aubhāsassa| naiva  andhakāratamaṃ tadā hauti na andhakāratimisā| atha rattindivā pañ‍ñāyanti, māsaddhamāsā pañ‍ñāyanti, utusaṃvaccharā pañ‍ñāyanti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yāvakīvañ‍ca tathāgatau laukai nuppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, naiva tāva mahatau ālaukassa pātubhāvau hauti mahatau aubhāsassa| andhatamaṃ tadā hauti andhakāratimisā| naiva tāva catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ ācikkhaṇā hauti daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, atha mahatau ālaukassa pātubhāvau hauti mahatau aubhāsassa| naiva andhatamaṃ tadā hauti na andhakāratimisā| atha khau catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ ācikkhaṇā hauti daisanā pañ‍ñāpanā paṭṭhapanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. indakhīlasuttaṃ

1109. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, tai añ‍ñassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā mukhaṃ ul‍laukainti aulaukainti (sī॰ syā॰) – ‘ayaṃ nūna bhavaṃ jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passatī’’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tūlapicu vā kappāsapicu vā lahukau vātūpādānau samai bhūmibhāgai  nikkhittau| tamainaṃ puratthimau  vātau pacchimaina saṃharaiyya, pacchimau vātau puratthimaina saṃharaiyya, uttarau vātau dakkhiṇaina saṃharaiyya, dakkhiṇau vātau uttaraina saṃharaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? lahukattā, bhikkhavai, kappāsapicunau| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, tai añ‍ñassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā mukhaṃ ul‍laukainti – ‘ayaṃ nūna bhavaṃ jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passatī’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? adiṭṭhattā, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai na añ‍ñassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa  vā mukhaṃ ul‍laukainti – ‘ayaṃ nūna bhavaṃ jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passatī’’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ayaukhīlau vā indakhīlau vā gambhīranaimau sunikhātau acalau asampakampī| puratthimāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi, naiva saṅkampaiyya naiva naṃ saṅkampaiyya (sī॰ pī॰) na sampakampaiyya na sampacālaiyya; pacchimāya caipi disāya…pai॰… uttarāya caipi disāya…pai॰… dakkhiṇāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi, naiva saṅkampaiyya na sampakampaiyya na sampacālaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? gambhīrattā, bhikkhavai, naimassa sunikhātattā indakhīlassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai ca khau kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti…pai॰… ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai na añ‍ñassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā mukhaṃ ul‍laukainti – ‘ayaṃ nūna bhavaṃ jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passatī’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? sudiṭṭhattā, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa  ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. vādatthikasuttaṃ

1110. ‘‘yau  hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, puratthimāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā vādatthikau  vādagavaisī – ‘vādamassa āraupaissāmī’ti, taṃ vata sahadhammaina saṅkampaissati vā sampakampaissati vā sampacālaissati vāti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| pacchimāya caipi disāya…pai॰… uttarāya caipi disāya…pai॰… dakkhiṇāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā vādatthikau vādagavaisī – ‘vādamassa āraupaissāmī’ti, taṃ vata sahadhammaina saṅkampaissati vā sampakampaissati vā sampacālaissati vāti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, silāyūpau sauḷasa kuk‍kukau| tassassu aṭṭha kuk‍ku haiṭṭhā naimaṅgamā, aṭṭha kuk‍ku uparinaimassa| puratthimāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi, naiva saṅkampaiyya na sampakampaiyya na sampacālaiyya; pacchimāya caipi disāya…pai॰… uttarāya caipi disāya…pai॰… dakkhiṇāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi, naiva saṅkampaiyya na sampakampaiyya na sampacālaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? gambhīrattā, bhikkhavai, naimassa sunikhātattā silāyūpassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yau hi kauci bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; puratthimāya  caipi disāya āgacchaiyya samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā vādatthikau vādagavaisī ‘vādamassa āraupaissāmī’ti, taṃ vata sahadhammaina saṅkampaissati vā sampakampaissati vā sampacālaissati vāti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| pacchimāya caipi disāya…pai॰… uttarāya  caipi disāya…pai॰… dakkhiṇāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā vādatthikau vādagavaisī – ‘vādamassa āraupaissāmī’ti, taṃ vata sahadhammaina saṅkampaissati vā sampakampaissati vā sampacālaissati vāti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? sudiṭṭhattā, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sīsapāvanavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sīsapā  khadirau daṇḍau, cailā sattisataina ca|

pāṇā suriyūpamā dvaidhā, indakhīlau ca vādinauti||

5. papātavaggau

1. laukacintāsuttaṃ

1111. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarau purisau rājagahā nikkhamitvā ‘laukacintaṃ  cintaissāmī’ti yaina sumāgadhā paukkharaṇī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sumāgadhāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai nisīdi laukacintaṃ cintaintau| addasā khau, bhikkhavai, sau purisau sumāgadhāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai caturaṅginiṃ sainaṃ caturaṅginisainaṃ (ka॰) bhisamuḷālaṃ bhisamūlālaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) pavisantaṃ| disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘ummattausmi nāmāhaṃ, vicaitausmi nāmāhaṃ! yaṃ laukai natthi taṃ mayā diṭṭha’’’nti|

‘‘atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, purisau nagaraṃ pavisitvā mahājanakāyassa āraucaisi – ‘ummattausmi nāmāhaṃ, bhantai, vicaitausmi nāmāhaṃ, bhantai! yaṃ laukai natthi taṃ mayā diṭṭha’’’nti| ‘‘kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, ummattau kathaṃ vicaitau? kiñ‍ca laukai natthi yaṃ tayā diṭṭha’’nti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, rājagahā nikkhamitvā ‘laukacintaṃ cintaissāmī’ti yaina sumāgadhā paukkharaṇī tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā sumāgadhāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai nisīdiṃ laukacintaṃ cintaintau| addasaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, sumāgadhāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai caturaṅginiṃ sainaṃ bhisamuḷālaṃ  pavisantaṃ| aivaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, ummattau aivaṃ vicaitau| idañ‍ca laukai natthi yaṃ mayā  diṭṭha’’nti| ‘‘taggha tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, ummattau taggha vicaitau| idañ‍ca laukai natthi yaṃ tayā diṭṭha’’nti|

‘‘taṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, sau purisau bhūtaṃyaiva addasa, nau abhūtaṃ| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivāsurasaṅgāmau samupabyūḷhau ahausi| tasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, saṅgāmai daivā jiniṃsu, asurā parājiniṃsu| parājitā  ca  khau, bhikkhavai, asurā bhītā bhisamuḷālaina asurapuraṃ pavisiṃsu daivānaṃyaiva mauhayamānā|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, mā laukacintaṃ cintaitha – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘antavā laukau’ti vā ‘anantavā laukau’ti vā, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā| taṃ kissa haitu? naisā, bhikkhavai, cintā atthasaṃhitā nādibrahmacariyakā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘cintaintā khau tumhai, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti cintaiyyātha…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti cintaiyyātha| taṃ kissa haitu? aisā, bhikkhavai, cintā atthasaṃhitā aisā ādibrahmacariyakā aisā nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya  sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. papātasuttaṃ

1112. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū  āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāma, bhikkhavai, yaina paṭibhānakūṭau tainupasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| atha  khau bhagavā sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ yaina paṭibhānakūṭau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu paṭibhānakūṭai mahantaṃ papātaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahā vatāyaṃ, bhantai, papātau subhayānakau, bhantai, papātau| atthi nu khau, bhantai, imamhā papātā añ‍ñau papātau mahantatarau ca bhayānakatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, bhikkhu, imamhā papātā añ‍ñau papātau mahantatarau ca bhayānakatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘katamau  pana, bhantai, imamhā papātā añ‍ñau papātau mahantatarau ca bhayānakatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, tai jātisaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu abhiramanti, jarāsaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu abhiramanti, maraṇasaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu abhiramanti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu abhiramanti| tai jātisaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu abhiratā jarāsaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu abhiratā maraṇasaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu abhiratā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu abhiratā jātisaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai abhisaṅkharaunti, jarāsaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai abhisaṅkharaunti, maraṇasaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai abhisaṅkharaunti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai abhisaṅkharaunti| tai jātisaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai abhisaṅkharitvā jarāsaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai abhisaṅkharitvā maraṇasaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai abhisaṅkharitvā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai abhisaṅkharitvā jātipapātampi papatanti, jarāpapātampi papatanti, maraṇapapātampi papatanti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsapapātampi  papatanti| tai na parimuc‍canti jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| ‘na parimuc‍canti dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai jātisaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu nābhiramanti, jarāsaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu nābhiramanti, maraṇasaṃvattanikaisu  saṅkhāraisu nābhiramanti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikaisu  saṅkhāraisu nābhiramanti| tai jātisaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu anabhiratā, jarāsaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu anabhiratā, maraṇasaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu anabhiratā, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu anabhiratā, jātisaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai nābhisaṅkharaunti, jarāsaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai nābhisaṅkharaunti, maraṇasaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai nābhisaṅkharaunti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikaipi  saṅkhārai nābhisaṅkharaunti| tai jātisaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai anabhisaṅkharitvā, jarāsaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai anabhisaṅkharitvā, maraṇasaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai anabhisaṅkharitvā, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsasaṃvattanikaipi saṅkhārai anabhisaṅkharitvā, jātipapātampi nappapatanti, jarāpapātampi nappapatanti, maraṇapapātampi nappapatanti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsapapātampi nappapatanti| tai parimuc‍canti jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| ‘parimuc‍canti dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. mahāpariḷāhasuttaṃ

1113. ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, mahāpariḷāhau nāma nirayau| tattha yaṃ kiñ‍ci cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, aniṭṭharūpañ‍ñaiva passati  nau iṭṭharūpaṃ; akantarūpañ‍ñaiva passati nau kantarūpaṃ; amanāparūpañ‍ñaiva passati nau manāparūpaṃ| yaṃ kiñ‍ci sautaina saddaṃ suṇāti…pai॰… yaṃ  kiñ‍ci kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusati…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci manasā dhammaṃ vijānāti, aniṭṭharūpañ‍ñaiva vijānāti nau iṭṭharūpaṃ; akantarūpañ‍ñaiva vijānāti nau kantarūpaṃ; amanāparūpañ‍ñaiva vijānāti nau manāparūpa’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahā vata sau, bhantai, pariḷāhau, sumahā vata sau, bhantai, pariḷāhau! atthi nu khau, bhantai, aitamhā pariḷāhā añ‍ñau pariḷāhau  mahantatarau caiva bhayānakatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, bhikkhu, aitamhā pariḷāhā añ‍ñau pariḷāhau mahantatarau ca bhayānakatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, aitamhā pariḷāhā añ‍ñau pariḷāhau mahantatarau ca bhayānakatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, tai jātisaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu abhiramanti…pai॰… abhiratā…pai॰… abhisaṅkharaunti…pai॰… abhisaṅkharitvā jātipariḷāhainapi pariḍayhanti, jarāpariḷāhainapi pariḍayhanti, maraṇapariḷāhainapi pariḍayhanti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsapariḷāhainapi pariḍayhanti| tai na parimuc‍canti jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| ‘na parimuc‍canti dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti| tai jātisaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu nābhiramanti…pai॰… anabhiratā…pai॰… nābhisaṅkharaunti…pai॰… anabhisaṅkharitvā jātipariḷāhainapi na pariḍayhanti, jarāpariḷāhainapi na pariḍayhanti, maraṇapariḷāhainapi na pariḍayhanti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsapariḷāhainapi na pariḍayhanti| tai parimuc‍canti jātiyā jarāya  maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| ‘parimuc‍canti dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. kūṭāgārasuttaṃ

1114. ‘‘yau hi, bhikkhavai yau ca khau bhikkhavai (syā॰ ka॰), aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ anabhisamaic‍ca…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ anabhisamaic‍ca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ kūṭāgārassa haiṭṭhimaṃ gharaṃ akaritvā uparimaṃ gharaṃ āraupaissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ anabhisamaic‍ca…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ anabhisamaic‍ca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaic‍ca…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaic‍ca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’ti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ kūṭāgārassa haiṭṭhimaṃ gharaṃ  karitvā uparimaṃ gharaṃ āraupaissāmī’ti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaic‍ca…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisamaic‍ca sammā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’ti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. vālasuttaṃ

1115. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau sambahulai licchavikumārakai santhāgārai upāsanaṃ karauntai, dūratauva sukhumaina tāḷacchiggaḷaina asanaṃ atipātaintai, pauṅkhānupauṅkhaṃ paukhānupaukhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) avirādhitaṃ| disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘sikkhitā vatimai licchavikumārakā, susikkhitā vatimai licchavikumārakā; yatra hi nāma dūratauva sukhumaina tāḷacchiggaḷaina asanaṃ atipātaissanti pauṅkhānupauṅkhaṃ avirādhita’’nti|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| addasaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai sambahulai licchavikumārakai santhāgārai upāsanaṃ karauntai dūratauva sukhumaina tāḷacchiggaḷaina asanaṃ atipātaintai pauṅkhānupauṅkhaṃ avirādhitaṃ’| disvāna mai aitadahausi – ‘‘sikkhitā vatimai licchavikumārakā, susikkhitā vatimai licchavikumārakā; yatra hi nāma dūratauva sukhumaina tāḷacchiggaḷaina asanaṃ atipātaissanti pauṅkhānupauṅkhaṃ avirādhita’’nti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ānanda, katamaṃ nu khau duk‍karataraṃ vā durabhisambhavataraṃ vā – yau dūratauva sukhumaina tāḷacchiggaḷaina asanaṃ atipātaiyya pauṅkhānupauṅkhaṃ avirādhitaṃ, yau vā sattadhā bhin‍nassa vālassa kauṭiyā kauṭiṃ paṭivijjhaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, duk‍karatarañ‍caiva durabhisambhavatarañ‍ca yau  vā yau (sī॰) sattadhā bhin‍nassa vālassa kauṭiyā kauṭiṃ paṭivijjhaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘atha khau atha khau tai (syā॰ kaṃ॰), ānanda, duppaṭivijjhataraṃ paṭivijjhanti, yai ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ paṭivijjhanti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ paṭivijjhanti’’|

‘‘tasmātihānanda, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. andhakārasuttaṃ

1116. ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, laukantarikā aghā asaṃvutā andhakārā andhakāratimisā, yatthamimaisaṃ candimasūriyānaṃ aivaṃmahiddhikānaṃ aivaṃ mahānubhāvānaṃ ābhāya nānubhauntī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahā vata sau, bhantai, andhakārau, sumahā vata sau, bhantai, andhakārau! atthi  nu khau, bhantai, aitamhā andhakārā añ‍ñau  andhakārau mahantatarau ca bhayānakatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, bhikkhu, aitamhā andhakārā añ‍ñau andhakārau mahantatarau ca bhayānakatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, aitamhā andhakārā añ‍ñau andhakārau mahantatarau ca bhayānakatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhu, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī  paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, tai jātisaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu abhiramanti…pai॰… abhiratā…pai॰… abhisaṅkharaunti…pai॰… abhisaṅkharitvā jātandhakārampi papatanti, jarandhakārampi papatanti, maraṇandhakārampi papatanti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsandhakārampi papatanti| tai na parimuc‍canti jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| ‘na parimuc‍canti dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhu, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai jātisaṃvattanikaisu saṅkhāraisu nābhiramanti…pai॰… anabhiratā…pai॰… nābhisaṅkharaunti…pai॰… anabhisaṅkharitvā jātandhakārampi nappapatanti, jarandhakārampi nappapatanti, maraṇandhakārampi nappapatanti, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsandhakārampi nappapatanti| tai parimuc‍canti jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi  dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| ‘parimuc‍canti dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamachiggaḷayugasuttaṃ

1117. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau mahāsamuddai aikacchiggaḷaṃ yugaṃ pakkhipaiyya| tatrāpissa kāṇau kacchapau| sau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina sakiṃ sakiṃ ummuj‍jaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu khau kāṇau kacchapau vassasatassa vassasatassa  ac‍cayaina sakiṃ sakiṃ ummuj‍jantau amusmiṃ aikacchiggaḷai yugai gīvaṃ pavaisaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘yadi  nūna, bhantai, kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayainā’’ti|

‘‘khippataraṃ khau sau, bhikkhavai, kāṇau kacchapau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina sakiṃ sakiṃ ummuj‍jantau amusmiṃ aikacchiggaḷai yugai gīvaṃ pavaisaiyya, na tvaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sakiṃ vinipātagataina bālaina vinītagataina bahulaina (ka॰) manussattaṃ vadāmi’’|

taṃ kissa haitu? na haittha, bhikkhavai, atthi dhammacariyā, samacariyā, kusalakiriyā, puñ‍ñakiriyā| añ‍ñamañ‍ñakhādikā aittha, bhikkhavai, vattati dubbalakhādikā| taṃ kissa haitu? adiṭṭhattā, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyachiggaḷayugasuttaṃ

1118. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ mahāpathavī aikaudakā assa| tatra  purisau aikacchiggaḷaṃ yugaṃ pakkhipaiyya| tamainaṃ puratthimau vātau pacchimaina saṃharaiyya, pacchimau vātau puratthimaina saṃharaiyya, uttarau vātau dakkhiṇaina saṃharaiyya, dakkhiṇau vātau uttaraina saṃharaiyya| tatrassa kāṇau kacchapau| sau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina sakiṃ sakiṃ ummuj‍jaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu khau kāṇau kacchapau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina sakiṃ sakiṃ ummuj‍jantau  amusmiṃ aikacchiggaḷai yugai gīvaṃ pavaisaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘adhic‍camidaṃ, bhantai, yaṃ sau kāṇau kacchapau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina sakiṃ sakiṃ ummuj‍jantau amusmiṃ aikacchiggaḷai yugai gīvaṃ pavaisaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ adhic‍camidaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaṃ manussattaṃ labhati| aivaṃ adhic‍camidaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaṃ tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| aivaṃ adhic‍camidaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaṃ tathāgatappavaiditau  dhammavinayau laukai dibbati| tassidaṃ tayidaṃ (?), bhikkhavai, manussattaṃ laddhaṃ, tathāgatau laukai uppan‍nau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, tathāgatappavaiditau ca dhammavinayau laukai dibbati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamasinairupabbatarājasuttaṃ

1119. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sinairussa pabbatarājassa satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhipaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yā vā yā ca satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā, yau vā yau ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.84 sinairupabbatarājā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ – sinairupabbatarājā; appamattikā satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā| saṅkhampi na upainti, upanidhampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upainti sinairupabbatarājānaṃ upanidhāya satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa  abhisamaitāvinau aitadaiva bahutaraṃ dukkhaṃ yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ; appamattakaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ| saṅkhampi na upaiti, upanidhampi na upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti, purimaṃ dukkhakkhandhaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ upanidhāya yadidaṃ sattakkhattuparamatā; yau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyasinairupabbatarājasuttaṃ

1120. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sinairupabbatarājāyaṃ parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, ṭhapaitvā satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yaṃ vā sinairussa pabbatarājassa parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ, yā vā satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā  avasiṭṭhā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ sinairussa pabbatarājassa yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ; appamattikā satta  muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā avasiṭṭhā| saṅkhampi na upainti, upanidhampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upainti sinairussa pabbatarājassa parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ upanidhāya satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā avasiṭṭhā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa abhisamaitāvinau aitadaiva bahutaraṃ dukkhaṃ  yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ; appamattakaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ| saṅkhampi na upaiti, upanidhampi na upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti, purimaṃ dukkhakkhandhaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ upanidhāya yadidaṃ sattakkhattuparamatā; yau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

papātavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cintā papātau pariḷāhau, kūṭaṃ vālandhakārau ca|
