chiggaḷaina ca dvai vuttā, sinairu aparai duvaiti||

6. abhisamayavaggau

1. nakhasikhasuttaṃ

1121. atha  khau bhagavā parittaṃ nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsuṃ āraupaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yau vāyaṃ mayā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau, ayaṃ vā mahāpathavī’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ yadidaṃ – mahāpathavī; appamattakāyaṃ bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau| saṅkhampi na upaiti, upanidhampi na  upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti mahāpathaviṃ upanidhāya bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa abhisamaitāvinau aitadaiva bahutaraṃ dukkhaṃ yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ; appamattakaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ| saṅkhampi na upaiti, upanidhampi na upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti purimaṃ dukkhakkhandhaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ upanidhāya yadidaṃ sattakkhattuparamatā; yau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paukkharaṇīsuttaṃ

1122. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, paukkharaṇī pañ‍ñāsayaujanāni āyāmaina, pañ‍ñāsayaujanāni vitthāraina, pañ‍ñāsayaujanāni ubbaidhaina, puṇṇā udakassa samatittikā kākapaiyyā| tatau purisau kusaggaina  udakaṃ uddharaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yaṃ vā kusaggaina ubbhataṃ, yaṃ vā paukkharaṇiyā udaka’’nti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ – paukkharaṇiyā udakaṃ; appamattakaṃ kusaggaina udakaṃ ubbhataṃ| saṅkhampi na upaiti, upanidhampi na upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti paukkharaṇiyā udakaṃ upanidhāya kusaggaina udakaṃ ubbhata’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa…pai॰… yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamasaṃbhaij‍jasuttaṃ

1123. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yatthimā mahānadiyau saṃsandanti samainti, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, tatau purisau dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni uddharaiyya| taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatāni, yaṃ vā saṃbhaij‍jaudaka’’nti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ – saṃbhaij‍jaudakaṃ; appamattakāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatāni| saṅkhampi na upainti, upanidhampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upainti saṃbhaij‍jaudakaṃ upanidhāya dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatānī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa…pai॰… yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyasaṃbhaij‍jasuttaṃ

1124. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yatthimā mahānadiyau saṃsandanti samainti, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, taṃ udakaṃ parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, ṭhapaitvā dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yaṃ vā saṃbhaij‍jaudakaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ, yāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhānī’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ saṃbhaij‍jaudakaṃ yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ; appamattakāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhāni| saṅkhampi na upainti, upanidhampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upainti saṃbhaij‍jaudakaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ upanidhāya dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhānī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa…pai॰… yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamamahāpathavīsuttaṃ

1125. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau mahāpathaviyā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā upanikkhipaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yā vā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā upanikkhittā, ayaṃ vā mahāpathavī’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ – mahāpathavī; appamattikā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā upanikkhittā| saṅkhampi na upainti, upanidhampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upainti mahāpathaviṃ upanidhāya satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā upanikkhittā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa…pai॰… yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyamahāpathavīsuttaṃ

1126. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāpathavī parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya ṭhapaitvā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yaṃ vā mahāpathaviyā parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ, yā vā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā avasiṭṭhā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ mahāpathaviyā yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ; appamattikā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā avasiṭṭhā| saṅkhampi na upainti, upanidhampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upainti mahāpathaviyā parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ upanidhāya satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā avasiṭṭhā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa…pai॰… yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamamahāsamuddasuttaṃ

1127. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau mahāsamuddatau dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni uddharaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatāni, yaṃ vā mahāsamuddai udaka’’nti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ – mahāsamuddai udakaṃ; appamattakāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatāni| saṅkhampi na upainti, upanidhampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upainti mahāsamuddai udakaṃ upanidhāya dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatānī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa…pai॰… yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyamahāsamuddasuttaṃ

1128. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai udakaṃ parikkhayaṃ mahāsamuddau parikkhayaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.81 pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya ṭhapaitvā dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yaṃ vā mahāsamuddai udakaṃ  parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ, yāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhānī’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ mahāsamuddai udakaṃ yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ; appamattakāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhāni| saṅkhampi  na upainti, upanidhampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upainti mahāsamuddai udakaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ  pariyādin‍naṃ upanidhāya dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhānī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa…pai॰… yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamapabbatūpamasuttaṃ

1129. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau himavatau pabbatarājassa satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhipaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yā vā satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā, ayaṃ vā himavā pabbatarājā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ – himavā pabbatarājā; appamattikā satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā| saṅkhampi na upainti, upanidhampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upainti himavantaṃ pabbatarājānaṃ upanidhāya satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa…pai॰… yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyapabbatūpamasuttaṃ

1130. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, himavā pabbatarājā parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, ṭhapaitvā satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yaṃ vā himavatau pabbatarājassa  parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ, yā vā satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā avasiṭṭhā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ himavatau pabbatarājassa yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ; appamattikā satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā avasiṭṭhā| saṅkhampi na upainti, upanidhampi na upainti, kalabhāgampi na upainti himavatau pabbatarājassa parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ upanidhāya satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā avasiṭṭhā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa abhisamaitāvinau aitadaiva bahutaraṃ dukkhaṃ  yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ; appamattakaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ| saṅkhampi na upaiti, upanidhampi na upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti purimaṃ dukkhakkhandhaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ upanidhāya  yadidaṃ sattakkhattuparamatā; yau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

abhisamayavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

nakhasikhā paukkharaṇī, saṃbhaij‍ja aparai duvai|

pathavī dvai samuddā dvai, dvaimā ca pabbatūpamāti||

7. paṭhamaāmakadhañ‍ñapaiyyālavaggau

1. añ‍ñatrasuttaṃ

1131. atha  khau bhagavā parittaṃ nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsuṃ āraupaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yau vāyaṃ mayā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau, ayaṃ vā mahāpathavī’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ – mahāpathavī; appamattakāyaṃ bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau| saṅkhampi na upaiti, upanidhampi na upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti mahāpathaviṃ upanidhāya bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appamattakā tai sattā yai manussaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai añ‍ñatra manussaihi manussaisu (pī॰ ka॰) pac‍cājāyanti| taṃ kissa haitu? adiṭṭhattā, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pac‍cantasuttaṃ

1132. atha khau bhagavā parittaṃ nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsuṃ āraupaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yau vāyaṃ  mayā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau, ayaṃ vā mahāpathavī’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ – mahāpathavī; appamattakāyaṃ bhagavatā  parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau| saṅkhampi na upaiti, upanidhampi na upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti mahāpathaviṃ upanidhāya bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appamattakā tai sattā yai majjhimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājāyanti aviñ‍ñātāraisu milakkhaisu milakkhūsu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) …pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. pañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

1133. … ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai pana ariyaina pañ‍ñācakkhunā saman‍nāgatā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai avij‍jāgatā sammuḷhā…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. surāmairayasuttaṃ

1134. … ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā apaṭiviratā…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. audakasuttaṃ

1135. …  ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai thalajā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai udakajā| taṃ kissa haitu…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. mattaiyyasuttaṃ

1136. … ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai mattaiyyā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai amattaiyyā…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paittaiyyasuttaṃ

1137. … ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai paittaiyyā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai apaittaiyyā…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. sāmañ‍ñasuttaṃ

1138. … ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai sāmañ‍ñā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai asāmañ‍ñā…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. brahmañ‍ñasuttaṃ

1139. … ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai brahmañ‍ñā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai abrahmañ‍ñā…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. pacāyikasuttaṃ

1140. … ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai kulai  jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai kulai ajaiṭṭhāpacāyinauti akulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) …pai॰…| dasamaṃ|

paṭhamaāmakadhañ‍ñapaiyyālavaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

añ‍ñatra pac‍cantaṃ pañ‍ñā, surāmairayaaudakā|

mattaiyya paittaiyyā cāpi, sāmañ‍ñaṃ brahmapacāyikanti||

8. dutiyaāmakadhañ‍ñapaiyyālavaggau

1. pāṇātipātasuttaṃ

1141. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai pāṇātipātā appaṭiviratā| taṃ kissa haitu? …pai॰…| paṭhamaṃ|

2. adin‍nādānasuttaṃ

1142. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai adin‍nādānā  paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai adin‍nādānā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. kāmaisumicchācārasuttaṃ

1143. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai kāmaisumicchācārā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. musāvādasuttaṃ

1144. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai musāvādā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai musāvādā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. paisuñ‍ñasuttaṃ

1145. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai pisuṇāya vācāya appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pharusavācāsuttaṃ

1146. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai pharusāya vācāya appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. samphappalāpasuttaṃ

1147. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai samphappalāpā  paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai samphappalāpā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. bījagāmasuttaṃ

1148. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārabbhā (ka॰) paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vikālabhaujanasuttaṃ

1149. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai vikālabhaujanā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai vikālabhaujanā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. gandhavilaipanasuttaṃ

1150. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| dasamaṃ|

dutiyaāmakadhañ‍ñapaiyyālavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

pāṇaṃ adin‍naṃ kāmaisu, musāvādañ‍ca paisuñ‍ñaṃ|

pharusaṃ samphappalāpaṃ, bījañ‍ca vikālaṃ gandhanti||

9. tatiyaāmakadhañ‍ñapaiyyālavaggau

1. nac‍cagītasuttaṃ

1151. …pai॰…|

‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā  sattā yai nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā appaṭiviratā| taṃ kissa haitu…pai॰…| paṭhamaṃ|

2. uc‍cāsayanasuttaṃ

1152. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. jātarūparajatasuttaṃ

1153. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. āmakadhañ‍ñasuttaṃ

1154. …pai॰…  ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. āmakamaṃsasuttaṃ

1155. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kumārikasuttaṃ

1156. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā itthikumārikāpaṭiggahaṇā (ka॰) paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dāsidāsasuttaṃ

1157. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā dāsīdāsapaṭiggahaṇā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. ajaiḷakasuttaṃ

1158. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā  paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kuk‍kuṭasūkarasuttaṃ

1159. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. hatthigavassasuttaṃ

1160. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai hatthigavassavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā hatthigavassavaḷavāpaṭiggahaṇā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai hatthigavassavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| dasamaṃ|

tatiyaāmakadhañ‍ñapaiyyālavaggau navamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

nac‍caṃ sayanaṃ rajataṃ, dhañ‍ñaṃ maṃsaṃ kumārikā|

dāsī ajaiḷakañ‍caiva, kuk‍kuṭasūkarahatthīti||

10. catutthaāmakadhañ‍ñapaiyyālavaggau

1. khaittavatthusuttaṃ

1161. …pai॰…  ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| paṭhamaṃ|

2. kayavik‍kayasuttaṃ

1162. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai kayavik‍kayā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. dūtaiyyasuttaṃ

1163. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai dūtaiyyapahinagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai dūtaiyyapahinagamanānuyaugā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. tulākūṭasuttaṃ

1164. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. uk‍kauṭanasuttaṃ

1165. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā  uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāviyaugā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā appaṭiviratā…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6-11. chaidanādisuttaṃ

1166-1171. …pai॰… aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā sāhasākārā (ka॰) paṭiviratā; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā appaṭiviratā| taṃ kissa haitu? adiṭṭhattā bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

catutthaāmakadhañ‍ñapaiyyālavaggau dasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

khaittaṃ  kāyaṃ dūtaiyyañ‍ca, tulākūṭaṃ uk‍kauṭanaṃ|

chaidanaṃ vadhabandhanaṃ, viparālaupaṃ sāhasanti||

11. pañ‍cagatipaiyyālavaggau

1. manussacutinirayasuttaṃ

1172. atha khau bhagavā parittaṃ nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsuṃ āraupaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ – yau vāyaṃ mayā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau, ayaṃ vā mahāpathavī’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ – mahāpathavī; appamattakāyaṃ  bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau| saṅkhampi na upaiti, upanidhampi na upaiti, kalabhāgampi na upaiti mahāpathaviṃ upanidhāya bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau’’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai manussā cutā manussaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai manussā cutā nirayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| paṭhamaṃ|

2. manussacutitiracchānasuttaṃ

1173. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai manussā cutā manussaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai manussā cutā tiracchānayauniyā pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. manussacutipaittivisayasuttaṃ

1174. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai manussā cutā manussaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai manussā cutā paittivisayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4-5-6. manussacutidaivanirayādisuttaṃ

1175-1177. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai manussā cutā daivaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva  bahutarā sattā yai  manussā cutā nirayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… tiracchānayauniyā pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… paittivisayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7-9. daivacutinirayādisuttaṃ

1178-1180. …pai॰…  ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai daivā cutā daivaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai daivā cutā nirayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… tiracchānayauniyā pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… paittivisayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10-12. daivamanussanirayādisuttaṃ

1181-1183. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai daivā cutā manussaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai daivā cutā nirayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… tiracchānayauniyā pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… paittivisayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| dvādasamaṃ|

13-15. nirayamanussanirayādisuttaṃ

1184-1186. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai nirayā cutā manussaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai nirayā cutā nirayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… tiracchānayauniyā pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… paittivisayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| pan‍narasamaṃ|

16-18. nirayadaivanirayādisuttaṃ

1187-1189. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai nirayā cutā daivaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai nirayā cutā nirayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… tiracchānayauniyā pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… paittivisayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| aṭṭhārasamaṃ|

19-21. tiracchānamanussanirayādisuttaṃ

1190-1192. …pai॰…  ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai tiracchānayauniyā cutā manussaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā  yai tiracchānayauniyā cutā nirayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… tiracchānayauniyā  pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… paittivisayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| aikavīsatimaṃ|

22-24. tiracchānadaivanirayādisuttaṃ

1193-1195. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai tiracchānayauniyā cutā daivaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai tiracchānayauniyā cutā nirayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… tiracchānayauniyā pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… paittivisayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| catuvīsatimaṃ|

25-27. paittimanussanirayādisuttaṃ

1196-1198. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai paittivisayā cutā manussaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai paittivisayā cutā nirayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… tiracchānayauniyā pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… paittivisayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| sattavīsatimaṃ|

28-29. paittidaivanirayādisuttaṃ

1199-1200. …pai॰… ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai paittivisayā cutā daivaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai paittivisayā cutā nirayai pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰… aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai paittivisayā cutā daivaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai paittivisayā cutā tiracchānayauniyā pac‍cājāyanti…pai॰…| aikūnatiṃsatimaṃ|

30. paittidaivapaittivisayasuttaṃ

1201. …pai॰…  ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai paittivisayā cutā daivaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiva bahutarā sattā yai paittivisayā cutā paittivisayai pac‍cājāyanti| taṃ kissa haitu? adiṭṭhattā bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa, dukkhasamudayassa ariyasac‍cassa, dukkhaniraudhassa ariyasac‍cassa, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yaugau karaṇīyau, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yaugau karaṇīyau’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| tiṃsatimaṃ|

pañ‍cagatipaiyyālavaggau aikādasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

manussatau cutā chāpi, daivā cutā nirayatau|

tiracchānapaittivisayā, tiṃsamattau gativaggauti||

sac‍casaṃyuttaṃ dvādasamaṃ|


mahāvaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

maggabaujjhaṅgaṃ  satiyā, indriyaṃ sammappadhānaṃ|

baliddhipādānuruddhā, jhānānāpānasaṃyutaṃ|

sautāpatti sac‍cañ‍cāti, mahāvaggauti vuc‍catīti||

mahāvaggasaṃyuttapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
